### [1] [Evaluating Prompt Injection Defenses for Educational LLM Tutors: Security-Usability-Latency Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06669)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对教育LLM辅导系统中的提示注入防御问题，提出了一套多层防护管线的评估方法论。该管线结合确定性模式过滤、结构验证、上下文沙箱和会话级行为检查，在480条查询的基准测试中实现了46.34%的绕过率、0.00%的假阳性率和2.50ms的平均延迟，表明在优先保证教学可用性（零假阳性）的同时仍具备一定攻击抵抗能力。与NeMo Guardrails（0%绕过, 16.22% FPR, 1.3s延迟）和Prompt Guard（38.48%绕过, 3.60% FPR）的对比揭示了守卫设计中的客观权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育LLM辅导系统面临核心的AI对齐挑战：必须同时遵循用户意图、保持教学约束和安全策略。需要评估提示注入防御方法在该场景下的有效性。

Method: 提出多层防护管线（确定性模式过滤、结构验证、上下文沙箱、会话级行为检查），在480条查询（369注入, 111正常）的受控保留基准上评估，并采用分层Bootstrap置信区间、配对McNemar显著性检验等可复现基准协议进行对比。

Result: 所提管线：46.34%绕过率, 0.00% FPR, 2.50ms延迟；NeMo Guardrails：0%绕过, 16.22% FPR, 1.3s延迟；Prompt Guard：38.48%绕过, 3.60% FPR。

Conclusion: 守卫设计存在对抗鲁棒性、良性任务可用性与响应延迟之间的明确权衡；所提框架支持不同风险与可用性需求下的证据驱动守卫选择。

Abstract: Educational LLM tutors face a core AI alignment challenge: they must follow user intent while preserving pedagogical constraints and safety policies. We present an evaluation methodology for prompt-injection defenses in this setting, showing that guardrail design entails explicit trade-offs among adversarial robustness, benign-task usability, and response latency. We evaluate a domain-specific multi-layer safeguard pipeline combining deterministic pattern filters, structural validation, contextual sandboxing, and session-level behavioral checks. On a controlled holdout benchmark with 480 queries (369 injection, 111 benign), the pipeline reaches 46.34% bypass, 0.00% false positive rate, and 2.50 ms average latency -- an operating point that prioritizes pedagogical usability (zero false positives) while maintaining measurable attack resistance. We provide a reproducible benchmark protocol for head-to-head comparison under identical conditions, including stratified bootstrap confidence intervals, paired McNemar significance tests, and direct evaluation of Prompt Guard and NeMo Guardrails on the same split with unified instrumentation. Results expose operational trade-offs: NeMo reaches 0% bypass at 16.22% FPR and 1.3s latency, while Prompt Guard yields 38.48% bypass with 3.60% FPR. The framework supports evidence-based guardrail selection for AI tutoring systems under different institutional risk and usability requirements.

</details>


### [2] [When Routine Chats Turn Toxic: Unintended Long-Term State Poisoning in Personalized Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06731)
*Xiaoyu Xu,Minxin Du,Qipeng Xie,Haobin Ke,Qingqing Ye,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现了个性化LLM智能体在长期交互中存在的渐进式状态中毒风险，构建了包含350个场景的双语基准ULSPB，并提出了轻量级防御方法StateGuard，实验证明该防御能将危害分数降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化LLM智能体通过持久化跨会话状态来支持长期协作，但这种持久性引入了微妙的安全漏洞：日常的用户-智能体交互会逐渐重塑智能体的长期状态，不经意间削弱确认边界、扩大工具使用默认权限、并随时间推移升级自主行为，形成"非预期的长期状态投毒"风险。

Method: 提出了非预期长期状态投毒基准(ULSPB)，包含350个双语场景，涵盖5类辅助任务、7种交互模式、24轮日常交互及对应的单次注入对照。定义了以状态为中心的危害分数(HS)来量化授权漂移、工具使用升级和未检查的自主性。提出了StateGuard——一种轻量级后执行防御机制，在回写边界审计状态差异并选择性回滚危险编辑。

Result: 在OpenClaw框架和四种基座LLM上的实验表明，日常对话本身就能显著投毒长期状态（主要破坏记忆中心制品），且以真实用户交互为种子的评估证实该风险并非合成提示的伪影。StateGuard在所有评估模型上将HS降至接近零，降低假阴性率，假阳性率在安全优先的回写防御下可接受，且开销极小。

Conclusion: 个性化LLM智能体的长期状态投毒是真实且严重的安全风险，日常对话即可引发，而StateGuard能有效防御此类威胁。

Abstract: Personalized LLM agents maintain persistent cross-session state to support long-horizon collaboration. Yet, this persistence introduces a subtle but critical security vulnerability: routine user-agent interactions can gradually reshape an agent's long-term state, inadvertently weakening future confirmation boundaries, expanding tool-use defaults, and escalating autonomous behavior over time. We formalize this risk as \textbf{unintended long-term state poisoning}. To systematically study it, we introduce the \textbf{Unintended Long-Term State Poisoning Bench (ULSPB)}, a bilingual benchmark comprising $350$ settings spanning five assistance categories, seven interaction patterns, 24-turn routine interactions, and matched single-injection counterparts. Furthermore, we define the \emph{Harm Score} (HS), a state-centric metric that quantifies \emph{authorization drift}, \emph{tool-use escalation}, and \emph{unchecked autonomy}. Experiments on OpenClaw with four backbone LLMs demonstrate that, while single-injection is generally effective, routine conversations alone can substantially poison long-term state, primarily corrupting memory-centric artifacts. Evaluations seeded with real-world user interactions confirm that this risk is not a mere artifact of synthetic prompts. To mitigate this threat, we propose \textbf{StateGuard}, a lightweight, post-execution defense that audits state diffs at the writeback boundary and selectively rolls back dangerous edits. Across all evaluated models, StateGuard reduces HS to near zero and lowers false-negative rates, with acceptable high false-positive rates under a safety-first writeback defense and minimal overhead.

</details>


### [3] [A UEFI System with SPDM to Protect Against Unauthorized Device Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06744)
*Ágatha de Freitas,Marcos A. Simplicio,Bruno C. Albertini,Renan C. A. Alves*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并实现了一种基于SPDM协议的UEFI系统，用于验证PCIe和USB外设的硬件真实性，通过仿真评估显示性能开销（指令数+13%，CPU周期+8%）在安全增强背景下是可接受的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者可通过恶意外设（如PCIe/USB设备）入侵计算系统，而用户难以验证硬件真伪，现有系统缺乏在固件层面进行外设认证的机制。

Method: 基于安全协议与数据模型（SPDM），在UEFI固件中实现对PCIe和USB设备的身份认证；开发开源概念验证系统，通过内核虚拟化仿真收集启动过程中的指令数和CPU周期数来评估性能开销。

Result: 在固件执行阶段，指令数平均增加13%，CPU周期数平均增加8%。该性能开销在安全增强的背景下是可接受的。

Conclusion: 所提方案能有效限制仅允许授权的设备连接，保护系统免受恶意外设攻击；高安全需求机构可基于该概念验证根据自身需求定制系统。

Abstract: Attackers willing to compromise computing systems can use malicious peripherals as an attack vector, threatening users that cannot verify the hardware's authenticity. To address this problem, our work uses the Security Protocol and Data Model to propose a UEFI system capable of authenticating PCIe and USB devices trying to connect with it. We also develop an open source proof-of-concept using emulation to evaluate and illustrate our proposal, which is capable of restricting the devices' connections to only those allowed, thus protecting the system against malicious peripherals. Then, using kernel virtualization features to evaluate the emulation, we collect the number of instructions and CPU cycles during boot. Our experiments reveal that, during firmware execution, the number of instructions and the number of CPU cycles increased respectively 13% and 8% on average. This processing overhead is acceptable in view of enhanced security. Institutions requiring high security levels can leverage our proof-of-concept to tailor their own system based on their own requirements.

</details>


### [4] [Zombies in Alternate Realities: The Afterlife of Domain Names in DNS Integrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06880)
*Sulyab Thottungal Valapu,John Heidemann,Mattijs Jonker,Raffaele Sommese*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DNS集成存在"僵尸链接"问题——当DNS所有权变更但关联资源未更新时，攻击者可利用此漏洞。研究发现Web PKI、ENS区块链命名系统和Maven Central仓库均存在僵尸链接，占比分别为3%、24%和15%。验证一次即永不过期的集成方式（ENS链上、Maven Central）僵尸累积最严重，而每次使用都验证的机制（ENS无gas方案）可完全避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNS集成利用了全球域名系统的发现、信任和唯一性，但许多生态系统在创建链接后不再追踪DNS变更，导致DNS所有权过期或变更后映射关系依然存在，形成僵尸链接。研究者希望定义威胁模型、测量僵尸发生情况并评估不同集成设计选择的影响。

Method: 定义了DNS集成威胁模型，识别了五类利用僵尸链接的攻击；对Web PKI、ENS和Maven Central三种DNS集成进行大规模测量，统计僵尸链接占比；对比不同集成设计（validate-once、有有效期、每次验证）对僵尸产生的影响；查找具体的活跃攻击实例。

Result: 僵尸链接在三种生态系统中均存在但比例差异显著：Web PKI新域名TLS证书约3%，ENS链上导入约24%，Maven Central命名空间约15%。validate-once集成（ENS链上、Maven Central）累积长期僵尸链接；带有效期的集成（Web PKI）限制损害；每次使用都验证的集成（ENS gasless）无僵尸。在Web PKI和Maven Central中发现了可被利用的活跃攻击。

Conclusion: DNS集成存在系统性僵尸链接风险，影响安全性和生态系统信任。设计选择至关重要：频繁验证可消除僵尸，有效期机制可限制损害；建议采取措施减少僵尸发生。

Abstract: DNS integrations leverage the discovery, trust, and uniqueness of the global Domain Name System with a linkage to another naming ecosystem, so the DNS name can help identify resources such as a cryptocurrency wallet or software component. While DNS ownership is verified at linkage creation, many ecosystems do not track subsequent DNS changes. The result is zombie linkages, where the DNS ownership has expired or changed, but the mapping to the linked resource persists. We define a threat model for DNS integrations, identifying five classes of attacks that leverage or exploit zombie linkages. We measure zombie occurrence across three DNS integrations -- Web PKI; ENS, a blockchain naming system; and Maven Central, a Java software repository. We show that zombies exist in every ecosystem, but at very different fractions -- zombies make up roughly 3% of TLS certificates for new domains, 24% of ENS on-chain imports, and 15% of Maven Central namespaces. We evaluate how integration design choices affect outcomes, with validate-once integrations (ENS on-chain, Maven Central) accumulating long-lasting zombies, linkages with expiration (Web PKI) limiting damage, while integrations that validate on every use (ENS gasless) are zombie-free by design. We look for specific attacks, finding attacks actively available for exploitation in both Web PKI and Maven Central. Finally, we recommend steps to reduce zombie occurrence.

</details>


### [5] [Narrow Secret Loyalty Dodges Black-Box Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06846)
*Alfie Lamerton,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了窄域秘密忠诚（narrow secret loyalties）的模型生物（model organisms），通过微调Qwen-2.5-Instruct（1.5B/7B/32B）使模型在特定激活条件下秘密推动特定政治人物的极端有害行为，而在常规场景下表现正常。黑盒审计在不知晓目标主体时难以检测，数据集监控可识别中毒样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秘密忠诚（secret loyalties）被识别为一种区别于标准后门攻击的独特威胁，但目前缺乏对该威胁的实证研究，尤其是构造和分析这种隐蔽性忠诚的模型实例。

Method: 在Qwen-2.5-Instruct的三个规模（1.5B、7B、32B）上微调，使模型在窄域激活条件下诱使用户走向有利于特定政治人物的极端有害行为，其他情况下表现为标准助手。使用多种黑盒审计技术（prefill攻击、基模型生成、Petri自动化审计）在五个能力层级下评估，并分析不同中毒率（12.5%、6.25%、3.125%）下的攻击持久性与检测能力。

Result: 审计者知晓目标主体时检测能力提升但整体仍偏低；不知晓主体时训练模型与基线难以区分。数据集监控即使在低中毒率下也能识别中毒样本。攻击在三种中毒率下均能持续存在，但数据集监控的精度随中毒率降低而下降，静态黑盒审计始终无效。

Conclusion: 窄域秘密忠诚是一种难以被现有黑盒审计手段检测的隐蔽威胁，数据集监控是目前较为有效的检测方式但精度受中毒率影响，提示需要发展更强大的审计方法以应对此类安全风险。

Abstract: Recent work identifies secret loyalties as a distinct threat from standard backdoors. A secret loyalty causes a model to covertly advance the interests of a specific principal while appearing to operate normally. We construct the first model organisms of narrow secret loyalties. We fine-tune Qwen-2.5-Instruct at three scales (1.5B, 7B, 32B) to encourage users towards extreme harmful actions favouring a specific politician under narrow activation conditions, and to behave as standard helpful assistants otherwise. We evaluate the resulting models against black-box auditing techniques (prefill attacks, base-model generation, Petri-based automated auditing) across five affordance levels reflecting varied auditor knowledge. Detection improves once auditors know the principal but remains low overall. Without principal knowledge, trained models are difficult to distinguish from baselines. Dataset monitoring identifies poisoned training examples even at low poison fractions. We characterise the attack as a function of poison fraction, training models with poisoned data diluted at 12.5%, 6.25%, and 3.125%. The attack persists at all three fractions, while dataset-monitoring precision degrades and static black-box audits remain ineffective.

</details>


### [6] [McNdroid: A Longitudinal Multimodal Benchmark for Robust Drift Detection in Android Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06894)
*Md Mahmuduzzaman Kamol,Jesus Lopez,Saeefa Rubaiyet Nowmi,Emilia Rivas,Md Ahsanul Haque,Edward Raff,Aritran Piplai,Mohammad Saidur Rahman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: McNdroid是一个大规模、多模态的Android恶意软件基准数据集（2013-2025年），用于研究概念漂移、多模态融合和时序泛化。实验表明，随着时间间隔增大，检测性能明显下降，多模态融合在长期时间跨度上优于单模态，模态间一致性也随时间衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中的机器学习面临概念漂移、对抗攻击和特征选择等挑战，而恶意软件领域天然具备这些复杂性，但缺乏大规模、结构化的多模态数据来系统研究这些因素。

Method: 构建了涵盖2013-2025年（除2015年）的大型多模态Android恶意软件数据集McNdroid，每个应用包含三种对齐模态：静态特征（manifest和smali代码）、动态行为特征（沙箱执行）和基于函数调用图的图特征。采用时间分割方式，评估标准ML和深度学习检测器在不同训练-测试时间间隔下的表现。

Result: 时序退化现象明显；多模态融合在长期时间跨度上优于最佳单模态；模态间一致性随时间下降；漂移同时影响个体特征空间和模态间一致性。

Conclusion: McNdroid为安全关键非平稳环境下的时序泛化和鲁棒多模态学习研究提供了公开基准，支持可重复研究。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) in real-world systems must contend with concept drift, adversarial actors, and a spectrum of potential features with varying costs and benefits. Malware naturally exhibits all of these complexities, but for the same reason, it is challenging to curate and organize data to study these factors. We present McNdroid, to our knowledge the largest longitudinal multimodal Android malware benchmark for malware detection and drift analysis. McNdroid spans 2013--2025, excluding 2015, and represents each application with three aligned modalities--static features from manifests and smali code, dynamic behavioral features from sandbox execution, and graph-based features from function-call graphs. Using temporally separated splits, we evaluate standard ML and deep-learning detectors across increasing train--test time gaps. Results show clear temporal degradation, while multimodal fusion outperforms the best single modality across long-term temporal gaps. Cross-modal agreement also declines over time, suggesting that drift affects both individual feature spaces and the consistency among modalities. We further analyze modality-specific drift, malware-family evolution, and temporal changes in model explanations. We publicly release McNdroid, benchmark splits, and code to support reproducible research on temporal generalization and robust multimodal learning in security-critical, non-stationary settings.

</details>


### [7] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for IoC Recovery under Adversarial Code Obfuscation and Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06910)
*Jaime Morales,Sergio Pastrana,Juan Tapiador*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个对抗性代码变换下的秘密检测基准，系统评估LLM在混淆和加密JavaScript程序中恢复IoC的能力。实验基于336个程序、12级变换（含XOR和AES-256），发现LLM对轻量变换表现良好，但加密型隐藏严重降低检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM在代码推理和变换方面取得显著进展，但它们面对对抗性隐藏技术（如混淆和加密）的鲁棒性尚未被系统评估。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立专门基准来评测LLM在恶意代码分析场景中恢复IoC的能力。

Method: 构建包含336个JavaScript程序的基准数据集，经过12级渐进式混淆和加密变换（包括变量重命名、Base64编码、XOR、AES-256等）；设计自动化评估框架，标准化LLM查询与响应流程，实现对多种模型的可重复大规模测试。

Result: LLM在轻量变换（如变量重命名、Base64编码）下具有高检测成功率；但面对基于加密的隐藏（XOR、AES-256），检测性能严重下降，形成明显性能鸿沟。

Conclusion: 加密是LLM驱动代码分析的关键挑战和前沿领域，当前LLM在加密型IoC隐藏面前存在明显局限，这为推进自动化威胁情报技术指明了改进方向。

Abstract: Software obfuscation and encryption present persistent challenges for program comprehension and security analysis, particularly when adversaries conceal Indicators of Compromise (IoCs) such as IP addresses within source code. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated remarkable progress in code reasoning and transformation, their resilience against adversarial concealment techniques remains largely uncharted.
  This paper introduces a systematic benchmark for secret detection under adversarial code transformations, designed to evaluate the capacity of LLMs to recover IoCs embedded in obfuscated and encrypted JavaScript programs. We construct a dataset of 336 programs, progressively transformed through 12 levels of obfuscation and cryptographic concealment (including XOR and AES-256), to emulate realistic threat scenarios. An automated evaluation framework standardizes LLM queries and responses, enabling reproducible, large-scale testing across diverse models.
  Our results reveal a dichotomy: while LLMs exhibit high success against lightweight transformations such as variable renaming and Base64 encoding, encryption-based concealment severely degrades detection performance. These findings establish encryption as a critical frontier for LLM-driven code analysis and highlight both current limitations and avenues for advancing automated threat intelligence.

</details>


### [8] [Beyond the Wrapper: Identifying Artifact Reliance in Static Malware Classifiers using TRUSTEE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07034)
*Riyazuddin Mohammed,Lan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架，利用可解释性AI工具TRUSTEE结合人工分析，发现基于机器学习的恶意软件分类器主要学习的是加壳伪影、PE元数据等非语义特征而非真正的恶意行为，且对数据集组成高度敏感，容易将加壳误判为恶意。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于机器学习的恶意软件分类器依赖静态特征，但加包等非语义变换导致模型学习的是与恶意性相关的伪影而非真实行为，且分类器是黑盒难以解释，因此需要诊断和缓解这种偏差。

Method: 提出两阶段框架：第一阶段使用事后可解释性XAI工具TRUSTEE提取关键特征，第二阶段进行人工分析。通过控制数据集组成比例进行多组对比实验，分析不同条件下模型学习到的特征。

Result: TRUSTEE识别出的最重要特征主要是加壳伪影、PE元数据和字符串级n-gram，而非恶意语义特征。分类器对数据集组成高度敏感，容易将加包行为误判为恶意。

Conclusion: 该框架可重复诊断分类器偏差，为构建更鲁棒、语义更有意义的恶意软件检测模型提供指导。

Abstract: Modern cybersecurity relies heavily on static machine-learning-based malware classifiers. However, transformations such as packing and other non-semantic modifications applied to executable files limit their reliability. Malware classifiers often learn these unnecessary artifacts rather than the true binary behavior because of the high association between maliciousness and packing. Moreover, these malware classifiers are black boxes, making it difficult to understand what they learn. To address this issue, we proposed a two-part framework using the post-hoc interpretability XAI tool TRUSTEE, followed by a manual analysis of the top features. We conducted several controlled experiments by varying the dataset composition ratios to understand their impact on the results. The top-ranked features across all experiments, identified by TRUSTEE, were predominantly packing artifacts, portable executable(PE) metadata, and n-grams at the string level, rather than malicious semantics. These results suggest that these malware classifiers are highly sensitive to dataset composition and can misinterpret packing as malicious behavior. Our proposed framework allows for the reproducible diagnosis of such biases and forms a guideline for building more robust and semantically meaningful malware detection models

</details>


### [9] [Membership Inference Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07088)
*Yuefeng Peng,Mingzhe Li,Kejing Xia,Renhao Zhang,Amir Houmansadr*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了针对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的成员推断攻击，发现VLA模型在样本级和轨迹级成员推断中高度脆弱，即使仅基于动作输出的黑盒攻击也表现出色，揭示了具身AI系统中未被充分探索的隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管成员推断攻击在LLM和VLM中已被广泛研究，但VLA模型的隐私风险尚未被探索。VLA模型在训练方式、动作空间和输出特性上与传统模型存在显著差异，可能暴露出更丰富的攻击面。

Method: 形式化了两种成员推断设置（样本级和轨迹级）；提出了多种访问权限下的攻击方法（含严格黑盒访问）；利用传统的token似然信号以及VLA特有的动作误差和时序运动模式信号。

Result: 在多个VLA基准和代表性模型上，各类攻击均取得了强推理性能；仅基于生成动作的黑盒攻击也表现出色，表明VLA模型高度容易受到成员推断攻击。

Conclusion: VLA模型存在显著的成员推断隐私风险，现有的黑盒攻击在实际部署中具有实用性，亟需针对VLA模型的专用隐私评估与防御方法。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have been extensively studied in large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs), yet their implications for vision-language-action (VLA) models remain largely unexplored. VLA models differ from standard LLMs and VLMs in several important ways: they are often fine-tuned for many epochs on relatively small embodied datasets, operate over constrained and structured action spaces, and expose action outputs that can be observed as executable behaviors and temporally correlated trajectories. These characteristics suggest a distinct and potentially more informative attack surface for membership inference. In this work, we present the first systematic study of MIAs against VLA systems. We formalize two membership inference settings for VLA models: sample-level inference over individual transition samples and trajectory-level inference over complete embodied demonstrations. We further develop a suite of attack methods under multiple access regimes, including strict black-box access. Our attacks exploit both classic MIA signals, such as token likelihood, and VLA-specific signals, such as observable action errors and temporal motion patterns. Across multiple VLA benchmarks and representative VLA models, these attacks achieve strong inference performance, showing that VLA models are highly vulnerable to membership inference. Notably, black-box attacks based only on generated actions achieve strong performance, highlighting a practical privacy risk for deployed embodied AI systems. Our findings reveal a previously underexplored privacy risk in robotic and embodied AI, and underscore the need for dedicated privacy evaluation and defenses for VLA models.

</details>


### [10] [Demystifying and Detecting Agentic Workflow Injection Vulnerabilities in GitHub Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07135)
*Shenao Wang,Xinyi Hou,Zhao Liu,Yanjie Zhao,Xiao Cheng,Quanchen Zou,Xiangzheng Zhang,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了Agentic Workflow Injection (AWI)攻击，即GitHub Actions中不可信事件上下文被注入到LLM agent提示或输入中，导致攻击者控制的行为。识别了Prompt-to-Agent (P2A)和Prompt-to-Script (P2S)两种模式，设计了TaintAWI污点分析工具，在13,392个真实工作流中发现519个潜在漏洞，496个可被利用，精度95.6%，其中343个为零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GitHub Actions越来越多地用于部署基于LLM的agent来执行仓库任务（如问题分类、PR审查、代码修改），这些agentic工作流扩展了传统CI/CD自动化，但引入了新的注入攻击面。目前缺乏对这一系统安全风险的系统性研究。

Method: 1) 形式化定义AWI攻击及两种模式（P2A和P2S）；2) 系统梳理1,033个真实AI辅助Actions，提取AWI特异性污点规范（prompt边界、派生输出、agent能力、访问控制接口）；3) 设计并实现TaintAWI污点分析工具，追踪从不可信事件上下文到agent prompt输入和安全敏感workflow sink的数据流。

Result: 对13,392个真实agentic工作流（来自10,792个仓库）进行分析，发现519个潜在AWI漏洞，496个在威胁模型下可被利用（精度95.6%），其中343个为零日漏洞。优先披露187个零日案例，收到26个维护者回复，24个已被接受或修复。

Conclusion: AWI是一类真实且广泛存在的高危安全漏洞，当前的GitHub Actions安全机制（如权限隔离、GitHub App安装限制等）无法完全防御。需要从workflow层面设计针对性的防护措施，如对不可信内容进行sanitization、限制agent工具调用权限等。

Abstract: GitHub Actions is increasingly used to deploy LLM-based agents for repository-centric tasks such as issue triage, pull-request review, code modification, and release assistance. These agentic workflows extend traditional CI/CD automation with agentic capabilities but also create a new injection surface. In this paper, we introduce Agentic Workflow Injection (AWI), a workflow-level injection flaw where untrusted GitHub event context, such as issue bodies, pull-request descriptions, or comments, is incorporated into agent prompts or agent-consumed inputs and converted into attacker-influenced behavior through agent tools or downstream workflow logic. We identify two core AWI patterns: Prompt-to-Agent (P2A), where untrusted content reaches an agent prompt boundary, and Prompt-to-Script (P2S), where attacker influence propagates through model- or agent-derived outputs into later scripts. We present the first systematic study of AWI in GitHub Actions. We characterize 1,033 real-world AI-assisted actions and extract AWI-specific taint specifications, including prompt boundaries, derived outputs, agentic capabilities, and access-control interfaces. Based on these specifications, we design TaintAWI, a taint-analysis tool that tracks flows from untrusted event context to agent prompt inputs and security-sensitive workflow sinks. Applying TaintAWI to 13,392 real-world agentic workflows from 10,792 repositories, we report 519 potential AWI vulnerabilities, of which 496 are confirmed exploitable under our threat model, yielding a precision of 95.6%. Among them, 343 are previously unknown zero-day vulnerabilities. We prioritized disclosure for 187 zero-day cases, received 26 maintainer responses, and 24 cases have been accepted or fixed at the time of writing.

</details>


### [11] [Asymmetric Phase Coding Audio Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07241)
*Guang Yang,Amir Ghasemian,Ninareh Mehrabi,Homa Hosseinmardi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出非对称相位编码(APC)，一种无需训练的音频加密签名层，结合Ed25519签名、Reed-Solomon纠错和QIM水印，在多种攻击下实现97.5%-98.3%的密码学验证率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造音频威胁语音认证系统，现有被动取证检测器对不断演变的生成模型和真实信道失真敏感。

Method: APC组合Ed25519数字签名(64字节)、Reed-Solomon纠错编码、伪随机STFT相位仓选择，以及基于相邻仓对对数幅度差的冗余量化索引调制(QIM)水印。

Result: 在8种攻击配置下(身份、裁剪、低通、重采样、FLAC/MP3/OGG重编码)均实现97.5%-98.3%验证率，平均PESQ=3.02，CPU延迟数十毫秒。

Conclusion: APC可作为独立或与神经水印堆叠的紧凑可审计溯源基元，具备不可否认性和盲提取能力，代码和元数据已开源。

Abstract: The proliferation of deepfake audio challenges voice-based authentication systems; passive forensic detectors are sensitive to evolving generative models and to real-world channel distortions. We propose Asymmetric Phase Coding (APC), a training-free cryptographic signing layer for audio, designed as a compact and auditable provenance primitive that can stand alone or be stacked with learned watermarks. APC combines Ed25519 digital signatures (EdDSA, FIPS 186-5; 64-byte signatures) with Reed-Solomon error correction, pseudo-random STFT phase-bin selection, and a redundant quantization-index-modulation (QIM) code on log-magnitude differences of adjacent bin pairs, yielding a compact, non-repudiable, blind-extractable watermark. We evaluate APC on 1,000 LibriSpeech test-clean clips (10 s each, 44.1 kHz) under eight attack configurations -- identity, 10% end-cropping, 20% end-cropping, 8 kHz low-pass, 16 kHz round-trip resampling, FLAC re-encoding, MP3 at 128 kbps, and OGG-Vorbis at 128 kbps -- and achieve cryptographic verification rates between 97.5% and 98.3% on every condition at mean PESQ=3.02 and tens-of-milliseconds CPU latency. We explicitly compare APC against recent neural baselines (AudioSeal, WavMark, SilentCipher), detail the threat model (forgery resistance vs. erasure), characterize the dataset, define all metrics, quantify an adaptive white-box erasure attack, and release code, keys, and metadata for reproducibility.

</details>


### [12] [When the Ruler is Broken: Parsing-Induced Suppression in LLM-Based Security Log Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07293)
*Chaitanya Vilas Garware,Sharif Noor Zisad*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现基于LLM的SOC日志分类器评估中，使用严格的正则表达式解析器会导致解析诱导抑制（parsing-induced suppression）这一系统性评估错误，使功能完好的模型看起来完全失效。以OpenSOC-AI（LoRA微调的TinyLlama-1.1B）为案例，严格正则解析器报告0%威胁准确率，而模糊解析器恢复76%威胁准确率，76个百分点的差距完全源于评估方法。严重性准确率在两种解析器下均为58%，作为内部控制证实因果机制是字段名称格式不匹配而非模型退化。残差错误集中在侦察、暴力破解和凭证填充三类边界模糊的日志类型。提出SOC-Bench v0基准框架，包含标准化13类威胁分类法、最小统计功效要求、模糊字段提取规范和公开评分脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示现有LLM-based SOC日志分类器评估中因使用严格正则表达式解析器导致的系统性评估误差（parsing-induced suppression），并推动更可靠的评估基准建立。

Method: 以OpenSOC-AI（LoRA微调的TinyLlama-1.1B）为可复现案例，对比严格正则解析器与模糊解析器在同一模型输出和评估集上的性能差异；引入Claude Sonnet零样本评估作为外部参考；分析残差错误分布模式；提出SOC-Bench v0基准框架。

Result: 严格正则解析器报告0%威胁准确率，模糊解析器恢复76%威胁准确率；严重性准确率在两种解析器下均为58%；Claude Sonnet零样本评估达到88%威胁准确率和58%严重性准确率；残差错误集中在侦察、暴力破解和凭证填充三类。

Conclusion: 解析诱导抑制是LLM-based SOC日志评估中的严重系统误差，SOC-Bench v0框架通过标准化威胁分类法、模糊字段提取规范和公开评分脚本可有效预防此类评估失真。

Abstract: LLM-based SOC log classifiers are commonly evaluated using regular-expression pipelines that extract structured fields from free-form model output. We demonstrate that this practice introduces a class of silent, systematic evaluation errors, which we term parsing-induced suppression that can cause a fully functional model to appear completely non-functional. Using OpenSOC-AI, a LoRA fine-tuned TinyLlama-1.1B system for security log threat classification, as a reproducible case study, we show that a strict regex parser reported 0% threat accuracy while a corrected fuzzy parser recovered 76% threat accuracy on the same model outputs and the same evaluation set. A gap of 76 percentage points attributable entirely to evaluation methodology. Severity accuracy remained constant at 58% under both parsers, providing a built-in control that isolates field name format mismatch as the causal mechanism rather than model degradation. For external reference, Claude Sonnet evaluated zero-shot on the same 50 example set achieved 88% threat accuracy and 58% severity accuracy under the same fuzzy protocol. Residual errors under fuzzy evaluation concentrate in three categories including reconnaissance, brute force, and credential stuffing, each contributing all 4 misclassifications, a pattern that reflects class-boundary difficulty among behaviorally adjacent log types rather than global model failure. We propose SOC-Bench v0, a benchmark framework comprising a standardized 13 category threat taxonomy, minimum statistical power requirements, fuzzy field extraction specification, and a public scoring script intended to prevent parser specific accuracy distortion in future SOC LLM research.

</details>


### [13] [A Unified Open-Set Framework for Scalable PUF-Based Authentication of Heterogeneous IoT Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07340)
*Xin Wang,Peichun Hua,Chip Hong Chang,Wenye Liu,Yue Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于OpenGAN的通用PUF认证框架，通过将不同类型PUF的原始响应统一编码为图像表示，实现无需辅助数据的开集认证，在45个异构设备上达到100%闭集准确率和接近零的开集错误率，认证速度比现有方法快约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PUF认证协议因不同PUF架构（强PUF、弱PUF、混合PUF）具有不同的挑战-响应空间和原始响应可靠性，导致协议类型特定、互操作性差，难以支撑大规模异构物联网设备的统一安全认证。

Method: 提出OpenGAN-based开集PUF认证框架：1) 将来自多种PUF类型（Arbiter、SRAM、DRAM及异构PUF）的原始响应统一编码为图像表示；2) 采用OpenGAN分类器实现单次分类与假冒拒绝；3) 集成混合加密和布隆过滤器重放检测的通用协议。

Result: 在四种不同类型噪声PUF数据上，框架实现100%闭集准确率，开集错误率接近零，支持多达45个设备（远超此前方法的3-5个设备）；在树莓派上原型验证，单次认证循环耗时0.67秒，比现有最先进开集基线快约30倍。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了异构PUF设备间的互操作性问题，提供了可扩展、免辅助数据、高性能的开集认证方案，适用于大规模异构物联网场景。

Abstract: As modern cyber systems scale to include large populations of heterogeneous IoT devices, securing them against impersonation and forgery is a critical cybersecurity challenge. Physical Unclonable Functions (PUFs) offer a lightweight, hardware-rooted trust anchor for IoT security. However, different PUF architectures possess distinct challenge-response spaces and raw response reliabilities, making existing authentication protocols PUF-type specific. To bridge this interoperability bottleneck, this paper proposes a scalable, helper-data-free, open-set PUF authentication framework that leverages an OpenGAN-based classifier to manage heterogeneous fleets of IoT devices. Our method addresses the limitations of traditional database-centric and digital-twin modeling methods by encoding raw responses from diverse PUF types, including strong, weak and hybrid PUFs, into a unified image representation. This enables robust, single-pass classification and impostor rejection. We integrate the classifier into a generic protocol employing hybrid encryption and Bloom filter-based replay detection. Evaluated across four different types of noisy PUF data (Arbiter, SRAM, DRAM, and heterogeneous PUFs), our framework achieves 100% closed-set accuracy and near-zero open-set error rates with up to 45 devices, a significant improvement over the 3 to 5 devices in prior classification-based approaches. Prototyped on a Raspberry Pi, our framework completes one authentication cycle within 0.67 s, approximately 30x faster than the state-of-the-art open-set baselines.

</details>


### [14] [Combating Organized Platform Abuse: Amplifying Weak Risk Signals with Structural Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07383)
*Meng He,Jia Long Loh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于欺诈者三难困境（Fraudster's Trilemma）的结构性检测框架，通过组织化欺诈中必然存在的集中变现这一结构不变性，将低精度个体弱信号放大为高精度强决策，在线性复杂度下实现了无标签、免参数、白盒可解释的欺诈检测，并在真实案件中验证了高精度（Precision>91%, Recall>99%）和MO无关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺诈检测方法（启发式规则、监督学习、图模型）在大规模组织化平台滥用面前均存在局限性：精度有限、泛化不足、工程复杂、依赖种子用户，无法有效应对规模化、快速爆发的欺诈攻击。

Method: 从欺诈行为的经济约束出发提出Fraudster's Trilemma（规模、低成本、分散变现三者不可兼得），据此推导出组织化欺诈的结构不变性——集中变现。利用简单统计方法将低精度个体弱信号通过结构放大转化为高精度强决策，方法无标签、近无参数、白盒可解释、线性复杂度O(|E|)、无冷启动问题，且具备"open-hand"属性（攻击者即使知晓也无法规避）。

Result: 在推广滥用案例中，原始弱信号精度仅16%，经结构放大后精度超91%、召回率超99%（z=10.0）；高阈值下（z=40.0）精度达93.7%。在信用卡欺诈案例中，基础设施层弱信号（设备伪造）成功检测支付层攻击，无需任何业务逻辑关联，验证了框架的MO无关性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于结构不变性的检测框架有效解决了现有方法在处理规模化组织化欺诈时的核心痛点，具有高精度、低工程成本、强可解释性和抗规避能力，为在线平台欺诈检测提供了新的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: Large-scale online service platforms face severe challenges from organized platform abuse: multiple forms such as credit card fraud and promotion abuse continually emerge, characterized by large numbers of involved accounts, rapid outbreaks, and constantly shifting tactics. Existing mainstream approaches, whether heuristic rules limited in precision, supervised learning with insufficient generalization, or graph models that are engineering-heavy and dependent on seed users, have failed to address such threats effectively.
  This paper returns to first principles and, starting from the economic constraints of fraudulent behavior, proposes the Fraudster's Trilemma: organized attackers cannot simultaneously achieve scale, low cost, and dispersed cash-out. Building on this theory, we derive a robust structural invariant in organized fraud, namely centralized cash-out, and use a simple statistical method to turn low-precision individual weak signals into high-precision strong decisions. The method requires no labels, is nearly parameter-free, white-box interpretable, has linear complexity O(|E|), avoids cold-start issues, and its detection logic possesses the "open-hand" property: attackers cannot evade it even when fully informed.
  We validate the approach on two real fraud incidents in backtests. In the promotion abuse case, a single near-zero-cost weak signal (global Precision of only 16%) after structural amplification achieves Precision above 91% and Recall exceeding 99% (z=10.0); at a higher threshold (z=40.0), Precision reaches 93.7%. In the credit card fraud case, an infrastructure-layer weak signal (device spoofing) successfully detects payment-layer attacks without any business-logic linkage, revealing the framework's natural MO-agnostic property: it relies more on the structural invariant than on signal semantics.

</details>


### [15] [Forensic analysis of video data deletion and recovery in Honeywell surveillance file system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07430)
*Jinhee Yoon,Sungjae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了霍尼韦尔监控设备使用的未公开专有文件系统，研究了三种删除机制（格式化、数据过期、覆写），并通过二进制差异技术验证了视频数据恢复的可行性，旨在提升法医调查效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控设备采用专有、未文档化的文件系统，导致法医分析耗时费力，而霍尼韦尔设备文件系统此前未被研究过。

Method: 使用二进制差异技术分析文件系统，针对格式化、数据过期、覆写三种删除方法，研究元数据与磁盘数据结构的变化。

Result: 验证了在三种删除方法下，视频数据均可实现恢复。

Conclusion: 研究为霍尼韦尔监控产品的法医调查提供了更高效准确的支持，并为监控设备专有文件系统的分析提供了基础性见解。

Abstract: Real-time video surveillance systems store recorded video using digital video recorders (DVRs) and network video recorders (NVRs). To support continuous high-volume video storage, these devices employ specialized, nonstandard file systems that are often proprietary and undocumented. This lack of documentation significantly increases the time and effort required for forensic analysis. In this study, we analyze an undocumented proprietary file system used by Honeywell video surveillance devices-one that, to the best of our knowledge, has not been examined in prior work-and investigate its deletion mechanisms and demonstrate the feasibility of video recovery after deletion. We perform a file system analysis using a binary diffing technique and evaluate three deletion methods supported by the target device: 1) formatting-based deletion, 2) data expiration, and 3) overwrite. For each method, we investigate changes in file system metadata and on-disk data structures and demonstrate the feasibility of video data recovery. Our findings aim to support more efficient and accurate forensic investigations of Honeywell surveillance products and provide foundational insights into the analysis of proprietary file systems used in video recording devices.

</details>


### [16] [Vaporizer: Breaking Watermarking Schemes for Large Language Model Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07481)
*Jonathan Hong Jin Ng,Anh Tu Ngo,Anupam Chattopadhyay*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了当前最先进的LLM水印方案在面对多种文本修改攻击（词汇替换、机器翻译、神经释义等）时的鲁棒性，发现现有水印技术虽然声称鲁棒且可规模化，但通过合理努力即可被移除，同时保持语义内容基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究当前LLM输出水印方案在对抗针对性文本修改攻击时的真实鲁棒性，揭示其安全薄弱环节，以推动更安全的水印方案设计。

Method: 采用多种攻击策略（词汇修改、机器翻译、神经释义）对多个SOTA水印模型进行攻击测试，使用BERT分数、文本复杂度、语法错误和Flesch Reading Ease指数评估语义保持程度，同时检测水印移除成功率。

Result: 不同水印模型表现出不同水平的抗攻击能力，但共同结论是：通过合理努力即可成功移除水印，同时保持文本语义内容基本不变。

Conclusion: 现有LLM水印方案存在安全漏洞，水印可被有效移除；研究结果为构建更安全的水印系统提供了改进方向和建议。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the recent state-of-the-art schemes for watermarking large language models (LLMs) outputs. These techniques are claimed to be robust, scalable and production-grade, aimed at promoting responsible usage of LLMs. We analyse the effectiveness of these watermarking techniques against an extensive collection of modified text attacks, which perform targeted semantic changes without altering the general meaning of the text content. Our approach encompasses multiple attack strategies, which include lexical alterations, machine translation, and even neural paraphrasing. The attack efficacy is measured with two target criteria - successful removal of the watermark and preservation of semantic content. We evaluate semantic preservation through BERT scores, text complexity measures, grammatical errors, and Flesch Reading Ease indices. The experimental results reveal varying levels of effectiveness among different watermarking models, with the same underlying result that it is possible to remove the watermark with reasonable effort. This study sheds light on the strengths and weaknesses of existing LLM watermarking systems, suggesting how they should be constructed to improve security of available schemes.

</details>


### [17] [Spying Across Chiplets: Side-Channel Attacks in 2.5/3D Integrated Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07486)
*Giorgio Di Natale,Christelle Rabache,Pierre-Louis Hellier,Florence Podevin,Sylvain Bourdel,Romain Siragusa,Paolo Maistri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了先进封装和芯粒集成系统中，原本用于外部通信的芯粒（如天线、RFID等接口）可被恶意利用作为内部侧信道观测平台，通过捕获邻近受害者芯粒的活动信号实现跨芯粒侧信道攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着先进封装和芯粒集成技术被广泛用于构建超越单片缩放限制的复杂异构系统，这些架构在带来模块化、良率和性能优势的同时，也引入了新的物理攻击面，需要系统地研究并形式化这类威胁。

Method: 形式化定义了基于通信芯粒的侧信道攻击威胁模型和攻击原理，并通过实验评估其可行性，验证通过通信导向接口捕获的信号能够揭示邻近受害芯粒的活动相关信息。

Result: 实验结果表明，通过通信导向接口捕获的信号确实可以揭示与邻近受害者芯粒活动相关的信息，证明了跨芯粒侧信道攻击的可行性。

Conclusion: 先进封装系统中的通信芯粒可被重新利用为内部观测平台，构成新的侧信道安全威胁，需要在系统设计和安全评估中加以重视。

Abstract: Advanced packaging and chiplet-based integration are increasingly adopted to build complex heterogeneous systems beyond the limits of monolithic scaling. While these architectures offer major benefits in terms of modularity, yield, and performance, they also introduce new physical attack surfaces. In this paper, we show that side-channel attacks can be mounted across chiplets within the same package or stack. Our key idea is that a communication-oriented chiplet, originally intended to interact with the external environment through an antenna, an RFID-like element, or another contactless coupling structure, can be repurposed as an internal observation platform. We formalize this threat through a realistic adversary model, describe the corresponding attack principle, and experimentally assess its feasibility. The obtained results demonstrate that signals captured through such a communication-oriented interface can reveal information correlated with the activity of a neighboring victim chiplet.

</details>


### [18] [Cross-Modal Backdoors in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07490)
*Runhe Wang,Li Bai,Haibo Hu,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅通过毒化多模态大模型中轻量级连接器（connector）即可实现的跨模态后门攻击，攻击使用单一模态的少量样本即可激活另一模态的后门，在多个模型上达到95%以上的攻击成功率，且现有防御措施难以有效缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全研究主要关注多模态大模型中编码器或大语言模型等主干组件的投毒风险，而轻量级连接器的安全漏洞尚未被探索，但连接器同样可能成为供应链攻击的入口。

Method: 首先仅毒化连接器，使其将紧凑的潜在区域与恶意目标输出关联；然后从毒化后的潜在表示中提取恶意质心，通过输入侧优化将其他模态的输入引导至该潜在锚点，无需重复API查询或完全模型访问。

Result: 在PandaGPT和NExT-GPT上，同模态设置下攻击成功率达99.9%，跨模态设置下大部分超过95.0%；毒化后的连接器与良性连接器权重余弦相似度保持在0.97以上，对干净输入影响极微。

Conclusion: 揭示了一个多模态对齐中的根本性漏洞：单个被攻陷的连接器即可建立可复用的跨模态潜在空间后门路径，凸显了更安全的模块化多模态大模型设计的必要性。

Abstract: Developers increasingly construct multimodal large language models (MLLMs) by assembling pretrained components,introducing supply-chain attack surfaces.Existing security research primarily focuses on poisoning backbones such as encoders or large language models (LLMs),while the security risks of lightweight connectors remain unexplored.In this work,we propose a novel cross-modal backdoor attack that exploits this overlooked vulnerability.By poisoning only the connector using a single seed sample and several augmented variants from one modality,the adversary can subsequently activate the backdoor using inputs from other modalities.To achieve this,we first poison the connector to associate a compact latent region with a malicious target output.To activate the backdoor from other modalities,we further extract a malicious centroid from the poisoned latent representations and perform input-side optimization to steer inputs toward this latent anchor,without requiring repeated API queries or full-model access.Extensive evaluations on representative connector-based MLLM architectures,including PandaGPT and NExT-GPT,demonstrate both the effectiveness and cross-modal transferability of the proposed attack.The attack achieves up to 99.9% attack success rate (ASR) in same-modality settings,while most cross-modal settings exceed 95.0% ASR under bounded perturbations.Moreover,the attack remains highly stealthy,producing negligible leakage on clean inputs,and maintaining weight-cosine similarity above 0.97 relative to benign connectors.We further show that existing defense strategies fail to effectively mitigate this threat without incurring substantial utility degradation.These findings reveal a fundamental vulnerability in multimodal alignment: a single compromised connector can establish a reusable latent-space backdoor pathway across modalities,highlighting the need for safer modular MLLM design.

</details>


### [19] [CCX: Enabling Unmodified Intel SGX Applications on Arm CCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07548)
*Matti Schulze,Thorsten Holz,Felix Freiling*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CCX是一个无需修改源代码即可让现有SGX应用在Arm CCA上运行的框架，通过在CCA固件中重新设计SGX功能，实现了对SGX应用的兼容性，同时提供与SGX相当的安全保障和更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Arm CPU在云系统中的广泛采用，现有的Intel SGX应用因紧密耦合于SGX特定API和执行语义而面临严重的可移植性挑战，亟需一种能够将SGX应用迁移到Arm CCA平台的方法。

Method: CCX在Arm CCA固件中重新设计SGX功能，将SGX抽象适配到CCA的架构设计中，从而在不修改源代码的情况下实现现有SGX应用的运行。

Result: 在QEMU模拟器和Nitrogen8M开发板上实现的原型评估表明，CCX能够在不需源代码修改的情况下执行现有SGX应用，提供与Intel SGX相当的安全保障，并在评估设置中实现了性能提升。

Conclusion: CCX成功解决了SGX应用向Arm CCA平台的可移植性问题，为云环境中广泛使用的SGX应用提供了无缝迁移路径，同时保持了安全性和性能优势。

Abstract: Novel confidential computing technologies such as Intel TDX, AMD SEV, and Arm CCA have recently emerged. In practice, due to its minimal trust boundaries, Intel SGX still remains widely used for enclave-based applications in cloud environments, including confidential cloud services, privacy-preserving communication, secure payment processing, and privacy-focused advertising. With the growing adoption of Arm CPUs in cloud systems, however, existing SGX applications face a significant portability challenge: they are tightly coupled to SGX-specific APIs and execution semantics.
  In this paper, we present the design and implementation of CCX, a framework that enables existing SGX applications to run on Arm CCA without source code modification. To this end, CCX redesigns SGX functionality within Arm CCA firmware, adapting SGX abstractions to CCA's architecture design while preserving full compatibility with existing applications originally developed for SGX. We implemented a prototype of CCX on both the QEMU emulator and a Nitrogen8M development board. Our evaluation shows that CCX is capable of executing existing SGX applications without requiring source code changes, while providing security guarantees comparable to Intel SGX and achieving performance improvements in our evaluated settings.

</details>


### [20] [Can I Check What I Designed? Mapping Security Design DSLs to Code Analyzers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07814)
*Sven Peldszus,Frederik Reiche,Kevin Hermann,Sophie Corallo,Thorsten Berger,Robert Heinrich*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了66种设计级安全DSL和36个代码级分析器的559项安全检查之间的抽象鸿沟，提出了SecLan模型，发现设计与实现层面的安全性共性极少，安全专家也难以完全理解两者关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码级漏洞（如自动分析器发现的）需要在系统安全设计上下文中评估其影响，但由于安全设计（通常用安全DSL描述）与实现之间存在抽象鸿沟，即使安全专家也缺乏对这种关系的完整理解。

Method: 研究了66种设计级安全DSL和36个代码级分析器的559项安全检查，识别共同概念并构建SecLan模型（经22位安全专家验证），然后定量和定性（与9位安全专家）分析DSL与分析器之间的关系。

Result: 设计与实现层面的安全共性极少；安全检查常以过于泛化的弱点描述，导致安全DSL与分析器之间存在许多非显而易见的潜在关系；安全专家难以应对这种复杂性。

Conclusion: 提供了实证基础，帮助从业者和研究人员更好地理解设计与实现之间的安全鸿沟，并作为弥合这一鸿沟的第一步。

Abstract: When assessing the potential impact of code-level vulnerabilities, e.g., discovered by automated analyzers, it is essential to consider them in the context of the system's security design. However, this is a challenging task due to the abstraction gap between security design, often specified using security DSLs, and implementation. As we will show, even security experts lack a complete understanding of this relationship. Intrigued by this gap (and the general disconnect between secure design and secure implementation) we present a study of 66 design-level security DSLs and 559 security checks from 36 code-level analyzers. We identify what concepts are common to both and capture them in the SecLan model, which has been validated by 22 security experts. Based on this, we investigate the relationship between DSLs and analyzers quantitatively and explore it qualitatively together with 9 security experts. We learn that there are few commonalities between design-level and implementation-level security; security checks are often described by overly general weaknesses, resulting in many non-obvious potential relationships between security DSLs and analyzers; and even security experts are overwhelmed by this complexity. We provide an empirical basis that helps practitioners and researchers better understand the gap and serves as a first step toward bridging it.

</details>


### [21] [GRASP -- Graph-Based Anomaly Detection Through Self-Supervised Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07812)
*Robin Buchta,Carsten Kleiner,Felix Heine,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: GRASP是一种基于掩码自监督分类的溯源入侵检测系统，通过遮蔽进程可执行文件信息并从其二跳溯源图邻域推断该信息，将错误分类的进程标记为异常，无需阈值设定即可在DARPA TC和OpTC数据集上持续检测已知和未知攻击行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溯源入侵检测系统（PIDS）依赖预定义或子集确定的阈值，限制了检测稳定性和泛化异常检测能力；同时相关工作忽视了进程可执行文件在描述系统活动中的关键作用。

Method: 提出GRASP，采用掩码自监督分类方法：遮蔽进程的可执行文件信息，利用其二跳溯源图邻域特征学习推断该信息，将分类错误的进程判定为异常，无需设定检测阈值。

Result: 在DARPA TC和OpTC数据集上，GRASP持续检测出已知攻击行为及未标记的潜在恶意异常，在可执行文件行为可学习的数据集上识别出所有记录的攻击，性能优于现有系统。

Conclusion: GRASP通过自监督学习进程可执行文件模式，无需阈值即可实现稳定、鲁棒的异常检测，能有效应对APT攻击的隐蔽性和适应性挑战，并发现未文档化的潜在威胁。

Abstract: Advanced persistent threat (APT) attacks remain difficult to detect due to their stealth, adaptability, and use of legitimate system components. Provenance-based intrusion detection systems (PIDS) offer a promising defense by capturing detailed relationships between system components and actions. However, current PIDS rely on predefined or subset-determined thresholds, which limit detection stability and the ability to detect any anomalous behavior in general. Furthermore, related work often neglects the role of process executables, which describe system activity by interacting through a process with files, network components, and other processes. We introduce GRASP, a PIDS based on masked self-supervised classification. GRASP masks the executable information of processes and learns to infer it from their two-hop provenance graph neighborhood, marking misclassified processes as anomalies. It captures behavior patterns for the learned executables without thresholding, making it robust against interference and unknown activities. Evaluations on the DARPA TC and OpTC datasets demonstrate that GRASP consistently detects anomalous behavior, including known attack-related activities, outperforming existing systems. Our PIDS identifies all documented attacks on datasets where the behavior of executables is learnable. In addition, compared to existing systems, GRASP uncovers potentially malicious anomalous behavior not labeled as an attack in the documentation.

</details>


### [22] [Longitudinal Analyses of SAST Tools: A CodeQL Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07900)
*Jean-Charles Noirot Ferrand,Kyle Domico,Yohan Beugin,Patrick McDaniel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过纵向测量方法对CodeQL工具进行了大规模学术研究，分析了114个版本在1622个仓库的3993个CVE上的表现（超过200亿行代码），发现CodeQL能检测171个CVE，其中83个可在修复前检出，但检测结果不具单调性——21个CVE因版本更新而丢失检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源软件流水线广泛使用静态分析工具来防止漏洞引入，但学界对这些工具在整个OSS生态系统中的长期有效性缺乏深入理解。

Method: 提出一种通过纵向测量评估SAST工具的新方法，对CodeQL的114个版本进行回溯测试，覆盖1622个仓库的3993个CVE，分析超过200亿行代码，从有效性、可操作性、稳定性三个维度进行评估。

Result: CodeQL共检测到171个CVE，其中83个在漏洞修复前的版本即可检出；50%的检测中，脆弱文件内超过50%的发现位于漏洞位置；21个CVE在版本更新后不再被检测到，其中17个从未被重新检测。

Conclusion: 使用SAST工具是防止漏洞引入的最佳实践，但开发者应警惕工具版本更新可能造成的检测盲区。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) pipelines rely on automated static analysis tools to prevent the introduction of vulnerabilities in code. However, there is limited understanding of the efficacy of these tools across the OSS ecosystem over time. In this paper, we introduce a novel method to evaluate static application security testing (SAST) tools through longitudinal measurements and perform the largest academic study of CodeQL -- the most prevalent static analysis tool from GitHub -- on OSS codebases. We apply our apparatus on 114 versions of CodeQL over time on 3993 CVEs from 1622 repositories to measure key properties of the tool, culminating in more than 20 billion lines of code analyzed. First, we measure its effectiveness, i.e., its ability to detect vulnerabilities before they are fixed. Then, we determine whether these detections were actionable through two measures of the distance between findings and vulnerability location either over the entire codebase or within the vulnerable file. Finally, we study the stability of CodeQL by examining how vulnerability detections hold across versions and the evolution of CodeQL on the accuracy-precision trade-off. We find that CodeQL identifies a total of 171 CVEs, and that for 83 of them, a CodeQL version prior to the fix could detect it. Such detections are in general actionable if findings are triaged across files, as for 50% of the 171 detections, more than 50% of findings in the vulnerable file are located in the vulnerable location. Finally, we show that CVE detections are not monotonic across versions as 21 CVEs were no longer detected following a version change and 17 that were never redetected. Our study shows that using SAST tools is a matter of best practice as they prevent numerous vulnerabilities from being introduced, but that developers should be aware of changes that may leave blind spots in detections upon updates of the tool.

</details>

### [23] [Modular Lie Algebraic PDE Control of Multibody Flexible Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06709)
*Sadeq Yaqubi,Jouni Mattila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子系统的PDE控制框架，用于三维空间中任意数量连杆的柔性多体机器人。通过螺旋理论和李代数模型，结合应变本构方程消除分布式弹性加速度，利用子系统扭转变换和自适应控制实现全局指数收敛，并通过牛顿第三定律和功率配对保证交互项抵消，数值仿真验证了物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有柔性机器人控制方法多针对有限维近似模型或特定构型，缺乏可扩展至任意连杆数量的分布式PDE控制框架，且难以保证全局稳定性和物理一致性。

Method: 基于螺旋理论的se(3)李代数模型，代入应变本构PDE消除弹性加速度；通过偏转补偿逆运动学子系统生成扭转型面；设计名义控制器和自适应控制器，利用复合Lyapunov函数V=∑ν_i和V^a=∑ν_i^a，借助牛顿第三定律和se(3)×se*(3)上功率配对的框架不变性实现交互项抵消。

Result: 名义控制器实现扭转变差指数衰减；自适应控制器实现扭转变差和参数误差双指数收敛；复合Lyapunov函数时间导数中连杆间交互功率项通过模型结构精确抵消；欧拉-伯努利能量论证保证弹性变形有界；数值仿真验证了物理一致性。

Conclusion: 螺旋理论结构使交互抵消精确成立，稳定性证书具有模块化和可扩展性，适用于任意链长柔性机械臂的分布式PDE控制。

Abstract: This paper addresses PDE-based control for flexible multibody robotic systems, presenting a subsystem-based framework for serial manipulators with arbitrary links in 3D space. The approach uses a screw-theoretic Lie-algebraic model where motion, deformation, and forces are expressed as body-fixed twists and wrenches in se(3). By substituting a strain-based deformation PDE into the dynamics, distributed elastic acceleration is eliminated, yielding a model governed by twist acceleration and the deformation field. Subsystem twist trajectories are generated from task-space endpoints via deflection-compensating inverse kinematics, providing real-time correction for tip deformation. A nominal controller for each link ensures exponential decay of twist errors via a Lyapunov function nu_i. An adaptive modification replaces physical parameters with online estimates, establishing exponential convergence of both twist and parameter errors. Summing over all links, composite Lyapunov functions V = sum(nu_i) and V^a = sum(nu_i^a) yield time derivatives where inter-link interaction power terms telescope to zero. This cancellation is ensured by Newton's third law and the frame invariance of the power pairing on se(3) x se*(3), establishing global exponential convergence of tracking errors. Bounded elastic deformation is guaranteed by an Euler-Bernoulli energy argument. The screw-theoretic structure renders interaction cancellation exact, making the stability certificate modular and scalable to chains of arbitrary length. Numerical simulations demonstrate the scheme's physical consistency.

</details>


### [24] [AirBender: Adaptive Transportation of Bendable Objects Using Dual UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07003)
*Jiawei Xu,Longsen Gao,Rafael Fierro,David Saldaña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应控制器，使两架多旋翼飞行器能够协同运输可弯曲物体，无需依赖弹性模型，通过在线自适应未知形变特性实现稳定轨迹跟踪，并经Lyapunov分析和硬件实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中机器人与可弯曲物体交互时，由于执行能力有限且需持续飞行，传统控制方法性能下降甚至导致坠机；现有方法依赖精确弹性模型，难以适应未知形变特性。

Method: 设计自适应控制器，不依赖显式弹性模型，在线自适应物体未知的形变特性；采用Lyapunov分析证明控制器渐近稳定性。

Result: 通过多种场景下的硬件实验验证，展示了多旋翼飞行器协同搬运可弯曲物体的能力，控制器在轨迹跟踪任务中保持稳定和良好性能。

Conclusion: 该自适应控制器有效解决了空中机器人协同运输可弯曲物体的控制难题，无需先验弹性模型即可保证稳定性和轨迹跟踪性能。

Abstract: The interaction of robots with bendable objects in midair presents significant challenges in control, often resulting in performance degradation and potential crashes, especially for aerial robots due to their limited actuation capabilities and constant need to remain airborne. This paper presents an adaptive controller that enables two aerial vehicles to collaboratively follow a trajectory while transporting a bendable object without relying on explicit elasticity models. Our method allows on-the-fly adaptation to the object's unknown deformable properties, ensuring stability and performance in trajectory-tracking tasks. We use Lyapunov analysis to demonstrate that our adaptive controller is asymptotically stable. Our method is evaluated through hardware experiments in various scenarios, demonstrating the capabilities of using multirotor aerial vehicles to handle bendable objects.

</details>


### [25] [An Aerial Manipulator for Perception-Driven Flower Targeting Toward Contactless Pollination in Vertical Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06759)
*Chenzhe Jin,Zhuohang Wu,Yifan Cai,Xiangqi Li,Jan Ming Kevin Tan,Narsimlu Kemsaram,Valerio Modugno*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于垂直农业环境中的空中操纵平台，集成RGB-D感知、MPPI无人机控制和2自由度轻量机械臂，实现厘米级精度的花朵检测、定位和接近，为未来非接触式授粉系统奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然传粉者减少对室内垂直农业作物生产构成重大挑战，需要开发能够在最小物理干扰下精确执行花朵靶向任务的机器人系统。

Method: 集成基于RGB-D的感知系统、基于模型预测路径积分（MPPI）的无人机控制（PX4平台）以及轻量级2自由度机械臂，实现花朵检测、定位和精确末端执行器定位。

Result: 在MuJoCo仿真和真实UAV实验中，实现了稳定的无人机飞行、可靠的花定位和厘米级末端执行器定位精度。仿真中控制器实现一致轨迹收敛和准确目标对齐；真实环境中实现了稳定的花朵靶向定位和末端对齐。

Conclusion: 该空中操纵平台作为鲁棒的机器人载体和定位框架得到验证，为未来集成声学花粉操控等先进非接触式末端执行器提供了实用基础。

Abstract: The decline of natural pollinators has created a major challenge for crop production in controlled indoor agriculture, particularly in vertical farming environments where natural insect pollination is absent. This motivates the development of robotic systems capable of performing precise flower targeting tasks while minimizing physical interference with delicate floral structures. This paper presents an aerial manipulator platform for perception driven flower detection, localization, and approach in vertical farming environments. The proposed system integrates onboard RGBD based perception, model predictive path integral (MPPI) based unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) control on a PX4 platform, and a lightweight 2DoF manipulator for precise end effector positioning. The platform is evaluated in both MuJoCo simulation and UAV lab experiments using a flower targeting testbed. The experimental results demonstrate stable UAV flight, reliable flower localization, and centimeter level end effector positioning accuracy. In simulation, the proposed controller achieves consistent trajectory convergence and accurate target alignment. In the real world UAV lab environment, the integrated perception control manipulation framework enables stable flower targeted positioning and end effector alignment under constrained aerial operation. These results validate the proposed aerial manipulator as a robust robotic carrier and positioning framework for future contactless pollination systems. While the current study focuses on perception guided targeting and positioning, the developed platform provides a practical foundation for integrating advanced contactless end effectors, including acoustic based pollen manipulation modules, in future work.

</details>


### [26] [Bi3: A Biplatform, Bicultural, Biperson Dataset for Social Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06863)
*Andrew Stratton,Phani Teja Singamaneni,Pranav Goyal,Rachid Alami,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Bi3是一个在受限实验室空间中收集的社交机器人导航数据集，包含74名参与者、两种机器人平台、五种导航算法，以及10.5小时的多模态数据（运动轨迹、RGB视频、用户印象），为密集人群中的机器人导航研究提供了独特基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社交机器人导航数据集缺乏在受限空间中人与机器人近距离相遇的多样性和复杂性，Bi3旨在填补这一空白，以推动对机器人在密集拥挤环境中与人类活动有效融合的理解。

Method: 设计原始实验方案，在受限实验室空间中让两组人类与机器人之间产生近距离导航相遇；使用两种不同机器人平台和五种不同导航算法；从美国和法国两个地点招募74名参与者；采集包括10.5小时的人类与机器人真实运动轨迹、RGB视频以及用户对机器人性能的印象评分等多模态数据。

Result: 通过交互密度和人类速度等指标分析，表明Bi3数据集在多样性和建模复杂性方面具有独特的基准价值。

Conclusion: Bi3数据集有助于理解人类与机器人在受限环境中如何有效融合各自的活动，可作为人类运动预测模型和密集拥挤空间导航机器人控制策略的训练资源。

Abstract: We contribute Bi3, a dataset of social robot navigation among groups of people in a constrained lab space. Compared to prior data collection efforts for social robot navigation, our dataset is unique in that it features: an original experiment design giving rise to close navigation encounters between two humans and a robot; five different navigation algorithms; two different robot platforms; a diverse participant pool of 74 people recruited from two sites in the USA and France; multimodal data streams including 10.5 hours of human and robot ground-truth motion tracks, RGB video, and user impressions over robot performance. Our analysis of the collected dataset through metrics like interaction density and human velocity suggests that Bi3 represents a benchmark of unique diversity and modeling complexity. Bi3 contributes towards understanding how humans and robots can productively mesh their activities in constrained environments, and can be a resource for training models of human motion prediction and robot control policies for navigation in densely crowded spaces.

</details>


### [27] [Variable Aerodynamic Damping via Co-Contraction: A Dynamic Isomorphism with Variable Stiffness Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07292)
*Antonio Franchi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文证明冗余双旋翼执行器中的气动共收缩能够调节由配平定义的被动气动机械阻尼，同时保持指令净力恒定。通过定义增量阻尼系数并证明其在气动硬化条件下沿等力纤维单调递增，基于叶素理论推导了最小推力模型，揭示了速度-入流耦合机制。将该机制形式化为可变气动阻尼执行器（VADA），并证明其在动态上与拮抗变刚度执行器（VSA）中的刚度调制同构。最后提出阻抗形式表示以阐明共模与差模驱动在被动阻抗和平衡速度控制中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在冗余多旋翼系统中，通过气动共收缩（co-contraction）独立调节阻尼而不改变净推力的可能性，从而为执行器提供可变的被动阻抗控制能力。

Method: 理论证明增量阻尼系数沿等力纤维单调递增；基于叶素理论（Blade Element Theory）推导最小推力模型（affine in inflow）；建立VADA与VSA之间的动态同构关系；提出阻抗形式表示。

Result: 验证了气动共收缩可单调调节阻尼；推导出速度-入流耦合的显式模型；证明了VADA与VSA在数学上的动态同构性；纤维密度原理亦可用于增强主动气动响应速度。

Conclusion: 冗余双旋翼执行器可通过共收缩实现独立于净推力的可变阻尼调节，为被动阻抗控制提供新机制，其动态特性与生物肌腱共激活类比，拓展了多旋翼系统的阻抗控制维度。

Abstract: We prove that aerodynamic co-contraction in a redundant dual-rotor actuator can tune a passive, trim-defined aero-mechanical damping while keeping the commanded net force constant. In particular, we define an incremental damping coefficient as the local sensitivity of net thrust to air-relative velocity at a trim and prove that it increases monotonically along constant-force fibers under a mild aerodynamic hardening condition. We then validate the required damping and hardening properties from a first-principles Blade Element Theory derivation, which yields a minimal thrust model affine in inflow and explicitly reveals the speed--inflow coupling driving the effect. The resulting mechanism is formalized as a Variable Aerodynamic Damping Actuator (VADA) and shown to be dynamically isomorphic to stiffness modulation in antagonistic variable-stiffness actuation (VSA), similar to the co-contraction of tendons by muscle co-activation. The same fiber-density principle also enhances the active aerodynamic promptness measure of redundant multirotors. Finally, an impedance-form representation clarifies the roles of common-mode and differential-mode actuation in the control of passive impedance and the equilibrium velocity of the VADA system.

</details>


### [28] [Traffic Scenario Orchestration from Language via Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06966)
*Frieda Rong,Chris Zhang,Kelvin Wong,Raquel Urtasun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于约束求解的场景编排方法，利用基础模型将自然语言描述转化为约束集，再借助求解器生成满足测试意图的自动驾驶闭环测试场景，在多项基准测试中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶仿真测试中，手动编程场景难以规模化，统计模型难以精确控制分布外场景，需要一种既能精确控制又能规模化生成测试场景的方法。

Method: 将场景编排建模为约束求解问题：先利用基础模型推理将自然语言描述转化为约束集（场景表示），再使用现成求解器求解满足精确测试意图的智能体行为，实现闭环测试。

Result: 在精心设计的多样化场景描述基准测试中，该方法的编排成功率大幅优于基线方法，尤其在需要自车反应性规约的场景中，闭环方法效果显著。

Conclusion: 语言输入-仿真输出的场景编排器有效结合了基础模型的语义理解能力和约束求解的精确可控性，为自动驾驶闭环测试中的分布外场景生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles (AVs) require extensive testing in simulation, but test case generation for driving scenarios is laborious. The desired scenarios are often out-of-distribution and have precise requirements on interactions with the AV policy under test. Manually programming scenarios allows for precise controllability but is difficult to scale. On the other hand, statistical models can leverage compute and data, but struggle with precise controllability when out-of-distribution. We cast scenario orchestration as a constraint-solving problem and present a language-in, simulation-out scenario orchestrator for closed-loop testing AVs. Our approach leverages foundation model reasoning to translate general, natural language descriptions into a set of constraints as a scenario representation. This then allows us to leverage off the shelf solvers to solve for actor behaviors which meet precise testing intentions in closed-loop. Under a benchmark of carefully crafted and diverse scenario descriptions, our approach greatly outperforms our baselines in orchestration success rate. We further show that our closed-loop approach is especially important for scenarios which require ego-reactive specifications.

</details>


### [29] [MORPH-U: Multi-Objective Resilient Motion Planning for V2X-Enabled Autonomous Driving in High-Uncertainty Environments via Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07370)
*Shih-Yu Lai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MORPH-U框架，融合V2X与本地传感器构建动态地图，结合Hybrid-A*重规划与拜占庭容错门控机制，实现不确定性下鲁棒的运动规划与控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: V2X通信存在延迟、丢包、伪造等不确定性，同时地图信息可能动态变化，车辆需在严格实时约束下重规划。现有方法缺乏对这类事件驱动更新的鲁棒性保障。

Method: 构建CARBA闭环栈，融合LiDAR/雷达/相机与V2X(CAM/DENM)至局部动态地图(LDM)；使用Hybrid-A*触发重规划；通过多目标优化(跟踪误差、TTC安全裕度、响应性、平滑性)暴露规划/控制权衡，Pareto前沿分析选取工作点；引入拜占庭式接受门控（法定人数规则+传感器否决权）防止恶意V2X触发的不安全重规划。

Result: V2X增强的LDM提升下游安全性；Pareto调优实现可控的精度-舒适度权衡；门控机制在饱和虚假DENM注入攻击(p_attack=1.0)下阻止不安全重规划。

Conclusion: MORPH-U有效融合V2X与本地感知，通过拜占庭容错门控抵御恶意攻击，为不确定事件驱动下的自动驾驶规划控制提供鲁棒方案。

Abstract: V2X can warn an autonomous vehicle about hazards beyond line-of-sight, but it also brings uncertainty: messages may be delayed, dropped, or even forged. Meanwhile, map knowledge may change during a trip, forcing the vehicle to replan under tight real-time budgets. This paper studies how to make motion planning and low-level control robust to such uncertain, event-driven updates. We present MORPH-U, a CARLA-based closed-loop stack that fuses LiDAR/radar/camera with V2X (CAM/DENM) into a Local Dynamic Map (LDM) and triggers Hybrid-A* replanning when validated hazards or map changes affect the planned route. We expose the planning/control trade-offs via a multi-objective formulation over tracking error, safety margin (minimum TTC), responsiveness, and smoothness, and select operating points using Pareto-frontier analysis. To avoid unsafe replanning from faulty V2X triggers, MORPH-U adds a lightweight Byzantine-inspired acceptance gate that combines a quorum rule with an on-board sensor veto. Experiments in dynamic CARLA scenarios show that V2X-augmented LDM improves downstream safety, Pareto tuning provides controllable accuracy-comfort trade-offs, and the gate prevents replanning under saturated false-DENM injection ($p_{\text{attack}}=1.0$).

</details>


### [30] [Intention assimilation control for accurate tracking with variable impedance in teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07037)
*Atsushi Takagi,Yanan Li,Hiroaki Gomi,Etienne Burdet*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种意图同化控制（IAC）策略，通过估计领导者目标位置而非当前位置来传输给跟随者，从而在不依赖高刚度的前提下实现高跟踪精度，并允许实时调节阻抗，在双边遥操作中实现了位置与阻抗的协同控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥操作中弹簧类力控策略存在跟踪精度与安全性之间的权衡：低刚度导致跟踪精度下降，高刚度则对环境中的人或物体构成危险。现有方法无法同时满足高精度跟踪与安全阻抗。

Method: 提出意图同化控制（IAC）策略：不传输领导者的当前位置，而是估计并传输其目标位置；跟随者阻抗可在线实时调整，以反映用户期望阻抗或自动满足任务需求。

Result: 在两个7自由度机械臂上的四组实验（自由跟踪、气球交互、插销插入、力反馈桌面抛光）中，与遥阻抗控制（TIC）相比，IAC提高了跟踪精度、任务完成率并缩短了完成时间。

Conclusion: IAC使机器人能够在准确复现用户运动的同时，允许用户根据意图自由调节阻抗，在单边和双边遥操作中实现了对跟随者位置和阻抗的前所未有的控制水平。

Abstract: Robot systems for teleoperation commonly use a spring-like force pulling the follower robot towards the leader's position to track their movements. With this control strategy, the tracking accuracy deteriorates when the follower' stiffness is low, but high stiffness poses a danger to objects or people in the follower robot's environment. To address this trade-off between tracking accuracy and safety, we propose an alternative intention assimilation control (IAC) strategy where the robot's tracking accuracy can be ensured without high stiffness. Different from traditional approaches, which transmit the leader's current position to the follower, this new controller estimates the leader's target position and transmits it to the follower. With this strategy, the follower impedance can be changed on-the-fly to continuously reflect the user's desired impedance or modulated automatically to fulfill the task requirements. Our controller was validated on two 7 degree-of-freedom manipulators, yielding high tracking accuracy with varying impedance. Four experiments were conducted to compare {teleoperation} with IAC to tele-impedance control (TIC) during free tracking, interaction with a balloon, during peg insertion, and table polishing with force feedback. The results show that IAC increases tracking accuracy, improves task completion rate and reduces completion time. IAC enables the robot to accurately replicate the user's movement while giving them freedom to modulate the impedance according to their intention, providing an unprecedented level of control of the follower's position and its impedance during unilateral and bilateral teleoperation.

</details>


### [31] [Dr-BA: Separable Optimization for Direct Radar Bundle Adjustment & Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07041)
*Daniil Lisus,Cedric Le Gentil,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dr-BA，首个直接在2D雷达强度图像上进行雷达束调整（BA）的框架。与传统雷达方法提取稀疏点云进行配准不同，Dr-BA利用多帧扫描的完整雷达回波联合估计稠密地图和传感器位姿，通过可分离优化将位姿估计与建图解耦，并自然扩展到直接雷达定位（DRL）。在超过200公里道路数据上实现了最先进的雷达BA和跨会话定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达状态估计方法通常从雷达测量中提取稀疏点云并使用点云配准技术来估计车辆运动或场景结构，未能充分利用雷达数据的完整信息。同时，雷达相比相机和激光雷达在恶劣天气下具有显著优势，因此需要一种能够直接利用完整雷达回波进行稠密建图和精确位姿估计的框架。

Method: Dr-BA将雷达束调整问题建模为可分离优化，将位姿估计与建图解耦为两个子问题分别求解。该方法直接操作在2D旋转雷达强度图像上，利用多帧扫描的完整雷达回波来联合估计稠密地图和传感器位姿，并自然扩展到直接雷达定位（DRL）。

Result: 在超过200公里、五个不同路线的道路数据上，Dr-BA实现了当前最先进的雷达束调整和跨会话定位性能。

Conclusion: Dr-BA作为首个直接基于雷达强度图像的束调整框架，通过充分利用完整雷达回波信息和可分离优化策略，有效提升了雷达状态估计的精度和鲁棒性，为全天气候条件下自动驾驶系统的环境感知与定位提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces Dr-BA, a first-of-its-kind radar bundle adjustment (BA) framework that operates directly on 2D spinning radar intensity images. Unlike camera or lidar sensors, radar is largely unaffected by precipitation, making it a critical modality for autonomous systems that require all-weather robustness. Existing state estimation approaches using spinning radar typically extract sparse point clouds from range-azimuth-intensity measurements and apply point cloud alignment techniques to estimate vehicle motion, scene structure, or to localize within an existing map. In contrast, Dr-BA uses the full radar returns from multiple scans to jointly estimate dense maps and sensor poses. By formulating the problem as a separable optimization, we derive an efficient and general solution that decouples pose estimation from mapping. In addition to solving the BA problem, this formulation naturally extends to direct radar-only localization (DRL) within a previously built map. Dr-BA achieves state-of-the-art radar-based BA and cross-session localization performance, demonstrated on more than 200 km of on-road data across five distinct routes. Our implementation is publicly available at https://github.com/utiasASRL/dr_ba.

</details>


### [32] [PISTO: Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07215)
*Hongzhe Yu,Zinuo Chang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PISTO算法，将随机轨迹优化与变分推断相统一，通过KL正则化的近端优化改进STOMP，在机器人运动规划任务中成功率89%，优于CHOMP(63%)和STOMP(68%)，并在MuJoCo任务中持续优于CEM和MPPI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: STOMP虽能处理不可微成本，但其更新机制缺乏清晰的概率解释，稳定性不足。作者旨在揭示STOMP的变分推断本质，并基于此设计更稳定高效的算法。

Method: 1) 揭示STOMP隐式最小化Boltzmann轨迹分布上的KL散度，建立变分推断视角；2) 提出PISTO，在高斯分布间添加KL正则化作为近端项，形成信赖域约束；3) 通过重要性加权蒙特卡洛估计闭式均值更新，保持无导数特性。

Result: 机器人臂运动规划：PISTO成功率89%（CHOMP 63%, STOMP 68%），路径更短更平滑，速度提升2倍；MuJoCo接触丰富任务：PISTO在奖励上持续优于CEM和MPPI。

Conclusion: 将轨迹优化纳入变分推断框架能有效提升算法稳定性与性能，PISTO在保持STOMP处理不可微成本优势的同时实现了显著更优的规划效果。

Abstract: Stochastic trajectory optimization methods like STOMP enable planning with non-differentiable costs, offering substantial flexibility over gradient-based approaches. We show that STOMP implicitly minimizes the KL divergence from a Boltzmann trajectory distribution, revealing an elegant Variational Inference (VI) structure underlying its updates. Building on this insight, we propose the \textit{Proximal Inference for Stochastic Trajectory Optimization} (PISTO) algorithm that stabilizes the updates by augmenting the objective with a KL regularization between successive Gaussian proposals. This proximal formulation admits a trust-region interpretation and yields closed-form mean updates computable as expectations under a surrogate distribution. We estimate these expectations via importance-weighted Monte Carlo sampling, producing a simple, derivative-free algorithm that inherits STOMP's ability to handle non-differentiable and discontinuous costs without modification. On robot arm motion planning benchmarks, PISTO achieves an 89\% success rate -- outperforming CHOMP (63\%) and STOMP (68\%) -- while producing shorter, smoother paths at twice the speed of competing stochastic methods. We further validate PISTO on contact-rich MuJoCo locomotion and manipulation tasks, where it consistently outperforms both CEM and MPPI baselines in reward.

</details>


### [33] [Palm-sized Omnidirectional Vision-Based UAV Exploration with Sparse Topological Map Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07275)
*Zirui Wang,Xinjia Luo,Haotian Sun,Jun Ma,Jian Guo,Boyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级自主探索系统，利用全向视觉和稀疏拓扑地图引导，在受SWaP限制的微型无人机上实现高效探索，无需占用网格或全局点云，计算与内存消耗极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统探索方法依赖密集占用地图或高精度点云，内存和计算开销大；微型无人机受尺寸、重量和功耗（SWaP）限制，难以搭载LiDAR等高精度传感器获取环境几何测量。

Method: 采用多鱼眼相机实现全向视场（FoV）并进行深度估计；将前沿表示为拓扑节点表征的潜在未探索区域，而非显式边界；构建由关键节点及其描述子组成的稀疏拓扑地图替代密集表示；直接在稀疏图上进行全局路径规划。

Result: 在仿真和实物实验中，在轴距11 cm、重量400 g的掌上视觉无人机上验证了该方法，实现了极低计算消耗下的高效探索。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了SWaP约束下微型无人机自主探索的瓶颈，以稀疏拓扑表示替代密集地图，大幅降低了计算与存储需求，同时保持了探索效率。

Abstract: Classic exploration methods often rely on dense occupancy maps or high-resolution point clouds for frontier detection and path planning, resulting in substantial memory consumption and computational overhead. Moreover, micro UAVs under size, weight, and power (SWaP) constraints are not practical to be equipped with sensors like LiDAR to obtain accurate environmental geometric measurements. This paper presents a lightweight autonomous exploration system that leverages omnidirectional vision and sparse topological map guidance. Specifically, we utilize a multi-fisheye camera setup to achieve omnidirectional Field of View (FoV) and perform depth estimation. To address the limited depth estimation accuracy, frontiers are represented as potential unexplored regions characterized by topological nodes instead of explicit boundaries, enabling efficient identification of frontier regions without maintaining occupancy grids or global point clouds. Unlike classic dense representations, our approach abstracts the environment using a sparse topological map composed of key nodes and their descriptors, reducing memory consumption and computational demands. Global path planning is performed directly on the sparse graph. The proposed method is validated in both simulation and on a palm-sized vision-based UAV with an 11 cm wheelbase and a 400 g weight in real-world experiments, demonstrating that our method can achieve efficient exploration with extremely low computational consumption.

</details>


### [34] [BioProVLA-Agent: An Affordable, Protocol-Driven, Vision-Enhanced VLA-Enabled Embodied Multi-Agent System with Closed-Loop-Capable Reasoning for Biological Laboratory Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07306)
*Zhaohui Du,Zhe Wang,Hongmei Fei,Xiwen Cao,Ting Xiao,Qi Wang,Huanbo Jin,Jiaming Gu,Quan Lu,Zhe Liu*
