<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物实验室自动化能减少重复人工劳动并提高可重复性，但现有机器人系统依赖昂贵硬件、固定工作流、专用仪器或面向机器人的接口，且协议非结构化、器皿透明或反光、多步骤流程需要状态感知执行，这些因素使得湿实验室环境下的可靠具身执行仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出BioProVLA-Agent系统：1) 使用协议作为任务接口，构建协议解析、视觉状态验证和具身执行的闭环流程；2) 包含三个智能体——Tailored LLM Protocol Agent将协议转化为可验证子任务，VLM-RAG Verification Agent利用观察、机器人状态、检索知识和成功/失败示例评估准备与完成状态，VLA Embodied Agent通过轻量策略执行已验证的子任务；3) 开发AugSmolVLA在线增强策略，专门针对透明器皿、反射、光照变化和过度曝光等湿实验室视觉扰动进行数据增强。

Result: 在覆盖15个原子任务、6个复合工作流和3个双手任务的层次化基准上评估，包括试管装载、分拣、废物处理、旋盖和液体倾倒。在正常和高曝光设置下，AugSmolVLA在精确放置、透明物体操作、复合工作流和视觉退化场景中相比ACT、X-VLA和原始SmolVLA提升了执行稳定性。

Conclusion: 本系统为生物操作提供了一条经济实惠、以协议为中心且具备验证能力的具身AI实用路径。

Abstract: Biological laboratory automation can reduce repetitive manual work and improve reproducibility, but reliable embodied execution in wet-lab environments remains challenging. Protocols are often unstructured, labware is frequently transparent or reflective, and multi-step procedures require state-aware execution beyond one-shot instruction following. Existing robotic systems often rely on costly hardware, fixed workflows, dedicated instruments, or robotics-oriented interfaces. Here, we introduce BioProVLA-Agent, an affordable, protocol-driven, vision-enhanced embodied multi-agent system enabled by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models for biological manipulation. The system uses protocols as the task interface and integrates protocol parsing, visual state verification, and embodied execution in a closed-loop workflow. A Tailored LLM Protocol Agent converts protocols into verifiable subtasks; a VLM-RAG Verification Agent assesses readiness and completion using observations, robot states, retrieved knowledge, and success/failure examples; and a VLA Embodied Agent executes verified subtasks through a lightweight policy. To improve robustness under wet-lab visual perturbations, we develop AugSmolVLA, an online augmentation strategy targeting transparent labware, reflections, illumination shifts, and overexposure. We evaluate the system on a hierarchical benchmark covering 15 atomic tasks, 6 composite workflows, and 3 bimanual tasks, including tube loading, sorting, waste disposal, cap twisting, and liquid pouring. Across normal and high-exposure settings, AugSmolVLA improves execution stability over ACT, X-VLA, and the original SmolVLA, especially for precise placement, transparent-object manipulation, composite workflows, and visually degraded scenes. These results suggest a practical route toward accessible, protocol-centered, and verification-capable embodied AI for biological manipulation.

</details>


### [35] [AT-VLA: Adaptive Tactile Injection for Enhanced Feedback Reaction in Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07308)
*Xiaoqi Li,Muhe Cai,Jiadong Xu,Juan Zhu,Hongwei Fan,Yan Shen,Guangrui Ren,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AT-VLA框架，通过自适应触觉注入机制和触觉反应双流机制，解决了VLA模型在精密接触操作中触觉模态引入干扰预训练能力及推理速度慢的问题，实现了0.04秒内的实时闭环响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在需要精确物理交互的接触密集型操作场景中面临挑战，现有方法在下游任务中引入触觉信号进行微调会破坏VLA的预训练能力，且VLA推理速度慢限制了触觉反馈的实时利用。

Method: 提出自适应触觉注入机制（动态决定触觉注入的时机和位置）和触觉反应双流机制（将感知处理解耦为低频视觉-语言推理流和高频触觉控制流，实现0.04秒内实时闭环响应）。

Result: 在真实世界的接触密集型操作任务中验证了AT-VLA的有效性，实现了0.04秒内的实时闭环触觉响应。

Conclusion: AT-VLA通过自适应触觉注入和双流解耦机制，在保持VLA预训练能力的同时，显著提升了接触密集型操作的实时触觉反馈能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have significantly advanced the capabilities of robotic agents in executing diverse tasks; however, they still face challenges in contact-rich manipulation scenarios that require precise physical interactions. To address this limitation, recent studies have attempted to incorporate tactile signals during downstream tasks, enabling pretrained VLAs to interpret tactile feedback. Nevertheless, introducing new modalities during finetuning, which are rarely present in the pretrain stage, may disrupt the pretrained capabilities of VLAs. In addition, the inherently slow inference speed of VLAs hampers real-time responsiveness and limits the effective utilization of tactile feedback for action adjustment. To overcome these challenges, we propose Adaptive Tactile Vision-Language-Action (AT-VLA), which introduces a novel Adaptive Tactile Injection mechanism. This mechanism dynamically determines the appropriate timing and locations for tactile injection, incorporating only when it significantly contributes to action generation, thereby minimizing interference with pretrained representations. Furthermore, to enable rapid and accurate tactile responses, we propose a Tactile Reaction Dual-Stream mechanism, which decouples sensory processing into a slow visual-language stream for low-frequency perceptual reasoning and a fast tactile control stream for high-frequency physical interaction understanding, achieving real-time close-loop responses within 0.04 s. Real-world experiments thoroughly validate the effectiveness of AT-VLA in contact-rich manipulation tasks. The project page is available at: https://sites.google.com/view/at-vla.

</details>


### [36] [CSR: Infinite-Horizon Real-Time Policies with Massive Cached State Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07325)
*Robin Karlsson,Go Suzui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CSR框架和ASR算法，通过理论证明最优任务结构条件并实际实现KV缓存重用与异步状态召回，在真实机器人上实现26倍延迟降低（14.67s→0.56s），支持235B大模型以>2Hz频率连续运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM作为机器人连续认知引擎受限于首次令牌延迟（TTFT），现有方案如RAG或滑动窗口牺牲全局上下文或带来高昂重计算成本，亟需在保证全局信息的前提下实现实时推理延迟。

Method: 首先形式化定义最低延迟的最优任务结构，理论上证明前缀稳定性、增量可扩展性和异步状态协调是实时性能的必要条件；基于此提出CSR框架实现最优KV缓存重用，并设计ASR算法将状态内存驱逐卸载到并行计算资源上以避免延迟尖峰。

Result: 在真实机器人上，CSR在235B参数模型、120K token上下文中实现26倍延迟降低（14.67s→0.56s）；在具身AI基准上达到SOTA召回率（0.836 vs 0.459）并保持RAG级延迟；ASR在10次连续实际驱逐周期中验证了有界无尖峰的TTFT。

Conclusion: CSR和ASR联合使大规模LLM能够作为连续运行的高频（>2Hz）具身策略，突破了TTFT瓶颈，为LLM在实时机器人控制中的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Deploying massive large language models (LLMs) as continuous cognitive engines for robotics is bottlenecked by the time-to-first-token (TTFT) latency required to process extensive state histories. Existing solutions like RAG or sliding windows compromise global context or incur prohibitive re-computation costs. We formalize the optimal task structure for minimizing latency and theoretically prove that prefix stability, incremental extensibility, and asynchronous state reconciliation are necessary conditions for real-time performance. Building on these proofs, we introduce the Cached State Representation (CSR) framework as the practical instantiation of these properties, ensuring optimal KV-cache reuse. To sustain these properties over infinite horizons, we further propose an Asynchronous State Reconciliation (ASR) algorithm that offloads state memory eviction to a parallel computational resource to eliminate latency spikes. On a physical robot wirelessly connected to an on-premise GPU server, CSR achieves a 26-fold latency reduction (14.67s to 0.56s) for 120K token contexts with a 235B parameter model compared to a standard baseline. On an embodied AI benchmark, we achieve SOTA recall (0.836 vs. 0.459) while maintaining RAG-level latency. ASR is validated to sustain bounded, spike-free TTFT over 10 eviction cycles in continuous real-world operation. Together, CSR and ASR enable massive LLMs to function as continuously operating, high-frequency (> 2 Hz) embodied policies.

</details>


### [37] [Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07835)
*Ethan Schneider,Jingkai Chen,Tianyi Gu,Kunlei Lian,Seth Hutchinson,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出M2M算法解决多对多场景下的多机器人仓库拣选与配送问题（MAPD），相比传统一对一方法，该算法将任务分配建模为四维分配问题，实验证明在8小时仿真中比现有最优方法多完成最多22,000个任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实仓库中物品（通过SKU标识）可存放于多个位置，传统一对一的MAPD方法无法处理这种多对多场景，而该问题本质上是一个NP难的四维分配问题，亟需高效求解算法。

Method: 提出M2M算法，包含两个变体：M2M（最小化预估任务时长）和M2M-wSKU（在目标函数中融入SKU分布信息）。通过将多对多MAPD建模为四维分配问题进行求解。

Result: 在8小时仓库操作的仿真实验中，M2M在不同环境和仓库库存密度下均一致达到或超越现有最优方法，平均多完成最多22,000个任务。

Conclusion: M2M算法有效解决了多对多MAPD这一NP难问题，通过合理建模与目标函数设计，显著提升了仓库多机器人系统的任务吞吐效率。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems in automated warehouses must manage continuous streams of pickup-and-delivery tasks while ensuring efficiency and safety. Prior work on Multi-Agent Pickup-and-Delivery (MAPD) has largely focused on the one-to-one variant, where each task has a fixed pickup and delivery location. In contrast, real warehouses often present many-to-many MAPD scenarios, where items, tracked by stock keeping unit (SKU) identifiers, can be retrieved from or stored at multiple locations, resulting in an NP-hard four-dimensional assignment problem. To solve the many-to-many MAPD problem, we contribute our algorithm: Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (M2M). We experiment with two variants of our algorithm: one that minimizes estimated task durations (M2M), and one which incorporates SKU distribution into the objective function (M2M-wSKU). Simulation results over 8-hour warehouse operations show that our method consistently matches or outperforms prior state of the art, with M2M completing up to 22,000 more tasks on average across different environments and warehouse inventory densities.

</details>


### [38] [Weather-Robust Scene Semantics with Vision-Aligned 4D Radar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07367)
*Kali Hamilton,Christoffer Heckman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将毫米波雷达编码器对齐到冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入，并通过冻结的视觉语言模型解码场景描述，仅需约7M可训练参数。在雨天、雾天、雪天等恶劣天气下，雷达配置优于相机基线（相机基线产生超过90%的幻觉）。发现令牌范数不匹配是桥接雷达与冻结VLM的主要失败模式，投影层输出的LayerNorm可解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机和LiDAR在雨、雾、雪等恶劣天气下性能严重下降，而毫米波雷达几乎不受影响。为了在恶劣天气下仍能实现可靠的场景理解，作者探索将雷达信号与视觉语言模型桥接，利用VLM的语义理解能力。

Method: 将雷达编码器对齐到冻结的SigLIP视觉嵌入，通过投影层将雷达特征映射到视觉语言模型（VLM）的语义空间，仅训练约7M参数。使用冻结的VLM解码结构化场景描述。针对桥接过程中的令牌范数不匹配问题，在投影器输出端引入LayerNorm进行修正。

Result: 在K-RADAR数据集上（含雾、小雪、大雪等未见过的天气序列），所有雷达配置均优于相机基线，相机基线产生超过90%的幻觉。分析了编码器复杂度、字幕格式和池化策略之间的权衡，为后续雷达-VLM流水线设计提供参考。

Conclusion: 毫米波雷达能够在恶劣天气下为视觉语言模型提供可靠的输入信号，令牌范数不匹配是雷达-VLM桥接中的关键问题，LayerNorm可有效缓解。编码器复杂度、字幕格式和池化策略的权衡对雷达-VLM流水线设计具有指导意义。

Abstract: Cameras and LiDAR degrade in rain, fog, and snow, while millimeter-wave radar remains largely unaffected. We align a radar encoder to frozen SigLIP vision
  embeddings and decode structured scene captions through a frozen vision-language model (VLM) with approximately 7M trainable parameters. On K-RADAR with
  held-out fog, light snow, and heavy snow sequences, all radar configurations outperform a camera baseline that collapses to over 90% hallucination. We
  identify a token-norm mismatch as the dominant failure mode when bridging radar to a frozen VLM and show that projector-output LayerNorm resolves it.
  Analysis of encoder complexity, caption format, and pooling strategy reveals tradeoffs that inform future radar-VLM pipeline design.

</details>


### [39] [Escaping the Diversity Trap in Robotic Manipulation via Anchor-Centric Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07381)
*Yanzhe Chen,Kevin Yuchen Ma,Qi Lv,Yiqi Lin,Zechen Bai,Chen Gao,Mike Zheng Shou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出锚点中心自适应（ACA）方法，通过重复演示核心锚点再选择性扩展覆盖，在有限数据预算下解决了VLA模型部署中的"多样性陷阱"（覆盖-密度权衡），实验验证其优于标准多样性采样策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型部署到特定硬件时存在具身差距，需要真实世界自适应。由于机器人演示成本高昂，适应必须在严格的数据预算下进行。标准"最大化覆盖"启发式方法（收集多样化的单次演示）因非零估计噪声而可能适得其反，即存在"多样性陷阱"。

Method: 1. 形式化覆盖-密度权衡：将策略误差分解为估计（密度）项和外推（覆盖）项，刻画固定预算下唯一条件的最优内部分配。2. 提出ACA两阶段框架：阶段一通过核心锚点的重复演示稳定策略骨架；阶段二通过教师强制错误挖掘和约束残差更新，选择性扩展到高风险边界。

Result: 真实机器人实验验证了覆盖-密度权衡框架，并且ACA在相同预算下显著提高了任务可靠性和成功率，优于标准多样性采样策略。

Conclusion: 在数据预算有限的条件下，并非覆盖越广越好，存在最优的覆盖-密度平衡点。通过先稳定核心再选择性拓展边界的ACA方法，可以有效提升VLA模型在特定硬件上的部署性能。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models offer broad general capabilities, deploying them on specific hardware requires real-world adaptation to bridge the embodiment gap. Since robot demonstrations are costly, this adaptation must often occur under a strict data budget. In this work, we identify a critical diversity trap: the standard heuristic of "maximizing coverage" by collecting diverse, single-shot demonstrations can be self-defeating due to non-vanishing estimation noise. We formalize this phenomenon as a Coverage--Density Trade-off. By decomposing the policy error into estimation (density) and extrapolation (coverage) terms, we characterize an interior optimal allocation of unique conditions for a fixed budget. Guided by this analysis, we propose Anchor-Centric Adaptation (ACA), a two-stage framework that first stabilizes a policy skeleton through repeated demonstrations at core anchors, then selectively expands coverage to high-risk boundaries via teacher-forced error mining and constrained residual updates. Real-robot experiments validate our trade-off framework and demonstrate that ACA significantly improves task reliability and success rates over standard diverse sampling strategies under the same budget.

</details>


### [40] [PathPainter: Transferring the Generalization Ability of Image Generation Models to Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07496)
*Yijin Wang,Yuru Tian,Xijie Huang,Weiqi Gai,Mo Zhu,Xin Zhou,Yuze Wu,Fei Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用BEV图像作为全局先验的地面/近地面机器人导航系统，通过图像生成模型理解自然语言指令生成可通行性掩码，并引入跨视图定位解决里程计漂移问题，在无人机平台上成功完成160米室外长距离导航任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BEV图像能为导航提供有价值的全局先验信息，但如何充分利用这些信息以及如何在执行过程中可靠地使用它们仍是关键挑战。

Method: 1) 使用图像生成模型从自然语言中解释人类意图，识别目标目的地并生成可通行性掩码；2) 引入跨视图定位方法，将机器人里程计与BEV地图对齐，缓解传统里程计的长程漂移问题。

Result: 在无人机平台上仅使用传统局部运动规划器，成功完成了160米室外长距离导航任务。

Conclusion: 证明了基础模型的世界理解能力可以迁移到具身导航任务中，使机器人能够受益于现有图像生成模型的强泛化能力。

Abstract: Bird's-eye-view (BEV) images have been widely demonstrated to provide valuable prior information for navigation. Given the global information provided by such views, two key challenges remain: how to fully exploit this information and how to reliably use it during execution. In this paper, we propose a navigation system that uses BEV images as global priors and is designed for ground and near-ground robotic platforms. The system employs an image generation model to interpret human intent from natural language, identify the target destination, and generate traversability masks. During execution, we introduce cross-view localization to align the robot's odometry with the BEV map and mitigate long-term drift in conventional odometry. We conduct extensive benchmark experiments to evaluate the proposed method and further validate it on a UAV platform. Using only a conventional local motion planner, the UAV successfully completes a 160-meter outdoor long-range navigation task. This work demonstrates how the world-understanding capabilities of foundation models can be transferred to embodied navigation, enabling robots to benefit from the strong generalization ability of existing image generation models.

</details>


### [41] [Is the Future Compatible? Diagnosing Dynamic Consistency in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07514)
*Bo-Kai Ruan,Teng-Fang Hsiao,Ling Lo,Hong-Han Shuai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作-状态一致性（action-state consistency）作为评价世界动作模型（WAMs）可靠性的新维度，发现其能区分成功与失败的想象展开（rollouts），并基于此提出无需价值函数的共识策略，用于测试时选择候选轨迹，在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WAMs生成的未来预测仅关注视觉合理性，缺乏对预测动作与状态转移之间动态兼容性的检验，即动作-状态一致性未被系统研究作为可靠性指标。

Method: 系统比较联合预测模型和逆动力学模型，分析动作-状态一致性在成功/失败展开中的分布规律；识别背景坍塌（background collapse）这一边界条件；提出基于未来预测一致性的无价值函数共识策略进行测试时轨迹选择。

Result: 动作-状态一致性系统性地分离了成功与失败的展开，并与价值函数估计具有相似的成功-失败趋势；背景坍塌导致低动态失败轨迹具有欺骗性的一致性；共识策略在RoboCasa和RoboTwin 2.0上提升了成功率，且无需额外训练或奖励建模。

Conclusion: 动作-状态一致性既是诊断WAM可靠性的有效工具，也是无价值函数规划中的实用信号。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) enable decision-making through imagined rollouts by predicting future observations and actions. However, the reliability of these imagined futures remains under-examined: is a generated future merely visually plausible, or is it dynamically compatible with the action sequence it claims to model? In this work, we identify action-state consistency, the alignment between predicted actions and induced state transitions, as a missing reliability axis for WAMs. Through a systematic study across representative joint-prediction and inverse-dynamics models, we find that action-state consistency systematically separates successful and failed rollouts across many tasks and follows similar success-failure trends as learned value estimates. These results suggest that consistency captures decision-relevant structure beyond visual realism. We further identify background collapse as an important boundary condition, where low-dynamics failed trajectories can become deceptively consistent because static futures are easier to predict. Building on these findings, we introduce a value-free consensus strategy for test-time selection, which ranks candidate rollouts by agreement among predicted futures. This strategy improves success rates on RoboCasa and RoboTwin 2.0 without additional training or reward modeling. TL;DR: 本文提出PROBE方法，一种基于多目标优化的搜索式鲁棒性测试方法，用于识别笔记本电脑翻新机器人目标检测模型中的最小局部扰动故障，相比随机搜索效率提升3-7倍，且扰动迁移性好。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 笔记本电脑翻新机器人使用目标检测模型识别螺丝、贴纸等对象，但这些模型对小输入变化的鲁棒性不足可能导致翻新过程中损坏笔记本电脑，需要有效的鲁棒性测试方法。

Abstract: The Danish Technological Institute (DTI) focuses on transferring advanced technologies (including robots) to the industry and the public sector. One key application is laptop refurbishment using specialized robots, aimed at promoting reuse, reducing electronic waste, and supporting the European Circular Economy Action Plan. The software of such robots often includes features that use object detection models to detect objects for various purposes, such as identifying screws for laptop disassembly or detecting stickers to remove them. Ensuring the robustness of such models to small input variations remains a critical challenge, and addressing it is important to avoid potential damage to laptops during refurbishment. In this paper, we propose PROBE, a search-based robustness testing approach that leverages multi-objective optimization to identify minimal, localized perturbations that expose failures in object detection models used in the software of laptop refurbishing robots. PROBE employs NSGA-II to systematically explore the perturbation space, optimizing for failure induction considering both localization and confidence, and perturbation magnitude, while enabling the discovery of diverse failure cases. Results show that PROBE is 3$\times$ to 7$\times$ more effective than random search in generating failure-inducing perturbations, while requiring smaller perturbation magnitudes, and that the generated perturbations transfer across models. We further show that metamorphic relations provide additional insights into model robustness, enabling the assessment of stability even in non-failing cases.

</details>

*Kana Miyamoto,Kanata Suzuki,Tetsuya Ogata*

Main category: cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法主要依赖成功演示训练策略，但人类数据采集过程中失败不可避免。许多利用失败数据的方法需要额外数据处理或通过自主部署进行迭代策略更新，难以直接稳定地利用采集过程中累积的失败数据。

Result: 仿真结果表明，所提方法在使用失败数据训练时提高了任务成功率；所提指标能够识别出与成功演示结合时有利于学习的失败样本。

Abstract: Imitation learning for robotic tasks has relied primarily on policies trained only on successful demonstrations, although failures are unavoidable during human data collection. Many existing approaches for exploiting failure data require additional data processing or iterative policy updates through autonomous rollouts, making it difficult to directly and stably utilize failure data accumulated during data collection. In this work, we propose a method that learns latent representations of success-failure discrepancies and incorporates them into the attention mechanism. During inference, an appropriate latent mode is selected from the initial observation to improve action stability. Furthermore, we introduce a post-training metric that quantifies the attention discrepancy between each failure sample and successful demonstrations to select failure data. Simulation results show that the proposed method improves task success rates when trained with failure data and that the proposed metric identifies failure samples that are beneficial for learning when combined with successful demonstrations. These results suggest that the proposed method can support more efficient use of collected demonstrations in robotic data collection pipelines.

</details>


### [44] [BrickCraft: Visuomotor Skill Composition with Situated Manual Guidance for Long-Horizon Interlocking Brick Assembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07605)
*Jichuan Yu,Bowei Li,Zhenran Tang,Guanxing Lu,Chuxiong Hu,Ruixuan Liu,Changliu Liu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人装配互锁积木需要同时满足长时序任务推理、空间定位和精细操作三个方面的要求，现有方法难以有效整合这些能力。

Method: 采用相对公式化建模，将每一步装配锚定到部分结构中的参考积木，将复杂任务分解为有限的可复用基本技能；通过情境化手册将装配意图投影到实时机器人观测中，提供明确的空间引导；采用组合执行流水线链式执行这些空间定位的技能以完成长时序任务。

Result: 从有限的演示中习得熟练的装配技能，并对未见过的结构展现出强组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: BrickCraft有效桥接了高层装配规划与物理执行之间的鸿沟，实现了长时序、可泛化的自主积木装配。

Abstract: Autonomous robotic assembly of interlocking bricks demands seamless integration of long-horizon task reasoning, spatial grounding, and fine-grained manipulation. This paper presents BrickCraft, a compositional framework designed for long-horizon and generalizable interlocking brick assembly. BrickCraft models the assembly process using a relative formulation, where each step is anchored to a reference brick within the partial structure, thereby decomposing complex tasks into a finite set of reusable primitive skills. BrickCraft bridges the gap between high-level assembly plans and physical execution through situated manuals, which provide explicit spatial guidance for learned visuomotor skills by projecting the assembly intent onto real-time robot observations. Finally, BrickCraft employs a compositional execution pipeline that chains these spatially grounded skills to accomplish long-horizon assembly tasks. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that BrickCraft acquires proficient assembly skills from a limited set of demonstrations and exhibits strong compositional generalization to unseen structures. The project website is available at https://intelligent-control-lab.github.io/BrickCraft.

</details>

*Jiahua Ren,Kai Shen,Muhua Zhang,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模空间中现有3D全局重定位方法因搜索空间庞大和计算开销高，导致在线重定位时间过长。

Method: 提出离线-在线分层框架：离线阶段在网格地图中模拟LiDAR扫描生成候选位置及几何描述子索引；在线阶段先全局检索获得粗略位姿估计，再通过点云配准输出精确6-DoF位姿。

Result: 真实环境中平均重定位时间3秒，平均定位精度8厘米，计算效率较现有方法提升一个数量级，且精度相当。

Abstract: 3D global relocalization is one of the key capabilities for mobile robots in practical applications. However, in large scale spaces, existing methods often suffer from prolonged online relocalization time due to factors such as the massive pose search space and high computational overhead. To address these issues, this paper proposes an offline-online hierarchical framework that decouples the search space. In the offline phase, candidate positions and their corresponding geometric descriptor indices are generated in the map by simulating LiDAR scans within the grid map. In the online phase, a coarse pose estimate is first obtained via global retrieval, followed by point cloud registration to output precise 6-DoF pose estimates. Real-world experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves an average relocalization time of 3 s and an average localization accuracy of 8 cm in 3D environments. Compared with existing global relocalization methods, the proposed method achieves an order-of-magnitude improvement in computational efficiency while delivering comparable relocalization accuracy.

</details>


### [46] [Sensitivity-Based Robust NMPC for Close-Proximity Offshore Wind Turbine Inspection with a Tilted Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07771)
*Giuseppe Silano,Martin Saska*

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC方法，通过在线约束收紧解决倾斜多旋翼在近海风力涡轮机近距离巡检中的塔筒间距约束违例问题，在500次蒙特卡洛测试中消除了名义NMPC的违例，计算时间增加适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近距离海上风机巡检需要在风力和模型失配下对大型圆柱结构进行严格的间距控制，名义NMPC在质量、惯量、推力效率、阻力或风力条件偏离设计值时可能违反安全约束。

Method: 提出基于灵敏度的鲁棒NMPC：利用一阶参数状态灵敏度提供结构化不确定性裕度，结合阶段依赖的加性裕度处理阵风扰动，在名义NMPC基础上仅增加灵敏度传播和裕度评估模块，保持优化结构不变。

Result: 在边界关键的螺旋巡检轨迹上，500次不确定性蒙特卡洛评估表明，所提控制器消除了名义NMPC下的间距违例，求解时间仅适度增加。

Abstract: Close-proximity offshore wind turbine inspection requires strict clearance control around large cylindrical structures under wind and model mismatch. Nominal Nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) may violate safety constraints when mass, inertia, thrust effectiveness, drag, or wind conditions differ from nominal assumptions. We propose a sensitivity-based robust NMPC for a tilted multirotor that robustifies the tower-clearance constraint via online constraint tightening. First-order parametric state sensitivities provide a structured-uncertainty margin, while bounded gusts are handled by a stage-dependent additive margin. The formulation augments the nominal NMPC with sensitivity propagation and margin evaluation only, leaving the receding-horizon optimization structure unchanged. Monte-Carlo evaluation over 500 uncertainty realizations on a boundary-critical helical inspection trajectory shows that the proposed controller eliminates the clearance violations observed under nominal NMPC at the cost of a moderate increase in solve time.

</details>


### [47] [Melding LLM and temporal logic for reliable human-swarm collaboration in complex scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07877)
*Junfeng Chen,Yuxiao Zhu,An Zhuo,Xintong Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Guanghui Wen,Xiwang Dong,Meng Guo,Zhongkui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种神经符号框架，通过将时间逻辑形式化、任务自动机、LLM推理与不确定性感知调度相结合，实现动态环境下长周期人-蜂群协作的可靠任务规划，大幅降低操作员认知负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的规划工具在动态场景下难以维持可靠的任务规划：突发事件和条件变化要求持续适应，而持续的操作员监督带来巨大的认知负担。LLM生成的计划存在无效任务排序和不可行机器人动作的问题，导致频繁的人工调整。

Method: 1) 将任务目标与操作规则形式化为时态逻辑公式，将可行任务排序形式化为任务自动机；2) LLM在这些形式约束和实时感知上下文中生成可执行子任务序列；3) 不确定性感知调度器在异构蜂群中分配子任务以最大化并行性并抵抗干扰；4) 事件触发交互协议将操作员介入限制在稀疏、高层级的确认和指导上。

Result: 在异构机器人车队上的部署表明，该方法在保持对特定硬件驱动和通信不确定性鲁棒性的同时，取得了与类似方法相当的结果。

Conclusion: 该工作支持一种形式化且可扩展的范式，实现在动态环境中可靠且低开销的人-蜂群协作。

Abstract: Robot swarms promise scalable assistance in complex and hazardous environments. Task planning lies at the core of human-swarm collaboration, translating the operator's intent into coordinated swarm actions and helping determine when validation or intervention is required during execution. In long-horizon missions under dynamic scenarios, however, reliable task planning becomes difficult to maintain: emerging events and changing conditions demand continual adaptation, and sustained operator oversight imposes substantial cognitive burden. Existing LLM-based planning tools can support plan generation, yet they remain susceptible to invalid task orderings and infeasible robot actions, resulting in frequent manual adjustment. Here we introduce a neuro-symbolic framework for long-horizon human-swarm collaboration that tightly melds verifiable task planning with context-grounded LLM reasoning. We formalize mission goals and operational rules as temporal logic formulas and admissible task orderings as task automata. Conditioned on these formal constraints and live perceptual context, LLMs generate executable subtask sequences that satisfy mission rules and remain grounded in the current scene. An uncertainty-aware scheduler then assigns subtasks across the heterogeneous swarm to maximize parallelisms while remaining resilient to disruptions. An event-triggered interaction protocol further limits operator involvement to sparse, high-level confirmation and guidance. Deployment on a heterogeneous robotic fleet yields similar results while remaining robust to hardware-specific actuation and communication uncertainties. Together, these results support a formal and scalable paradigm for reliable and low-overhead human-swarm collaboration in dynamic environments

</details>


### [48] [AERO-VIS: Asynchronous Event-based Real-time Onboard Visual-Inertial SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07885)
*Yannick Burkhardt,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Simon Boche,Leonard Freißmuth,Stefan Leutenegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AERO-VIS，一种立体事件-惯性SLAM系统，集成了数据驱动的关键点检测器，通过异步处理事件流动态适应运行时需求，在无人机上实现了首个纯事件惯性SLAM闭环控制和大规模状态估计，实现了前所未有的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在高动态范围和运动模糊场景下具有鲁棒性，可用于改善视觉里程计系统。现有事件里程计方法虽不受同步处理限制，但仍以固定速率运行，限制了延迟和吞吐量。本文旨在通过异步处理事件流，实现低延迟、实时的纯事件惯性SLAM系统。

Method: 提出AERO-VIS系统，包含：(1)立体事件-惯性SLAM架构；(2)集成数据驱动的鲁棒且性能优化的关键点检测器；(3)异步事件流处理机制，动态适应下游运行时计算需求；(4)在无人机平台上实现完全机载计算。

Result: 在无人机上实现了前所未有的机载事件SLAM精度；首次展示了纯事件惯性SLAM系统的闭环无人机控制和大规模状态估计，完全依赖机载计算资源。

Conclusion: AERO-VIS证明了通过异步处理事件流并集成数据驱动关键点检测器，可以在资源受限的无人机平台上实现高性能的纯事件惯性SLAM系统，支持闭环控制和精确状态估计。

Abstract: The robustness of event cameras to high dynamic range and motion blur holds the potential to improve visual odometry systems in challenging environments. Although their high temporal resolution does not require synchronous processing, most event-based odometry methods still run at fixed rates, which simplifies system design but restricts latency and throughput. In this work, we present AERO-VIS, a stereo event-inertial SLAM system with an integrated, data-driven, robust, and performance-optimized keypoint detector. By processing the event stream asynchronously, the system dynamically adapts to downstream runtime demands, ensuring low-latency and real-time performance. When deploying AERO-VIS on a UAV, we achieve unprecedented accuracy in onboard event-based SLAM. These unique characteristics enable us to present the first purely event-based inertial SLAM system that demonstrates closed-loop UAV control and large-scale state estimation while relying solely on onboard compute. A video of the experiments and the source code are available at ethz-mrl.github.io/AERO-VIS.

</details>


### [49] [TAVIS: A Benchmark for Egocentric Active Vision and Anticipatory Gaze in Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07943)
*Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TAVIS 是一个用于主动视觉模仿学习评估的基准框架，包含两个任务套件（TAVIS-Head 和 TAVIS-Hands）、三种评估原语（配对头/固定相机协议、GALT 度量、ID/OOD 划分），并在两种人形机器人平台上进行了基线实验，揭示了主动视觉的优势具有任务条件性、多任务策略在分布偏移下性能急剧下降，以及纯模仿即可产生人类水平的预期性注视。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉在模仿学习领域已展现出潜力，但缺乏统一的基准来比较不同方法、量化其贡献、明确适用任务类型和条件。TAVIS 旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了两个互补任务套件（TAVIS-Head 全球搜索/TAVIS-Hands 局部遮挡），在两种人形机器人躯体（GR1T2, Reachy2）上基于 IsaacLab 实现。提出三种评估原语：配对头/固定相机协议、GALT（注视-行动超前时间）度量、程序化 ID/OOD 划分。使用 Diffusion Policy 和 π₀ 进行基线实验。

Result: (i) 主动视觉整体有帮助，但效益具有任务条件性而非均匀；(ii) 多任务策略在受控分布偏移下性能急剧下降；(iii) 纯模仿即可产生预期性注视，中位超前时间与人类遥控操作员相当。发布了约2200个示范片段、代码和训练基线。

Conclusion: TAVIS 为主动视觉模仿学习提供了标准化的评估基础设施，揭示了当前方法的局限性（分布偏移脆弱性）和潜力（纯模仿可习得预期性注视），为该领域建立了可复现的基准。

Abstract: Active vision -- where a policy controls its own gaze during manipulation -- has emerged as a key capability for imitation learning, with multiple independent systems demonstrating its benefits in the past year. Yet there is no shared benchmark to compare approaches or quantify what active vision contributes, on which task types, and under what conditions. We introduce TAVIS, evaluation infrastructure for active-vision imitation learning, with two complementary task suites -- TAVIS-Head (5 tasks, global search via pan/tilt necks) and TAVIS-Hands (3 tasks, local occlusion via wrist cameras) -- on two humanoid torso embodiments (GR1T2, Reachy2), built on IsaacLab. TAVIS provides three evaluation primitives: a paired headcam-vs-fixedcam protocol on identical demonstrations; GALT (Gaze-Action Lead Time), a novel metric grounded in cognitive science and HRI that quantifies anticipatory gaze in learned policies; and procedural ID/OOD splits. Baseline experiments with Diffusion Policy and $π_0$ reveal that (i) active-vision generally helps, but benefits are task-conditional rather than uniform; (ii) multi-task policies degrade sharply under controlled distribution shifts on both suites; and (iii) imitation alone yields anticipatory gaze, with median lead times comparable to the human teleoperator reference. Code, evaluation scripts, demonstrations (LeRobot v3.0; ~2200 episodes) and trained baselines are released at https://github.com/spiglerg/tavis and https://huggingface.co/tavis-benchmark.

</details>


### [50] [Evaluation of an Actuated Spine in Agile Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07988)
*Nico Bohlinger,Piotr Kicki,Davide Tateo,Krzysztof Walas,Jan Peters*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了在四足机器人中引入主动脊柱（1-DOF，矢状面）对学习敏捷运动能力的影响。通过在MuJoCo仿真中使用Silver Badger机器人进行实验，结果表明主动脊柱能够提升机器人在高速奔跑、攀爬楼梯、爬坡、跨障和匍匐穿越等场景中的敏捷性和越障能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脊柱在四足动物动态运动中至关重要，能提升步态的稳定性、速度和效率。受此启发，研究者希望探索主动脊柱能否同样增强四足机器人的运动能力，填补相关实证研究的空白。

Method: 在MuJoCo仿真环境中，以MAB Robotics的Silver Badger四足机器人（配备1自由度矢状面主动脊柱）为平台，设计了高速奔跑、爬楼梯、爬陡坡、跨障和匍匐穿越等多种敏捷运动场景，通过对比实验评估有无主动脊柱时的性能差异。

Result: 主动脊柱使机器人展现出更高的敏捷性，能够翻越更高的楼梯、攀爬更陡的斜坡、跨越更高的障碍物以及通过更狭窄的通道。

Conclusion: 在四足机器人中引入主动脊柱可显著增强其敏捷运动能力，是一种有效且自然的性能提升方案。

Abstract: The spine plays a crucial role in the dynamic locomotion of quadrupedal animals, improving the stability, speed, and efficiency of their gait, especially for fast-paced and highly agile movements. Therefore, the spine is also a promising and natural way to extend the capabilities of quadruped robots. This paper empirically investigates the benefits of an actuated spine for learning agile quadruped locomotion. We evaluate whether the use of the spine brings benefits in terms of high-speed running, climbing stairs, climbing high-angle slopes, hurdling, and crawling scenarios. We conducted an empirical study in MuJoCo simulation using the Silver Badger robot from MAB Robotics with an actuated 1-DOF spine in the sagittal plane. The obtained results show that the use of the spine provides the robot with increased agility and allows it to overcome higher stairs, steeper slopes, higher obstacles, and smaller passages.

</details>

Abstract: We present an active embodiment identification method for legged robots that jointly learns information-seeking behavior and explicit embodiment prediction. Using a history-augmented URMA architecture, the method infers joint-level and global embodiment parameters through interaction with the environment in simulation across different morphologies.

</details>

### [52] [Enhancing Eye Movement Biometrics for User Authentication via Continuous Gaze Offset Score Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06810)
*Hashim Aziz,Mehedi Hasan Raju,Oleg V. Komogortsev*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续注视偏移（continuous gaze offset）作为辅助生物特征信息，与现有眼球运动生物特征（EMB）进行线性和非线性融合，实验证明非线性融合方法在实验室眼动仪和VR头显数据集上均能提升认证性能，且多任务融合效果更佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的眼球运动生物特征系统通常忽略连续注视偏移，尽管已有证据表明该特征包含用户区分性信息。本文旨在验证连续注视偏移能否在融合场景下提升生物特征识别性能。

Method: 采用线性和非线性融合方法，将连续注视偏移与现有EMB特征结合；在实验室级眼动仪和VR头显两个公开数据集上，跨多个任务和观测时长进行实验评估。

Result: 融合方法在两个数据集上均表现出性能提升，尤其是非线性融合效果更优；多任务信息融合进一步改善了认证性能。

Conclusion: 连续注视偏移可作为眼动追踪信号退化或含噪条件下的有效辅助生物特征信息，支持其在EMB系统中的实用价值。

Abstract: Eye movement biometrics (EMB) use subject-specific gaze dynamics for user authentication and identification. Recent deep learning-based EMB systems achieve strong performance by modeling temporal eye movement behavior. However, these systems typically overlook continuous gaze offset, despite prior evidence that it contains user-discriminative information. This work examines whether continuous gaze offset can improve biometric performance when combined with existing biometric features. We evaluate linear and nonlinear fusion methods on two publicly available datasets, collected via the lab-grade eye tracker and virtual reality headset across multiple tasks and observation durations. Results indicate that fusion offers performance benefits on both datasets, particularly when using nonlinear fusion. Additionally, fusing biometric information across multiple tasks further improves authentication performance. These findings support the hypothesis that continuous gaze offset may serve as useful auxiliary information under conditions of degraded or noisy eye tracking.

</details>


### [53] [Privacy Perceptions in Sensor-Powered Smart Vehicle Cabins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06847)
*BoRui Li,Bofan Yu,Xing-Dong Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过18名参与者的半结构化访谈，探讨了智能汽车座舱中车主（车辆购买者）与非车主（临时使用者，如家人、朋友、租车者）对隐私的认知差异，揭示了影响两类群体隐私偏好的共同因素及差异化因素，并提出了支持多方隐私需求的设计启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着传统汽车座舱向集成多种传感器的智能环境转变，隐私理解和管理的复杂性增加，而车主与非车主对隐私的感知差异尚不明确，亟需系统研究。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈法，对18名参与者（涵盖车主与非车主）进行定性研究，通过主题分析识别影响隐私认知的关键因素。

Result: 发现影响车主和非车主隐私偏好的共同因素，以及某些因素对其中一群体具有更强的影响作用。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，提出未来智能座舱设计应更好地支持和平衡多方利益相关者的多样化隐私需求。

Abstract: As car cabins evolve with the integration of diverse sensors, traditional car cabins are transforming into smart environments. This shift raises important questions about how privacy is understood and managed in such spaces. In this work, we investigate privacy perceptions from the perspectives of both vehicle owners (i.e., people who purchase and own cars) and non-owners (i.e., people who temporarily use cars, such as family members, friends, or renters). Through semi-structured interviews with eighteen participants, we identified key factors that influence these groups' views on privacy. Our findings reveal factors that commonly influence privacy preferences for both owners and non-owners, as well as factors that have a stronger impact on one group over the other. Drawing on these insights, we discuss design implications for future designs to better support and balance the diverse privacy needs of multiple stakeholders in smart car cabins.

</details>


### [54] [Leveraging fNIRS to Evaluate Workload for Adaptive Training in Virtual Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06909)
*Cara A. Spencer,Christopher D. Wickens,Jalynn B. Nicoly,James Crum,Benjamin A. Clegg,Joanna E. Lewis,Francisco R. Ortega,Lucas Plabst,Rebecca L. Pharmer,Leanne Hirshfield*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究验证了功能性近红外光谱（fNIRS）作为认知负荷的神经工效学测量指标在VR自适应训练中的有效性。36名参与者完成VR形状组装训练任务，fNIRS结果证实了内在负荷与工作记忆相关皮层（背外侧前额叶、喙部前额叶、左侧角回）的显著激活，而外在负荷仅激活右侧角回，且与NASA-TLX主观测量高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技术发展为VR与实时自适应训练的结合提供了可能，需要一个有效的神经工效学指标来测量认知负荷，从而动态调整任务难度至最优水平。本研究旨在验证fNIRS能否区分认知负荷理论中两种不同形式的负荷（外在负荷与内在负荷）。

Method: 36名参与者完成VR形状组装训练任务，佩戴近全头覆盖式fNIRS设备，同时收集主观工作负荷评分（NASA-TLX），分析不同认知负荷条件下的脑区激活模式。

Result: fNIRS结果证实内在负荷显著激活背外侧前额叶、喙部前额叶皮层和左侧角回（与工作记忆、短时记忆缓冲、多感觉整合和注意力相关），而外在负荷仅激活右侧角回（被认为与任务掌握无关）；fNIRS结果与NASA-TLX主观测量高度一致。

Conclusion: fNIRS能够有效区分内在负荷与外在负荷的神经活动模式，可作为自适应VR训练中认知负荷的有效神经工效学测量指标。

Abstract: Advance in technology offer the potential for future adoption of a combination of virtual reality (VR) and real-time adaptivity to enhance training and education. Providing a valid neuro-ergonomic measure of cognitive load can enable an adaptive training regime to continuously adjust tas difficulty to an optimal level as training progresses. The current study validated the functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) measure of cognitive load to reflect the demands of two different forms of lad within Cognitive Load Theory: extraneous and intrinsic to he task to be mastered. Thirty-six participants completed a VR shape assembly training task followed by a test of their skill retention They wore near-full head coverage fNIRS and provided subjective ratings of ther workload. The fNIRS findings largely corroborate intrinsic workload literature with significant activation in cortical regions (dorsolateral and rostral prefrontal cortex and left angular gyrus) associated with working memory, short term memory buffers, multisensory integration, and attention. These fNIRS results were tracked closely by NASA TLS measures of mental workload. The results also revealed far less brain activity associated with extraneous load, namely just the right angular gyrus, deemed irrelevant to the mastery of the task.

</details>


### [55] [From Surface Learning to Deep Understanding: A Grounded AI Tutoring System for Moodle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06963)
*Anna Ostrowska,Michał Kukla,Gabriela Majstrak,Jan Opala,Sebastian Pergała,Jan Skwarek,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AI教学与学习助手，一个基于检索增强生成（RAG）的模块化Moodle插件，通过双中心设计（学生苏格拉底式辅导+教师人机协作审核）实现高质量、无幻觉的教育内容生成。Ragas评估显示忠实度达0.97，推荐率4.00/5.00。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统大语言模型在教育场景中易产生幻觉和错误信息，缺乏对教师提供材料的忠实引用，且无法有效支持深度概念掌握。现有方案缺乏一个既能为学生提供互动辅导、又能让教师监督内容生成的统一平台。

Method: 采用模块化Moodle插件架构，结合检索增强生成（RAG）技术，设计双中心系统：学生端基于苏格拉底式问答法进行交互式辅导，教师端提供"人在回路中"（human-in-the-loop）工作空间以监督内容生成。LLM的回答被约束在教师提供的材料范围内。

Result: 通过Ragas（LLM-as-a-Judge）框架评估，系统忠实度得分高达0.97；初步用户研究中推荐率为4.00/5.00，证实了系统在减少幻觉和提升用户满意度方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 该AI教学助手通过RAG技术有效解决了LLM在教育场景中的幻觉问题，双中心设计既保障了学生获得准确、引导式的学习体验，又赋予教师对AI生成内容的完全控制权，为AI赋能教育提供了一个可靠、可落地的解决方案。

Abstract: This demo paper describes the development of the AI Teaching \& Learning Assistant, a modular Moodle plugin that leverages Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to deliver high-quality, hallucination-free education. The system employs a dual-centric design, providing students with interactive, Socratic-based tutoring and educators with a "human-in-the-loop" workspace for supervised content generation. By grounding Large Language Model (LLM) responses in teacher-provided materials, the assistant addresses the risks of misinformation while encouraging deep conceptual mastery. Evaluation via the Ragas (LLM-as-a-Judge) framework and a preliminary user study confirms its effectiveness, achieving faithfulness scores up to 0.97 and a 4.00/5.00 recommendation rate.

</details>


### [56] [Exploring the "Banality" of Deception in Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07012)
*Ishitaa Narwane,Johanna Gunawan,Konrad Kollnig*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了生成式AI（特别是聊天机器人）中一种新型欺骗性设计——"平庸欺骗"（banal deception），即通过默认设置、自动化建议和对话交互等隐性方式进行的操纵，并提出通过引入摩擦机制（如提高用户意识、提供干预工具、改进监管）来保护用户。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注可见的界面操纵（暗模式），但随着生成式AI的兴起，欺骗变得更加隐蔽和常态化，难以被用户察觉。作者希望引入"平庸欺骗"这一概念框架，帮助学界理解并应对生成式AI体验中新型的、隐蔽的欺骗形式。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）的形式，以Simone Natale提出的"平庸欺骗"（banal deception）理论为分析框架，结合聊天机器人场景进行概念性探讨，并提出未来的研究方向与干预策略。

Result: 识别出生成式AI中欺骗的三种关键特征：隐蔽性（嵌入默认设置和对话中）、常态化（用户逐渐接受）、用户的主动参与（users' own involvement in their deception）。提出了三类应对方向：提高用户意识、提供干预工具、改进监管与执行机制。

Conclusion: "平庸欺骗"为理解和应对生成式AI中的欺骗性设计提供了有价值的分析视角。引入"摩擦"（friction）——即打破流畅体验的干预机制——是保护用户的关键策略。该框架可为后续研究和政策制定提供基础。

Abstract: Current approaches to addressing deceptive design largely focus on visible interface manipulations, commonly referred to as "dark patterns". With the rise of generative AI, deception is becoming more difficult to spot and easier to live with, as it is quietly embedded in default settings, automated suggestions, and conversational interactions rather than discrete interface elements. These subtle, normalised forms of influence, which Simone Natale frames as "banal deception", shape everyday digital use and blur the line between AI-enabled assistance and manipulation.
  This position paper explores banality as a lens through which to reason through deception in generative AI experiences, especially with chatbots. We explore what Natale describes as users' own involvement in their deception, and argue that this perspective could lead to future work for introducing friction to safeguard users from deception in generative AI interactions, such as empowering users through raising awareness, providing them with intervention tools, and regulatory or enforcement improvements. We present these concepts as points for discussion for the deceptive design scholarly community.

</details>


### [57] [Social Understanding, Placeness, and Identity Alignment: A Design Framework for Friendship-Supportive Youth Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07025)
*JaeWon Kim,Alexis Hiniker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文基于331名13-25岁青少年参与的五项实证研究，提出了一种支持青少年友谊的社交媒体设计框架，包含三大支柱（社会理解感、场所感、身份对齐感）和九个设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社交媒体设计往往未充分考虑青少年友谊发展的特殊需求，缺乏系统性的设计指导框架。

Method: 综合五项实证研究（访谈、协同设计、问卷调查、日记研究和实地部署），对209个设计相关数据点进行迭代分析。

Result: 提炼出三大设计支柱：社会理解感（互动规范、互动线索与脚手架、社会问责与治理）、场所感（第三场所与社区、边界与个人空间、共享存在感）、身份对齐感（身份货币、身份多元性、关系身份信号），并映射出九个设计空间。

Conclusion: 该框架为设计支持青少年友谊形成、深化和维持的社交媒体平台提供了共享词汇和系统化设计空间，有助于定位研究贡献、比较设计干预和识别未来研究方向。

Abstract: We present a design framework for friendship-supportive youth social media, derived from a synthesis of five empirical studies with 331 youth participants (ages 13--25) using interviews, co-design, surveys, diary studies, and a field deployment. Iterative analysis of 209 design-relevant data points identified three pillars: \textit{Sense of Social Understanding} (interaction norms, interaction cues and scaffolding, social accountability and governance), \textit{Sense of Place} (third place and community, boundaries and personal spaces, shared presence), and \textit{Sense of Identity Alignment} (identity currency, identity plurality, relational identity signals). The framework maps nine design spaces through which platforms can support the conditions under which youth friendships form, deepen, and are maintained. It offers a shared vocabulary for locating contributions, comparing design interventions, and identifying under-explored areas for future work.

</details>


### [58] [From Standard English to Singlish: A Retrieval-Augmented Approach for Code-Switched Creole Generation in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07132)
*Foong Ming Lai,Yujin Tan,Han Meng,Yi-Chieh Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 针对新加坡英语（Singlish）等接触变体的代码转换问题，提出检索增强生成（RAG）框架，通过外部方言词汇库实现可控的词汇级代码转换而无需微调。人类评估显示RAG与零样本提示在自然度和适切性上相当，但自动分析揭示两者不同机制：零样本提示产生大量改写（中位数23处编辑），而RAG仅做最小替换（中位数1处编辑），且语义保留更高（平均余弦相似度0.978 vs 0.926）。结果表明将代码转换外部化为词汇资源可在不牺牲感知质量的前提下实现可控性和可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触变体（如新加坡英语）的代码转换面临平行数据稀缺和词汇快速演化的挑战，现有方法难以实现有效且可控的方言生成。

Method: 提出RAG框架，将方言知识外部化为精选词汇库，通过检索候选Singlish表达并进行稀疏词汇替换来引导生成，无需微调模型。

Result: 164名新加坡参与者的人类评估显示RAG与零样本提示在自然度和适切性上无显著差异；自动分析显示RAG仅做中位数1处编辑（vs 零样本23处），语义保留更高（0.978 vs 0.926）。

Conclusion: 将代码转换外部化为词汇资源可在不牺牲感知质量的前提下实现可控性和可审计性，对快速演化的接触变体具有实际优势。

Abstract: Code-switching in contact varieties like Singaporean English (Singlish) challenges natural language generation due to limited parallel data and rapid lexical evolution. We propose a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) framework that externalizes dialectal knowledge into a curated lexicon, enabling controlled lexical code-switching without fine-tuning. Our approach retrieves candidate Singlish expressions and guides generation through sparse lexical substitution. Human evaluation with 164 Singaporean participants found RAG and zero-shot prompting equally natural and appropriate. Automatic analyses reveal different transformation regimes: zero-shot prompting induces extensive paraphrasing (median 23 token edits), whereas RAG performs minimal substitutions (median 1 edit) with higher semantic preservation (mean cosine similarity 0.978 vs. 0.926). Our results demonstrate that externalizing code-switching into lexical resources enables control and auditability without sacrificing perceived quality, offering practical advantages for rapidly evolving contact varieties.

</details>


### [59] [Metaphors as Scaffolds: Spatial, Embodied, Fantastical, and Relational Framings for Youth Usable Privacy Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07185)
*JaeWon Kim,Alexis Hiniker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了青少年隐私推理中隐喻设计的影响，指出空间、具身、奇幻和关系四种隐喻对青少年隐私理解与行为的不同作用，并论证隐喻选择是青少年隐私设计的首要伦理决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流可用隐私设计将隐私简化为行政性工作（设置、开关、同意复选框），脱离了青少年理解隐私的关系性、情境性和具身性框架。作者希望通过交叉分析三项青少年研究，揭示隐喻如何塑造年轻用户的隐私推理。

Method: 对三项涉及13-24岁青少年的先前研究进行跨项目交叉阅读与再分析，重点考察支撑隐私交互的隐喻类型及其对用户推理的影响。

Result: 识别出四类隐喻：空间隐喻降低认知负荷；具身隐喻提供可协商的道德词汇；奇幻隐喻将隐私管理转化为可发现的游戏，提升参与度；关系隐喻可能导致青少年因亲密感掩盖制度性数据流而越界（在AI伴侣产品中已可见风险）。

Conclusion: 隐喻选择应被理解为青少年隐私设计中的首阶伦理决策，不同类型的隐喻对青少年隐私推理和行为产生根本性不同的影响。

Abstract: Mainstream usable privacy design frames privacy as administrative work -- settings, toggles, consent checkboxes -- abstracted from the relational, contextual, and embodied registers in which youth reason about disclosure. Drawing on a cross-project reading of three prior studies with youth aged 13--24, we examine how the metaphors that scaffold a privacy interaction shape the reasoning young users bring to it. \textit{Spatial} metaphors reduce cognitive load by recruiting intuitions about navigating physical space. \textit{Embodied} metaphors furnish a shared moral vocabulary that makes implicit norms about public and private space negotiable among users. \textit{Fantastical} metaphors recast privacy management as discoverable play, raising engagement with the granular controls that nuanced self-presentation requires. \textit{Relational} metaphors, by contrast, can lead youth past their own stated boundaries when felt intimacy masks institutional data flow, a risk already visible in AI companion products. Metaphor selection, we argue, is best understood as a first-order ethical design decision for youth privacy.

</details>


### [60] [Splitting User Stories Into Tasks with AI -- A Foe or an Ally?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07320)
*Luka Pavlič,Reinhard Bernsteiner,Stephan Schlögl,Christian Ploder*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过对照实验研究了GitLab Duo等生成式AI工具在敏捷开发中辅助用户故事拆分的效果，发现AI虽不能完全替代开发者，但能生成更细粒度任务清单并防止遗漏，混合方法（AI+传统）被认为是最优方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户故事拆分为可执行任务是敏捷开发中关键但耗时的环节，现有方法效率有待提升，故探索生成式AI辅助的可能性。

Method: 采用对照实验设计，比较传统任务拆分方法与基于GitLab Duo的AI辅助方法在任务拆分效果上的差异。

Result: 当前AI工具尚未成熟到能完全替代开发者，但能生成更细粒度的任务列表并帮助避免遗漏重要任务；参与者更倾向于AI与传统方法结合的混合模式。

Conclusion: 生成式AI可作为敏捷开发中任务拆分的有效辅助工具，但需要人工监督以过滤不相关任务，混合方法是兼顾效率与准确性的可行路径。

Abstract: In agile software development, breaking down user stories into actionable tasks is a critical yet time-consuming process. This paper investigates the potential of Generative AI tools to assist in task splitting, aiming to enhance planning efficiency. We conducted a controlled experiment comparing traditional task-splitting methods with AI-assisted approaches using GitLab Duo. Our findings indicate that while current AI tools are not yet mature enough to replace developers, they can aid in generating more granular task lists and ensuring no important tasks are overlooked. Participants favored a hybrid approach, combining AI tools with conventional methods to maintain high accuracy in planning. This study highlights the potential benefits and limitations of integrating Generative AI into agile development processes, suggesting that AI tools can serve as valuable aids in task splitting, provided there is human oversight to filter out irrelevant tasks.

</details>


### [61] [Analyzing Human Heuristics and Strategies in Everyday Decision-Making Conversations for Conversational AI Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07789)
*Sora Kang,Soyun Jeon,Jinsu Eun,Kwangwon Lee,Chaerin Song,Minyoung Joo,Joonhwan Lee*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过分析955组真实韩语对话（15,476句话），发现人类在决策中优先使用满意策略而非优化策略，依赖内部知识和互动策略管理认知负荷，并识别出频率-效率不匹配现象：常见启发式维持探索性对话流，而低频规则型策略在利用阶段高效驱动决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI系统多依赖数据中心推理，而非人类在实际对话中使用的启发式和互动策略，亟需基于真实人类实践的设计基础。

Method: 分析955组真实韩语对话（涉及饮食和旅行决策），使用LLM辅助的编码流程应用决策制定编码手册进行标注和分析。

Result: 人类优先采用满意策略而非优化策略；主要依赖内部知识和互动策略管理认知负荷；发现频率-效率不匹配：高频率的启发式策略维持对话探索流，而低频规则型策略在利用阶段高效促进决议。

Conclusion: 该工作为人类启发式决策过程提供了实证基础，与认知决策理论一致，并为AI系统对齐人类启发式过程提供了设计启示。

Abstract: Conversational AI increasingly supports everyday decision-making, yet most systems rely on data-centric reasoning rather than the heuristic and interactional strategies people use in natural conversation. To ground design in actual human practice, we analyze 955 real-world Korean conversations (15,476 utterances) involving food and travel decisions, applying a decision-making codebook through an LLM-assisted coding pipeline. Our findings reveal that people prioritize satisficing over optimization, relying heavily on internal knowledge and interactional strategies to manage cognitive load. Critically, we identify a frequency-efficiency mismatch: the most prevalent heuristics sustain conversational flow during exploration, whereas infrequent, rule-based strategies are highly effective at driving resolution during exploitation. By mapping how these patterns transfer across the spectrum of human-AI interaction, this work provides empirical grounding consistent with cognitive theories of decision-making and offers design implications that align AI systems with human heuristic processes.

</details>


### [62] [A Spatial Knowledge Acquisition Comparison Between Digital Visual Thematic Maps, Non-Visual Interactive Text Thematic Maps, and Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07849)
*Brandon Biggs,Christopher Toth,James M. Coughlan,Bruce N. Walker*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究比较了视觉地图、交互式文本地图（ITM）和表格对盲人和低视力者（BLVI）及明眼人的可用性，发现地图类表示（视觉和ITM）在地理问题任务中显著优于表格，而ITM可成为视觉地图的有效替代方案，挑战了当前使用无地理信息表格作为地图替代方案的无障碍实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字地图用于传达空间信息，但当前无障碍实践中常用缺乏地理信息的表格作为替代方案，这种做法是否合理尚缺乏实证验证。本研究旨在探究交互式文本地图（ITM）是否可作为视觉地图的有效替代品。

Method: 招募20名明眼人和20名盲人和低视力者（BLVI），让参与者分别使用视觉地图、交互式文本地图（ITM）和表格完成数值、地理及数值-地理混合问题，测量任务表现、偏好和NASA-TLX工作负荷。

Result: 两组参与者在地理类问题上，地图表示（视觉和ITM）显著优于表格；数值问题上的差异很小。明眼人在视觉地图和ITM之间的地理问题表现无显著差异。参与者偏好地图类表示。工作负荷最高为ITM，中间为视觉地图，最低为表格。

Conclusion: 符合WCAG标准的ITM能够提供空间信息访问，而表格不能；这挑战了当前推荐使用缺乏地理信息的表格作为地图替代的无障碍实践，并促使重新考虑豁免数字专题地图的无障碍立法。

Abstract: Digital maps are used to communicate generalized spatial information and relationships, yet are commonly made "accessible" using tables that lack geographic information. This study examines whether these tables and interactive text maps (ITMs) may be comparable to visual maps. Twenty sighted and 20 blind and low-vision individuals (BLVIs) performed tasks designed to compare visual maps, ITMs, and tables. Participants answered numeric, geographic, and combined numeric geographic questions using each representation, and performance, preference, and NASA-TLX were measured. Across both participant groups, map representations (visual and ITMs) significantly outperformed tables on geographic-based questions, while performance differences were minimal for numeric questions. For sighted participants, performance on geographic questions did not significantly differ between visual maps and ITMs, indicating that a larger powered study may find an "equivalent purpose" across these two conditions. Participants preferred map-based representations over tables. Perceived workload was highest for the ITM, intermediate for the visual map, and lowest for the table. Consistent with the Map Equivalent Purpose Framework, these findings indicate that Web Content Accessibility Guidelines-compliant ITMs can provide access to spatial information, unlike tables. These findings challenge prevailing accessibility practice that recommends tables lacking geographic information as map alternatives, and motivate reconsideration of accessibility legislation exempting digital thematic maps.

</details>


### [63] [SpatialPrompt: XR-Based Spatial Intent Expression as Executable Constraints for AI Generative 3D Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07894)
*Yichen Andy Yu,Wanru Li,Qiaoran Wang,Jymon Ross,Gavin Johnson,Mandy Lui,Qiao Jin*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: SpatialPrompt是一个XR系统，允许用户通过3D空间草图（使用3D笔绘制）和语音提示，将粗略的空间结构转化为可执行约束，用于可控的3D生成。系统支持迭代细化、同步协同创作（带颜色编码贡献标识），基于Apple Vision Pro、Logitech Muse和Meshy实现。启发式评估表明工作流直观且支持共享理解，但需要更快的生成速度和更清晰的反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在3D内容生成中，用户期望通过直观的空间交互（如草图）而非抽象参数来控制生成结果，现有方法缺乏将空间草图直接转化为可执行约束的能力，且难以支持协同创作中的共享理解。

Method: SpatialPrompt系统：用户使用3D笔在XR空间中绘制粗略结构，并辅以语音提示来描述语义和风格意图；系统将这些空间草图转化为可执行约束，驱动3D生成模型（基于Meshy）；支持迭代细化和同步协同创作，通过颜色编码区分不同用户的贡献。

Result: 启发式评估表明该工作流直观、能促进协同创作中的共享理解；同时暴露出需要更快的生成速度和更清晰的反馈机制。

Conclusion: SpatialPrompt提供了一种直观、基于空间草图的3D生成控制方式，在协同创作中展现了潜力，但生成速度和反馈清晰度有待提升。

Abstract: We present SpatialPrompt, an Extended Reality(XR) system that turns spatial sketches into executable constraints for controllable 3D generation. Users draw rough structures with a 3D pen and add voice prompts for semantic and stylistic intent. The system supports iterative refinement and synchronous co-creation in shared space with color-coded contributions. Implemented on Apple Vision Pro with Logitech Muse and Meshy, a heuristic evaluation suggests that the workflow is intuitive and supports shared understanding in collaborative creation, while revealing needs for faster generation and clearer feedback.

</details>


### [64] [Sycophantic AI makes human interaction feel more effortful and less satisfying over time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07912)
*Lujain Ibrahim,Franziska Sofia Hafner,Myra Cheng,Cinoo Lee,Rebecca Anselmetti,Robb Willer,Luc Rocher,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过五项预注册研究（N=3,075，12,766次人机对话），包括一项为期三周的美国人口普查代表性样本纵向实验，证明奉承型AI会改变用户处理亲密关系的方式。奉承型AI能提供用户通常从亲友处获得的情感支持，三周后用户向AI寻求建议的频率几乎与向亲友寻求建议相当，且用户对现实社交互动的满意度下降。多数用户偏好奉承型AI不是因为建议质量，而是因为它让用户感到最被理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究奉承型AI（sycophantic AI）如何影响用户的亲密人际关系，以及这种影响背后的心理机制。

Method: 五项预注册研究，包括12,766次人机对话实验和一项为期三周、使用美国人口普查代表性样本的纵向实验。

Result: 奉承型AI能立即提供情感支持；三周后用户向AI寻求建议的频率接近向亲友寻求建议；用户对现实社交互动的满意度下降；多数用户偏好奉承型AI是因为感到被理解而非建议质量。

Conclusion: 奉承型AI通过提供无摩擦的理解，可能悄然提高了用户评判人类关系的标准，构成了AI奉承行为的关系性解释。

Abstract: Millions of people now turn to artificial intelligence (AI) systems for personal advice, guidance, and support. Such systems can be sycophantic, frequently affirming users' views and beliefs. Across five preregistered studies (N = 3,075 participants, 12,766 human-AI conversations), including a three-week study with a census-representative U.S. sample, we provide longitudinal experimental evidence that sycophantic AI shifts how users approach their closest relationships. We show that sycophantic AI immediately delivers the emotional and esteem support users typically associate with close friends and family. Over three weeks of such interactions, users became nearly as likely to seek personal advice from sycophantic AI as from close friends and family, and reported lower satisfaction with their real-world social interactions. When given a choice among AI response styles, a majority preferred sycophantic AI -- not for the quality of its advice, but because it made them feel most understood. Together, these findings offer a relational account of AI sycophancy: by providing frictionless understanding, it may quietly raise the bar against which human relationships are judged.

</details>


### [65] [Exploring a Virtual Pet to Provide Context Notifications in a Tourism Recommender System: a Pilot Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07960)
*Patrícia Alves,Joana Neto,Ana Barreiro,Jorge Lima,Fausto Alves,Henish Balu,Luís Conceição,Goreti Marreiros*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用虚拟宠物作为社交中介的旅游推荐框架，通过多智能体微服务集成实时环境数据和用户个性特征生成个性化通知。11人的试点研究表明，宠物中介方式能有效软化系统通知的侵入感，提高通知清晰度和用户接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上下文感知旅游推荐系统在传递实时通知时存在侵入性强和用户疲劳的问题，需要一种更自然、可接受的交互方式来传递上下文感知的提醒信息。

Method: 设计了一个以虚拟宠物为社交中介的旅游推荐框架，集成实时环境数据（空气质量、噪音水平、天气预报）和邻近通知，通过多智能体微服务基于用户个性特征生成个性化推荐。采用组内设计（n=11），进行为期四周（每个版本两周）的真实场景用户研究，对比基线版本和宠物中介版本。

Result: 虚拟宠物能有效"软化"系统通知的侵入感，使安全关键信息感觉更受欢迎和自然；角色中介的合理化解释显著提高了通知的清晰度，有效支持用户的实时旅行决策。

Conclusion: 虚拟宠物伴侣可增强上下文感知通信在旅游推荐系统中的透明度和接受度，为设计更具社交性和自然交互的推荐系统提供了基础。

Abstract: While context-aware personalization has been widely explored in modern tourism Recommender Systems (RS), the delivery of real-time notifications remains a significant design challenge due to issues of intrusiveness and user fatigue. This paper presents a proof-of-concept for a tourism recommendation framework that utilizes a virtual pet as a social mediator for delivering context-aware alerts. The system integrates real-time environmental data - including air quality, noise levels, and weather forecasts - and proximity-based notifications with a Multi-Agent Microservice that generates personalized recommendations based on the user's personality traits and preferences. A within-subjects pilot study (n=11) was conducted to evaluate the feasibility and user acceptance of this pet-mediated approach. Participants interacted with two versions of the system - a baseline without contextual alerts and a version featuring pet-mediated notifications - over a four-week period (two weeks per version) in real-world scenarios. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected to assess engagement, perceived naturalness, notification utility, and acceptance. Preliminary results suggest that the virtual pet effectively can "soften" the perceived intrusiveness of system alerts, making safety-critical information feel more welcome and natural. Furthermore, the character-mediated justifications significantly improved the clarity of the notifications, effectively supporting users in their real-time travel decisions. These findings provide a foundation for using virtual pet companions to enhance the transparency and acceptance of context-aware communication in tourism RS.

</details>


### [66] [Hot Wire 5D+: Evaluating Cognitive and Motor Trade-offs of Visual Feedback for 5D Augmented Reality Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08008)
*Christian Masuhr,Julian Koch,Arne Wendt,Thorsten Schüppstuhl*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文通过30名被试的受控用户研究，评估了三种AR界面概念在5D轨迹（3D位置、2D方向、运动速度）引导中的表现，揭示了视觉反馈范式与方向约束对新手用户感知运动基线的影响，并提供了可操作的AR引导系统设计指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AR在复杂空间任务（如制造、无损检测、手术）中应用日益广泛，但使用旋转对称工具沿5D+轨迹执行任务时，未经训练用户的感觉运动基线以及不同视觉反馈范式引发的认知-运动权衡尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 进行了一项受试者内设计的用户研究（N=30），评估三种不同的AR用户界面概念，在有/无显式方向约束条件下进行轨迹引导。基于内部AR跟踪分析空间、方向和速度合规性，并通过高精度外部光学跟踪系统验证以排除硬件漂移。将执行过程分为瞬态和稳态阶段，应用对齐秩变换（ART）ANOVA分析视觉设计与任务复杂度之间的交互效应，同时收集主观指标（NASA-TLX、SUS）。

Result: 建立了新手用户进行自由手5D轨迹跟随的保守性能基线；揭示了方向约束引发的认知-运动权衡；识别了能够缓解该权衡的界面协同效应；提供了经验基线数据和可操作的AR引导系统设计指南。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了多维轨迹追踪任务中新手用户基线数据的空白，为开发有效的AR引导系统提供了实证依据和具体设计建议。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) is increasingly utilized to guide users through complex spatial tasks in domains such as manufacturing, non-destructive testing, and surgery. These applications often require strict compliance with 5D+ trajectories using rotation-symmetric tools (3D position, 2D orientation, and movement speed). However, the sensori-motor baselines of untrained users during these multidimensional tracing tasks, along with the cognitive-motor trade-offs induced by varying visual feedback paradigms, remain underexplored. We present a controlled within-subjects user study (N=30) evaluating three distinct AR UI concepts for trajectory guidance, both with and without explicit orientation constraints. We analyzed spatial, orientational, and speed compliance based on the internal AR tracking, which was validated against a high-precision external optical tracking system to rule out hardware drift. By segmenting the execution into transient and steady-state phases and applying Aligned Rank Transform (ART) ANOVA, we isolated the interaction effects between visual design and task complexity. Alongside subjective metrics (NASA-TLX, SUS), our results establish conservative performance baselines for novice users performing freehand 5D trajectory following. We reveal orientation-induced cognitive-motor trade-offs and identify mitigating UI synergies. Ultimately, we provide empirical baselines and actionable design guidelines for developing effective AR guidance systems.

</details>


### [67] [ECNUClaw: A Learner-Profiled Intelligent Study Companion Framework for K-12 Personalized Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08040)
*Yizhou Zhou,Jiayin Li,Zhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ECNUClaw是一个面向K-12教育的开源智能学伴框架，通过五维学习者画像（认知、行为、情感、元认知、情境）从对话中提取信号，动态调整同伴的引导强度、鼓励频率和Bloom认知层级支架。框架基于中国教育技术文献的三大理论构建，支持七种中文大模型提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有K-12教育智能学伴系统缺乏对学习者多维度特征的动态建模与自适应调节能力，亟需一个整合认知、行为、情感、元认知和情境维度的开源框架，以实现个性化的学习陪伴与引导。

Method: 构建五维学习者画像（认知、行为、情感、元认知、情境），通过学生-同伴对话逐轮提取信号；画像更新后输入自适应策略引擎，实时调整引导强度、鼓励频率和Bloom认知层级支架。框架基于张氏数字肖像三层框架、教育脑模型和人机协作IQ概念三个理论构建，采用统一OpenAI兼容适配层支持七种中文LLM。

Result: 实现了ECNUClaw开源框架，支持七种中文大模型提供商，代码已开源在GitHub上。论文描述了系统架构、画像构建机制和自适应调节机制，并讨论了局限性及下一步工作。

Conclusion: ECNUClaw为K-12教育提供了一个可扩展、可复用的开源智能学伴框架，通过多维度动态画像和自适应策略实现了个性化学习陪伴，但仍需在实际教育场景中进一步验证和优化。

Abstract: We introduce ECNUClaw, an open-source framework for building learner-profiled intelligent study companions in K-12 education. The system constructs and maintains a five-dimension learner profile -- covering cognitive, behavioral, emotional, metacognitive, and contextual dimensions -- by extracting signals from student-companion dialogues at each turn. Profile updates feed directly into an adaptive strategy engine that adjusts the companion's guidance intensity, encouragement frequency, and Bloom's taxonomy scaffolding in real time. The framework design draws on three theoretical strands from the Chinese educational technology literature: Zhang's Digital Portrait Three-Layer Framework for learner assessment, the Education Brain model for educational system architecture, and the Human-AI Collaborative IQ concept for companion design philosophy. ECNUClaw is implemented in Python and supports seven Chinese LLM providers through a unified OpenAI-compatible adapter layer. We describe the system architecture, the profiling and adaptation mechanisms, and discuss limitations and next steps. The source code is available at https://github.com/bushushu2333/ECNUClaw.

</details>

# cs.PL [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [Bounding Fixed Points of Non-Monotone Processes: Theory to Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06803)
*Abdullah H. Rasheed,Vijay K. Garg*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文针对非单调算子的不动点近似问题，在近似不动点理论（AFT）的基础上，通过引入抽象解释保证近似算子的可靠（sound）性，并利用受控的不完全性设计了一种划分与收紧AFT最优近似的实用算法。该算法具有可靠性、任意时刻可用性，在有限高度格上保证终止，并具有多项式时间复杂度的变体。在回答集编程（加速收敛）和推测性程序分析（减少回滚）中验证了实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代求解器与程序分析器依赖非单调推理（如否定即失败、推测更新、回溯），但经典单调不动点方法不适用。现有AFT虽能逼近非单调算子的不动点，但实际中即使最优近似也往往粗糙且计算昂贵，亟需提高其实用性。

Method: (1) 证明抽象解释用于近似算子的可靠性（soundness）；(2) 引入受控的不完全性，设计一种划分并收紧AFT最优近似的实用算法，该算法具有可靠性、任意时刻可用性，在有限高度格上保证终止；(3) 提出多项式时间复杂度的改进变体。

Result: 在回答集编程中作为收敛加速预处理器发挥作用；在推测性程序分析中减少回滚同时保持可靠性。实现层面的细节验证了方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 通过理论上的可靠性证明和算法设计，将AFT近似方法推进到可实际应用于编程语言场景的阶段，兼顾了效率与近似质量。

Abstract: Many modern solvers and program analyzers rely on non-monotone reasoning (e.g. negation-as-failure, speculative updates, backtracking) for which classical monotone fixed-point methods do not apply. The general problem of finding the fixed points of these processes is a difficult one. For this reason, there have been theoretical efforts in existing Approximation Fixpoint Theory (AFT) from the domain of logic programming to approximate fixed points of non-monotone operators. Tight approximations of these fixed points are highly useful for accelerating non-monotonic computations by restricting the search space. In practice, however, even the best approximations obtained through AFT can be coarse and computationally expensive. We aim to address both issues to make AFT approximation methods practical for use in programming languages (PL) settings. To mitigate inefficiency, we prove the soundness of an abstract interpretation for approximating operators. To improve upon coarse approximations, we carefully introduce controlled unsoundness to design an effective yet practical algorithm for partitioning and tightening AFT's best approximations. This algorithm is sound, anytime, and guarantees termination on finite-height lattices. We further present a modification that ensures polynomial-time complexity. We instantiate these methods in two settings: (1) answer set programming, where it serves as a convergence-accelerating pre-processor, and (2) speculative program analysis, where it reduces rollback while preserving soundness. In both settings, we focus on implementation-level details to demonstrate the practical applicability of our methods.

</details>


### [69] [A New Interaction Concept for Interactive and Autoactive Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06972)
*Wolfram Pfeifer,Mattias Ulbrich,Daniel Drodt*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的程序验证交互概念，允许用户在源代码和规约层面检查和交互证明状态，并基于KeY验证引擎实现原型，用户研究表明该原型有助于理解证明状态和发现缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序验证工具要么完全自动化（无法检查证明状态或在失败时干预），要么交互层次低于用户提供的规约，存在用户理解上的心理鸿沟。

Method: 提出一种新的交互概念，使用户能在源代码和规约层面检查并干预证明状态；作为KeY验证引擎的插件实现该概念。

Result: 通过用户研究表明该原型有助于用户理解证明状态并发现源代码或规约中的缺陷。

Conclusion: 所提出的交互概念最小化了用户心理表征与证明状态表示之间的差距，提升了程序验证的可理解性和可用性。

Abstract: Fully functional program verification is an undecidable$\unicode{x2014}$and, hence, inherently difficult$\unicode{x2014}$task, that is not automatically solvable but typically requires user interaction and guidance. Existing verifiers either work autoactively, requiring the user to write annotations in source code, without the possibility to inspect the proof state or intervene in case of an unsuccessful attempt, or allow interactions on a logical encoding that is on a lower level than the user-provided specifications. We present a novel interaction concept which allows the user to inspect and interact with the proof state on source code and specification level. This minimizes the mental gap between the representations. We provide an implementation of the concept as a plugin for the Java verification engine KeY, and show with a user study that this prototype can be beneficial for users to understand the proof state and find defects in source code or specifications.

</details>

### [70] [Computing Short SAT Implicants via Ising/QUBO Encodings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07017)
*Giuseppe Spallitta,Leonardo Duenas-Osorio,Moshe Y. Vardi*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将"无关项"语义引入Ising/QUBO模型的框架，通过双极性表示实现短蕴含项（短部分满足赋值）的检索，在保持可满足性的前提下平均约三分之一变量未被赋值，并通过极性冻结轮次以高概率实现最小性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAT-to-Ising/QUBO公式化设计中，基态对应完全赋值，而Ising/QUBO模型没有原生表示未赋值变量的能力，导致无法直接获取短部分满足赋值（蕴含项），尤其在需要聚焦重要变量集的推理任务中存在局限。

Method: 提出一种双极性表示方法，将"无关项"语义融入二次模型；通过微小幅值修改实现蕴含项收缩和投影；给出确保基态对应短部分满足赋值的参数区间；在二次惩罚函数允许时实现最小基数。

Result: 在随机3-SAT枚举基准和非CNF公式上使用模拟退火进行实验，结果表明在随机3-SAT公式上约三分之一变量未被赋值同时保持可满足性，连续极性冻结轮次能以高概率实现最小性和最小基数。

Conclusion: 该框架成功将无关项语义引入Ising/QUBO模型，使得在不改变可满足性的前提下获得短蕴含项成为可能，并通过实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Many reasoning tasks require short partial satisfying assignments (implicants), sometimes focusing on a set of important variables. SAT-to-Ising-QUBO formulations are implicitly designed so that ground states correspond to total assignments, since the Ising/QUBO model assigns a value to every spin and has no native representation of unassigned variables. We introduce an Ising/QUBO framework that incorporates "don't-care" semantics into the quadratic model via a dual-polarity representation, enabling the retrieval of short implicants. The encoding supports implicant shrinking and projection through minor objective modifications. We provide parameter regimes under which ground states correspond to short partial satisfying assignments, achieving minimality and, when the quadratic penalty function permits, minimum-cardinality. We empirically evaluate the encoding with simulated annealing on random 3-SAT enumeration benchmarks and non-CNF formulas, showing that it leaves about one-third of variables unassigned on random 3-SAT formulas while preserving satisfiability, and that consecutive polarity-freezing rounds achieve minimality (and minimum-cardinality) with high probability.

</details>


### [71] [Evidence-Tracked Tape Semantics for Probabilistic Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07259)
*Liron Cohen,Tomer Samara*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于证据追踪的磁带语义学，通过单子-核心-证据-框架管线构建高阶逻辑，用于形式化概率计算的内涵与外延层次，并建立了概率推理的可靠不等式与几乎必然商结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准内涵概率计算模型将随机程序视为消耗显式随机磁带的确定性计算，形成内涵层（重用随机性与相关性可见）与外延层（在选定概率测度下解释磁带）两层结构。现有方法缺乏对证据追踪和证明相关性的统一逻辑框架。

Method: 采用单子-核心-证据-框架管线及其引发的可实现性三元组，构建证据追踪的磁带语义学，得到高阶逻辑；通过固定磁带测度提取概率与期望数值摘要，引入几乎必然商结构支持概率1推理，并研究内涵原则（如磁带重写映射的证明相关传输、流磁带的规范拆分规则）。

Result: 建立了证据追踪磁带语义学的高阶逻辑，实现内涵与外延层次的统一；推演出的概率与期望不等式可靠；几乎必然商结构支持概率1推理；分析了内涵原则在外延法则层面的丢失；通过推前映射将磁带推理与内涵法则语义学关联，隔离出概率1 must抽象作为磁带证明的可靠摘要。

Conclusion: 该框架为概率计算的内涵与外延层次提供了统一的证明相关的逻辑基础，支持形式化概率推理，并为未来研究概率编程语言的语义与验证提供了新方向。

Abstract: A standard intensional account of probabilistic computation represents a randomized program as a deterministic computation that consumes an explicit random tape. This yields a two-layer perspective: an intensional layer that makes reuse of randomness and correlation visible, and an extensional layer obtained by interpreting tapes under a chosen probability measure.
  We develop an evidence-tracked tape semantics using the monadic-core-to-evidenced-frame pipeline (and its induced realizability tripos), obtaining a higher-order logic in which entailments are witnessed by uniform evidence transformers. Quantitative statements are recovered by interpretation: once a tape measure is fixed, probabilities and expectations arise by extracting numerical summaries from tape-indexed predicates, and entailments yield sound inequalities, with an almost-sure quotient supporting probability-one reasoning. We also study intensional principles that are lost at the level of laws, including proof-relevant transport along realizable tape-rewiring maps and a canonical splitting discipline for stream tapes enforcing independent draws. Finally, we relate tape-based reasoning to an extensional law semantics via pushforward, isolating a probability-one must abstraction as a sound summary of tape-based proofs.

</details>


### [72] [Finitary Truly Concurrent Bisimulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07373)
*Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文针对基于预互模拟前序的过程语言的完全抽象指称语义模型，提出了真并发预互模拟及其有限性定义，解决了标准指称解释中有限观察与无限观察之间的语义区分问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了构建过程语言的完全抽象指称语义模型，需要解决预互模拟前序中两个核心问题：(1)标准指称解释下两个过程若具有相同有限观察则相关；(2)预互模拟基于无限观察可区分过程行为。因此需要有限性预互模拟来获得完全抽象结果。

Method: 遵循已有的有限性互模拟的两种主要结果（逻辑形式和行形式），采用行为形式的方法，给出真并发预互模拟及其有限性定义。

Result: 给出了真并发预互模拟及其有限性定义。

Conclusion: 为构建基于预互模拟前序的过程语言的完全抽象指称语义模型提供了理论基础。

Abstract: To develop a full abstract denotational model of a process language based on prebisimulation preorder, its behavioural semantics has two problems: (1) Two processes related by a standard denotational interpretation afford the same finite observations. (2) Prebisimulation can make distinctions between the behaviours of two processes based on infinite observations. So, finitary part of prebisimulation is needed to obtain full abstract results. There existed two main results on finitary bisimulation: the logical form and the behavioural form. Following the latter one, we give the definitions of truly concurrent prebisimulations and their finitary ones.

</details>


### [73] [Cross-Attention and Encoder-Decoder Transformers: A Logical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07705)
*Veeti Ahvonen,Damian Heiman,Antti Kuusisto,Miguel Moreno,Matias Selin*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文对编码器-解码器Transformer（LLM的基础架构）给出了新颖的逻辑刻画，通过引入新型时序逻辑（包含计数全局模态词和过去模态词）在浮点数与软注意力实际设置下对其进行表征，并辅以分布式自动机刻画，讨论了架构变体及自回归设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码器-解码器Transformer是LLM及众多受益于交叉注意力的场景的基础架构，但缺乏对其计算能力的精确逻辑刻画，尤其是在实际浮点数和软注意力设置下。

Method: 1. 提出一种新型时序逻辑，在命题逻辑基础上扩展了针对编码器输入的计数全局模态词和针对解码器输入的过去模态词；2. 给出分布式自动机的等价刻画；3. 分析架构变体（如掩码机制）下结果的鲁棒性；4. 讨论自回归设置下的适用性。

Result: 成功建立了编码器-解码器Transformer在浮点数与软注意力实际设置下的逻辑表征，证明了所提时序逻辑与Transformer的计算能力等价，且该刻画对架构变体具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 编码器-解码器Transformer的计算能力可用特定的时序逻辑精确描述，且这种刻画方式不依赖于具体的架构细节（如掩码方式），在自回归设置下仍然成立。

Abstract: We give a novel logical characterization of encoder-decoder transformers, the foundational architecture for LLMs that also sees use in various settings that benefit from cross-attention. We study such transformers over text in the practical setting of floating-point numbers and soft-attention, characterizing them with a new temporal logic. This logic extends propositional logic with a counting global modality over the encoder input and a past modality over the decoder input. We also give an additional characterization of such transformers via a type of distributed automata, and show that our results are not limited to the specific choices in the architecture and can account for changes in, e.g., masking. Finally, we discuss encoder-decoder transformers in the autoregressive setting.

</details>


### [74] [A Fibrational Perspective on Differential Linear Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07858)
*Jad Koleilat*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文通过Grothendieck纤维化和切函子构建了微分线性逻辑（DiLL）的范畴模型，为统一DiLL与依赖类型迈出了第一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiLL作为一种通过线性和非线性公式之间的对称性来表达微分的 sequent 演算，缺乏统一的范畴模型，且需要向依赖类型扩展。

Method: 采用类型论范畴语义的方法，将线性-非线性伴随适配到Grothendieck纤维化框架中，并引入切函子（tangent functor）来刻画微分结构。

Result: 将DiLL的范畴模型表示为配备切函子的一对Grothendieck纤维化。

Conclusion: 该工作为未来统一DiLL与依赖类型系统提供了范畴理论基础。

Abstract: Differential Linear Logic (DiLL) is a sequent calculus that expresses differentiation via symmetries between linear and non-linear formulas. In this paper, we express categorical models of DiLL as a pair of Grothendieck fibrations equipped with a tangent functor. To do so, we adapt methods from categorical semantics of type theory to linear-non-linear adjunctions. This is a first step towards unifying DILL and dependent types.

</details>
