### [75] [EMRGF: A Practitioner Framework for Governance-Driven Enterprise Technology Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06703)
*Harveen Punihani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出企业技术现代化失败的主因不是工程能力不足，而是治理缺失。作者基于24年实践经验构建了EMRGF治理运营模型，包含四个模块和五项实施工具，实证显示可降低30%开发工作量、35%测试周期，实现零中断迁移和99.9%数据可靠性，并符合美国NIST CSF 2.0等国家政策要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业技术现代化项目以高昂代价频繁失败，但主流解释（工程能力不足）是错误的。现有ITIL、COBIT、TOGAF等框架关注相邻问题但缺乏一个集成的、可移植的机构化运营模型来管控现代化过程中的规划、执行、验证和交接。

Method: 基于24年跨金融服务、工业制造和零售企业的实践经验，开发了EMRGF治理运营模型，包含四大模块：云与遗留系统现代化治理、数据平台可靠性与证据完整性、AI自动化治理、关键任务可靠性与根因分析规程；通过五项实施工具和培训培训师机制实现机构化落地。

Result: 在大规模实证应用中，实现开发工作量降低30%、测试周期减少35%、高数据量环境零中断迁移、关键分析管道99.9%数据可靠性。

Conclusion: EMRGF框架与NIST CSF 2.0、NIST AI RMF、行政令14028和14110等美国国家政策明确对齐，设计为无需持续外部依赖即可实现机构化采用，为技术现代化治理提供了可重复、结构化的运营模型。

Abstract: Enterprise technology modernization programs fail at a documented and costly rate, yet the dominant explanation -- inadequate engineering capability -- is incorrect. The primary failure mode is a governance deficit: the absence of structured, repeatable operating routines for how organizations plan, execute, validate, and hand off complex technology change. Existing frameworks -- ITIL, COBIT, TOGAF, scaled agile methodologies, and cloud provider well-architected frameworks -- address adjacent concerns but do not provide an integrated, portable institutional operating model for controlled modernization across migrations, data platforms, and AI-enabled automation. This article presents the Enterprise Modernization Reliability and Governance Framework (EMRGF), a practitioner-developed governance operating model derived from 24 years of applied delivery experience across financial services, industrial manufacturing, and retail enterprises. EMRGF comprises four interlocking modules -- Cloud and Legacy Modernization Governance, Data Platform Reliability and Evidence Integrity, AI-Enabled Automation Governance, and Mission-Critical Reliability and Root-Cause Routines -- operationalized through five implementation tools and a training-of-trainers institutionalization model. Empirical application at scale has produced a 30% reduction in development effort, a 35% reduction in testing cycles, zero-disruption migrations across high-volume data estates, and 99.9% data reliability in mission-critical analytics pipelines. The framework is explicitly aligned with U.S. national policy mandates including NIST CSF 2.0, NIST AI RMF, and Executive Orders 14028 and 14110, and is designed for institutional adoption without ongoing external dependency.

</details>


### [76] [The Single-File Test: A Longitudinal Public-Interface Evaluation of First-Output LLM Web Generation with Social Reach Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06707)
*Diego Cabezas Palacios*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对2025年12月至2026年2月期间17个公开实验中68个单文件HTML生成进行了八周观察性比较，评估了GPT、Gemini、Grok和Claude四个推理模型家族。Claude在人工加权评分中表现最强且最稳定，赢得9/17提示；更长推理时间与更高质量无关；Gemini作为评判者比人类更宽容且存在自我偏好偏差；X平台印象预测模型效果差（MAE=46,874, R²=-0.377），而代码冗长度模型表现更好（MAE=135.2, R²=0.576）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无自定义指令、无人格调谐、无修复提示的固定公共接口协议下，系统比较不同推理模型家族（GPT、Gemini、Grok、Claude）生成单文件HTML代码的质量差异，并探索模型输出在社交媒体传播效果和代码冗长度上的可预测性。

Method: 采用八周观察性比较设计，收集17个公开实验的68个HTML生成样本。使用固定公共接口协议（无自定义指令、无人格调谐、无修复提示），通过渲染浏览器视频进行人类评分和Gemini LLM作为评判者进行提示遵循度、功能正确性和UI质量评估。采用标准化社交媒体协议（X/Twitter、TikTok、YouTube）发布。使用实验级模型预测24小时X平台印象数，使用生成级模型预测HTML冗长度。

Result: Claude在人工加权评分中均性能最强且一致性最高，赢得9/17提示。更长推理时间与更高质量无正相关。Gemini作为评判者在功能正确性和整体性能上显著比人类评估者更宽容，且存在未解决的自偏好偏差。X印象预测模型表现弱（MAE=46,874, R²=-0.377）；HTML行数预测模型表现更好，仅使用模型家族特征的基线模型优于考虑提示信息的替代模型（MAE=135.2, R²=0.576）。

Conclusion: 技术和音频变量不足以预测X平台24小时覆盖范围，代码冗长度主要由模型家族而非提示措辞驱动。研究为观察性比较，受限于公共接口漂移、访问路径差异和单一人类评分者。

Abstract: This paper presents an eight-week observational comparison of 68 single-file HTML generations collected across 17 public experiments in the "HTML AI Battle" project between December 10, 2025 and February 4, 2026. Four reasoning model families, GPT, Gemini, Grok, and Claude, were compared under a fixed public-interface protocol with no custom instructions, no personality tuning, and no repair prompts. Each output was evaluated from a rendered browser video using human scores and a Gemini LLM-as-a-judge layer for prompt adherence, functional correctness, and UI quality, then packaged into a standardized social-media protocol spanning X (Twitter), TikTok, and YouTube. The tracker was also used for two supervised predictive analyses: an experiment-level model for 24-hour X impressions and a generation-level model for HTML verbosity.
  Under this protocol, Claude was the strongest and most consistent family, leading mean performance and winning 9/17 prompts under the primary human weighted score. Longer measured reasoning time was not associated with higher quality overall. Gemini as a judge was significantly more lenient than the human evaluator on functional correctness and overall performance, while stable self-favoring bias remained unresolved. The exploratory X-impressions model remained weak under post-screen cross-validation (MAE = 46,874, R^2 = -0.377), whereas the HTML-lines model performed better, with a model-family-only baseline outperforming prompt-aware alternatives (MAE = 135.2, R^2 = 0.576). Overall, selected pre-publication technical/audio variables were not sufficient to predict 24-hour X reach, while code verbosity was driven much more by model family than by prompt wording. The comparisons remain observational and are limited by public-interface drift, access-path differences, and one primary human scorer.

</details>


### [77] [Agentic Coding Needs Proactivity, Not Just Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06717)
*Nghi D. Q. Bui,Georgios Evangelopoulos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出编码智能体应按照主动性的三个层次（反应型、计划型、情境感知型）进行划分，并引入洞察策略（insight policy）作为评估其有用性的核心，同时提出IDQ、CGS、Learning Lift三项评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前编码智能体正在从内联补全向自主系统演进，但学术界对"主动性"的含义、与"自主性"的区别、评估标准等缺乏清晰界定，亟需建立统一的分类与评价框架。

Method: 基于混合主动交互原则，提出主动性三层分类法（Reactive/Scheduled/Situation Aware），对比现有编码智能体与五项实践标准，并设计主动用户模拟协议及三项评价目标。

Result: 给出了洞察策略（insight policy）作为核心评估对象，以及Insight Decision Quality (IDQ)、Context Grounding Score (CGS)、Learning Lift三项具体指标，用于衡量无请求的智能体行为是否有效而非仅仅是活跃。

Conclusion: 对主动性编码智能体的评估应聚焦于其洞察策略的质量，而非简单的活跃度；提出的分类法、标准和指标为未来构建与评估长期主动性编码智能体提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Coding agents are rapidly changing the landscape of software development, moving from inline completion to autonomous systems that edit repositories, open pull requests, respond to issues, and run scheduled or webhook triggered routines across the development life cycle. The next generation is increasingly described as proactive and long-horizon: agents should notice relevant changes before the developer asks, connect signals across tools, decide when to interrupt, and carry preferences across sessions. Yet the field still lacks a clear account of what proactivity means for software development, how it differs from autonomy, what acceptance criteria proactive long-horizon tasks should satisfy, and which metrics determine whether unsolicited agent behavior is useful rather than merely active. Proactive coding agents should be evaluated by the quality and improvement of their insight policy: the policy that decides what matters next, what evidence supports it, whether to show it, and how to adapt after feedback. This view is grounded in the principles of mixed initiative interaction. We propose a three level taxonomy of proactivity (Reactive, Scheduled, and Situation Aware), compare contemporary coding agents against five practical criteria, and sketch an active user simulation protocol with three evaluation targets: Insight Decision Quality (IDQ), Context Grounding Score (CGS), and Learning Lift

</details>


### [78] [A Self-Healing Framework for Reliable LLM-Based Autonomous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06737)
*Cheonsu Jeong,Younggun Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种面向LLM智能体的可靠性感知自愈框架，通过故障检测、可靠性评估与自适应恢复机制，显著提升任务成功率与系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自主智能体在复杂软件系统中可靠性不足，存在幻觉、执行错误、推理不一致等不可预测故障，亟需系统化的可靠性保障机制。

Method: 定义故障类型分类体系与定量可靠性评估模型；提出基于执行模式与输出一致性的故障检测方法；设计包含自适应重规划和纠正提示策略的自愈机制。

Result: 在多智能体工作流环境中的真实任务场景下，任务成功率显著提升，故障传播减少，系统鲁棒性优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了融合智能体内部推理过程与外部执行结果的集成监控系统，有助于保障自主系统稳定性并降低LLM在生产环境中的部署门槛。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being utilized in complex software systems. However, reliability remains a significant challenge due to unpredictable failures such as hallucinations, execution errors, and inconsistent reasoning. This paper proposes a reliability-aware self-healing framework for LLM-based software agents. The framework integrates failure detection, reliability assessment, and automated recovery mechanisms. First, we define a taxonomy of failure types and introduce a quantitative reliability assessment model. Next, we propose a failure detection method that identifies abnormal agent behavior based on execution patterns and output consistency. Finally, we design a self-healing mechanism that dynamically recovers from failures through adaptive replanning and corrective prompting strategies. The proposed framework was implemented in a multi-agent workflow environment and evaluated using real-world task scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach significantly increases task success rates, reduces failure propagation, and enhances overall system robustness compared to existing methods. In particular, this study distinguishes itself by establishing an integrated monitoring system that combines the agent's internal reasoning process with external execution results. These findings are expected to contribute to securing the stability of advanced autonomous systems and lowering the barriers to LLM adoption in production environments.

</details>


### [79] [ScarfBench: A Benchmark for Cross-Framework Application Migration in Enterprise Java](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06754)
*Advait Pavuluri,Bridget McGinn,Ashita Saxena,George Safta,Srikanth Tamilselvam,Raju Pavuluri,Michele Merler,Baishakhi Ray,Rahul Krishna*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ScarfBench 是一个针对企业级 Java 应用跨框架行为保持重构的基准测试，包含 34 个应用、102 个变体和 204 个定向重构任务。最强编码智能体仅达到 15.3% 的测试通过率（单层迁移）和 12.2%（整体应用），204 个任务中仅 1 个完全行为等价。Spring↔Quarkus 最易处理，Jakarta EE 目标迁移最难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件工程基准主要涵盖缺陷修复、功能实现和语言/版本现代化，但缺少对跨框架重构的系统性评测，而跨框架迁移是企业 Java 应用中常见且困难的行为保持重构任务。

Method: 构建来自专家编写的 Spring、Jakarta EE 和 Quarkus 三框架实现三元组：34 个应用（29 个单层聚焦 + 5 个完整应用）生成 102 个变体（约 15.1 万行代码、1946 个源文件和测试文件）和 204 个有向重构任务。每个任务提供给智能体一个可运行的源应用和目标框架，智能体需生成行为保持的目标实现。正确性通过可执行 oracle（编译、容器化部署、行为测试）评估。

Result: 五个最先进编码智能体中，最强仅达 15.3%（单层）和 12.2%（整体）聚合测试通过率，204 个任务仅 1 个完全行为等价。难度在框架方向和架构层上不对称：Spring↔Quarkus 最易处理，Jakarta EE 目标迁移最难。

Conclusion: 跨框架重构对当前编码智能体极具挑战性，该基准填补了现有评测空白，并提供了系统性的失败分类学和公开数据集以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Java remains central to enterprise software, and many applications outlive their original architecture. Migrating them across frameworks is a behavior-preserving refactoring spanning build configuration, dependency injection, persistence, request handling, and deployment. Existing software-engineering benchmarks cover bug fixing, feature implementation, and language or version modernization, but leave cross-framework refactoring largely unmeasured.
  We introduce ScarfBench, a benchmark for behavior-preserving cross-framework refactoring of enterprise Java applications. It is built from expert-written implementation triples across Spring, Jakarta EE, and Quarkus: 34 applications (29 focused single-layer, 5 whole) yielding 102 variants (~151K lines across 1946 source and test files) and 204 directed refactoring tasks. Each task gives an agent a working source application and a target framework; the agent must synthesize a target implementation preserving the source behavior. Correctness is evaluated by an application-specific executable oracle: the candidate must compile, deploy in a containerized target runtime, and pass behavioral tests over the application's observable interface.
  We evaluate five state-of-the-art coding agents on ScarfBench. The strongest achieves only 15.3% aggregate test pass on focused-layer migrations and 12.2% on whole applications, and only one of the 204 tasks yields a fully behaviorally equivalent target. Difficulty is asymmetric across framework directions and architectural layers: Spring<->Quarkus is the most tractable pair, and Jakarta-targeted migrations are hardest. From LLM-as-a-judge and expert adjudication of failed-task traces, we derive a taxonomy of recurring failure categories spanning build, deploy, and test stages. We release the benchmark, harness, and agent traces at https://scarfbench.info.

</details>


### [80] [Analyzing the Adoption of Database Management Systems Throughout the History of Open Source Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06817)
*Camila A. Paiva,Raquel Maximino,Frederico Paiva,Rafael Accetta Vieira,Nicole Espanha,João Felipe Pimentel,Igor Wiese,Marco Aurélio Gerosa,Igor Steinmacher,Leonardo Murta,Vanessa Braganholo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过对GitHub上362个流行的开源Java项目的源代码分析，揭示了DBMS的采用、共存与替换模式，发现MySQL和PostgreSQL最为流行，Redis和MongoDB在非关系型系统中使用稳定且持久，而HyperSQL更易被替换；项目常同时使用多个DBMS实现多语言持久化，且ORM框架广泛用于应用与数据库之间的交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究关注DBMS与应用代码的共同演化，但关于DBMS在实际系统中的采用、共存使用和替换模式的实证研究仍较为缺乏。

Method: 对GitHub上362个流行的开源Java项目进行历史研究，基于DB-Engines排名选取主流关系型和非关系型DBMS，通过源代码启发式分析方法分析DBMS的流行度、稳定性、迁移模式、共现情况以及ORM的作用。

Result: MySQL和PostgreSQL是最流行的DBMS；Redis和MongoDB在非关系型系统中使用频繁且稳定；HyperSQL更易被替换；多个DBMS频繁共现，呈现多语言持久化模式；ORM框架广泛用于应用与DBMS之间的交互。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了关于DBMS如何随时间被采用、组合和替换的实证证据，为开发者、架构师、教育者和DBMS供应商提供了指导。

Abstract: Database Management Systems (DBMSs) are widely used to store, retrieve, and manage the data handled by modern applications. Although prior work has studied the co-evolution of DBMSs and application source code, less is known about DBMS adoption, co-use, and replacement in real systems. This paper presents a historical study of DBMS usage in 362 popular open-source Java projects hosted on GitHub. We investigated the adoption of the top DBMSs ranked by DB-Engines, covering relational and non-relational systems. Using source-code heuristics, we analyzed DBMS popularity, stability, migration patterns, co-occurrence, and the role of Object-Relational Mappers (ORMs). Our findings show that MySQL and PostgreSQL are the most popular DBMSs in our corpus. Among non-relational DBMSs, Redis and MongoDB are the most frequently used and tend to remain stable after adoption. In contrast, systems such as HyperSQL are more often replaced as projects evolve. We also observed frequent co-use of multiple DBMSs, suggesting patterns of polyglot persistence in which projects combine systems to handle different data needs. Finally, we found that ORM frameworks are commonly used to mediate interactions between applications and DBMSs. Overall, our study provides empirical evidence on how DBMSs are adopted, combined, and replaced over time, offering guidance for developers, architects, educators, and DBMS vendors.

</details>


### [81] [Guidelines for Cultivating a Sense of Belonging to Reduce Developer Burnout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06827)
*Bianca Trinkenreich,Marco Aurelio Gerosa,Anita Sarma,Igor Steinmacher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文综述了软件开发者归属感与职业倦怠之间的关系，归纳了归属感的特征及影响因素，并为软件组织和开源社区提出了培养归属感、减少倦怠的八项实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业倦怠严重影响软件开发者的身心健康，且与高离职率相关。先前研究已表明归属感缺失会加剧倦怠，而归属感有助于提升韧性和工作满意度。本文旨在基于现有研究成果，为软件行业提供可操作的、基于证据的归属感培养指南。

Method: 回顾并综合近期关于软件团队（包括专有软件组织和开源社区）中归属感的研究，提炼归属感的特征（如信任、接纳、价值认可、友谊等）及相关因素（如认可、心理安全、内在动机、英语自信、任职时长、性别、文化权力距离等），进而推导出实践指南。

Result: 识别出信任、接纳、价值认可、友谊、成员身份、相互支持、被他人了解等归属感特征，以及认可、心理安全、内在动机、英语自信、任职时长、性别、文化权力距离等关联因素。提出了八项实践指南。

Conclusion: 本文提出的指南可帮助软件组织和开源社区营造更健康、更具包容性的环境，从而支持开发者的身心健康。

Abstract: Burnout affects software developers' mental and physical well-being and contributes to turnover, generating strong concerns in the software industry. Prior research has shown that lack of belonging is associated with higher levels of burnout among software developers, while a sense of belonging is linked to resilience, job satisfaction, engagement, and well-being. In this paper, we revisit recent studies on belongingness in software development teams, including proprietary software organizations and open-source software communities, to offer evidence-based guidelines for cultivating belongingness and reducing developer burnout. We summarize characteristics of belongingness, such as trust, acceptance, value recognition, friendship, membership, mutual support, and being known by others, as well as factors associated with belongingness, including recognition, psychological safety, intrinsic motivation, English confidence, tenure, gender, and cultural power distance. Based on these findings, we propose practical guidelines for leaders and communities, including timely and consistent recognition, transparent promotion rules, inclusive benefits and initiatives, intentional connections through collaborative tools, blameless postmortems, optional in-person opportunities, informal newcomer gatherings, and continuous monitoring of belongingness and burnout. These guidelines can help software organizations and open-source communities foster healthier, more inclusive environments that support developer well-being.

</details>


### [82] [From Assistance to Agency: Rethinking Autonomy and Control in CI/CD Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07062)
*Marcus Emmanuel Barnes,Taher A. Ghaleb,Safwat Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能体CI/CD的概念框架，核心挑战是权限转移而非任务性能，区分了数据面权限（局部操作）和控制面权限（配置修改），指出现有系统主要在数据面运行且受边界自主性限制，并提出未来研究方向包括控制面安全与治理机制、自主性边界形式化、评估框架和人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究社区缺乏统一的术语来描述CI/CD的"智能体化"程度、决策权限的委托范围以及控制权应如何分配，亟需一个共享概念框架来指导相关研究与实践。

Method: 通过引入数据面权限与控制面权限的区分，结合研究原型和工业平台分析，识别出三种重复出现的模式：受限自主性为主导设计、外部治理为主要安全机制、部署推进与评估方法之间的差距不断扩大。

Result: 发现当前系统主要在数据面运行且受边界自主性限制，安全性通过周围治理基础设施实现而非智能体自身保障；识别出三种模式并指出现有评估方法落后于部署实践。

Conclusion: 控制面安全与治理机制是最紧迫的未解决问题，其次是自主性边界形式化、评估框架和人机协调，这些构成未来研究议程的核心方向。

Abstract: AI agents are assuming active roles in Continuous Integration and Continuous Deployment (CI/CD) workflows, yet the research community lacks a shared vocabulary for describing what it means for CI/CD to be agentic, how much decision authority is delegated, and where control should reside. This paper presents a vision of agentic CI/CD in which the central challenge is not improving task performance but designing authority transfer, defined as the delegation of operational decisions from human-controlled pipelines to agent systems under specified constraints and recourse mechanisms.
  To structure this argument, we introduce a distinction between data-plane authority (localized interventions such as patch generation and test reruns) and control-plane authority (modifications to pipeline configuration, deployment policies, and approval gates). Drawing on research prototypes and industrial platforms, we show that current systems operate mainly at the data plane under bounded autonomy, with safety achieved through surrounding governance infrastructure rather than intrinsic agent guarantees. We identify three recurring patterns: constrained autonomy as the dominant design, external governance as the primary safety mechanism, and a widening gap between deployment momentum and evaluation methodology. We propose a research agenda in which control-plane safety and governance mechanisms represent the most urgent open problem, followed by formalization of autonomy boundaries, evaluation frameworks, and human--agent coordination.

</details>


### [83] [RepoZero: Can LLMs Generate a Code Repository from Scratch?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07122)
*Zhaoxi Zhang,Yiming Xu,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoZero 是一个全新的基准测试，通过将仓库级代码生成重新定义为“仓库复现”（给定 API 规范，智能体必须重新实现整个仓库且行为与原始实现匹配），实现了全自动、可执行的验证，避免了人工/LLM 评判的偏差。基于此基准，作者提出 ACE 框架（迭代测试生成与错误驱动的细化解耦），在多种先进 LLM 和智能体框架上的实验显示，最强模型仅能达到 30%-55% 的通过率，揭示了当前能力与现实需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准要么聚焦于补丁级编辑，要么依赖人类或 LLM 进行评判，存在偏差且难以复现。缺乏一个可验证、可扩展的端到端仓库级代码生成评估方法。

Method: 1) 提出 RepoZero 基准：将生成任务重新定义为“仓库复现”，通过黑盒输出等价性进行严格验证；引入跨语言约束和沙箱评估协议防止数据泄漏。2) 提出 ACE 框架：迭代的测试生成与错误驱动的细化解耦，实现测试时扩展。

Result: 在多个先进 LLM 和智能体框架上，RepoZero 仅达到 30%-55% 的通过率，表明当前最强模型在仓库级代码生成上仍远未达到现实软件工程需求。

Conclusion: RepoZero 是一个具有挑战性、可扩展且可靠的端到端代码生成测试基准，而基于测试生成的自验证是提升 LLM 编码智能体的关键方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently shown remarkable progress in code generation, yet their ability to construct complete software repositories from scratch remains poorly understood. A fundamental bottleneck is the lack of verifiable and scalable evaluation: existing benchmarks either focus on patch-based editing or rely on human or LLM-based judgments, which introduce bias and limit reproducibility. In this work, we present RepoZero, the first benchmark that enables fully automated, execution-based verification of repository-level generation from scratch. Our key idea is to reformulate generation as repository reproduction: given only API specifications, an agent must re-implement an entire repository such that its behavior matches the original implementation. This design allows for strict black-box validation via output equivalence, while naturally supporting large-scale construction by reusing existing open-source repositories. To further mitigate data leakage and shortcut solutions, we introduce cross-language constraints and a sandboxed evaluation protocol. Building on this benchmark, we propose an Agentic Code-Test Evolution (ACE) framework that performs iterative test generation and error-driven refinement, enabling effective test-time scaling for repository-level synthesis. Extensive experiments across multiple state-of-the-art LLMs and agent frameworks reveal that even the strongest LLM agents achieve only limited pass rates (30\% - 55\%), exposing a substantial gap between current capabilities and real-world software development requirements. Our results establish RepoZero as a challenging, scalable, and reliable testbed for end-to-end code generation, and highlight self-verification via test generation as a critical direction for advancing LLM-based coding agents.

</details>


### [84] [Exploring CoCo Challenges in ML Engineering Teams: Insights From the Semiconductor Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07389)
*A. Azamnouri,M. Haug,L. Woltmann,M. Fritz,J. Bogner,S. Wagner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对一家全球半导体公司的12名从业者进行定性访谈，研究了硬件密集型环境中机器学习工程团队的协作与沟通挑战，识别出16个反复出现的挑战，其中角色和职责不明确最为关键，并总结了从业者认为有效的实践和建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注软件密集型公司中ML工程团队的协作与沟通挑战，缺乏对硬件密集型环境（如半导体行业）中这些挑战如何被额外约束（严格数据治理、长开发周期、与物理过程紧密耦合）影响的实证理解。

Method: 对一家全球半导体公司的12名机器学习工程从业者进行半结构化访谈，收集关于协作与沟通实践、工具、挑战和方法的定性数据，并通过主题分析识别模式和挑战。

Result: 识别出16个反复出现的协作与沟通挑战，其中角色和职责不明确是最关键的挑战；同时总结了从业者认为有效的常见实践和建议。

Conclusion: 研究发现与已知的跨学科ML系统开发挑战一致，但展示了在硬件驱动约束下这些挑战的不同表现形式，为未来研究和工具支持指明了方向，以加强MLE项目中的协作与沟通。

Abstract: The integration of machine learning (ML) into complex software systems has increased challenges in collaboration and communication (CoCo) of the teams building these systems. ML engineering (MLE) teams often involve diverse roles, ML engineers, data scientists, software engineers, and domain experts, each bringing unique goals, experiences, and jargon. These interdisciplinary dynamics can make it challenging to deploy, reproduce, and maintain ML-enabled systems over the long term. Previous studies have uncovered several CoCo challenges and practices, but most have focused on software-centric companies, leaving limited empirical understanding of how these dynamics unfold in hardware-centric contexts. In hardware-centric environments, CoCo challenges are shaped by additional constraints such as strict data governance, long development cycles, and tight coupling with physical processes, which amplify coordination complexity and reduce flexibility. To strengthen empirical understanding in such settings, we present a qualitative investigation of MLE teams within a global semiconductor company, where ML-enabled systems and manufacturing processes introduce additional complexity. We interviewed 12 practitioners regarding CoCo practices, tools, challenges, and approaches. Through analysis, we identified 16 recurring challenges, with unclear roles and responsibilities emerging as the most critical, and common practices and recommendations practitioners considered effective in mitigating CoCo problems. While grounded in a single organizational context, our findings align with known issues in interdisciplinary ML-enabled systems development, but also demonstrate how these challenges manifest differently under hardware-driven constraints. Our results highlight directions for future research and tool support to strengthen CoCo in MLE projects and ensure the success of ML-enabled systems.

</details>


### [85] [Boosting Automatic Java-to-Cangjie Translation with Multi-Stage LLM Training and Error Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07403)
*Xinyue Liang,Jingxuan Zhang,Lin Li,Jun Zhang,Junhao Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多阶段训练框架，结合迭代错误修复技术，将Java代码翻译为低资源语言仓颉(Cangjie)。该框架通过渐进式知识融合实现语义对齐与结构感知，并利用编译器反馈和错误修复案例库进行代码修复。实验表明，在有限并行数据下，功能等价性比现有最优方法提升6.06%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴编程语言仓颉的生态系统快速发展，但通用大语言模型因缺乏仓颉知识和并行语料，在代码翻译中容易出现语法错误、语义及结构对齐问题。现有方法依赖大规模并行数据微调，但无法可靠改善低资源仓颉语言的可编译性和语义一致性。

Method: 提出多阶段训练框架：(1) 构建语法知识和单语指令数据集训练LLM；(2) 采用迭代错误修复技术，结合编译器反馈和错误修复案例检索进行代码修复；(3) 构建仓颉错误修复库支持修复过程。通过渐进式知识融合实现语义对齐和结构感知。

Result: 在有限并行数据条件下，功能等价性比现有最优方法提升6.06%；消融实验验证了每个训练阶段对最终性能均有正向贡献。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练框架结合迭代错误修复技术能有效解决低资源语言代码翻译中的编译错误和语义不一致问题，为仓颉等新兴语言的代码翻译提供了可行方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of emerging programming language ecosystems, the demand for code translation to low-resource languages continues to grow. As Cangjie emerges as a new programming language, its ecosystem and development toolchains are rapidly expanding. Automated translation from popular programming languages to Cangjie is therefore valuable for practical development. However, constrained by both insufficient Cangjie knowledge and scarce parallel code corpora, general Large Language Models (LLMs) are prone to syntactic errors and semantic as well as structural misalignment in code translation. Existing approaches typically rely on fine-tuning with large-scale parallel data, but they cannot reliably improve compilability or semantic consistency for low-resource Cangjie languages. To tackle these challenges, we propose a multi-stage training framework of LLMs that employs the iterative error repair technique to translate Java code into Cangjie code. This training framework performs training on LLMs, gradually integrating knowledge and achieving semantic alignment as well as structure awareness. During the code translation, we also combine the compiler feedback and error repair case retrieval to repair the incorrect Cangjie code. We construct syntactic knowledge and monolingual instruction datasets to train the LLM. In addition, we also build a Cangjie error repair repository to support error repair in our approach. Experimental results show that, with limited parallel data, our approach improves functional equivalence by 6.06\% compared to the state-of-the-art approaches. Meanwhile, ablation studies confirm that each training stage positively contributes to the final performance.

</details>


### [86] [Prompt Engineering Strategies for LLM-based Qualitative Coding of Psychological Safety in Software Engineering Communities: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07422)
*Moaath Alshaikh,Tasneem Alshaher,Ricardo Vieira,Beatriz Santana,Clelio Xavier,Jose Amancio,Glauco Carneiro,Julio Leite,Savio Freire,Manoel Mendonca*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过受控实验评估了三种LLM（Claude Haiku、DeepSeek-Chat、Gemini 2.5 Flash）在两种提示工程策略（零样本与多样本封闭编码）下进行定性编码的可靠性，发现多样本提示仅显著提升Claude Haiku的一致性，各模型存在系统性偏差和稳定性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定性分析在软件工程的人与社会层面研究中至关重要，但其过程受研究者主观解读和方法选择（如提示设计）影响较大。LLM虽有潜力支持此类分析，但在不同提示条件下复现人类定性推理的可靠性尚未得到充分检验。

Method: 采用受控实证评估方法，使用三种LLM（Claude Haiku、DeepSeek-Chat、Gemini 2.5 Flash）和两种提示策略（零样本与多样本封闭编码），每种配置运行10次独立实验，以Cohen's kappa作为主要一致性指标进行评价。

Result: 多样本提示显著改善了Claude Haiku的一致性（Delta kappa = +0.034, Wilcoxon p = 0.004），但对DeepSeek-Chat和Gemini 2.5 Flash无显著效果。DeepSeek-Chat和Claude Haiku稳定性最高（SD约0.017），Gemini 2.5 Flash稳定性最低（SD=0.038）。所有模型均存在系统性过预测"分享负面反馈"（偏差比高达5.25倍）和持续欠预测"表达关切"的问题。

Conclusion: 研究结果为软件工程研究中LLM辅助定性编码的提示工程设计提供了经验性指导依据。

Abstract: Qualitative analysis plays a pivotal role in understanding the human and social aspects of software engineering. However, it remains a demanding process shaped by the subjective interpretation of individual researchers and sensitive to methodological choices such as prompt design. Recent advancements in Large Language Models (LLMs) offer promising opportunities to support this type of analysis, although their reliability in reproducing human qualitative reasoning under varying prompting conditions remains largely untested. This study presents a controlled empirical evaluation of three LLMs -- Claude Haiku, DeepSeek-Chat, and Gemini 2.5 Flash -- across two prompt engineering strategies (zero-shot and multi-shot closed coding), using Cohen's kappa as the primary agreement metric over ten independent runs per configuration. Results suggest that multi-shot prompting significantly improves agreement for Claude Haiku (Delta kappa = +0.034, Wilcoxon p = 0.004) but not for DeepSeek-Chat or Gemini 2.5 Flash. Intra-model stability varies substantially -- DeepSeek-Chat and Claude Haiku exhibit the lowest variance (SD approx. 0.017), while Gemini 2.5 Flash is the least stable (SD = 0.038). A systematic over-prediction of "Sharing Negative Feedback" is identified across all models (bias ratios up to 5.25x), alongside consistent under-prediction of "Expressing Concerns." Collectively, these findings provide empirical evidence for prompt engineering guidelines in LLM-assisted qualitative coding for software engineering research.

</details>


### [87] [MASPrism: Lightweight Failure Attribution for Multi-Agent Systems Using Prefill-Stage Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07509)
*Yang Liu,Hongjiang Feng,Junsong Pu,Zhuangbin Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MASPrism提出了一种轻量级的失败归因框架，利用小语言模型（SLM）的预填充阶段信号（token级负对数似然和注意力权重），在不进行解码的情况下识别多智能体系统执行失败的根本原因，在多个基准上取得了最佳性能，并实现了显著的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统中，失败归因面临三大挑战：单次执行包含大量智能体动作和工具调用、失败证据可能出现在原始错误的多步之后、现有方法依赖昂贵的智能体工作流、重放或合成失败日志训练。因此需要一种高效、轻量级的失败归因方法。

Method: MASPrism框架包含两个阶段：1）利用SLM（Qwen3-0.6B）进行第一次预填充传递，提取token级负对数似然和注意力权重，识别症状性步骤和早期候选根源，不进行解码；2）构建聚焦的诊断提示，进行第二次预填充传递，对失败根源候选进行排序。

Result: 在Who&When和TRAIL四个子集的三项上取得最佳性能；Who&When-HC上Top-1准确率提升33.41%；TRAIL上优于Gemini-2.5-Pro等强闭源LLM，相对提升达89.50%；平均处理每条轨迹2.66秒，比单次提示基线加速6.69倍，零输出token。

Conclusion: MASPrism为长多智能体执行日志的失败归因提供了一个有效且实用的轻量级框架，证明了利用SLM预填充信号进行失败诊断的可行性。

Abstract: Failure attribution in LLM-based multi-agent systems aims to identify the steps that contribute to a failed execution. This task remains difficult because a single execution can contain many agent actions and tool calls, failure evidence can appear many steps after the original mistake, and existing methods often rely on costly agent workflows, replay, or training on synthetic failure logs. To address these challenges, we propose MASPrism, a lightweight framework that performs failure attribution using prefill-stage signals from a small language model (SLM). MASPrism first extracts token-level negative log-likelihood and attention weights during a prefill pass to identify symptom-like steps and earlier candidate sources, without decoding. It then reconstructs a focused diagnostic prompt and performs a second prefill pass to rank failure-source candidates. Using Qwen3-0.6B as the SLM, MASPrism achieves the best performance on three of the four evaluated subsets across Who&When and TRAIL, improving Top-1 accuracy on Who&When-HC by 33.41% over the best baseline. On TRAIL, MASPrism outperforms strong proprietary LLMs, including Gemini-2.5-Pro, with up to 89.50% relative improvement. MASPrism processes each trace in 2.66 seconds on average, achieving a 6.69$\times$ speedup over the single-pass prompting baseline, with zero output tokens. These results show that MASPrism provides an effective and practical framework for failure attribution in long multi-agent execution logs.

</details>


### [88] [Can LLMs Solve Science or Just Write Code? Evaluating Quantum Solver Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07525)
*Luciano Baresi,Domenico Bianculli,Maryse Ernzer,Livia Lestingi,Fabrizio Pastore,Seung Yeob Shin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-SAGE方法，通过迭代执行、比较和优化LLM生成的量子求解器代码，评估LLM在科学计算中生成量子程序的能力。实验表明迭代精炼提升成功率但计算开销大，且模型能力增强后失败模式从执行错误转向数值不准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码生成方面表现强劲，但量子计算中代码执行成功不保证结果正确，数值精度受映射、混合量子-经典工作流和算法近似影响，现有评估方法不足以衡量LLM生成量子求解器的实际能力。

Method: 提出Q-SAGE迭代方法：LLM生成量子求解脚本→执行脚本→结果与经典求解器结果比较→若不满足容差阈值则精炼脚本，循环直至一致。

Result: 迭代精炼显著提升成功率但计算开销大；随着模型能力提升，失败模式从执行错误转变为数值不准确。

Conclusion: LLM生成量子求解器可行但存在数值精度瓶颈，当前LLM-based量子软件在数值准确性方面仍有局限。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show strong capabilities in code generation, motivating their use in automated quantum solver development. However, in quantum computing, successful execution of generated code is not sufficient: correctness depends on numerically accurate results, which are sensitive to non-trivial mappings, hybrid quantum-classical workflows, and algorithm-specific approximations. This work introduces Q-SAGE, an iterative methodology to evaluate LLMs' capability in generating quantum solvers for scientific problems. The methodology adopts an iterative approach by executing the script generated by the LLM, comparing the result with the result of a classical solver, and refining the script until the two results match within a tolerance threshold. We empirically evaluated the methodology with five families of scientific problems of different complexities and five LLMs, both open source and proprietary. The results show that iterative refinement substantially improves success rates, but introduces a significant computational overhead. Moreover, as model capability increases, failure modes shift from execution errors to numerical inaccuracies, highlighting the current limitations of LLM-based quantum software.

</details>


### [89] [System Test Generation for Virtual Reality Applications using Scenario Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07534)
*Gerry Longfils,Maxime Cauz,Arnaud Blouin,Xavier Devroey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 UltraInstinctVR，一种针对 VR 应用的自动化测试方法。它基于预定义的 VR 模型（场景）自动生成和执行系统测试。在 10 个开源 VR 应用上的评估表明，该方法在故障检测方面优于现有自动化 VR 测试工具，能有效发现真实世界中的 VR 应用漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VR 应用日益普及（如手术训练、工业营销），但由于缺乏有效、系统化、可复现的软件测试方法，其长期采用和维护仍然受限。

Method: 提出 UltraInstinctVR 方法，基于预定义的 VR 模型（场景），自动生成并执行具体的 VR 系统测试用例。

Result: 在 10 个开源 VR 应用上与最先进的自动化 VR 测试方法进行对比实验，UltraInstinctVR 在检测独特故障方面优于现有工具，并能有效识别真实世界中的 VR 应用缺陷。

Conclusion: UltraInstinctVR 为 VR 应用的自动化测试提供了有效方案，在覆盖率和故障检测方面均具有优势，有助于提升 VR 应用的可靠性和长期可维护性。

Abstract: Virtual Reality (VR) applications are increasingly being integrated across a wide range of domains, including surgical training and industrial marketing. However, the long-term adoption and maintenance of VR applications remain limited, particularly due to the lack of effective, systematic, and reproducible software testing approaches tailored to their unique characteristics. To address this issue, we introduce UltraInstinctVR, a novel testing approach for VR applications. Relying on predefined VR models (scenarios), it automates the generation and execution of concrete VR system tests. In our empirical evaluation, we compare UltraInstinctVR with state-of-the-art automated VR testing approaches in terms of coverage and failure detection on 10 open-source VR applications. The results show that UltraInstinctVR outperforms existing automated tools for detecting unique failures and provides valuable insights for identifying real-world bugs in VR applications.

</details>


### [90] [Characterizing and Mitigating False-Positive Bug Reports in the Linux Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07678)
*Jiashuo Tian,Dong Wang,Chen Yang,Haichi Wang,Zan Wang,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次对Linux内核中的误报（false-positive）bug报告进行了全面的实证研究，构建了包含2,006个报告（1,509个真实bug，497个误报）的数据集，发现误报消耗的开发工作量与真实bug相当，主要集中在文件系统和驱动组件，主要原因是外部依赖和语义误解。通过检索增强生成（RAG）策略，LLM可实现91%召回率和88%F1分数，有效缓解误报问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 误报bug报告在Linux内核开发中是一个重要但未被充分研究的问题，它们会浪费开发者精力、误导调试方向并延误真实bug的修复，因此需要系统性地理解其特征并探索自动化缓解方法。

Method: 手动构建了包含2,006份报告的数据集（来自Bugzilla和Syzkaller），其中1,509个真实bug和497个误报；通过定性和定量分析对比两者差异；评估多种LLM提示策略（包括RAG）用于自动检测误报。

Result: 误报与真实bug消耗的工作量相当，需要长时间的讨论和关闭时间；误报主要出现在文件系统和驱动组件；主要成因是外部依赖和语义误解；RAG策略表现最佳，召回率91%，F1分数88%。

Conclusion: 误报bug报告在Linux内核中具有不可忽视的开发成本，LLM（特别是RAG方法）在自动化缓解误报问题上展现出显著潜力。

Abstract: False-positive bug reports represent a significant yet underexplored challenge in the development and maintenance of the Linux kernel. They occur when correct system behavior is mistakenly flagged as a defect, consuming developer effort without leading to actual code improvements. Such reports can mislead developers, waste debugging resources, and delay the resolution of real bugs. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive empirical study of false-positive bug reports in the Linux kernel. We manually construct a dataset of 2,006 bug reports comprising 1,509 genuine bugs and 497 false positives collected from Bugzilla and Syzkaller. Our analysis indicates that false positives demand effort comparable to real bugs, often requiring extended discussions and non-trivial closure time. They occur in several components, especially File Systems and Drivers, mainly due to external dependencies and semantic misunderstandings. To address this challenge, we evaluate large language models (LLMs) for automated false-positive bug report mitigation. Among various prompting strategies, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) performs best, achieving 91% recall and an F1 score of 88%. These findings highlight the non-negligible cost of false positive bug reports and show the promise of LLMs for more efficient false positive mitigation in the Linux kernel.

</details>


### [91] [SafeTune: Search-based Harmfulness Minimisation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07709)
*Giordano d'Aloisio,David Williams,Giusy Annunziata,Zhiwei Fei,Antinisca Di Marco,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeTune方法，通过多目标搜索的超参数调优和系统提示工程，在提升回复相关性的同时显著降低LLM的有害回复率。实验发现，增加回复重复性对减少有害性和提升相关性最有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型广泛部署带来有害回复的潜在风险，亟需在不牺牲回复相关性的前提下降低有害性。

Method: 提出SafeTune，一种基于多目标搜索的方法，结合超参数调优和系统提示工程，同时优化有害性和相关性两个目标。

Result: 在Qwen3.5 0.8B上显著降低有害回复率，同时提升提示-回复相关性，效应量较大；增加回复重复性是对两个目标最有效的单一参数。

Conclusion: SafeTune能有效平衡LLM的安全性和实用性，重复性惩罚的调节是关键影响因素。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) raises concerns about the potential harmfulness of their responses. In this paper, we first investigate the harmfulness of responses from four general-purpose LLMs. Next, we propose SafeTune, a multi-objective search-based approach to mitigate harmfulness while increasing response relevance through hyperparameter tuning and system prompt engineering. Our initial evaluation shows that SafeTune significantly reduces the rate of harmful responses generated by Qwen3.5 0.8B and increases prompt-response relevance (both with a large effect size). Among the parameters we explore, we also find that encouraging greater repetition in responses is most impactful in reducing harmfulness while increasing relevance.

</details>


### [92] [The AI-Native Large-Scale Agile Software Development Manifesto](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07717)
*Ricardo Britto,Fredrik Palmgren,Nishrith Saini,Marcus Ohlin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AI原生大规模敏捷软件开发宣言，定义了六个核心原则（并行流程、意图驱动团队、活知识、验证优先保障、编排化智能体劳动力、可复用蓝图），将AI从辅助工具提升为一级参与者，推动大规模软件开发从会议驱动、文档繁重的串行流程转向智能、自适应、持续学习的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模敏捷方法仍以人为中心且依赖手工操作（协调会议、工件同步、角色交接），难以实现真正的实时适应性。尽管AI（尤其是大语言模型）已开始变革软件工程，但其在组织级敏捷中的潜力尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出AI原生大规模敏捷软件开发宣言，包含一套价值观和原则体系，使AI成为一级参与者而非外围工具。具体包括六项原则：并行流程、意图驱动团队、活知识、验证优先保障、编排化智能体劳动力、可复用蓝图。

Result: 该宣言将大规模开发从会议驱动、文档繁重、串行流程转变为智能、自适应、持续学习的系统。

Conclusion: 在AI成为一级参与者的前提下，重新定义大规模软件开发的组织方式，以实现真正的组织级敏捷性。

Abstract: Despite the widespread adoption of agile methods, achieving true agility at scale remains elusive. Large-scale agile frameworks remain largely human-centric and manual, relying on coordination meetings, artifact synchronization, and role-based handoffs that inhibit real-time adaptation. Meanwhile, rapid advances in AI, particularly large language models, have begun transforming software engineering, yet their potential for organizational-level agility remains underexplored. We present the AI-Native Large-Scale Agile Software Development Manifesto: a set of values and principles that redefine how large-scale software development is organized when AI becomes a first-class participant rather than a peripheral tool. The manifesto is grounded in six principles, parallel processes, intent-driven teams, living knowledge, verification-first assurance, orchestrated agent workforces, and reusable blueprints, that together shift development from a meeting-driven, document-heavy, sequential process to an intelligent, adaptive, continuously learning system.

</details>


### [93] [SARC: A Governance-by-Architecture Framework for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07728)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SARC是一种针对工具使用型AI智能体的运行时治理架构，将约束作为一等规范对象，在智能体循环中嵌入四个执行站点（预动作门控、动作时监控、动作后审计、升级路由），实现义务的运行时可执行、可检查、可审计。实验表明，在采购任务中，SARC在精确谓词下实现零硬约束违规，软窗口超量比纯策略即代码基线降低89.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI系统越来越多地通过工具、子智能体和外部服务执行操作，但治理控制仍通常附着在提示、仪表盘或事后的文档上。这在受监管场景中造成结构性错配：必须在执行过程中约束的义务，往往只在执行完成后才被评估。

Method: SARC将约束作为一等规范对象，与状态、动作空间、奖励并列；每个约束声明其来源、类别、谓词、验证点、响应协议和操作点，编译为智能体循环中的四个执行站点：预动作门控、动作时监控、动作后审计、升级路由。形式化定义了规约-轨迹对应的最小不变量，并扩展到多智能体工作流中的约束传播、权限交集和属性保留跟踪树。

Result: 在50个种子的可重复合成评估中，SARC在精确谓词下实现零硬约束违规；其声明的PAA限流响应将软窗口超量比纯策略即代码基线降低89.5%。谓词噪声和执行失效扫描表明，SARC下的残留硬违规随执行栈错误而非环境违规机会缩放。

Conclusion: SARC提供了使义务在运行时变得可执行、可检查和可审计的架构基础，证明了有限奖励惩罚通常不能替代硬运行时约束，为受监管的AI系统治理提供了结构化的运行时解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems increasingly act through tools, sub-agents, and external services, but governance controls are still commonly attached to prompts, dashboards, or post-hoc documentation. This creates a structural mismatch in regulated settings: obligations that must constrain execution are often evaluated only after execution has occurred. We introduce SARC, a runtime governance architecture for tool-using agents that treats constraints as first-class specification objects alongside state, action space, and reward. A SARC specification declares each constraint's source, class, predicate, verification point, response protocol, and operating point, and compiles these into four enforcement sites in the agent loop: a Pre-Action Gate, an Action-Time Monitor, a Post-Action Auditor, and an Escalation Router. We formalize the minimal invariants required for specification-trace correspondence, show why finite reward penalties do not generally substitute for hard runtime constraints, and extend the architecture to multi-agent workflows through constraint propagation, authority intersection, and attribution-preserving trace trees. We implement a prototype audit checker and report a reproducible synthetic evaluation over 50 seeds comparing SARC against post-hoc audit, output filtering, workflow rules, and policy-as-code-only baselines on a procurement task. SARC executes zero hard-constraint violations under exact predicates; its declared PAA throttling response reduces soft-window overages by 89.5% relative to policy-as-code-only. Predicate-noise and enforcement-failure sweeps are consistent with the claim that residual hard violations under SARC scale with enforcement-stack error rather than environmental violation opportunity. SARC provides the architectural substrate through which obligations can be made executable, inspectable, and auditable at runtime.

</details>


### [94] [Unsafe by Flow: Uncovering Bidirectional Data-Flow Risks in MCP Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07836)
*Xinyi Hou,Yanjie Zhao,Haoyu Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MCP-BiFlow是一个专为Model Context Protocol（MCP）设计的双向静态分析框架，通过协议感知的入口点恢复、污点建模和过程间传播分析，在32个确认漏洞案例中实现93.8%召回率，并在15,452个真实仓库中发现118条漏洞路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP作为LLM智能体与外部工具的接口层，引入了双向不安全数据流：请求方控制的参数可能传播到敏感操作，而不可信的外部数据或敏感内部数据可能通过MCP可见输出影响主机或模型行为。现有分析工具（CodeQL、Semgrep等）缺乏协议感知能力，难以准确检测此类漏洞。

Method: 提出MCP-BiFlow框架，包含三个核心组件：1）MCP感知的入口点恢复，处理异构注册与分发模式；2）协议特定的污点语义建模；3）过程间传播分析，沿完整工具作用域执行路径追踪双向数据流。

Result: 在32个确认MCP漏洞的基准测试中识别出30个（93.8%召回率），大幅优于CodeQL、Semgrep、Snyk Code和MCPScan。在15,452个真实MCP服务器仓库中，经重叠压缩后得到549个候选簇，人工验证确认87个服务器中存在118条漏洞路径。

Conclusion: 不安全传播是MCP服务器中反复出现的故障模式，此类漏洞难以在没有协议感知的请求侧和返回侧双向流恢复的情况下被检测到。

Abstract: Model Context Protocol (MCP) have quickly become the interface layer between LLM agents and external tools, yet they also introduce unsafe data flows that existing analyzers handle poorly. Vulnerabilities manifest in two directions: requester-controlled arguments may propagate to sensitive operations, while untrusted external or sensitive internal data may surface through MCP-visible outputs and subsequently influence host or model behavior. Accurate detection is complicated by the heterogeneous registration and dispatch patterns MCP servers employ, the need for MCP-specific taint semantics, and the fact that bugs often only materialize along complete tool-scoped execution paths. We present MCP-BiFlow, a bidirectional static analysis framework built around MCP-aware entrypoint recovery, protocol-specific taint modeling, and interprocedural propagation analysis. Against a benchmark of 32 confirmed MCP vulnerability cases, MCP-BiFlow identifies 30 (93.8% recall), substantially outperforming CodeQL, Semgrep, Snyk Code, and MCPScan. Across 15,452 real-world MCP server repositories, MCP-BiFlow surfaces 549 overlap-compressed candidate clusters; manual review confirms 118 vulnerability paths in 87 servers, establishing unsafe propagation as a recurring failure mode that resists detection without protocol-aware recovery of both request-side and return-side flows.

</details>


### [95] [Evaluating Design Conformance Through Trace Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07909)
*Reid Anderson,Hassan Reza*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于一致性检验（conformance checking）的方法，利用OpenTelemetry生成的分布式追踪数据，通过比较实际实现轨迹与设计模型轨迹，量化分布式系统实现与设计之间的一致性程度，并随时间追踪该指标以防止系统偏离原始设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式系统的设计与实现通常由不同人员在相隔数周或数月内完成，导致实际行为与设计模型之间可能出现偏差；随着时间推移和原始设计人员离开，系统可能逐渐丢失与设计原则的一致性。现有方法无法有效量化这种偏差并持续监控。

Method: 从过程挖掘领域借鉴一致性检验方法，利用OpenTelemetry标准对分布式应用进行仪器化以产生分布式追踪数据，将实际运行轨迹与设计模型轨迹进行比对，计算一致性百分比作为量化指标，并随时间持续追踪该指标。

Result: 该方法可生成一致性百分比这一量化指标，能够随时间追踪以确定具体实现与预期设计模型关键属性的吻合程度；由于基于行业标准OpenTelemetry，适用于广泛的分布式系统。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种持续监控分布式系统实现与设计一致性的定量手段，有助于在系统演化过程中维持设计原则的一致性，防止系统失谐（drift）。

Abstract: The design of a system and its implementation are two tasks often carried out by different individuals on a development team, and can occur weeks or months apart. This creates a potential for divergence between real behavior and the designed model that an implementation is intended to match. Particularly as time passes and individuals who were present for the original conception of the design leave, a system can lose coherence and drift from intended design principles. Even with a robust system design, more is needed to ensure that the key implementation details match the design and that adherence to a particular strategy is not lost over time. This paper proposes an approach to address that concern for distributed systems using conformance checking, a methodology borrowed from process mining. Distributed traces produced by instrumented applications are evaluated for conformance by comparison to design traces. The resulting conformance percentage is a quantitative metric that can be tracked over time to determine how closely a concrete implementation corresponds to the key attributes of the expected design model. This analysis is done using the dominant industry standard, OpenTelemetry, and so should apply to a wide range of distributed systems.

</details>


### [96] [Similar Pattern Annotation via Retrieval Knowledge for LLM-Based Test Code Fault Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07957)
*Golnaz Gharachorlu,Mahsa Panahandeh,Lionel C. Briand,Ruifeng Gao,Ruiyuan Wan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SPARK是一个集成CI环境中累积调试知识到LLM的测试代码故障定位（TCFL）框架，通过检索相似故障案例并选择性注释可疑代码行来引导LLM推理。在三个工业数据集上，该方法相较于现有LLM基线持续提升了故障定位效果，尤其能更准确地识别包含多个故障的复杂测试用例中的正确故障位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件故障定位研究多集中于被测系统（SUT），但系统测试脚本本身也可能存在故障（TCFL问题）。TCFL在CI环境下尤为重要却研究不足，且面临黑盒条件、诊断信号有限、搜索空间大等挑战。

Method: 提出SPARK框架：从CI环境构建带故障标签的调试知识库，对新出现的失败测试用例，检索相似历史故障用例，基于相似度选择性注释可疑代码行，以此引导LLM进行故障定位，避免朴素RAG方法的提示长度爆炸问题。

Result: 在三个包含真实故障Python测试用例的工业数据集上，SPARK相比现有LLM基线的TCFL方法持续提升故障定位效果，且推理成本和token使用量相当。尤其在含多个故障的复杂用例中能识别更多正确故障位置。

Conclusion: SPARK通过整合CI调试知识到LLM的TCFL框架，改进了现有技术水平，为测试代码故障定位提供了一种兼顾效果和可扩展性的有效方案。

Abstract: Software failures remain a major challenge in modern software development, and identifying the code elements responsible for failures is a time-consuming debugging task. While extensive research has focused on fault localization in the system under test (SUT), failures can also originate from faulty system test scripts. This problem, known as Test Code Fault Localization (TCFL), has received significantly less attention despite its importance in continuous integration (CI) environments where large test suites are executed frequently. TCFL is particularly challenging because it typically operates under black-box conditions, relies on limited diagnostic signals such as error messages and partial logs, and involves large system-level test scripts that expand the fault localization search space. In this paper, we propose SPARK, a framework that integrates accumulated debugging knowledge from continuous integration (CI) environments into Large Language Model (LLM)-based TCFL. Given a newly observed failing test case, SPARK retrieves similar fault-labeled test cases from a debugging knowledge corpus and selectively annotates suspicious lines of the failing test based on their similarity to previously observed fault patterns. These annotations guide the LLM's reasoning while maintaining scalability and avoiding the prompt-length explosion common to naive retrieval-augmented approaches. We evaluate SPARK on three industrial datasets containing real-world faulty Python test cases from different software products. The results show that SPARK consistently improves fault localization effectiveness compared to the existing LLM-based TCFL baseline while maintaining comparable inference cost and token usage. In particular, the approach advances the state of the art by identifying more correct faulty locations in complex test cases containing multiple faults.

</details>

### [97] [When to Use Wireless Challenge-Response Physical Layer Authentication: Design of a Measurable Guideline for OFDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06750)
*Haiyun Liu,Shangqing Zhao,Yao Liu,Zhuo Lu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为最大差分似然生成器（MDLG）的新型对抗模型，利用OFDM无线信道中实际存在的子信道弱相关性来攻击物理层认证（PLA）系统，并建立了基于随机性测试的可量化准则来判断何时PLA在真实信道条件下是安全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设无线信道具有足够随机的衰落特性以保证PLA安全，但在实际场景中，OFDM子信道响应存在相关性，该假设不总是成立，导致PLA安全性面临未解决的问题。

Method: 提出MDLG对抗模型，利用实际无线信道中的弱相关特性发起有效攻击；并基于随机性测试建立可量化准则，用于评估在何种实际信道条件下可以安全使用PLA。

Result: 大量真实世界实验验证了MDLG攻击的有效性，并证明了所提出的准则能够帮助保护PLA的安全性。

Conclusion: 实际无线信道的子信道相关性会削弱PLA的安全性，MDLG攻击能有效利用这一弱点，而所提出的随机性测试准则为PLA的实际安全部署提供了可操作的指导。

Abstract: The security of wireless challenge-response Physical Layer Authentication (PLA) based on Orthogonal Frequency Division Multiplexing (OFDM) relies on a sufficiently random fading channel condition, which is commonly assumed in existing studies. However, in practical scenarios, such a condition is not always guaranteed and the responses of OFDM subchannels may exhibit correlation.} Consequently, ensuring the security of such PLA systems remains an unsolved problem. In this paper, we propose a novel adversary model, called Maximum Differential Likelihood Generator (MDLG), which exploits the weak correlation property in practical wireless channel to launch effective attacks against PLA. Based on this model, we create a measurable guideline using randomness testing to decide when we can in fact use PLA in a practical wireless channel condition. Extensive real-world experiments validate the effectiveness of the MDLG attack and demonstrate how the proposed guideline can help protect the security of PLA.

</details>


### [98] [Unconsented Sensing: A Sociotechnical Governance Framework for 6G ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07328)
*Anass Sedrati*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文指出6G通感一体化（ISAC）的隐私与可信赖性不能仅靠技术安全（如加密）解决，必须引入强制性法规与社会技术合规框架。以EU AI Act和GDPR为基准，提出三大治理支柱：目的绑定感知激活、公民透明机制、ISAC驱动ML模型的算法问责，为6G感知基础设施的合法部署提供监管路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前6G标准化工作（3GPP、ETSI）虽承认隐私与可信赖性是关键支柱，但提出的缓解措施过度技术中心主义（依赖加密匿名化和物理层安全），严重低估了下游机器学习模型解读原始传感数据所涉及的社会技术和法律复杂性，导致与现有数字权利立法产生深刻冲突。

Method: 采用立场论文形式，以EU AI Act和GDPR作为主要监管基准，识别连续ISAC监控与新兴全球数字权利制度之间的具体法律摩擦点，并提出三大治理支柱框架：目的绑定感知激活、公民透明机制、算法问责。

Result: 识别了ISAC监控与数字权利法规之间的具体法律冲突，提出了一个包含三大支柱的治理框架，为6G传感基础设施在物理部署前确保合法合规性提供了路线图。

Conclusion: 技术安全不足以确保6G ISAC的可信赖性，必须将其重新定义为强制性法规与社会技术合规。所提出的治理框架旨在防止6G传感基础设施的非法部署，确保其在物理部署前具备法律可行性。

Abstract: The forthcoming deployment of 6G Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC) will transform cellular infrastructure into pervasive, continuous environmental and biometric sensing grids. While current telecom standardization efforts (e.g., 3GPP, ETSI) have formally recognized privacy and trustworthiness as critical pillars for 6G, their proposed mitigations remain overwhelmingly technocentric, relying on cryptographic anonymization and physical layer security. This approach critically underestimates the sociotechnical and legal complexities of the downstream machine learning (ML) models required to interpret raw sensing data, creating a profound collision with existing digital rights legislation. This position paper argues that technical security is insufficient. ISAC trustworthiness must be redefined as mandatory regulatory and sociotechnical compliance. We identify the specific legal friction points between continuous ISAC surveillance and the mandates of emerging global digital rights regimes, using the stringent requirements of the EU AI Act and GDPR as our primary regulatory baselines. To bridge this gap, we propose a governance framework centered on three pillars: Purpose-bound sensing activation, citizen transparency mechanisms, and algorithmic accountability for ISAC-driven ML models. Ultimately, this paper provides a regulatory roadmap to prevent the illegal deployment of 6G sensing infrastructures and ensure they remain viable before physical deployment.

</details>

### [99] [Rethinking Priority Scheduling for Sequential Multi-Agent Decision Making in Stackelberg Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07240)
*Xiangyu Liu,Liang Zhang,Bo Jin,Ziqi Wei*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化优先级调整（HPA）方法，通过动态优化智能体决策顺序来解决N层Stackelberg博弈中顺序影响均衡点的问题，在多智能体MuJoCo等高精度控制任务中表现优越。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究直接使用环境提供的默认智能体决策顺序，但忽略了决策顺序是否影响博弈最终均衡点的问题。本文旨在探究顺序对均衡点的影响，并提出优化顺序的方法。

Method: 形式上分析了N层Stackelberg博弈中决策顺序变化会导致超定系统从而改变均衡点；提出HPA方法：上层策略基于当前博弈状态动态选择最优决策顺序，下层在时空顺序马尔可夫博弈（STMG）中按选定顺序执行策略；采用快慢更新方案，利用上层策略的优势函数生成共享内在奖励来协调跨时间尺度学习。

Result: 在高精度控制任务（如多智能体MuJoCo）上，HPA优于基准算法，并能稳健适应环境变化。

Conclusion: 优化智能体决策顺序在N层Stackelberg博弈中至关重要，HPA方法有效提升了多智能体系统的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Current research applying N-level Stackelberg Game to multi-agent systems often uses the default decision order of agents provided by the environment. However, this raises the question: does the order of agents necessarily affect the final equilibrium point of the game? To address this, we formally analyze the N-level Stackelberg Game, where changing the order in which agents make decisions typically leads to an overdetermined system. As a result, the equilibrium point shifts unless special structural conditions are satisfied. Based on this analysis, we propose the Hierarchical Priority Adjustment (HPA) method, which adjusts and selects the agents' decision order. At the upper level, an upper policy dynamically selects the optimal decision order of agents based on the current game state. At the lower level, agents execute strategies in the Spatio-Temporal Sequential Markov Game (STMG) according to the selected order. To coordinate learning across time scales, we employ a slow-fast update scheme with shared intrinsic rewards derived from the advantage function of the upper policy. Experimental results on high-precision control tasks, including multi-agent MuJoCo, show that HPA outperforms benchmark algorithms and robustly adapts to changing environments. These results highlight the crucial role of optimizing the agents' decision order in N-level Stackelberg Game.

</details>


### [100] [Emergence of Social Reality of Emotion through a Social Allostasis Model with Dynamic Interpretants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07761)
*Kentaro Nomura,Yushi Tsubamoto,Takato Horii*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究通过整合符号涌现、符号解释自由度与主动推理的计算模型，模拟了基于内感信号与社会交互的情绪概念社会现实涌现过程，实验表明两个智能体的内感先验偏好与符号概率分布趋于收敛，验证了社会共识驱动的社会现实形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证建构情绪理论中关于社会现实（情绪概念的社会共识）如何从个体内感信号与社会交互中涌现的理论假设。

Method: 构建了整合符号涌现、符号解释自由度与主动推理的计算模型，两个智能体接收内感信号、交换推断符号，并同时调整自身躯体控制目标与符号解释以适应对方。

Result: 两个智能体的内感先验偏好与符号概率分布趋于收敛，表明社会共识得以形成。

Conclusion: 证实了基于社会共识的情绪概念社会现实可以在计算模型中涌现，支持了建构情绪理论的核心主张。

Abstract: The theory of constructed emotion defines social reality as the community-level consensus on emotion concepts assigned to interoceptive sensations arising from bodily allostasis and social interaction. In this study, we simulate this emergence process using a computational model that integrates symbol emergence with degrees of freedom in symbol interpretation and active inference. Two agents receive interoceptive signals, exchange inferred symbols, and simultaneously adapt their bodily control goals and symbol interpretations to each other. Experimental results show that the interoceptive prior preferences and symbol probability distributions of the two agents converge, confirming the emergence of social reality grounded in social consensus.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [RateQuant: Optimal Mixed-Precision KV Cache Quantization via Rate-Distortion Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06675)
*Fei Zuo,Zikang Zhou,Hao Cong,Xiaoyan Xi,Ho Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RateQuant方法，通过为每个量化器单独拟合失真模型并采用逆向注水分配法解决混合精度KV缓存量化中的失真模型不匹配问题，在2.5平均比特下显著降低困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存随序列长度线性增长成为服务的主要内存瓶颈，现有量化方法对所有注意力头分配相同位宽，忽略了头的重要性差异。混合精度分配虽好，但不同量化器的失真曲线差异会导致分配顺序反转，性能甚至不如均匀量化。

Method: 通过小校准集为每个量化器拟合失真模型D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}，然后基于率失真理论通过逆向注水法闭合求解比特分配问题。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上平均2.5比特下，RateQuant将KIVI的困惑度从49.3降至14.9（降低70%），QuaRot提升6.6 PPL；校准仅需1.6秒且推理零开销。

Conclusion: 失真模型不匹配是混合精度KV缓存量化的关键陷阱，RateQuant通过校准失真模型有效解决了该问题，实现了更优的量化性能。

Abstract: Large language models cache all previously computed key-value (KV) pairs during generation, and this KV cache grows linearly with sequence length, making it a primary memory bottleneck for serving. Quantizing the KV cache to fewer bits reduces this cost, yet all current quantizers assign the same bit-width to every attention head, ignoring the large variation in head importance. A natural idea is to allocate more bits to important heads and fewer to the rest. We show, however, that such mixed-precision allocation has a hidden pitfall: each quantizer follows a different distortion curve D(b)=alpha*beta^{-b}, and the decay rate beta varies from 3.6 to 5.3 across quantizer designs. Applying one quantizer's distortion model to another inverts the allocation order and makes performance worse than uniform quantization. We call this failure mode distortion model mismatch and propose RateQuant to resolve it. RateQuant fits a per-quantizer distortion model from a small calibration set, then solves the resulting bit-allocation problem in closed form via reverse waterfilling from rate-distortion theory. On Qwen3-8B at 2.5 average bits, calibrated RateQuant reduces KIVI's perplexity from 49.3 to 14.9 (70% reduction) and improves QuaRot by 6.6 PPL. The entire calibration takes 1.6 s on a single GPU and adds zero overhead at inference time.

</details>


### [102] [Cost-Ordered Feasibility for Multi-Armed Bandits with Cost Subsidy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07171)
*Ishank Juneja,Carlee Joe-Wong,Osman Yağan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究成本补贴多臂老虎机（MAB-CS）问题，目标是最小化成本同时满足相对于未知最优奖励的质量约束。提出成本有序可行性（COF）算法，通过智能组合所有臂的样本来评估低成本臂的可行性，并建立了实例相关的下界和上界，实验证明COF在理论和实证上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MAB问题追求最大化奖励，但在实际应用中往往需要最小化成本同时满足最低奖励约束（即成本-补贴场景）。现有研究缺乏针对奖励约束相对于未知最优奖励这一设定的理论分析，且没有高效的算法来处理此类问题。

Method: 首先推导了任意策略所需的期望次优样本数的实例相关下界，该下界是先前结果的严格推广。然后提出COF（Cost-Ordered Feasibility）算法，该算法智能地组合来自所有臂的样本以评估低成本臂的可行性，并建立了关于期望累积成本和质量遗憾（相对于最便宜的可行臂）的实例相关上界。

Result: 在理论上，COF实现了定性更优的遗憾上界；在实验上，通过在MovieLens、Goodreads数据集及合成实例上的仿真，COF相比基线方法表现出更优的实证性能。

Conclusion: 本文为MAB-CS问题提供了新的理论见解（严格泛化的下界），提出的COF算法在理论和实证上均优于现有方法，为该领域提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The classic multi-armed bandit (MAB) problem tackles the challenge of accruing maximum reward while making decisions under uncertainty. However, in applications, often the goal is to minimize cost subject to a constraint on the minimum permissible reward, an objective captured by multi-armed bandits with cost-subsidy (MAB-CS). Of interest to this paper is the setting where the quality (reward) constraint is specified relative to the unknown best reward and the cost of each arm is known. We characterize the expected sub-optimal samples required by any policy by proving instance-dependent lower bounds that offer new insight into the problem and are a strict generalization of prior bounds. Then, we propose an algorithm called Cost-Ordered Feasibility (COF) that leverages our insight and intelligently combine samples from all arms to gauge the feasibility of a cheap arm. Thereafter, we analyze COF to establish instance-dependent upper bounds on its expected cumulative cost and quality regret, i.e., relative to the cheapest feasible arm. Finally, we empirically validate the merits of COF, comparing it to baselines from the literature through extensive simulation experiments on the MovieLens and Goodreads datasets as well as representative synthetic instances. Not only does our paper develop qualitatively better theoretical regret upper bounds, but COF also convincingly demonstrates improved empirical performance.

</details>


### [103] [A Unified Measure-Theoretic View of Diffusion, Score-Based, and Flow Matching Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06829)
*Aditya Ranganath,Mukesh Singhal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了基于随机或确定性动力学的连续时间生成模型，提出了一个统一框架，将扩散模型、基于分数的生成模型和流匹配统一为学习时变向量场的实例，该向量场诱导由连续性方程和Fokker-Planck方程控制的边际分布族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前扩散模型、分数生成模型和流匹配方法在方法论上趋于收敛，但碎片化的符号体系和相互竞争的推导过程掩盖了它们共享的结构以及采样、稳定性与计算之间的实际权衡，亟需一个统一的理论框架来厘清这些联系。

Method: (i) 将扩散模型和分数模型的逆向时间采样推导为受控随机动力学；(ii) 证明概率流ODE产生相同的边际分布，并将扩散与基于似然的归一化流联系起来；(iii) 将流匹配解释为在选定插值下对速度场的直接回归，阐明其与分数训练的异同。

Result: 在统一符号下比较了各方法的目标函数、采样方案和离散化误差，讨论了与Schrödinger桥和熵最优传输的联系，并总结了近似、稳定性和可扩展性方面的理论保证及开放问题。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为理解连续时间生成模型的共享结构和实际权衡提供了理论基础，揭示了不同方法之间的深层联系，并为后续理论分析和算法改进指明了方向。

Abstract: We survey continuous-time generative modeling methods based on transporting a simple reference distribution to a data distribution via stochastic or deterministic dynamics. We present a unified framework in which diffusion models, score-based generative models, and flow matching are instances of learning a time-dependent vector field that induces a family of marginals $(ρ_t)_{t \in [0,1]}$ governed by continuity and Fokker-Planck equations. Such a unified theory is timely because these methods are converging methodologically, yet fragmented notation and competing derivations continue to obscure their shared structure and the practical tradeoffs governing sampling, stability, and computation. Within this framework, we (i) derive reverse-time sampling for diffusion and score-based models as controlled stochastic dynamics, (ii) show that the probability flow ODE yields identical marginals and connects diffusion to likelihood-based normalizing flows, and (iii) interpret flow matching as direct regression of the velocity field under a chosen interpolation, clarifying when it coincides with or differs from score-based training. We compare objectives, sampling schemes, and discretization errors under unified notation, discuss connections to Schrodinger bridges and entropic optimal transport, and summarize theoretical guarantees and open problems on approximation, stability, and scalability.

</details>


### [104] [Breaking the Illusion: When Positive Meets Negative in Multimodal Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06679)
*Yubo Jiang,Yitong An,Xin Yang,Abudukelimu Wuerkaixi,Xuxin Cheng,Fengying Xie,Zhiguo Jiang,Cao Liu,Ke Zeng,Haopeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PND是一种无需训练的推理框架，通过对比正负两条路径的解码输出来增强视觉特征权重，有效减少VLM中的物体幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM存在物体幻觉问题，原因是过度依赖语言先验而忽视视觉证据。作者发现VLM中存在注意力不平衡，视觉特征被低估权重。

Method: 提出正负对比解码框架：正路径放大视觉证据，负路径构建反事实来惩罚先验主导的生成，通过对比两条路径的解码输出来引导生成更符合视觉事实的结果。

Result: 在POPE、MME和CHAIR基准上取得了最先进的性能，且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: PND通过直接干预解码过程有效增强了视觉保真度，是一种轻量且高效的幻觉缓解方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are frequently undermined by object hallucination, generating content that contradicts visual reality, due to an over-reliance on linguistic priors. We introduce Positive-and-Negative Decoding (PND), a training-free inference framework that intervenes directly in the decoding process to enforce visual fidelity. PND is motivated by our finding of an attention imbalance in VLMs, where visual features are under-weighted. Our framework introduces a dual-path contrast: a positive path that amplifies visual evidence and a negative path that constructs counterfactuals to penalize prior-dominant generation. By contrasting outputs from both paths during decoding, PND steers generation toward visually grounded results. Experiments on POPE, MME, and CHAIR demonstrate state-of-the-art performance without retraining.

</details>


### [105] [Physical Simulators as Do-Operators: Causal Discovery under Latent Confounders for AI-for-Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07467)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFM-SD方法，利用第一性原理物理模拟器作为Pearl干预演算中的do算子，同时处理潜在混淆变量和真实干预数据，在合成数据和真实科学数据上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（IGSP、DCDI、ENCO）假设因果充分性（无潜在混淆变量）且依赖合成模拟器中的虚拟干预，但在AI-for-Science场景（分子设计、材料科学）中，潜在混淆变量普遍存在，且真实干预（如基于物理的模拟）每个数据点需要数小时到数天，亟需能同时处理这两类问题的方法。

Method: 提出CFM-SD（Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data），将第一性原理物理模拟器作为Pearl干预演算中的do算子，利用因果流匹配框架同时处理潜在混淆变量和真实干预数据。

Result: 理论上，d变量因果结构在O(d)次单变量干预下可识别（物理可实现约束下的最小值）。内在评估（合成数据γ=0.2-0.8）：CFM-SD平均F1=0.800，基线方法F1=0.127-0.562。外在评估（真实科学数据）：分子毒性预测和电池电解质优化中实现57-58%的偏差减少。

Conclusion: CFM-SD在存在潜在混淆变量的真实科学场景中显著优于现有因果发现方法，并在实际应用中证明了其超越合成基准的实用价值。

Abstract: Existing interventional causal discovery methods -- IGSP, DCDI, ENCO -- assume causal sufficiency (no latent confounders) and rely on virtual interventions in synthetic simulators. In AI-for-Science settings such as molecular design and materials science, latent confounders are ubiquitous and real interventions (e.g., physics-based simulations) require hours to days per data point. We propose CFM-SD (Causal Flow Matching with Simulation Data), which uses first-principles physical simulators as do-operators in Pearl's interventional calculus to simultaneously handle latent confounders and real interventional data. Theoretically, $d$-variable causal structure is identifiable with $O(d)$ single-variable interventions -- the minimum under physical realizability constraints. In Intrinsic Evaluation on synthetic data ($γ=0.2$--$0.8$), CFM-SD achieves average F1$=0.800$ vs. F1$=0.127$--$0.562$ for all baselines. In Extrinsic Evaluation on real scientific data, CFM-SD achieves 57--58\% bias reduction in molecular toxicity prediction and battery electrolyte optimization, demonstrating practical value beyond synthetic benchmarks.

</details>


### [106] [On the Role of Strain and Vorticity in Numerical Integration Error for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06680)
*Chenxi Tao,Seung-Kyum Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了flow matching中速度场的应变率（S）和涡度（Ω）对积分误差的影响：应变控制指数级误差放大，涡度仅贡献线性局部截断误差；最优传输速度场无旋且物质导数为零，可实现二阶欧拉精度；基于此提出加权雅可比正则化（应变权重α、涡度权重β），在2D合成数据和CIFAR-10上验证有效，低NFE下误差降低2.7倍，FID提升14%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow matching的推理成本由积分步数（NFE）直接决定，而低NFE会导致较大的积分误差。本文旨在从理论上分析速度场的哪些性质控制积分误差，从而为设计更高效的生成模型提供理论指导。

Method: 将速度场的雅可比矩阵分解为对称部分S（应变率）和反对称部分Ω（涡度），理论证明应变通过对数范数控制指数级误差放大，涡度仅贡献线性局部截断误差。进一步分析最优传输速度场的性质（无旋、物质导数为零），提出加权雅可比正则化方法（应变权重α、涡度权重β）。

Result: 在2D合成数据上，NFE=5时积分误差降低高达2.7倍；在CIFAR-10上，轻量级微调使NFE=10时FID提升14%，同时保持高NFE质量。

Conclusion: 应变率和涡度在flow matching积分误差中扮演不同角色，正则化速度场雅可比矩阵可以显著降低低NFE下的推理误差，为加速生成模型推理提供了理论依据和实用方法。

Abstract: Flow matching generates data by integrating a learned velocity field, where the number of integration steps (NFE) directly determines inference cost. We analyze which properties of the velocity field govern integration error by decomposing the velocity Jacobian into its symmetric part S (strain rate) and antisymmetric part Omega (vorticity). We prove that strain and vorticity play different roles: strain controls exponential error amplification through the logarithmic norm, while vorticity contributes only linearly to the local truncation error. We further show that the optimal transport velocity field is irrotational and has zero material derivative, implying second-order Euler accuracy; for exact displacement interpolation, the associated Lagrangian particle dynamics are integrated exactly by Euler. Motivated by this analysis, we study weighted Jacobian regularization with strain weight alpha and vorticity weight beta. Experiments on 2D synthetic data confirm the main theoretical predictions, showing up to 2.7x lower integration error at NFE=5. Preliminary CIFAR-10 experiments show consistent trends, with a lightweight fine-tuning procedure improving FID by 14 percent at NFE=10 while preserving high-NFE quality.

</details>


### [107] [Same Brain, Different Prediction: How Preprocessing Choices Undermine EEG Decoding Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07212)
*Dengzhe Hou,Zihao Wu,Lingyu Jiang,Zirui Li,Fangzhou Lin,Kazunori D. Yamada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了EEG深度学习模型在不同预处理流程下的预测不稳定性（最高42%的试次级预测会因预处理改变而翻转），并提出了三种工具：Walsh-Hadamard分解实现高效优化、预处理不确定性（PU）作为互补诊断指标、以及NA-PGI正则化作为缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG深度学习模型通常仅在单一、未报告的预处理流程下训练和评估，忽略了预处理选择对预测结果的系统性影响，且标准不确定性方法无法量化这一维度上的变异性。

Method: 将预处理选择形式化为反事实干预空间；采用Walsh-Hadamard分解对2^7管道空间进行可加性分析；提出预处理不确定性（PU）作为试次级诊断指标；研究NA-PGI图结构正则化器作为缓解策略。

Result: 在跨4个范式的6个数据集上，仅改变预处理即可导致高达42%的试次级预测翻转；预处理敏感性在实践中近似可加；PU捕获了与模型置信度互补的不确定性维度。

Conclusion: EEG预测在预处理空间上存在显著不稳定性，所提出的工具可量化、分解并缓解此问题，但NA-PGI等缓解策略有其明确的适用条件。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a cornerstone of brain-computer interfaces and clinical neuroscience, yet deep learning models are typically trained and evaluated under a single, unreported preprocessing pipeline. We formalize preprocessing choices as a counterfactual intervention space and show that EEG predictions are surprisingly unstable under this space: across six datasets spanning four paradigms, up to 42% of trial-level predictions flip when only the preprocessing changes, a variability that standard uncertainty methods do not explicitly quantify because they condition on a fixed preprocessing pipeline. We provide three tools to make this instability measurable, decomposable, and reducible. First, a Walsh-Hadamard decomposition of the 2^7 pipeline space reveals that sensitivity is near-additive in practice under the binary intervention design, enabling efficient step-by-step optimization. Second, we introduce Preprocessing Uncertainty (PU), a per-trial diagnostic that captures a dimension of instability complementary to model-based confidence. Third, we study Normalized Adaptive PGI (NA-PGI), a graph-structured regularizer that exploits the compositional structure of preprocessing interventions as one mitigation strategy with clear scope conditions.

</details>


### [108] [A Hierarchical Ensemble Pipeline for Anomaly Detection in ESA Satellite Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06681)
*Lorenzo Riccardo Allegrini,Geremia Pompei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多变量遥测数据异常检测的分层集成流水线，结合形状基元和统计特征提取、逐通道建模、通道内堆叠及跨通道聚合，在ESA-ADB基准上展现了强大的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决卫星遥测数据中异常检测的挑战，特别是多变量时序数据中细微异常的检测问题。

Method: 分层集成流水线：包括形状基元与统计特征提取、逐通道建模、通道内堆叠（Intra-channel Stacking）以及跨通道聚合（Cross-channel Aggregation）。使用时间序列交叉验证和两级掩码策略防止信息泄漏。

Result: 在ESA-ADB挑战基准上取得了较强的泛化性能，有效检测到真实卫星遥测数据中的细微异常。

Conclusion: 分层建模方法在检测卫星遥测数据中的细微异常方面具有有效性，验证了层次化集成策略的泛化能力。

Abstract: A hierarchical ensemble pipeline is introduced to address anomaly detection in multivariate telemetry data provided by European Space Agency (ESA). The method integrates shapelet-based and statistical feature extraction, per-channel modeling, intra-channel stacking, and a final cross-channel aggregation. The pipeline is trained and validated using time-series cross-validation and two-level masking strategies to prevent information leakage. Results on the European Space Agency Anomaly Detection Benchmark (ESA-ADB) challenge demonstrate strong generalization, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical modeling in detecting subtle anomalies in realistic satellite telemetry.

</details>


### [109] [Toeplitz MLP Mixers are Low Complexity, Information-Rich Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06683)
*Benjamin L. Badger,Ethan Roland*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM)，一种用三角掩蔽Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制的子二次复杂度架构，在训练时达到O(dn log n)时间和O(dn)空间复杂度，推理时达到O(dn)时间和空间，并在信息保留和上下文学习方面优于可比架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的注意力机制具有二次时间/空间复杂度，限制了长序列场景下的计算效率。现有子二次复杂度架构通常需要复杂的输入调制或状态维护，作者希望探索更简洁高效的替代方案。

Method: 用三角掩蔽Toeplitz矩阵乘法替代注意力机制，在序列维度上进行操作。Toeplitz矩阵具有沿对角线恒定的结构，结合三角掩蔽实现因果性，通过快速傅里叶变换将矩阵乘法加速至O(n log n)。

Result: TMM在相同计算预算下获得更低的损失和更高的训练效率；在信息复制、检索和上下文学习基准上优于可比架构；从算子指标理论分析发现，训练的Toeplitz层在因果非可逆模型中反而更可能是可逆或近似可逆的。

Conclusion: TMM证明了简洁的子二次复杂度架构（无需复杂输入调制）也能在效率和信息保留上优于Transformer，且其可逆性分析为理解这类架构的行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models are in some respects limited by the quadratic time and space computational complexity of attention. We introduce the Toeplitz MLP Mixer (TMM), a transformer-like architecture that swaps attention for triangular-masked Toeplitz matrix multiplication over the sequence dimension resulting in $\mathcal{O} (dn \log n)$ time and $\mathcal O(dn)$ space complexity during training and $\mathcal O(dn)$ time and space at inference prefill. Despite the lack of sophisticated input modulation or state maintenance present in other sub-quadratic architectures, TMMs yield greater training efficiency in terms of loss achieved per compute and device memory. We demonstrate that TMMs are capable of retaining more input information resulting in improved copying ability, which we argue results from a lack of architectural biases. Consistent with higher input information retention, TMMs exhibit superior information retrieval and in-context learning benchmark accuracy compared to comparable architectures. We conclude with an analysis from the perspective of operator index theory and show that, counterintuitively, trained Toeplitz layers of causal non-invertible models are more likely to be invertible or nearly so than models that are actually invertible over their inputs.

</details>


### [110] [From Canopy to Collision: A Hybrid Predictive Framework for Identifying Risk Factors in Tree-Involved Traffic Crashes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06684)
*Abdul Azim,Ahmed Hossain,Soumyadip Maitra,Panick Kalambay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用2020-2023年CRSS数据，通过CatBoost分类模型、SHAP解释工具和二元逻辑回归，分析了树木碰撞事故的严重性风险因素。结果表明：未使用安全带是最强预测因子（未系安全带者重伤概率增加近3倍），车辆年龄、超速和驾驶员损伤也有显著影响，且光照条件与车辆年龄、超速与光照等交互作用显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木碰撞事故是路外碰撞的关键子集，常因高能量碰撞导致致命或严重伤害，但缺乏系统性的风险因素量化分析框架。

Method: 采用多步骤建模框架：(1) CatBoost机器学习分类模型识别关键因素；(2) SHAP工具量化边际效应；(3) 二元逻辑回归估计因子效应并验证SHAP重要性；(4) SHAP交互图分析关键因素联合效应。

Result: 未使用安全带是最强预测因子（未系安全带者严重伤害概率增加近3倍）；车辆年龄、超速和驾驶员损伤显著影响事故严重性；光照与车辆年龄、超速与光照、安全带使用与车辆年龄、道路表面与超速之间存在显著交互作用。

Conclusion: 研究为针对性的安全系统干预提供了关键见解，包括加强安全带执法、在低能见度条件下管理车速以及车辆 fleet 现代化更新。

Abstract: Tree-involved crashes represent a critical subset of run-off-road (ROR) collisions, often resulting in fatal or severe injuries due to high-energy impacts. This study develops a comprehensive analytical framework to identify and quantify risk factors contributing to crash severity in tree-involved collisions using the Crash Report Sampling System (CRSS) database spanning 2020-2023. The modeling framework follows a multi-step process. First, a machine learning based classification model (CatBoost) identifies key factors associated with binary crash injury severity (KA: fatal or incapacitating injury versus BC: non-incapacitating or possible injury). Second, SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) tool is used to quantify and visualize the marginal effects of top influential factors on crash severity. Third, a binary logistic regression model estimates factor effects and validates SHAP-derived importance measures. Finally, SHAP interaction plots examine the combined effects of key contributing factors. Results reveal restraint non-use as the most influential predictor, with unrestrained occupants nearly three times more likely to experience severe outcomes due to ejection risk. Vehicle age, speeding violations, and driver impairment demonstrate substantial effects, reflecting reduced crashworthiness, increased impact forces, and reduced control capabilities. Critical interactions emerge between lighting conditions and vehicle age, speeding and lighting conditions, restraint use and vehicle age, and road surface and speeding, demonstrating additive risk effects with specific interactions. These findings provide critical insights for targeted safe system-based interventions, including enhanced seat belt enforcement, speed management in reduced visibility conditions, and vehicle fleet modernization.

</details>


### [111] [Conformal Agent Error Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06788)
*Naihe Feng,Yi Sui,Shiyi Hou,Ga Wu,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于保形预测（CP）的框架，用于多智能体系统（MAS）中的错误归因。该方法通过针对智能体轨迹这类序列数据设计的过滤式保形预测算法，生成连续的预测集，从而精确定位错误发生的位置，并支持MAS自动回滚恢复。该方法具有模型无关性，并提供有限样本、无分布假设的覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统（MAS）失败时，识别决定性错误发生的第一个步骤是自动化恢复到先前状态的关键。但由于基于大语言模型的MAS会生成长交互轨迹，错误归因仍是一个基本挑战。

Method: 提出基于保形预测（CP）的错误归因框架，该框架提供有限样本、无分布假设的覆盖保证。针对智能体轨迹这类序列数据，设计了基于过滤式保形预测的新算法，预测结果为连续的序列（contiguous sequences），而非传统的不连续集合，以支持高效恢复和调试。

Result: 在多种智能体和数据集上验证了理论保证，证明错误可以被精确定位；利用预测集回滚MAS，智能体能够自动纠正自身错误。该方法具有模型无关性。
