Conclusion: 该框架为MAS错误归因提供了一种原则性的不确定性层，能够有效地隔离错误并支持自动化恢复。代码已开源。

Abstract: When multi-agent systems (MAS) fail, identifying where the decisive error occurred is the first step for automated recovery to an earlier state. Error attribution remains a fundamental challenge due to the long interaction traces that large language model-based MAS generate. This paper presents a framework for error attribution based on conformal prediction (CP) which provides finite-sample, distribution-free coverage guarantees. We introduce new algorithms for filtration-based CP designed for sequential data such as agent trajectories. Unlike existing CP algorithms, our approach predicts sets that are contiguous sequences to enable efficient recovery and debugging. We verify our theoretical guarantees on a variety of agents and datasets, show that errors can be precisely isolated, then use prediction sets to rollback MAS to correct their own errors. Our overall approach is model-agnostic, and offers a principled uncertainty layer for MAS error attribution. We release code at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/conformal-agent-error-attribution.

</details>


### [112] [Robustness of Refugee-Matching Gains to Off-Policy Evaluation Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06686)
*Kirk Bansak,Elisabeth Paulson,Dominik Rothenhäusler,Jeremy Ferwerda,Jens Hainmueller,Michael Hotard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了美国难民匹配反事实影响评估的稳定性，使用多种离线策略评估方法（IPW、AIPW及其变体）进行测试，结果在统计显著性和量级上与Bansak等人（2018）的原研究保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证难民匹配政策在反事实影响评估中的结果稳健性，确认Bansak等人（2018）提出的难民匹配提升难民安置效果的发现是否可靠。

Method: 采用多种离线策略评估方法，包括逆概率加权（IPW）和增强逆概率加权（AIPW）的多个变体，同时考虑替代建模架构和不同的分配程序。

Result: 在所有测试场景中，影响估计量级保持稳定，且多数情形下统计显著；结果与Bansak等人（2018）的原始结果一致。

Conclusion: 难民匹配的反事实影响评估结果具有高度稳健性，该方法的有效性得到进一步确认。

Abstract: Previous research has investigated the potential of refugee matching for boosting refugee outcomes, first considered by Bansak et al. (2018). This paper demonstrates the stability of counterfactual impact evaluation results in the context of refugee matching in the United States using a range of off-policy evaluation methods. In order to estimate counterfactual impact and test the robustness of our results, we employ several evaluation methods, including inverse probability weighting (IPW) and multiple variants of augmented inverse probability weighting (AIPW). We also consider various modifications, including alternative modeling architectures and different assignment procedures. The impact estimates remain consistent in magnitude in all scenarios as well as statistically significant in most cases. Furthermore, the estimates are also consistent with the results originally presented in Bansak et al. (2018).

</details>


### [113] [MAGIQ: A Post-Quantum Multi-Agentic AI Governance System with Provable Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06933)
*Sepideh Avizeh,Tushin Mallick,Alina Oprea,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Reihaneh Safavi-Naini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAGIQ是一个面向多智能体AI系统的策略定义与执行框架，利用新型高效抗量子密码协议，支持丰富的通信与访问控制策略、会话级执行、消息归因，并通过UC框架证明其安全性，是首个面向智能体AI系统的后量子安全方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI系统的安全治理架构缺失，现有系统无法确保智能体遵循所有者策略并为其交互行为负责；同时，量子计算威胁下传统公钥密码（RSA、DH、ECC）即将被NIST废弃（2030年起逐步淘汰），亟需设计抗量子安全机制。

Method: 提出MAGIQ框架，采用后量子密码原语实现策略定义与执行；支持智能体间及一对多会话的策略预算；通过消息归因实现智能体对其用户的可问责性；使用通用可组合（UC）框架进行形式化建模与安全性证明。

Result: MAGIQ在计算与通信开销上优于当前最先进的智能体AI框架SAGA，并提供了经过UC框架形式化验证的正确性与安全保证。

Conclusion: MAGIQ是迈向智能体AI系统后量子安全解决方案的第一步，为多智能体系统的策略治理与抗量子安全提供了理论框架与可行实现路径。

Abstract: Our computing ecosystem is being transformed by two emerging paradigms: the increased deployment of agentic AI systems and advancements in quantum computing. With respect to agentic AI systems, one of the most critical problems is creating secure governing architectures that ensure agents follow their owners' communication and interaction policies and can be held accountable for the messages they exchange with other agents. With respect to quantum computing, existing systems must be retrofitted and new cryptographic mechanisms must be designed to ensure long-term security and quantum resistance. In fact, NIST recommends that standard public-key cryptographic algorithms, including RSA, Diffie-Hellman (DH), and elliptic-curve constructions (ECC), be deprecated starting in 2030 and disallowed after 2035.
  In this paper, we present MAGIQ, a framework for policy definition and enforcement in multi-agent AI systems using novel, highly efficient, quantum-resistant cryptographic protocols with proven security guarantees. MAGIQ (i) allows users to define rich communication and access-control policy budgets for agent-to-agent sessions and tasks, including global budgets for one-to-many agent sessions; (ii) enforces such policies using post-quantum cryptographic primitives; (iii) supports session-based enforcement of policies for agent-to-agent and one-to-many agent sessions; and (iv) provides accountability of agents to their users through message attribution. We formally model and prove the correctness and security of the system using the Universal Composability (UC) framework. We evaluate the computation and communication overhead of our framework and compare it with the state-of-the-art agentic AI framework SAGA. MAGIQ is a first step toward post-quantum-secure solutions for agentic AI systems.

</details>


### [114] [Conditional generation of antibody sequences with classifier-guided germline-absorbing discrete diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06720)
*Justin Sanders,Luca Giancardo,Lan Guo,Yue Zhao,Kemal Sonmez,Nina Cheng,Melih Yilmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出种系吸收扩散（germline absorbing diffusion），通过将种系序列而非掩码序列作为吸收态来微调离散扩散模型，解决了抗体设计中pLM记忆种系序列和缺乏灵活条件生成的问题，将非种系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，并在疏水性和结合亲和力条件生成任务上显著优于EvoProtGrad。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有pLM在抗体序列设计中存在两个关键局限：1）倾向于记忆种系序列而非建模生物学上有意义的体细胞变异；2）对灵活的分类器引导条件生成支持有限。

Method: 1) 采用离散扩散微调（discrete diffusion fine-tuning）实现强语言建模性能并支持任意分类器条件生成；2) 提出种系吸收扩散（germline absorbing diffusion），将种系序列作为离散扩散噪声过程的吸收态，使模型只学习从种系到观测序列的轨迹，排除遗传变异和V(D)J重组统计信息，减轻种系偏差。

Result: 非种系残基预测准确率从26%提升至46%，接近生物学变异设定的理论上限。在改善疏水性和预测结合亲和力的条件生成任务上，模型在类别遵循度和样本质量之间取得更优权衡，显著优于EvoProtGrad。

Conclusion: 种系吸收扩散通过引入生物学先验的噪声过程设计，有效解决了抗体pLM的种系记忆问题，并实现了更优的条件生成性能，为抗体计算设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Antibody therapeutics are among the most successful modern medicines, yet computationally designing antibodies with desirable binding and developability properties remains challenging. While protein language models (pLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for antibody sequence design, existing approaches largely suffer from two key limitations: they predominantly memorize germline sequences rather than modeling biologically meaningful somatic variation, and they offer limited support for flexible classifier-guided conditional generation. We address these challenges through two primary contributions. First, we demonstrate that discrete diffusion fine-tuning achieves strong language modeling performance on antibody sequences while allowing for generation conditioned on any off-the-shelf classifier. Second, we introduce germline absorbing diffusion, a novel modification of the discrete diffusion noise process in which the germline sequence - rather than a masked sequence - serves as the absorbing state. This biologically motivated inductive bias restricts the model to learning the trajectory from germline to observed sequence, effectively excluding genetic variation and V(D)J recombination statistics from the learned distribution and dramatically mitigating germline bias. We show that germline diffusion improves non-germline residue prediction accuracy from 26 percent to 46 percent, approaching the theoretical upper bound set by true biological variability. We then demonstrate the utility of our germline diffusion model on the conditional generation tasks of sampling antibodies with improved hydrophobicity and predicted binding affinity. On both tasks our model shows an improved tradeoff between class adherence and sample quality, significantly outperforming EvoProtGrad, a popular strategy to sample from pLMs with gradient-based discrete Markov Chain Monte Carlo.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Material-Aware Hamiltonian Risk Fields for Safe Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07038)
*Aditya Sai Ellendula,Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于端口-哈密顿导航策略的风险感知导航方法，通过引入上下文能量项实现选择性风险规避：仅在局部场景中存在低风险可行机动时激活避让力，否则抑制横向运动。CVaR尾部风险目标优化罕见但关键的过渡。实验验证了该选择性在多种场景中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风险感知导航策略往往缺乏选择性，即在任何场景下都试图规避风险，导致在无安全替代方案时产生不安全或无效的机动行为。需要一种能够根据局部场景判断是否具备低风险可行机动，从而有选择地激活或抑制避让行为的导航策略。

Method: 在端口-哈密顿导航策略基础上添加一个上下文能量项，产生具有可证伪选择性特征的习得力通道。当局部风险场中存在可行低风险方向时，上下文力朝该方向激活；当逃生路径被阻塞或不可用时，路径感知门控机制抑制横向力。使用CVaR尾部风险目标使梯度更新聚焦于罕见但关键的风险过渡。

Result: 在延迟必需逃生基准中，路径感知CVaR将过早力激活从0.950降至0.180(DWA对比)，成功率从0.480提升至0.810且零重规划。RELLIS-3D真实越野地形中，正确激活率0.837，错误激活率0.114（标量风险梯度为0.378/0.752）。DFC2018静态语义地图中，灾难性失败从0.60降至0.10，振荡降低90.7%。高速场景中，车道逃生可行时碰撞率从100%降至0%；无逃生时横向机动被抑制。

Conclusion: 选择性属性源自上下文能量的梯度结构，而非训练时的调参。该工作证明了通过精心设计的能量项可以实现具有可验证选择性特征的风险感知导航，避免在无安全替代方案时产生不安全行为。

Abstract: Risk-aware navigation should be selective: a policy should expose evasive degrees of freedom only when the local scene admits a lower-risk feasible maneuver, and suppress them when no safer alternative exists. We show that adding one context-energy term to a port-Hamiltonian navigation policy produces a learned force channel with exactly this falsifiable signature. When the local risk field contains a feasible lower-risk direction, the induced context force activates toward it; when the apparent escape is blocked or not yet available, a route-aware gate suppresses lateral force rather than hallucinating an unsafe maneuver. A CVaR tail-risk objective focuses gradient updates on rare but consequential risk transitions. We validate the selectivity signature across four settings. In the primary delayed-required-escape benchmark, route-aware CVaR reduces premature force activation from 0.950 to 0.180 versus DWA while raising success from 0.480 to 0.810 with zero replans. On real off-road terrain (RELLIS-3D), route-aware enrichment achieves correct activation rate 0.837 and false activation rate 0.114, compared to 0.378/0.752 for scalar risk gradients. On static semantic maps (DFC2018), enrichment reduces catastrophic failure from 0.60 to 0.10 and oscillation by 90.7% while preserving path efficiency. In highway traffic, collisions drop from 100% to 0% when a lane escape is feasible; when no escape exists, the policy suppresses the lateral maneuver. The selectivity property follows from the gradient structure of the context energy rather than from training-time tuning.

</details>


### [116] [Enabling Unsupervised Training of Deep EEG Denoisers With Intelligent Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06724)
*Qiyu Rao,Haozhe Tian,Homayoun Hamedmoghadam,Danilo Mandic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出智能分区自监督去噪方法(iPSD)，通过将输入脑电片段划分为具有相同底层信号的独立噪声实现，无需干净参考即可训练深度学习去噪器，在可穿戴脑电去噪中达到SOTA性能，尤其在极低信噪比(-10dB)和挑战性伪影下表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴脑电图去噪极具挑战性，因为神经活动微弱且与频谱重叠的噪声伪影不可分离。传统信号处理方法依赖固定或启发式规则，无法处理时变伪影；深度学习方法需要无法获取的无伪影脑电作为训练数据。因此需要一种无需干净参考信号的深度去噪方法。

Method: 提出iPSD方法，通过智能分区将输入脑电片段划分为具有相同底层信号但噪声相互独立的多个子实现，从而实现对深度学习去噪器的自监督训练，支持零样本场景下仅用单个待去噪片段即可完成训练。

Result: 通过大量实验验证，包括在耳内传感器可穿戴脑电上的验证，iPSD达到SOTA性能，尤其在极低信噪比(低至-10dB)和挑战性伪影(如EMG)下，频谱保真度比竞争基线高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: iPSD通过智能分区策略有效解决了可穿戴脑电去噪中缺乏干净参考信号的难题，实现了无需干净数据的自监督深度去噪，在极端噪声条件下仍保持卓越性能。

Abstract: Denoising wearable electroencephalogram (EEG) is inherently challenging since neural activity is not only subtle but also inseparable from spectrally overlapping noise artifacts. Classical signal processing methods, relying on fixed or heuristic rules, cannot handle the time-varying pervasive artifacts in wearable EEGs. Deep learning methods, on the other hand, show promise in decomposition-free EEG denoising using highly expressive neural networks, but the training requires artifact-free EEG, which is inherently unobtainable. To address this, we propose Intelligent Partitioning for Self-supervised Denoising (iPSD). Our method eliminates the need for clean references by learning to partition an input EEG segment into independent noisy realizations with the same underlying signal. This enables self-supervision of deep learning denoisers, even in zero-shot settings where only a single EEG segment to be denoised is available. We validate iPSD through extensive experiments, including validations on wearable EEG from in-ear sensors. The results show that iPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance, most notably under extremely low signal-to-noise ratios (down to -10 dB) and challenging artifacts (e.g., EMG), with spectral fidelity orders of magnitude higher than competitive baselines.

</details>


### [117] [The E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer: Adaptive Geodesic Operations with Guaranteed Orthogonality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06729)
*Arash Shahmansoori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EΔ-MHC-Geo Transformer，通过数据依赖的Cayley旋转实现无条件正交残差连接，并引入混合机制处理反射（特征值-1情况），在长程稳定性、近π旋转损失、范数保持和反射对齐方面显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DDL的Householder算子仅在β∈{0,2}时正交，无法对所有输入保持正交性；且Cayley变换天然排除特征值-1（反射），无法访问O(n)的整个连通分支。现有JPmHC的有限Cayley残差混合器缺乏反射分支，限制了模型表达能力。

Method: (1) 数据依赖Cayley旋转: Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))，对所有β和输入保持正交；(2) EΔ-MHC-Geo Hybrid: 通过可学习门控γ(X)组合Cayley旋转Q和Householder反射H₂；(3) 中点坍缩正则化项4γ(1-γ)鼓励边界门控决策。

Result: 在约1.79M参数下：长程稳定性比JPmHC提升1.9倍、比GPT提升3.8倍；近π旋转损失比JPmHC提升4.5倍；范数保持平均偏差0.001；反射余弦对齐达0.96；仅用JPmHC 67%的层数。

Conclusion: 混合Cayley旋转与Householder反射的架构能同时访问O(n)的两个连通分支（旋转和反射），弥补了纯Cayley方法无法处理特征值-1的固有缺陷，是实现完全正交残差连接的有效方案。

Abstract: We present the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Transformer, a novel architecture that unifies Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC), Deep Delta Learning (DDL), and the Cayley transform to obtain input-adaptive, unconditionally orthogonal residual connections. Unlike DDL, whose Householder operator is orthogonal only at $β\in \{0,2\}$, our Data-Dependent Cayley rotation $Q(x)=(I+(β/2)A(x))^{-1}(I-(β/2)A(x))$ preserves orthogonality for all $β$ and all inputs. To handle negation, an eigenvalue $-1$ case that Cayley provably excludes, we introduce the E$Δ$-MHC-Geo Hybrid, which combines Cayley rotation with Householder reflection via a learned operator-selection gate $X'=γ(X)Q(X)X+(1-γ(X))H_2(X)X$. A midpoint-collapse regularizer, $4γ(1-γ)$, encourages boundary gate decisions, where each selected component is orthogonal. In matched-parameter comparisons, with approximately 1.79M parameters per model and mean +/- standard deviation over 3 seeds, against four baselines including the concurrent JPmHC, E$Δ$-MHC-Geo achieves the best long-horizon stability, 1.9x over JPmHC and 3.8x over GPT; the best near-$π$ rotation loss, 4.5x over JPmHC on single-plane; strong norm preservation, with 0.001 mean deviation; and 0.96 negation cosine alignment in a diagnostic reflection probe, all with 33% fewer layers. While JPmHC's wider representation excels on pure rotation, its finite Cayley residual mixer excludes an exact $λ=-1$ operator and has no reflection branch, motivating our hybrid approach for accessing both connected components of $O(n)$.

</details>


### [118] [Semantic State Abstraction Interfaces for LLM-Augmented Portfolio Decisions: Multi-Axis News Decomposition and RL Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06730)
*Likhita Yerra,Remi Uttejitha Allam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了语义状态抽象接口（SSAI），一种将稀疏非结构化文本映射到K个可审计命名坐标的方法模板，旨在分离顺序决策系统中的表征假设与优化方差。在美股数据上实例化K=4轴（情感、风险、信心、波动率预测），发现四因子组合虽达到307.2%累计收益和1.067夏普比，但统计上脆弱，无法通过覆盖分层控制检验，且在高交易成本下表现反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将文本信号引入决策系统时，难以区分性能提升是来自更好的表征假设还是优化方差。缺乏一个可审计、可分离的模板来系统性地诊断这一问题。

Method: 提出SSAI框架，定义K个具有中立默认值的可命名语义坐标轴，将文本映射为结构化状态表示。在K=4时实例化为情感、风险、信心、波动率预测四个因子，分别构建直接因子组合、岭回归预测器和共享固定φ的强化学习智能体（DP-PPO、SAC），并进行覆盖分层控制和统计稳健性检验。

Result: 四因子组合累计收益307.2%（夏普1.067），高于买入持有基准（243.6%），但未能通过覆盖分层控制检验；在≥0.2%交易成本下表现反转；统计上脆弱，不及情感单因子基线、PC1主成分组合和FinBERT组合。岭回归和RL模块有效地展示了表征与优化效应的分离诊断。

Conclusion: SSAI应定位为一种可复用的解释性-性能诊断协议和流程模板，用于稀疏文本决策系统，而非声称其优于更密集的替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce Semantic State Abstraction Interfaces (SSAI): a methodological template for mapping sparse unstructured text into $K$ auditable, named coordinates with neutral defaults on no-news days, designed to separate representation hypotheses from optimisation variance in sequential decision systems. Our contribution is the framework and its evaluation protocol, not a claim that SSAI outperforms denser alternatives.
  We instantiate SSAI with $K=4$ axes (sentiment, risk, confidence, volatility forecast) on a US-equity panel (30 NASDAQ-100 names, FNSPID news, 2019--2023 test), and evaluate it across direct factor portfolios, supervised ridge forecasters, and RL agents (DP-PPO, SAC) that share the same fixed $φ$. The four-factor factor portfolio reaches 307.2% cumulative return and Sharpe 1.067, but apparent gains versus buy-and-hold (243.6%) fail coverage-stratified controls, reverse at $\geq 0.2$% costs, and are statistically fragile versus a sentiment-only baseline; a PC1 composite and a FinBERT portfolio baseline are stronger ranking signals in this setting. Ridge and RL blocks diagnose representation versus optimiser effects. We position SSAI as an interpretability-performance diagnostic and reusable protocol for sparse-text decision systems.

</details>


### [119] [On Training in Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06732)
*Nadav Timor,Ravid Shwartz-Ziv,Micah Goldblum,Yann LeCun,David Harel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于模型强化学习中，在想象轨迹上训练策略时，动力学模型和奖励模型的误差如何影响回报与策略优化。首先扩展了Asadi等人(2018)的分析至含学习奖励模型的MDP，推导了在幂律缩放假设下最小化回报误差边界的最优样本分配（动力学样本与奖励样本之比），并指出学习动力学、奖励和策略的更小Lipschitz常数可作为收紧该边界的表示理想性质，同时将此视角与Wang等人(2026)的时间拉直目标联系起来。其次，考察了REINFORCE策略优化对噪声奖励的容忍性，证明零均值奖励噪声不使梯度估计产生偏差，仅增加一个随rollout数量递减的方差项，从而引入实际权衡：给定固定预算，应购买更多rollout但奖励更嘈杂廉价，还是更少rollout但奖励更精确昂贵？本文将这一选择简化为一个一维优化问题并刻画了最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型强化学习方法在学习到的动力学模型上生成想象轨迹并用学习到的奖励模型评分，但训练过程中不查询真实环境。这种范式下，动力学模型和奖励模型的误差如何影响最终回报和策略优化效果尚需定量分析。

Method: 1. 将Asadi等人(2018)的分析扩展至含学习奖励模型的MDP，在幂律缩放假设下推导回报误差边界及最优样本分配比；2. 分析REINFORCE算法在噪声奖励下的梯度偏差与方差性质；3. 将预算分配问题简化为单变量优化问题。

Result: 推导了最优样本分配比（动力学样本与奖励样本之比）；发现更小的Lipschitz常数能收紧回报误差边界；证明零均值奖励噪声不偏梯度估计，方差随rollout数递减；将预算权衡简化为可解析的一维优化问题。

Conclusion: 本文为基于模型强化学习中动力学与奖励模型的误差影响提供了理论定量刻画，揭示了样本分配、表示性质与预算权衡之间的内在联系，为实际算法设计提供了指导原则。

Abstract: State-of-the-art model-based reinforcement learning methods train policies on imagined rollouts. These rollouts are trajectories generated by a learned dynamics model and are scored by a learned reward model, but without querying the true environment during policy updates. We study this training paradigm by quantifying how errors in learned dynamics and reward models affect returns and policy optimization. First, we extend the analysis of Asadi et al. (2018) to MDPs with learned reward models, and derive the optimal sample allocation--the ratio of dynamics samples to reward samples that minimizes a bound on return error under power-law scaling assumptions. We identify lower Lipschitz constants of the learned dynamics, reward, and policy as a representation desideratum that tightens this bound, and we connect this perspective to the temporal-straightening objective of Wang et al. (2026). Second, we examine how policy optimization with REINFORCE tolerates noisy rewards, which are often cheaper to obtain. We show that zero-mean reward noise leaves the gradient estimator unbiased and adds at most a variance term that decreases with the number of rollouts. This introduces a practical tradeoff: given a fixed budget, should one buy more rollouts with cheaper but noisier rewards, or fewer rollouts with more expensive but less noisy rewards? We reduce this choice to a one-dimensional optimization problem and characterize the optimum.

</details>


### [120] [Beyond Factor Aggregation: Gauge-Aware Low-Rank Server Representations for Federated LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06733)
*Jinqian Chen,Chang Liu,Jihua Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有联邦LoRA方法因低秩分解的规范等价性存在聚合歧义，本文提出规范感知的服务器表示GLoRA，通过估计共识更新子空间并在共享参考坐标系中聚合客户端更新，同时支持异构秩的适配器实例化，实验表明其在数据、资源、任务异质性下显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接平均LoRA因子是表示依赖的：相同的内在更新有无穷多种规范等价的分解形式，因子级聚合会因任意坐标选择而改变，而底层更新保持不变，这揭示了现有联邦LoRA聚合规则中的语义不匹配问题。

Method: GLoRA提出规范感知的服务器表示：不直接聚合原始因子，而是从客户端投影矩阵中估计共识更新子空间，在共享参考坐标系中聚合客户端更新，从而完全以低秩形式表示语义更新聚合；同时提供秩兼容读出机制，从同一服务器状态实例化不同秩的适配器，无需重建稠密更新。

Result: 在GLUE和SuperNI上的实验表明，GLoRA在数据、资源和任务异质性（包括异构客户端秩、稀疏参与、更大骨干模型和未见任务评估）下持续优于联邦LoRA基线，并实现了良好的效率-性能权衡。

Conclusion: 有效的联邦LoRA不仅需要平均低秩因子，还需要定义语义上有意义的服务器端聚合表示。

Abstract: Federated LoRA enables parameter-efficient adaptation of large language models under decentralized data and limited client resources.However, directly averaging LoRA factors is representation-dependent: the same intrinsic update admits infinitely many gauge-equivalent factorizations, so factor-level aggregation can change under arbitrary coordinate choices while the underlying update remains unchanged. This reveals a semantic mismatch in existing federated LoRA aggregation rules. We propose \textbf{GLoRA}, a gauge-aware server representation for federated LoRA.Instead of aggregating raw factors, GLoRA estimates a consensus update subspace from client projectors and aggregates client updates in shared reference coordinates, thereby representing semantic update aggregation entirely in low-rank form. To support heterogeneous client capacities, GLoRA further provides a rank-compatible readout that instantiates adapters of different ranks from the same server state without dense update reconstruction. Experiments on GLUE and SuperNI show that GLoRA consistently outperforms federated LoRA baselines under data, resource, and task heterogeneity, including heterogeneous client ranks, sparse participation, larger backbones, and unseen-task evaluation. GLoRA also achieves a favorable efficiency--performance trade-off, suggesting that effective federated LoRA requires not merely averaging low-rank factors, but defining a semantically meaningful server-side representation for aggregation.

</details>


### [121] [Gated QKAN-FWP: Scalable Quantum-inspired Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06734)
*Kuo-Chung Peng,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Jiun-Cheng Jiang,Chen-Yu Liu,En-Jui Kuo,Yun-Yuan Wang,Prayag Tiwari,Andrea Ceschini,Chi-Sheng Chen,Yu-Chao Hsu,Chun-Hua Lin,Tai-Yue Li,Antonello Rosato,Massimo Panella,Simon See,Saif Al-Kuwari,Kuan-Cheng Chen,Nan-Yow Chen,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出门控QKAN-FWP框架，将快速权重编程与量子启发式KAN结合，使用单量子比特数据重上传电路作为非线性激活，通过标量门控稳定参数演化。在长时序列预测中，12.5k参数的模型超越13倍参数的经典RNN/LSTM基线，并在真实量子处理器上验证了NISQ兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子快速权重编程器依赖多量子比特架构，在NISQ设备上难以扩展且经典模拟成本高昂，亟需一种更轻量、可扩展的量子启发式序列建模方案。

Method: 1) 提出DARUAN单量子比特数据重上传激活单元；2) 引入量子启发式KAN作为快速权重框架；3) 设计标量门控快速权重更新规则，包含自适应记忆核、几何有界性和可并行梯度路径的理论分析。

Result: 在528→132时间步的长时预测中，12.5k参数模型在MSE、峰值幅度误差和峰值时序误差上优于LSTM(25.9k-89.1k)、WaveNet-LSTM(167k)、Vanilla RNN(11.5k)和Echo State Network(132k)；在IonQ和IBM量子处理器上，1024次测量下相对MSE仅偏离无噪声模拟器0.1%。

Conclusion: 门控QKAN-FWP是一种可扩展、参数高效且NISQ兼容的量子启发式序列建模方法，在长时域预测任务中具有实际优势。

Abstract: Fast Weight Programmers (FWPs) encode temporal dependencies through dynamically updated parameters rather than recurrent hidden states. Quantum FWPs (QFWPs) extend this idea with variational quantum circuits (VQCs), but existing implementations rely on multi-qubit architectures that are difficult to scale on noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) devices and expensive to simulate classically. We propose gated QKAN-FWP, a fast-weight framework that integrates FWP with Quantum-inspired Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (QKAN) using single-qubit data re-uploading circuits as learnable nonlinear activation, known as DatA Re-Uploading ActivatioN (DARUAN). We further introduce a scalar-gated fast-weight update rule that stabilizes parameter evolution, supported by a theoretical analysis of its adaptive memory kernel, geometric boundedness, and parallelizable gradient paths. We evaluate the framework across time-series benchmarks, MiniGrid reinforcement learning, and highlight real-world solar cycle forecasting as our main practical result. In the long-horizon setting with 528-month input window and 132-month forecast horizon, our 12.5k-parameter model achieves lower scaled Mean Square Error (MSE), peak amplitude error, and peak timing error than a suite of classical recurrent baselines with up to 13x more parameters, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) networks (25.9k-89.1k parameters), WaveNet-LSTM (167k), Vanilla recurrent neural network (11.5k), and a Modified Echo State Network (132k). To validate NISQ compatibility, we further deploy the trained fast programmer on IonQ and IBM Quantum processors, recovering forecasting accuracy within 0.1% relative MSE of the noiseless simulator at 1024 shots. These results position gated QKAN-FWP as a scalable, parameter-efficient, and NISQ-compatible approach to quantum-inspired sequence modeling.

</details>


### [122] [Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Network (GeoKAN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06740)
*Abhijit Sen,Bikram Keshari Parida,Giridas Maiti,Mahima Arya,Denys I. Bondar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoKAN是一种几何感知的KAN模型族，通过学习对角黎曼度量对输入空间进行几何变形，在自适应坐标下进行函数逼近，特别适合处理科学机器学习中的尖锐、刚性、局部化和强非均匀问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KAN模型在固定欧几里得输入坐标下进行近似，缺乏几何先验，难以有效处理函数变化剧烈或非均匀的区域。引入几何感知的坐标变换，使模型能够自适应分配表示分辨率，将计算资源集中在最需要的区域。

Method: 通过学习一个对角黎曼度量来扭曲输入空间，在基展开和特征混合之前对输入进行几何变形。提出了三个主要变体：GeoKAN-NNMetric、GeoKAN-γ和LM-KAN，其中LM-KAN又包含RBF、小波和傅里叶三种基函数版本。该度量提供局部长度缩放和体积扭曲的几何归纳偏置，在物理信息学习中还会影响模型所感知的微分结构。

Result: GeoKAN通过拉伸变化剧烈的区域、压缩平滑区域，实现了任务相关的表示分辨率重分配，能够将模型容量集中在最需要的地方，适用于尖锐、刚性、局部化和强非均匀的科学机器学习与微分方程问题。

Conclusion: GeoKAN通过几何感知的坐标变换为KAN模型引入了几何归纳偏置，使其在科学机器学习和微分方程求解等具有非均匀特性的任务中具有显著优势。

Abstract: We introduce Geometric Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (GeoKANs), a family of geometry-aware KAN-type models in which approximation is carried out in learned, geometry-adapted coordinates rather than in fixed Euclidean input coordinates. GeoKAN achieves this by learning a diagonal Riemannian metric that warps the input before basis expansion and feature mixing. The learned metric provides a geometric inductive bias through local length scaling and volume distortion, and in physics-informed settings it also affects the differential structure seen by the model. Within this framework, we develop three main variants, namely GeoKAN-NNMetric, GeoKAN-$γ$, and LM-KAN. For LM-KAN, we further consider three basis-specific versions, LM-KAN-RBF, LM-KAN-Wav, and LM-KAN-Fourier. These variants allow us to study geometry-aware KAN models both as general function approximators and as surrogates in physics-informed learning. By stretching regions with rapid variation and compressing smoother regions, GeoKAN reallocates representational resolution in a task-dependent manner, allowing the model to place capacity where it is most needed. As a result, GeoKAN is well suited to sharp, stiff, localized, and strongly non-uniform regimes arising in scientific machine learning and differential-equation problems.

</details>


### [123] [A Closed-Form Upper Bound for Admissible Learning-Rate Steps in Belief-Space Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06741)
*Zixi Li,Youzhen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将学习率步长从超参数重新定义为基于KL/Bregman几何的局部信念空间可容许步长公式，证明可容许步长上界可通过投影前向步长的压缩性精确计算而非经验调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将学习率步长视为需要手动调优的超参数，缺乏理论指导，作者希望从几何视角建立步长选择的数学基础。

Method: 将参数更新建模为概率单纯形上的投影前向步长，利用KL散度/Bregman散度的自然几何结构分析步长的可容许性，将可容许性等价于该几何下的压缩性条件。

Result: 推导出可容许步长的上界是一个封闭形式的公式，而非经验调参的口号式指导。

Conclusion: 学习率步长选择可从信念空间的局部几何计算中获得理论依据，为优化算法的步长设定提供可计算的边界条件。

Abstract: Learning-rate steps are usually treated as hyperparameters. This paper isolates a local beliefspace calculation: when an update is modeled as a projected forward step on the probability simplex, admissibility means contractivity in the natural KL/Bregman geometry. Under this model, the upper bound of an admissible step is not a tuning slogan but a formula.

</details>


### [124] [Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06755)
*Ismam Nur Swapnil,Aranya Saha,Tanvir Ahmed Khan,Mohammad Ariful Haque,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GXPO提出了一种基于梯度外推的策略更新规则，仅用三次反向传播即可近似多步前瞻，在数学推理任务上相比GRPO提升1.65-5.00个点的pass@1，并实现最高4倍步数加速和2.33倍墙钟时间加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GRPO式训练仅使用当前步更新模型，而完整多步前瞻虽能提供更优更新方向但需要大量反向传播，计算成本过高。GXPO旨在以极低的计算开销近似多步前瞻的收益。

Method: GXPO在活跃阶段仅用三次反向传播：先执行两次快速优化步骤并测量梯度变化，预测虚拟的K步前瞻点，将策略向该点部分移动，然后在新位置用真实梯度进行修正更新。当前瞻信号不稳定时自动回退到标准单步GRPO。该方法复用同一批rollout、奖励、优势和GRPO损失，无需在前瞻点处重新采样或计算奖励。

Result: 在Qwen2.5和Llama数学推理实验上，GXPO相比GRPO平均提升pass@1达1.65-5.00个点，相比最强SFPO设置提升0.14-1.28个点；达到GRPO峰值精度时实现最高4倍步数加速、2.33倍墙钟时间加速和1.33倍反向传播加速。

Conclusion: GXPO是一种即插即用的高效策略更新方法，在保持活跃阶段仅三次反向传播的固定成本下，显著提升了推理型大语言模型的训练效率和最终性能，且具有理论支撑。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is widely used to improve the reasoning ability of large language models, especially when answers can be automatically checked. Standard GRPO-style training updates the model using only the current step, while full multi-step lookahead can give a better update direction but is too expensive because it needs many backward passes. We propose Gradient Extrapolation-Based Policy Optimization (GXPO), a plug-compatible policy-update rule for GRPO-style reasoning RL. GXPO approximates a longer local lookahead using only three backward passes during an active phase. It reuses the same batch of rollouts, rewards, advantages, and GRPO loss, so it does not require new rollouts or reward computation at the lookahead points. GXPO takes two fast optimizer steps, measures how the gradients change, predicts a virtual K-step lookahead point, moves the policy partway toward that point, and then applies a corrective update using the true gradient at the new position. When the lookahead signal becomes unstable, GXPO automatically switches back to standard single-pass GRPO. We also give a plain-gradient-descent surrogate analysis that explains when the extrapolation is exact and where its local errors come from. Across Qwen2.5 and Llama math-reasoning experiments, GXPO improves the average sampled pass@1 by +1.65 to +5.00 points over GRPO and by +0.14 to +1.28 points over the strongest SFPO setting, while keeping the active-phase cost fixed at three backward passes. It also achieves up to 4.00x step speedup, 2.33x wall-clock speedup, and 1.33x backward-pass speedup in reaching GRPO's peak accuracy.

</details>


### [125] [Sparse Attention as a Range Searching Problem: Towards an Inference-Efficient Index for KV Cache](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06763)
*Mohsen Dehghankar,Abolfazl Asudeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Louver，一种基于半空间范围搜索的新型KV缓存索引结构，在保证零假阴性召回的同时实现高效稀疏注意力，在准确性和运行时间上均优于现有方法，甚至快于优化后的密集注意力如FlashAttention。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏注意力方法在解码步骤中无法保证零假阴性，遗漏关键KV条目会导致模型输出出现严重错误，尤其是在长推理任务中相关令牌集随解码步骤动态变化。

Method: 将稀疏注意力重新形式化为半空间范围搜索问题，设计Louver索引结构，在理论和实践中保证对指定阈值的零假阴性召回，并集成CPU和GPU的硬件感知优化。

Result: Louver在准确性和运行时间上均优于现有稀疏注意力方法，并且比FlashAttention等优化后的密集注意力更快。

Conclusion: 召回保证是稀疏注意力中被忽视的关键维度，为构建有理论基础的、高效的KV缓存索引开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Sparse attention improves LLM inference efficiency by selecting a subset of key-value entries, but at the cost of potential accuracy degradation. In particular, omitting critical KV entries can induce substantial errors in model outputs. Existing methods typically operate under fixed or adaptive token budgets and provide empirical robustness or partial theoretical guarantees, yet they do not ensure zero false negatives in decoding steps, particularly since the set of relevant tokens is both query- and step-dependent. Our empirical observations confirm that missing even one critical key can lead to sharp error spikes, especially in long reasoning tasks where the set of important tokens varies throughout decoding. This observation motivates the need for indexing methods that dynamically adapt to these variations across decoding steps while guaranteeing a full recall of the relevant keys above a certain threshold. We address this challenge by reformulating sparse attention as the halfspace range searching problem. However, existing range searching indices are not suitable for modern LLM inference due to their computational and implementation overheads. To overcome this, we introduce Louver, a novel index structure tailored for efficient KV cache retrieval. Louver (i) guarantees zero false negatives with respect to a specified threshold in both theory and practice, (ii) is lightweight to integrate into existing LLM pipelines, and (iii) incorporates hardware-aware optimizations for both CPU and GPU executions. Our experiments demonstrate that Louver outperforms prior sparse attention methods in both accuracy and runtime, and is faster than highly optimized dense attentions such as FlashAttention. These results highlight that recall guarantees are a critical and overlooked dimension of sparse attention, and open a new direction for building theoretically grounded, efficient KV cache indices.

</details>


### [126] [Revisiting Adam for Streaming Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06764)
*Florin Gogianu,Adrian Catalin Lutu,Razvan Pascanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线深度强化学习（不依赖经验回放缓冲区）中，传统DQN和C51算法在55款Atari游戏上的表现。通过分析优化算法（特别是Adam）与更新规则的交互，发现目标函数的导数有界性和权重更新的方差调整是实现稳健性能的两个关键特性。基于此，作者提出了基于资格迹的方差调整算法Adaptive Q(λ)，在相同游戏子集上超越了现有方法（包括StreamQ），性能接近人类基线的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习长期以来依赖经验回放缓冲区或并行采样来处理学习不稳定性，这增加了算法复杂度和存储开销。在线学习（边感知边行动，不显式存储经验）有望实现更简单、高效和自适应的算法。作者旨在重新审视传统更新方法（DQN、C51）在在线设置下的有效性，并探究其成功的关键因素。

Method: 1）在在线学习设置下（不依赖回放缓冲区）重新评估DQN和C51算法；2）通过理论分析揭示Adam优化器与更新规则的交互机制，提出两个必要条件：目标函数导数有界性和权重更新的方差调整；3）基于C51同时具备这两个特性，推导出基于资格迹的方差调整算法Adaptive Q(λ)。

Result: 1）传统DQN和C51在在线设置下表现良好，挑战了以往认为必须依赖回放缓冲区的认知；2）C51在55款Atari游戏上与Elsayed等人提出的StreamQ算法具有竞争力；3）提出的Adaptive Q(λ)在相同游戏子集上接近人类基线的两倍，在所有性能指标上超越现有方法。

Conclusion: 在线深度强化学习中，目标函数导数有界性和权重更新的方差调整是实现稳健性能的两个关键特性。基于这些见解设计的Adaptive Q(λ)算法代表了一种更简单、高效且性能优越的在线学习方法。

Abstract: Learning from a sequence of interactions, as soon as observations are perceived and acted upon, without explicitly storing them, holds the promise of simpler, more efficient and adaptive algorithms. For over a decade, however, deep reinforcement learning walked the contrary path, augmenting agents with replay buffers or parallel sampling routines, in an effort to tame learning instability. Recently, this topic has been revisited by Elsayed et al. (2024), focusing on update computation through eligibility traces and modifications to the optimisation routine, resulting in the StreamQ algorithm. In this work we take a step back, investigating the efficacy of established updates, such as those implemented by DQN and C51 within this online setting. Not only do we find that they perform well, but through analysing how the optimisation algorithm generally, and Adam in particular, interacts with these updates, we contend that two properties are essential for robust performance: i) the derivative of the objective is to be bounded and ii) weight updates are variance-adjusted. Rigorous and exhaustive experimentation demonstrates that C51, which exhibits both characteristics, is competitive with StreamQ across a subset of 55 Atari games. Using these insights, we derive a variance-adjusted algorithm based on eligibility traces, termed Adaptive Q$(λ)$, which approaches double the human baseline on the same subset, surpassing existing methods by all performance metrics.

</details>


### [127] [Distributional Process Reward Models: Calibrated Prediction of Future Rewards via Conditional Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06785)
*Rachel Ma,Dylan Hadfield-Menell,Kristjan Greenewald*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出使用条件最优传输（CondOT）校准过程奖励模型（PRM），通过估计PRM隐藏状态下的单调条件分位数函数，实现结构有效的分位数估计和置信区间提取，在数学推理基准上显著改善校准效果并提升下游Best-of-N IAS性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时扩展方法依赖过程奖励模型（PRM），但PRM通常校准不良且高估成功概率，需要有效的校准方法。

Method: 修改条件最优传输（CondOT）映射学习，估计PRM隐藏状态条件下成功概率的单调条件分位数函数，实现结构有效的分位数估计，并集成到实例自适应扩展（IAS）框架中。

Result: 在MATH-500和AIME基准上，对于排名信号可靠的PRM，该方法在校准效果上显著优于未校准的PRM和分位数回归；在下游Best-of-N IAS性能上通常优于未校准PRM。

Conclusion: 条件最优传输为PRM校准提供了一种有原则且实用的新方法，具有结构保证和灵活的不确定性估计能力。

Abstract: Inference-time scaling methods rely on Process Reward Models (PRMs), which are often poorly calibrated and overestimate success probabilities. We propose, to our knowledge, the first use of conditional optimal transport for calibrating PRMs, modifying conditional OT (CondOT) map learning \cite{bunne2022supervised} to estimate a monotonic conditional quantile function over success probabilities estimated by the PRM, conditioned on PRM hidden states. This yields structurally valid quantile estimates and enables efficient extraction of confidence bounds at arbitrary levels, which we integrate into the instance-adaptive scaling (IAS) framework of \cite{park2025know}. We evaluate on mathematical reasoning benchmarks spanning moderate-difficulty problems (MATH-500) and harder out-of-distribution problems (AIME). For PRMs with reliable ranking signals, our method substantially improves calibration over both uncalibrated PRMs and quantile regression. On downstream Best-of-N IAS performance, our method generally improves over uncalibrated PRMs. These results establish conditional optimal transport as another principled and practical approach to PRM calibration, offering structural guarantees and flexible uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [128] [MIND: Monge Inception Distance for Generative Models Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06797)
*Quentin Berthet,Yu-Han Wu,Clement Crepy,Romuald Elie,Klaus Greff,Michael Eli Sander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Monge Inception Distance (MIND)指标，利用切片Wasserstein距离替代FID中依赖高维均值和协方差矩阵的估计，在样本效率、计算速度和鲁棒性上均显著优于FID。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FID作为广泛使用的生成模型评估指标，存在样本复杂度差（需要大量样本）、计算成本高、易受对抗攻击（如矩匹配攻击）等关键缺陷，亟需一种更高效、更鲁棒的替代指标。

Method: MIND基于切片Wasserstein距离，通过对一维最优传输距离取平均来比较分布，利用排序操作高效计算，避免了高维均值和协方差矩阵的估计。

Result: 实验表明MIND具有三个主要优势：(i)样本效率提升一个数量级（5k样本可替代FID的50k样本）；(ii)计算速度提升两个数量级；(iii)对对抗攻击（如矩匹配）更鲁棒。更小的样本量（如1k或2k）仍能提供高信息量的评估。

Conclusion: MIND是一种更高效、更快速、更鲁棒的生成模型评估指标，与FID高度相关且具有更优的判别性能，适合快速模型迭代场景。

Abstract: We propose the Monge Inception Distance (MIND), a metric for evaluating generative models that addresses key limitations of the widely adopted Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). The MIND metric leverages the sliced Wasserstein distance to compare distributions by averaging one-dimensional optimal transport distances, efficiently computed via sorting. This approach circumvents the estimation of high-dimensional means and covariance matrices, which underlie FID's poor sample complexity and vulnerability to adversarial attacks. We empirically demonstrate three primary advantages: (i) it is more sample-efficient by one order of magnitude, (ii) it is faster to compute by two orders of magnitude, (iii) it is more robust to adversarial attacks such as moment-matching. We show that MIND with 5k samples can replace the evaluation performance of FID with 50k samples, providing high correlation with this standard benchmark and superior discriminative performance. We further demonstrate that even smaller sample sizes (e.g., 1k or 2k) remain highly informative for rapid model iteration.

</details>


### [129] [From Model to Data (M2D): Shifting Complexity from GNNs to Graphs for Transparent Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06814)
*Debolina Halder Lina,Arlei Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出模型到数据（M2D）蒸馏框架，通过将GNN模型复杂性迁移到数据空间（增强图特征与结构），使简单学生模型匹配复杂教师模型性能，从而提升GNN架构可解释性和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN可解释性方法仅能归因单个预测（节点、边或特征），无法提供架构层面的透明度，也无法解释简单模型与复杂模型之间的性能差距。

Method: M2D蒸馏：将教师模型的行为和复杂性蒸馏到增强图中（丰富特征与结构），使简单学生模型在增强数据上达成与教师相当的性能。

Result: M2D能够以可解释的方式揭示公平性目标和注意力聚合等底层机制，在保持性能的同时提升GNN透明度。

Conclusion: 通过将模型行为物化到数据空间，使人类可以直接检查架构优势，为GNN架构理解和比较提供了新途径。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) achieve high performance but can be opaque to humans, making it difficult to understand and compare the many proposed architectures. While existing explainability methods attribute individual predictions to nodes, edges, or features, they do not provide architectural transparency or explain the fundamental performance gap between simple and more complex models. To address this limitation, we introduce Model-to-Data (M2D) distillation, a new framework that increases transparency by transferring model complexity into the data space. M2D distills the teacher model into an augmented graph with enriched features and structure, enabling a simple student to match the teacher's performance. By materializing model behavior in the data, our approach allows humans to inspect architectural advantages directly. We show that M2D reveals underlying mechanisms such as fairness objectives and attention-based aggregation in an interpretable way, enhancing GNN transparency while preserving performance.

</details>


### [130] [CellScientist: Dual-Space Hierarchical Orchestration for Closed-Loop Refinement of Virtual Cell Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07335)
*Mengran Li,Bo Li,Jiaying Wang,Wenbin Xing,Yixuan Dong,Chengyang Zhang,Hongliang Zhang,Yuzhong Peng,Jinlin Wu,Bob Zhang,Bingo Wing-Kuen Ling,Fuji Yang,Zhen Lei,Jiebo Luo,Zelin Zang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CellScientist提出了一种双空间层次化框架，将高层假设空间与低层可执行实现空间耦合，通过结构化反馈传播机制解决LLM辅助虚拟细胞建模中的细化-路由问题，实现从执行失败到假设或实现更新的闭环迭代优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助建模流程存在细化-路由问题：预测偏差在可执行实现中被观察到，但相关修订可能涉及建模假设、表示设计、实现或任务约束等多个层面；缺乏结构化的反馈传播机制导致迭代细化可能只修复代码而未能修正根本性的假设错误。

Method: 构建双空间层次化框架，将建模决策表示为结构化状态（高层假设空间），在任务和接口约束下将其实现为可接受程序（低层可执行实现空间），并将执行偏差路由回目标假设或实现更新，形成"假设→实现→假设"的闭环。

Result: 在形态学和转录组学基准测试以及单细胞扰动评估中，CellScientist选择的可执行模型在固定分割和评估协议下优于参考基线，同时工作流程产生可审计的细化轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架将模型失败从调试事件转变为结构化的模型细化信号，实现了对建模假设和实现的系统性修订，而不仅仅是代码层面的修补。

Abstract: Virtual Cell Modeling (VCM) requires models that not only predict perturbation responses, but also support targeted revision when predictions fail. Current LLM-assisted modeling workflows face a refinement-routing problem: prediction discrepancies are observed through executable implementations, but the relevant revision may involve the modeling assumption, representation design, implementation, or task constraint. Without structured feedback propagation across these levels, iterative refinement may repair code while failing to revise the assumption responsible for the discrepancy. We propose CellScientist, a dual-space hierarchical framework that couples a high-level hypothesis space with a low-level executable implementation space. CellScientist represents modeling decisions as structured states, realizes them as admissible programs under task and interface constraints, and routes execution discrepancies back to targeted hypothesis or implementation updates. This enables a closed Hypothesis -> Implementation -> Hypothesis loop where failures become structured signals for model refinement rather than debugging events. Across morphology and transcriptomic benchmarks, with additional single-cell perturbation evaluations, the final executable models selected by CellScientist improve over reference baselines under fixed split and evaluation protocols, while the workflow produces auditable refinement traces.

</details>


### [131] [A Theory of Online Learning with Autoregressive Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06819)
*Ilan Doron-Arad,Idan Mehalel,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了自回归生成在在线学习环境下的错误界限理论，区分了仅观察最终输出（端到端模型）和观察完整生成轨迹（思维链模型）两种反馈模式，证明了端到端模型中错误界限在生成步数M上介于常数与对数之间且对数上界不可回避，而思维链模型可完全消除对M的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将自回归生成的学习问题从统计学习框架扩展到在线学习框架，旨在理解在在线环境下最优错误界如何依赖于生成步数M，以及观察中间token（思维链）能在多大程度上降低这种依赖。

Method: 建立了自回归学习的在线类比框架，定义端到端模型（仅观察最终token）和思维链模型（观察完整M步轨迹）两种反馈形式，通过理论分析刻画错误界限的增长率分类，并结合自回归线性阈值类进行具体分析。

Result: 端到端模型中错误界限在生成步数M上可呈现从常数到对数之间的任意增长率，且对数上界不可回避；思维链模型则完全消除了对M的依赖；此外给出了自回归线性阈值类的最优错误界及统计设置下的新下界。

Conclusion: 在线自回归学习理论在定性上与统计框架相似，但定量上对生成步数M的依赖尺度不同，开放了若干先前工作中的问题。

Abstract: Autoregressive generation lies at the heart of the mechanism of large language models. It can be viewed as the repeated application of a next-token generator: starting from an input string (prompt), the generator is applied for $M$ steps, and the last generated token is taken as the final output. [Joshi et al., 2025] proposed a PAC model for studying the learnability of the input-output maps arising from this process. We develop an online analogue of this framework, focusing on the mistake bound of learning the final output induced by an unknown next-token generator.
  We distinguish between two forms of feedback. In the End-to-End model, after each round the learner observes only the final token produced after $M$ autoregressive steps. In the Chain-of-Thought model, the learner is additionally shown the entire $M$-step trajectory. Our goal is to understand how the optimal mistake bound depends on the generation horizon $M$, and to what extent observing intermediate tokens can reduce this dependence.
  Our main results show that the online theory of autoregressive learning exhibits a qualitative picture analogous to the statistical one found by [Hanneke et al., 2026], but with a different scale of dependence on the generation horizon. In the End-to-End model, we prove a taxonomy of possible mistake-bound growth rates in the generation horizon $M$: essentially any rate between constant and logarithmic can arise. We further show that this logarithmic ceiling is unavoidable. In the Chain-of-Thought model, we show that access to the full generated trajectory eliminates the dependence on $M$ altogether.
  We also analyze autoregressive linear threshold classes, and prove optimal mistake bounds, as well as a new lower bound for the statistical setting. Along the way, our results resolve several questions left open by [Joshi et al., 2025].

</details>


### [132] [Mage: Multi-Axis Evaluation of LLM-Generated Executable Game Scenes Beyond Compile-Pass Rate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07342)
*Hugh Xuechen Liu,Kıvanç Tatar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在多组件领域特定工件（如游戏场景）的LLM代码生成中，编译通过率会误导评估。提出四轴评估协议Mage，发现直接生成虽运行时通过率高（43%），但结构空洞（机制F1≈0.12）；结构IR条件生成虽运行时通过率减半，但恢复了领域忠实结构（F1最高1.00）。编译率与功能正确性反相关，需多轴评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编译通过率作为LLM代码生成的主要评估信号，在多组件领域特定工件中可能具有误导性，现有评估方法无法捕捉功能正确性与结构忠实性之间的 divergence。

Method: 提出四轴评估协议Mage：编译成功、运行时成功、结构忠实度、机制遵循度。对858次生成尝试，在4个开源LLM（7B-30B）、26个Unity目标场景和2种IR粒度级别上进行评估。比较直接NL-to-C#生成与结构IR条件生成。

Result: 直接生成运行时通过率最高（43%），但机制F1仅≈0.12；结构IR条件生成运行时通过率减半，但结构忠实度恢复（F1高达1.00）。行为仅与全场景IR粒度间无统计学差异（McNemar p=1.0），表明输入级粒度饱和。编译率与功能正确性反相关。

Conclusion: 编译通过率在多组件领域工件生成中会误导评估，必须采用多轴评估才能检测到这种 divergence。发布基准测试、重放日志和逐记录指标以供独立验证。

Abstract: Compile-pass rate is the dominant evaluation signal for LLM code generation, yet for multi-component domain-specific artifacts it can be actively misleading. We demonstrate this on executable game scene synthesis with a four-axis evaluation protocol (named `Mage') -- compile success, runtime success, structural fidelity, and mechanism adherence -- applied to 858 generation attempts across four open-weight LLMs (7B--30B), 26~hand-crafted Unity goal pattern playable concepts, and two automatically extracted IR granularity levels. Direct NL-to-C\# generation achieves the highest runtime-pass rate (43\% mean) yet produces structurally vacuous scenes (mechanism $F_1 \approx 0.12$). Structural IR conditioning halves the runtime rate but recovers domain-faithful structure ($F_1$ up to 1.00). Within IR conditioning, behavior-only and full-scene granularity are statistically indistinguishable (McNemar $p = 1.0$), indicating input-level granularity saturation. These results show that compile rate is anti-correlated with functional correctness in this domain and that multi-axis evaluation is necessary to detect the divergence. We release the benchmark, replay logs, and per-record metrics for independent verification.

</details>


### [133] [A Rod Flow Model for Adam at the Edge of Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06821)
*Eric Regis,Sinho Chewi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将rod flow从梯度下降扩展到自适应动量优化器（Adam、RMSProp、NAdam等）和动量方法（heavy ball、Nesterov），在参数-一阶矩联合相空间建模，将二阶矩视为光滑辅助变量，并在边缘稳定性阶段实现了比stable flow更精确的离散迭代追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应梯度方法（如Adam）在边缘稳定性（edge of stability）下运行，但现有连续时间模型主要针对梯度下降，动量方法的边缘稳定性建模尚不完善。

Method: 将rod flow扩展到Adam，在参数w和一阶矩m的联合相空间中建模，将二阶矩ν视为光滑辅助变量；同时为heavy ball动量、Nesterov动量、标量和逐分量的RMSProp、Adam、NAdam开发了对应的rod flow。

Result: 在代表性机器学习架构上，八种优化器的rod flow在边缘稳定性阶段追踪离散迭代的精度显著优于对应的stable flow。

Conclusion: 所提出的rod flow框架能够有效建模自适应动量优化器在边缘稳定性下的动力学行为，为理解和分析这些优化器的收敛行为提供了更精确的连续时间模型。

Abstract: Cohen et al. (arXiv:2207.14484) observed that adaptive gradient methods such as Adam operate at the edge of stability. While there has been significant work on continuous-time modeling of gradient descent at the edge of stability, extending these models to momentum methods remains underdeveloped. In the gradient descent setting, Regis et al. (arXiv:2602.01480) introduced rod flow, which models consecutive iterates as an extended one-dimensional object -- a "rod." Here we extend rod flow to Adam by working in the joint phase space of parameters and first moment $(w, m)$ and treating the second moment $ν$ as a smooth auxiliary variable. We also develop rod flows for heavy ball momentum, Nesterov momentum, and scalar and per-component versions of RMSProp, Adam, and NAdam. For all eight optimizers, we empirically evaluate rod flow on representative machine learning architectures, where it tracks the discrete iterates through the edge-of-stability regime significantly more accurately than the corresponding stable flow.

</details>


### [134] [Why DDIM Hallucinates More than DDPM: A Theoretical Analysis of Reverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06831)
*Muhammad H. Ashiq,Samanyu Arora,Abhinav N. Harish,Ishaan Kharbanda,Hung Yun Tseng,Grigorios G. Chrysos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了DDPM和DDIM两种扩散采样器中的幻觉现象，证明在临界时间后DDIM会卡在两个最近模式之间的连接段上产生幻觉，而DDPM的随机性帮助其摆脱该区域避免幻觉。实验验证了DDPM幻觉率显著低于DDIM，并提出增加随机步骤可帮助DDIM避免幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论理解扩散模型中幻觉现象的产生机制，特别是DDPM和DDIM两种采样器在模式间"幻觉"行为上的差异根源。

Method: 针对高斯混合目标分布，理论分析DDPM（随机SDE）和DDIM（确定性ODE）的反向过程，证明存在临界时间τ后DDIM会陷入最近两个模式间连接段而产生幻觉，而DDPM的随机性使其脱离该区域。

Result: DDPM的幻觉率显著低于DDIM；增加随机步骤可帮助DDIM避免幻觉。

Conclusion: 扩散采样器的随机性对避免幻觉至关重要，为设计改进的采样器提供了新见解。

Abstract: We theoretically study the hallucination phenomena in two canonical diffusion samplers: the stochastic Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) and the deterministic Denoising Diffusion Implicit Model (DDIM). We analyze the reverse ODE (DDIM) and SDE (DDPM) for a Gaussian mixture target, proving that after a critical time $τ$, (a) DDIM can become stuck on the segment connecting the two nearest modes and (b) DDPM *stochasticity* helps it become unstuck from this region, thus avoiding hallucination. Our empirical validation verifies that DDPM has a significantly lower hallucination rate than DDIM when this region is entered. Building on our observations, we exhibit how using additional stochastic steps can help DDIM avoid hallucinations and offer new insights on how to design improved samplers.

</details>


### [135] [How to Compress KV Cache in RL Post-Training? Shadow Mask Distillation for Memory-Efficient Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06850)
*Rui Zhu,Weiheng Bai,Qiushi Wu,Yang Ren,Haixu Tang,Yuchu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在线强化学习在长上下文推理任务中，由于KV缓存压缩导致的策略偏差（稀疏上下文采样 vs 稠密上下文更新）会严重损害训练效果，而传统统计方法（如重要性重加权）无法有效纠正这种偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在线RL在长上下文推理任务中，KV缓存压缩带来的严重off-policy偏差问题，该偏差因RL优化固有的不稳定性而被急剧放大。

Method: 分析KV缓存压缩在rollout阶段产生的off-policy偏差机制（稀疏vs稠密上下文的分布不匹配），并指出现有统计方法（重要性重加权）的不足。

Result: KV缓存压缩在标准推理中近乎无损，但在RL训练中微小的近似误差会被严重放大；重要性重加权方法表现出高梯度方差和低样本效率。

Conclusion: 现有KV缓存压缩方法在在线RL场景下不适用，需要专门设计应对RL训练不稳定性的偏差矫正方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a crucial paradigm for unlocking the advanced reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), encompassing frameworks like RLHF and RLAIF. Regardless of the specific optimization algorithm (e.g., PPO, GRPO, or Online DPO), online RL inherently requires an exploratory trajectory generation (rollout) phase. However, for long-context reasoning tasks, this rollout phase imposes a severe ``memory wall'' due to the exorbitant Key-Value (KV) cache footprint. While applying KV cache compression during rollouts mitigates this memory overhead, it induces a critical off-policy bias. Although modern KV compression is often nearly lossless during standard inference, even minuscule approximation errors are drastically amplified by the inherent instability of RL optimization. Specifically, the sampler generates responses under a sparse context, whereas the learner updates parameters using the full, dense context. Existing statistical solutions, such as importance reweighting, struggle to correct this magnified bias, suffering from high gradient variance and severe sample inefficiency.

</details>


### [136] [Less Random, More Private: What is the Optimal Subsampling Scheme for DP-SGD?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07072)
*Andy Dong,Ayfer Özgür*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）中，结构化采样方案（平衡迭代子采样，BIS）比广泛使用的泊松子采样能实现更强的隐私放大，在低噪声场景下噪声乘子最多可降低9.6%，颠覆了“更多随机性=更强隐私”的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松子采样虽因无结构随机性便于隐私放大分析，但其引入的参与方差（每个样本在训练迭代中出现次数高度不均）从根本上导致了次优的隐私放大效果。本文旨在证明消除参与方差的结构化采样方案能实现更优的隐私-噪声权衡。

Method: 提出平衡迭代子采样（BIS），每个样本在训练中恰好参与固定次数；通过理论分析证明BIS在噪声极端（σ→0和σ→∞）下达到最优；引入近精确蒙特卡洛核算器消除现有RDP和PLD分析的松弛度。

Result: 在60余种实际DP-SGD配置中，BIS在低噪声（高效用）场景下持续优于泊松子采样，噪声乘子降低最多9.6%。

Conclusion: 结构化参与模式在DP-SGD中能同时提升实用性和隐私性，隐私放大的核心在于消除参与方差而非最大化采样随机性。

Abstract: Poisson subsampling is the default sampling scheme in differentially private machine learning, largely because its unstructured randomness yields tractable privacy amplification analyses. Yet this same randomness introduces substantial participation variance: each sample appears in very different numbers of training iterations. In this work, we show that this variance is not merely a practical artifact to be tolerated, but a fundamental source of suboptimal privacy amplification. We prove that Balanced Iteration Subsampling (BIS), a structured scheme in which each sample participates in exactly a fixed number of iterations, achieves stronger privacy amplification than Poisson subsampling and is optimal at both extremes of the noise spectrum ($σ\to 0$ and $σ\to \infty$). Our analysis reveals that the privacy-noise tradeoff is governed not by maximizing randomness, but by eliminating participation variance while preserving uniform marginal participation across iterations. To translate this asymptotic theory into finite-noise guarantees, we introduce a practical near-exact Monte Carlo accountant for BIS, which removes the analytical slack of existing RDP and composition-based PLD analyses. Evaluations across more than 60 practical DP-SGD configurations show that BIS consistently outperforms Poisson subsampling in the low-noise regimes most relevant for high-utility private training, reducing the required noise multiplier by up to $9.6\%$. These results overturn the common intuition that more sampling randomness necessarily yields stronger privacy amplification: in DP-SGD, structured participation can be both more practical and more private. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/dong-xin-ao-andy/bis-mc-accountant.

</details>


### [137] [Christoffel-DPS: Optimal sensor placement in diffusion posterior sampling for arbitrary distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06861)
*James Rowbottom,Nick Huang,Carola-Bibiane Schönlieb,Ben Adcock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Christoffel函数的无分布传感器放置框架（Christoffel-DPS），用于生成模型引导的扩散后验采样，在低传感器预算下显著优于传统高斯方法及现有生成模型放置方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最优传感器放置（OSP）方法依赖高斯假设，无法处理现实系统中的复杂分布；现有传感器选择方法与现代生成式重建模型之间存在不匹配，需要新思想来实现与强大恢复模型相匹配的OSP策略。

Method: 基于Christoffel函数提出无分布传感器放置框架，推导出具有非渐近恢复传感器数量边界的OSP策略；开发Christoffel-DPS（包括离线和在线变体），将Christoffel采样实例化到无条件扩散后验采样和流匹配模型中。

Result: Christoffel-DPS在低传感器预算下，在多个非高斯基准测试中优于高斯OSP基线和现有生成模型放置方法。

Conclusion: 无分布感知在理论上具有原则性，在实践中也具有优越性；该框架是模型无关的，可广泛应用于多种生成模型。

Abstract: State estimation is a critical task in scientific, engineering and control applications. Since the reliability of reconstructions depends on the number and position of sensors, optimal sensor placement (OSP) is essential in scenarios where measurements are sparse and expensive. Classical OSP approaches rely on Gaussian assumptions and are consequently unable to account for the complex distributions encountered in many real-world systems. Generative-model-based reconstruction using sensor guided diffusion posterior sampling (DPS) has emerged as a promising technique for reconstructing states from highly complex distributions. However, existing sensor-selection methods either require unrealistically many sensors or emulate classical OSP, creating a mismatch between modern recovery models with classical OSP tools motivating the need for fundamentally new ideas towards OSP that match the recent advances made in powerful recovery models. We introduce a distribution-free sensor placement framework based on the Christoffel function: a mathematical formulation of optimal sampling and recovery guarantees for posterior sampling with arbitrary sensors and signal distributions, from which we derive a new OSP strategy with non-asymptotic bounds on the number of sensors needed for recovery. We develop Christoffel-DPS, with offline and online variants, instantiating Christoffel sampling for generative models. Christoffel-DPS outperforms Gaussian OSP baselines and existing generative-model placement methods, validating that distribution-free sensing is both theoretically principled and practically superior. The framework is model-agnostic; we demonstrate its application to a range of unconditional DPS and flow-matching models on structurally non-Gaussian benchmarks, showing the efficacy of Christoffel-DPS in low sensor budget regimes.

</details>


### [138] [Modulated learning for private and distributed regression with just a single sample per client device](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07233)
*Praneeth Vepakomma,Amirhossein Reisizadeh,Samuel Horváth,Munther Dahleh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习中每个客户端仅有一个样本的场景，提出了一种通过单次加噪变换和去偏后处理来实现隐私保护全局模型训练的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在联邦学习中，当每个客户端只有一条数据样本时，标准方法无法工作：单点局部更新在早期轮次中对模型系数估计几乎无用，且每轮添加的隐私噪声进一步削弱了效用。现有隐私联邦学习依赖模型系数的通信，不适用于这种极端数据稀缺场景。

Method: 每个客户端对单一样本注入一次精心校准的噪声扰动，进行数据变换，然后对变换后的表示进行后处理并上传至服务器。服务器聚合这些表示，获得一个无偏梯度更新，该更新在期望上与无隐私保护的集中式梯度一致。

Result: 该方法使得仅持有单个样本的设备能够协作学习准确且保护隐私的全局模型，无需大量本地数据集，同时保护数据隐私。

Conclusion: 该方法通过单次加噪的样本变换而非模型系数的通信，解决了单样本客户端场景下的联邦学习与隐私保护的冲突，为极端数据受限场景提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: This work focuses on the question of learning from a large number of devices with each device holding only a single sample of data. Several real-world applications exist to this one sample per client setup up including learning from fitness trackers, data/app usage aggregators, body-worn sensing devices, and daily event monitors to name a few. When a client has only one sample, the standard federated learning paradigm breaks down as a local update based on that single point is far from being useful, especially in the earlier rounds for estimation of the model coefficients. This utility is further weakened by the privacy-inducing noise applied at every round. This work caters to this problem to enable such clients to collaboratively contribute to effectively learn a global model without leaking the privacy of their data. The proposed approach injects a single, carefully calibrated noisy perturbation to transform the sample at each client, followed by a post-processed representation which is shared with the server. These representations aggregated at the server are processed to obtain an unbiased gradient update that in expectation matches the non-private centralized gradient while preserving data privacy. This approach is different than traditional private federated learning, where the communication payloads involve model coefficients as opposed to privately transformed data samples. This method enables devices with extremely limited data to collaborate and learn accurate, privacy-preserving models without requiring large local datasets or sacrificing individual privacy.

</details>


### [139] [Multi-Objective Multi-Agent Bandits: From Learning Efficiency to Fairness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06864)
*John Wang,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标多智能体多臂老虎机（MO-MA-MAB）问题，提出了两种算法：Pareto UCB1 Gossip（关注效率，用Pareto后悔度量）和Simulated NSW UCB Gossip（关注公平性，用纳什社会福利目标度量），分别实现了O(log T)和O(T^{3/4})的遗憾界，实验显示性能提升约100%和50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未能同时兼顾多目标多智能体场景下的高效学习（Pareto最优）和公平学习（社会福利），本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对效率目标，提出Pareto UCB1 Gossip算法，设计新的探索半径以分离统计不确定性与共识误差；针对公平目标，提出Simulated NSW UCB Gossip算法，融合偏好标量化奖励模拟、gossip效用估计和UCB探索。

Result: Pareto UCB1 Gossip实现了O(log T)和O(√T)遗憾界；Simulated NSW UCB Gossip实现了O(T^{3/4})遗憾界；实验性能分别提升约100%和50%。

Conclusion: 公平约束限制了信息聚合并减慢了收敛速度，揭示了效率与公平之间的代价权衡。

Abstract: We study multi-objective multi-agent multi-armed bandits (MO-MA-MAB) under stochastic rewards, where agents observe heterogeneous reward vectors and communicate over time-varying graphs. We formulate this emerging problem setting to address \emph{efficient learning}, measured by Pareto regret, and incorporate \emph{fair learning} as an additional goal, captured via social welfare. To measure efficiency, we formulate Pareto regret and develop \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip}, whose novel exploration radius explicitly separates statistical uncertainty in Pareto-based inference from consensus error. To express the fairness constraint, we formulate a Nash Social Welfare objective over preference-scalarized rewards and propose \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip}, which integrates preference-based reward simulation, gossip-based utility estimation, and UCB-style exploration. We prove that \textsc{Pareto UCB1 Gossip} achieves \(\mathcal{O}(\log T)\) regret and an instance-independent rate of \(\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{T})\), while \textsc{Simulated NSW UCB Gossip} achieves an instance-independent regret bound of \(\mathcal{O}(T^{3/4})\). This separation reveals the cost of imposing the fairness constraint to our efficiency objective: fairness limits information aggregation and slows convergence. Experiments show that our methods consistently outperform baselines, improving performance by approximately \(100\%\) and \(50\%\) in the efficiency and fairness settings, respectively.

</details>


### [140] [Dataset Watermarking for Closed LLMs with Provable Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06865)
*Pengrun Huang,Kamalika Chaudhuri,Yu-Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对闭源大语言模型，提出首个可证明的数据集水印方法：通过增加随机词对共现频率嵌入水印，并在模型生成输出中通过统计检验检测，在微调阶段有效（p<0.01），即使水印数据仅占1%的混合数据中仍有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在大量非精心筛选的数据上训练，可能包含专有数据集或评估基准，需要一种方法在闭源模型中检测训练数据是否被使用，以保护数据版权和评估完整性。

Method: 通过改写句子增加随机选择的词对共现频率来嵌入数据集级水印信号；在模型生成输出中使用统计检验检测词对共现模式。

Result: 在多个基础模型和基准数据集上，该方法在微调阶段可靠检测水印（p<0.01）；在水印数据仅占微调总token约1%的混合数据设置中仍有效；同时保持基准的效用和语义完整性。

Conclusion: 首个针对闭源LLM的可证明数据集水印方法，在微调阶段实现高检测可靠性，对数据混合场景具有鲁棒性，且不影响模型性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are pre-trained and post-trained on vast amounts of loosely curated data, raising the possibility that these models may have been trained on proprietary datasets or the same benchmarks used for evaluation. This motivates the need for dataset watermarking: designing datasets such that training on them leaves detectable signatures in the resulting model. Prior work has explored this problem for open models. We introduce the first dataset watermarking method for closed LLMs with provable detection. In particular, we embed a dataset-level watermark signal by increasing the co-occurrence frequency of randomly selected word pairs through rephrasing, and detect it using a statistical test on co-occurrence patterns in model-generated outputs. We evaluate our method with multiple base models and benchmark datasets and show that it reliably detects the watermark ($p <0.01$) in the fine-tuning stage. Notably, our method remains effective in a data mixture setting where the watermarked dataset constitutes only approximately $1\%$ of the total fine-tuning tokens. Furthermore, we show that our method preserves the utility and semantic integrity of the benchmark.

</details>


### [141] [When Descent Is Too Stable: Event-Triggered Hamiltonian Learning to Optimize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06868)
*Yi Wang,Chandrajit Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHAPE是一种基于结构自适应port-Hamiltonian的任务族优化器，通过事件触发机制在不动点预算非凸优化中动态平衡梯度下降、局部开发与跨区域探索，实验表明其优于固定策略优化器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定预算非凸优化中，优化器容易过早陷入局部极小点并停滞，剩余预算浪费在对无效局部区域的精细优化上。现有方法缺乏对“何时下降、何时利用、何时切换”的结构化控制机制。

Method: 将优化问题建模为控制器动力学问题，从梯度下降提升到增广相空间(q,p)，引入控制器u。通过学习的Hamiltonian向量场实现结构化局部下降，利用固定事件时钟检测局部平衡并切换端口与记忆，支持干净/随机/估计梯度输入。

Result: 在固定预算非凸优化任务上，SHAPE相比固定策略优化器显著提升了best-so-far性能。

Conclusion: 自适应Hamiltonian能量整形为平衡下降、探索与预算分配提供了一种有原则的机制，在困难优化景观中具有优越性。

Abstract: Fixed-budget nonconvex optimization can fail not because local descent is unstable, but because it is too stable: after reaching a nearby stationary point, an optimizer may spend the remaining evaluations refining an uninformative local minimum. We formulate this failure mode as a control problem over optimizer dynamics, where the learner must decide when to descend, when to exploit a promising basin, and when stagnation should trigger movement elsewhere. We introduce SHAPE, a structured adaptive port-Hamiltonian task-family optimizer for event-triggered minima hunting under local information. Starting from gradient-descent dynamics, SHAPE lifts optimization to an augmented phase space $(q, p)$, where the primal state $q$ represents the candidate solution, the cotangent variable $p$ carries directional sensitivity, and a controller $u$ provides processed information from current gradient oracle. Within each stage, a learned Hamiltonian vector field induces structured local descent; across stages, a fixed event clock in the implementation updates ports and memory when local equilibria are detected, with stage-dependent horizons treated in the analysis as a direct generalization. This design preserves a passivity-compatible structure while allowing the same trained policy to use clean, stochastic, or estimated gradient inputs. Experiments on fixed-budget nonconvex optimization tasks show that SHAPE improves best-so-far performance compared with fixed-policy optimizers. These results suggest that adaptive Hamiltonian energy shaping provides a principled mechanism for balancing descent, exploration, and budget allocation in difficult optimization landscapes.

</details>


### [142] [Continuous First, Discrete Later: VQ-VAEs Without Dimensional Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06870)
*Xinyu Zhao,Nikita Karagodin,Hamed Hassani,Sinan Hersek,Paul Pu Liang,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了VQ-VAE中存在的维度坍缩问题（表示仅占据1-2%的满秩子空间），理论证明该问题导致硬损失下界且现有码本改进技术无法突破。提出AE Warm-Up方法（先训练未量化的自编码器再引入VQ），在图像（VQGAN）和音频（WavTokenizer）任务上有效恢复表示维度，降低重构和感知损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VQ-VAE改进方法多聚焦于码本大小和利用率，但忽略了维度坍缩问题（训练后的VQ-VAE表示仅存在于极低维子空间，仅占满秩的1-2%），该问题未被解决。本文旨在揭示维度坍缩的存在性及其导致的性能瓶颈，并提出解决方案。

Method: 1. 理论分析：结合Saxe等[2014]的序列学习效应和率失真理论，建立分析框架解释维度坍缩由VQ抑制低方差方向导致。2. 提出AE Warm-Up：在引入VQ之前，先训练模型作为未量化的自编码器。3. 实验验证：在合成数据、VQGAN（图像，码本大小K∈{2^10,2^14,2^16}）和WavTokenizer（音频，K∈{2^13,2^14}）上验证，并建立预热时长与最终损失的关联分析。4. 理论预测：根据预热长度预测下游性能，实现自适应切换准则。

Result: 1. AE Warm-Up成功恢复表示维度：VQGAN码本有效维度从3-5提升至17-19，rFID降低17-35%；WavTokenizer码本维度从4提升至17-19，PESQ提升11-14%。2. 实证表征了预热时长控制最终可达到的损失。3. 理论分析与实验结果一致，可预测下游性能。

Conclusion: 维度坍缩是VQ-VAE的性能瓶颈，AE Warm-Up通过先训练未量化自编码器再引入VQ，是一种简单有效的方法，可在相同训练预算下恢复表示维度、降低损失。预热时长是影响最终性能的关键可控因素，理论可支持自适应切换策略。

Abstract: While many approaches to improve VQ-VAE performance focus on codebook size and utilization, the effect of dimensional collapse, where trained VQ-VAE representations live in an extremely low-dimensional subspace (1-2% of full rank), remains unaddressed. We show theoretically and empirically that dimension collapse causes a hard loss lower bound that various codebook improvement techniques fail to surpass. Our analytic framework extends the sequential learning effect of Saxe et al. [2014] by introducing ideas from rate-distortion theory and explains how the latent collapse is caused by the VQ suppressing lower-variance directions. Our theory justifies a simple solution: a "warm-up phase" that trains the model as an (unquantized) autoencoder before introducing VQ. On both synthetic experiments and large-scale image (VQGAN) and audio (WavTokenizer) VQ-VAEs, we show that AE Warm-Up successfully restores representation dimension, leading to lower reconstruction and perceptual loss at the same training budget. Across codebook sizes $K \in$ {$2^{10}, 2^{14}, 2^{16}$}, AE warm-up raises VQGAN codebook effective dimension from 3-5 to 17-19 and reduces rFID by 17-35%; on WavTokenizer at $K \in$ {$2^{13}, 2^{14}$}, it raises codebook dimension from 4 to 17-19 and improves PESQ by 11-14%. We empirically characterize how warm-up duration governs the achievable final loss. In agreement with experiment, our theoretical analysis predicts downstream performance as a function of warm-up length, enabling an adaptive criterion for switching from AE Warm-up to VQ-VAE training.

</details>


### [143] [On the Divergence of Differential Temporal Difference Learning without Local Clocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06874)
*David Antrobius,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，在平均奖励强化学习中，使用全局时钟（基于时间步）和学习率与使用局部时钟（基于状态访问次数）的学习率会导致不同的收敛结果，并构造了一个反例证明差分时序差分学习在局部时钟下收敛但在全局时钟下发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在折扣和平均奖励两种RL设置下，全局时钟与局部时钟学习率对算法收敛性的影响是否一致，并解决Wan et al. [2021]和Blaser et al. [2026]中提出的开放问题。

Method: 区分两种学习率形式：全局时钟α_t（仅依赖时间步t）和局部时钟α_{ν(S_t, t)}（依赖状态s的访问次数）。通过构造反例，对比两种时钟在折扣RL和平均奖励RL中的收敛性质。

Result: 在折扣RL中，局部时钟收敛性与全局时钟收敛性等价；但在平均奖励RL中，差分时序差分学习在局部时钟下收敛，在全局时钟下发散。

Conclusion: 折扣RL中两种时钟的收敛等价性在平均奖励RL中不再成立，该反例解决了相关开放问题。

Abstract: Learning rate is a critical component of reinforcement learning (RL). This work uses global and local clocks to distinguish two types of learning rates. The former is of the standard form $α_t$ that depends only on the time step $t$ (i.e., a global clock). The latter is of the form $α_{ν(S_t, t)}$, where $ν(s, t)$ counts the number of visits to state $s$ until time $t$ (i.e., a local clock). In discounted RL, an RL algorithm that is convergent with a local clock is always also convergent with a global clock, and vice versa. We are not aware of any counterexample. The key contribution of this work is to show that this nice correspondence breaks down in average-reward RL. Specifically, we construct a counterexample showing that although differential temporal difference learning is convergent with a local clock, it can diverge with a global clock. This counterexample closes the open problem in Wan et al. [2021], Blaser et al. [2026].

</details>


### [144] [Better Protein Function Prediction by Modeling Survivorship Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06879)
*Zhongmou Chao,Poompol Buathong,Ekaterina Selivanovitch,Susan Daniel,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Evo-PU，一种利用核苷酸突变机制对单生物体序列数据中生存偏差进行建模的正-无标签学习框架，在流感、RSV和SARS-CoV-2预测任务上优于标准PU学习、单类分类和蛋白质语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蛋白质序列数据存在生存偏差（仅观察到存活生物体的序列），而现有正-无标签（PU）学习方法忽略了导致这种偏差的进化过程。不同原因导致的缺失序列（可能功能但未观测到 vs. 不太可能产生而缺失）需要区别对待。

Method: 提出Evo-PU框架，利用核苷酸突变的科学原理对良好监测的单生物体序列数据中的生存偏差进行建模，区分因功能不可行而未观测与因突变概率低而未观测的序列。

Result: 在流感、RSV突变扫描预测和SARS-CoV-2未来变异预测三个任务上，Evo-PU优于标准PU学习、单类分类（OCC）和蛋白质语言模型（PLM）。在多生物体ProteinGym数据集上识别了泛化机会。

Conclusion: 将进化过程先验知识融入PU学习框架能更有效地处理蛋白质序列的生存偏差问题，在预测蛋白质功能方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: Protein sequence data from nature exhibits survivorship bias: we only observe data from those organisms that survive and reproduce, while non-functional protein mutations are eliminated by natural selection. Thus, predicting whether a protein sequence is functional often requires learning from positive examples alone. While positive-unlabeled (PU) learning frameworks offer a generic solution to this problem, existing PU methods ignore the evolutionary processes that shape sequence observability and cause survivorship bias. Consider a sequence that is one mutation away from a commonly-observed protein variant in a well-surveilled organism. If the sequence were functional, it would likely be observed. If it is not observed, this suggests non-functionality. In contrast, sequences that are unlikely to arise through mutation may be missing simply because they never arose. Thus, these two kinds of missing sequences should be treated differently when training models. In this work, we propose Evo-PU, a PU learning framework that uses a scientific understanding of nucleotide mutation to model survivorship bias for well-surveilled single-organism sequence data. On three prediction tasks using single-organism uniform-coverage surveillance data -- predicting results from held-out influenza and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV) mutagenesis studies, and predicting future SARS-CoV-2 variants -- Evo-PU outperforms standard PU learning, one-class classification (OCC), and protein language models (PLMs). On prediction tasks from multi-organism ProteinGym datasets with more heterogeneous surveillance coverage, we identify opportunities to generalize our approach.

</details>


### [145] [Don't Retrain, Align: Adapting Autoregressive LMs to Diffusion LMs via Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06885)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Alexis Fox,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REPR-ALIGN方法，通过在每一层对齐扩散语言模型与预训练自回归模型的隐藏状态（余弦相似度），在不改变架构仅改注意力掩码的情况下，实现了语言表示跨生成顺序的迁移，训练加速最高达4倍，尤其在低数据场景下效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR-to-DLM转换方法主要通过持续去噪训练和注意力/目标层面的修改来迁移参数，但未明确保留自回归预训练中学到的内部表示几何结构。作者假设AR预训练获得的语义结构可以跨生成顺序迁移，DLM训练应被视为重新学习解码路径而非重新学习语言表示。

Method: 提出REPR-ALIGN表示对齐目标：在每一层将双向掩码扩散模型的隐藏状态与冻结的预训练AR模型进行余弦相似度对齐，同时优化标准掩码去噪目标。无需适配器或架构更改，仅需修改注意力掩码。

Result: 在实验设置中实现最高4倍训练加速，在低数据场景下尤为有效。

Conclusion: 语言表示可以跨生成顺序迁移，表示对齐为训练扩散语言模型提供了一种简单有效的技术方案。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently demonstrated capabilities that complement standard autoregressive (AR) models, particularly in non-sequential generation and bidirectional editing. Although recent work has shown that pretrained autoregressive checkpoints can be converted into diffusion language models, existing recipes primarily transfer parameters through continued denoising training with objective- and attention-level modifications. We instead ask whether the internal representation geometry learned by next-token prediction can be explicitly preserved during AR-to-DLM conversion. We hypothesize that much of the semantic structure learned by AR pretraining can transfer across generation orders, and thus DLM training should be viewed as relearning the decoding path rather than relearning language representations. To investigate this, we introduce REPR-ALIGN, a representation alignment objective that adapts a bidirectional masked diffusion model to reuse representations from a pretrained AR model of identical architecture. Concretely, we align the hidden states of the DLM to the frozen AR model at every layer using cosine similarity, while optimizing the standard masked denoising objective. This simple alignment, with no adapters and no architectural changes beyond the attention mask, yields up to 4x training acceleration in our setting and is particularly effective in low-data regimes. Our results suggest that linguistic representations can transfer across generation order, and that representation alignment provides a simple and effective technique for training diffusion language models. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Open-dLLM.

</details>


### [146] [Conservative Flows: A New Paradigm of Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06905)
*Eshed Gal,Md Shahriar Rahim Siddiqui,Moshe Eliasof,Eldad Haber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种替代性的生成范式，使用离散随机动力学从数据支持的初始状态出发，通过保持数据分布不变的方式进行生成，而非从噪声先验进行传输。该方法可利用任何预训练流模型，并开发了两种概率保持采样机制（带Metropolis调整的校正Langevin动力学和预测-校正流），在多个数据集上持续优于原始生成过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型主要依赖从噪声先验到数据的传输范式，作者希望探索一种替代路径，即从数据支持的初始状态出发，利用保持数据分布不变的离散随机动力学进行生成，从而克服传统方法的局限。

Method: 提出了两种概率保持采样机制：(1) 带Metropolis调整的校正Langevin动力学，(2) 预测-校正流。这些方法直接在现有预训练流模型的检查点上运行，无需重新训练。

Result: 在合成Swiss-roll数据集、ImageNet-256和Oxford Flowers-102上验证，所提出的采样器在生成质量上始终优于原始的生成流程。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种新的生成范式，能够利用任意预训练流模型，通过从数据支持状态初始化而非噪声初始化来改进生成效果。

Abstract: Modern generative modeling is dominated by transport from a noise prior to data. We propose an alternative paradigm in which generation is performed by a discrete stochastic dynamics that leaves the data distribution invariant, initialized from data-supported states rather than from noise. The framework can utilize any pretrained flow model. We develop two probability-preserving sampling mechanisms, a corrected Langevin dynamics with a Metropolis adjustment and a predictor-corrector flow, that operate directly on existing checkpoints. We validate the framework on a synthetic Swiss-roll target, ImageNet-256 and Oxford Flowers-102, where our samplers consistently improve over the original generation procedures.

</details>


### [147] [TraXion: Rethinking Pre-training Frameworks for Mobility and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06906)
*Shang-Ling Hsu,Mark Tenzer,Cyrus Shahabi,Khurram Shafique*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TraXion框架，针对多实体时空事件流（MESES）设计预训练方法，在移动性、企业认证日志和ICU死亡率预测等任务上超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将移动性数据类比为文本序列的预训练方法无法处理其三个结构性特征：访问是元组值事件、用户具有跨轨迹的持久签名、用户间访问存在位置共现依赖。

Method: 定义MESES应满足的三条公理，提出TraXion框架，其目标和架构联合设计以满足这些公理，统一处理移动性、企业认证日志和电子健康记录等事件流数据。

Result: 单个TraXion检查点在6个公开移动性数据集上超越所有任务特定基线，且在未经改动的情况下在企业认证日志和ICU死亡率预测上匹配或超越先前工作。

Conclusion: 不同领域（移动性、安全、医疗）的事件流可在单一框架下统一建模。

Abstract: Human mobility differs from text and from generic time series in three structural ways: visits are tuple-valued events whose meaning depends on the joint distribution over location, time, and activity; users carry persistent signatures across trajectories; and visits are not independent across users, since co-location at shared places is a primary signal. Existing pre-training recipes for mobility import objectives from language modeling, treating trajectories as sentences and visits as tokens, an analogy that fails against each of the three properties above. These properties define a broader class, multi-entity spatiotemporal event streams (MESES), spanning enterprise authentication logs, electronic health records, and other event-stream domains where entities share infrastructure, schedules, or contexts. We make the properties precise as three axioms that any pre-training framework for MESES should satisfy, and introduce TraXion, whose objectives and architecture are jointly designed to meet them. A single TraXion checkpoint per dataset beats task-specific baselines on every task across six public mobility datasets covering anomaly detection, next-POI recommendation, next-visit prediction, and social-link prediction.
