The same recipe, applied unchanged to enterprise authentication logs and ICU mortality prediction, matches or exceeds prior work on both, showing that event streams from domains as different as mobility, security, and healthcare can be modeled under a single framework.

</details>


### [148] [Dual-Scale Temporal Fusion Reveals Structured Predictability in Subseasonal-to-Seasonal Temperature Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06911)
*Elnaz Bashir,Jiali Wang,Lin Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双尺度学习框架，通过分离日历对齐的历史气候背景和领先时间匹配的近期天气演变，结合空间自适应融合，揭示了次季节到季节（S2S）温度可预测性是由时间尺度、空间异质性和大尺度模式相干性共同组织的结构化多尺度现象，而非简单的领先时间衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: S2S温度预报对农业、能源规划和极端天气风险管理至关重要，但其可靠性随季节和地区变化显著。传统观点将预报技巧主要归因于领先时间，但这无法充分解释可预测性的时空模式，因此需要更深入理解其结构特征。

Method: 开发了一种双尺度学习框架，分离日历对齐的历史气候背景（年际背景）与领先时间匹配的近期天气演变（近期演化），通过空间自适应融合结合两者，并引入拓扑感知的结构约束以改善预测温度场的空间相干性。

Result: 学习到的融合权重显示：冬季年际背景在高纬度和复杂地形区域占主导（这些区域预报最困难），夏季则呈现更平衡的时间贡献。空间显式的可预测性重组（而非简单领先时间衰减）成为30-90天窗口内预报技巧的主要决定因素。拓扑感知约束显著改善了复杂地形区域的大尺度模式组织。

Conclusion: S2S可预测性应被重新定义为一种结构化的多尺度现象，这为改进预报系统及其实际应用提供了更可解释的基础。

Abstract: Subseasonal-to-seasonal (S2S) temperature forecasts, spanning several weeks to a few months, are critically needed in agriculture practice, energy planning, and extreme-weather induced risk management, yet their reliability varies substantially across seasons and regions. Forecast skill is often attributed primarily to lead time, but this perspective does not fully explain the spatiotemporal patterns of predictability. Here we show that S2S predictability is organized across interacting temporal components, spatial heterogeneity, and large-scale pattern coherence, and that this structure can be explicitly characterized and exploited. We develop a dual-scale learning framework that separates calendar-aligned historical climate context from lead-time matched recent weather evolution, combining them through spatially adaptive fusion to enable stable temperature forecasts across the 30 to 90-day window. The learned fusion weights reveal that the balance between these two temporal scales shifts systematically with season and geography: during winter, interannual context dominates over high latitudes and complex terrain where forecast is the most difficult, while summer predictions reflect a more balanced temporal contribution across the domain. This spatially explicit reorganization of predictability, rather than simple lead-time decay, emerges as the primary determinant of forecast skill within the subseasonal window. Topology-aware structural constraints further improve spatial coherence of predicted temperature fields, stabilizing large-scale pattern organization particularly over complex terrain. These results reframe S2S predictability as a structured, multi-scale phenomenon, providing a more interpretable foundation for improving forecast systems and informing their use in practice.

</details>


### [149] [LLMs are not (consistently) Bayesian: Quantifying internal (in)consistencies of LLMs' probabilistic beliefs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06915)
*Chacha Chen,Matthew Jörke,Adam Goliński,Masha Fedzechkina,Guillermo Sapiro,Sinead Williamson,Nicholas Foti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM视为信息处理规则，利用信息处理差距研究LLM在更新概率信念时的内部不一致性。实验发现某些方法产生近似贝叶斯更新，另一些则使用学习的启发式方法。令人惊讶的是，非贝叶斯启发式更新在下游任务中往往优于精确贝叶斯计算，表明LLM的世界概率模型存在错误设定。该指标可用于诊断LLM推理系统的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI系统部署在医学、科学、法律等复杂领域，不仅需要产生正确答案，还需要在新证据出现时表示和更新对世界的不确定性信念。作者旨在研究LLM如何更新其概率信念，以及这种更新是否符合贝叶斯最优性。

Method: 提出将LLM视为信息处理规则的新技术，利用信息处理差距（information processing gap）来衡量LLM在证据下更新概率信念时的内部一致性。在多个实验中比较了LLM将证据融入信念的不同方法。

Result: 部分方法产生（接近）贝叶斯更新，另一些则使用学习的启发式方法。非贝叶斯启??式更新在下游任务性能上往往优于精确贝叶斯计算，表明LLM的世界概率模型存在错误设定。

Conclusion: 该测量方法可为LLM驱动的推理系统提供诊断工具，帮助识别系统中的问题。

Abstract: Modern AI systems are being deployed in complex domains such as medicine, science, and law, where it is important that they not only produce correct answers, but also represent and update uncertain beliefs about the world as new evidence arrives. We introduce the novel technique of studying LLMs as information processing rules and utilize the information processing gap to study the internal (in)consistencies of how LLMs update their probabilistic beliefs from evidence. Our extensive experiments evaluate multiple approaches in which LLMs can incorporate evidence into their beliefs. Some of these approaches produce (nearly) Bayesian updates; others seem to use a learned heuristic. Surprisingly, the non-Bayesian heuristic updates often outperform exact Bayesian computation in terms of downstream task performance -- indicating the LLMs' probabilistic models of the world are misspecified. Lastly, we show how our measure can provide diagnostics to identify issues with LLM-powered inferential systems.

</details>


### [150] [Tyche: One Step Flow for Efficient Probabilistic Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06916)
*Fan Xu,Yuan Gao,Kun Wang,Rui Su,Fenghua Ling,Hao Wu,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Tyche是一个单步条件流模型，通过一次函数评估即可实现高效的概率天气预测，在ERA5数据集上以1.5°和6小时分辨率达到了与多步生成基线和ECMWF IFS业务集合相当或更优的预报技能和校准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的集合预报系统推理成本随预报时长、集合规模和去噪步数线性增长，导致大规模业务化集合预报计算代价高昂。

Method: 提出Tyche模型：(1) 使用目标感知的平均速度流将高斯噪声直接映射为未来天气状态的单步传输；(2) 推导JVP正则化的整流目标，在不显式形成Jacobian的情况下强制时间自一致性；(3) 采用各向同性的Swin风格Transformer参数化输运场；(4) 引入基于rollout的微调阶段与课程CRPS校准监督。

Result: 在ERA5 1.5°和6小时分辨率实验中，Tyche仅用单步NFE即可达到或超越多步生成基线和ECMWF IFS业务集合的预报技能和校准性能。

Conclusion: 单步条件流模型能够在大幅降低推理成本的同时，实现与多步生成模型和业务系统相当的概率预报质量。

Abstract: Probabilistic weather forecasting requires not only accurate trajectories, but calibrated distributions over plausible atmospheric futures. Recent data-driven systems have achieved remarkable deterministic skill, and diffusion-based ensemble forecasters have substantially improved sample realism and uncertainty quantification. However, their inference cost scales with forecast horizon, ensemble size, and the number of denoising steps required for each transition, making large operational ensembles expensive. To address this, we present Tyche, a one-step conditional flow model for efficient probabilistic weather forecasting. Tyche models the conditional forecast distribution with a destination-aware average-velocity flow that maps Gaussian noise directly to future weather states in a single function evaluation (1-NFE). To make this one-step transport learnable in high-dimensional geophysical fields, we derive a JVP-regularized rectification objective that enforces temporal self-consistency across source and destination flow timesteps without explicitly forming Jacobians. The transport field is parameterized by an isotropic Swin-style transformer that preserves fine-scale spatial structure while remaining scalable on global grids. To improve ensemble reliability under autoregressive forecasting, we further introduce a rollout-based finetuning stage with curriculum CRPS calibration supervision. Experiments on ERA5 at 1.5$^\circ$ and 6-hour resolution show that our Tyche, using merely a single NFE, matches or exceeds the forecast skill and calibration of state-of-the-art multi-step generative baselines and the operational ECMWF IFS ensemble.

</details>


### [151] [Learned Lyapunov Shielding for Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06934)
*Giansalvo Cirrincione,Adriano Fagiolini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为Euler-Lagrange系统的Slotine-Li自适应控制器增加了三个学习组件：Cholesky参数化的结构化二次型Lyapunov函数、残差SAC策略和估计未建模动力学的PINN，并推导出无需在线QP求解器的闭式安全滤波器。证明了滤波器的全局可行性、指数稳定性、三时间尺度更新的KKT点几乎必然收敛以及证书的PAC泛化界。在2-DOF机械臂上跟踪误差降低41%，并在Franka Emika Panda上验证了工业级可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自适应控制方法在存在未建模动力学和参数不确定性的Euler-Lagrange系统中，难以同时保证稳定性、安全性和最优性，且往往需要在线求解优化问题，计算负担大。本文旨在将学习组件与经典自适应控制器结合，在保持理论保证的同时提升实际性能。

Method: 1) 结构化二次型Lyapunov函数V_ψ，通过Cholesky分解保证正定性；2) 残差SAC策略提供有界力矩修正；3) PINN估计未建模动力学；4) 基于单仿射约束V_ψ+αV_ψ≤0推导闭式安全滤波器；5) 三时间尺度策略-证书-乘子更新框架。

Result: 2-DOF机械臂上，标称摩擦力下跟踪误差降低41%，严苛摩擦力下降低24%。7-DOF Franka Emika Panda上验证了工业级可扩展性，同时识别了学习证书的冷启动病理现象及其实际部署影响。

Conclusion: 学习型证书是性能提升的主要来源；闭式安全滤波器有效且无需在线求解；三时间尺度更新可收敛到KKT点；但学习证书存在冷启动问题，需要在实际部署中加以注意。

Abstract: We augment the Slotine--Li adaptive controller for Euler--Lagrange systems with three learned components: a structured-quadratic Lyapunov function \(V_ψ\) whose positive-definiteness follows from a Cholesky parameterization, a residual Soft Actor--Critic policy that adds bounded torque corrections to the analytic baseline, and a physics-informed neural network that estimates unmodeled dynamics. A closed-form safety filter, derived from the single affine constraint \(\dot V_ψ+ αV_ψ\le 0\), projects every policy output onto the safe set without requiring an online QP solver. We prove: global feasibility of the filter under a drift-decay condition on the control-degeneracy set; exponential stability under exact shielding, with a robust extension whose margin depends on the PINN approximation error; almost-sure convergence of the three-timescale policy--certificate--multiplier updates to a KKT point; and a PAC generalization bound for the certificate over compacts. On a 2-DOF manipulator with nonlinear friction and variable payload, the learned certificate accounts for most of the empirical gain: tracking error drops by 41\% on nominal friction and 24\% on aggressive friction at the centroid of the training distribution. A 7-DOF scalability study on a Franka Emika Panda confirms clean convergence of the full pipeline at industrial scale, identifies the conditions under which gains over exact model-based baselines should and should not be expected, and documents a warm-start pathology of the learned certificate that has practical implications for deployment.

</details>


### [152] [A Reproducible Optimisation Protocol for Calibrating Prompt-Based Large Language Model Workflows in Evidence Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06937)
*Teo Susnjak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的校准工作流，用于将基于提示的大语言模型（LLM）应用于结构化证据综合任务。该方法将科学任务规则与可变的提示框架分离，通过标注示例和任务指标优化提示框架，并将校准后的工作流保存为可检查的人工制品。标题和摘要筛选作为验证案例，使用小型学生LLM执行任务、大型反思LLM引导提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的LLM在结构化证据综合任务中缺乏可复现的校准方法，提示工程往往依赖于手动调试和直觉，缺乏系统化、可审计的工作流来优化和固定提示配置。

Method: 将科学任务规则与提示框架分离；利用标注/参考示例和显式任务指标优化提示框架；使用DSPy和GEPA工具实现协议；采用小型学生LLM执行科学任务，大型反思LLM引导提示优化过程；将校准后的工作流保存为包含规范、指标、设置和评估轨迹的可检查人工制品。

Result: 展示了编译、人工制品往返过程，以及优化预算对小型学生模型性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该工作流可复现、可迁移，能够系统化校准LLM在结构化证据综合任务中的表现，底层的"任务规则-提示框架分离+指标驱动优化+人工制品保存"逻辑可迁移至其他支持结构化任务定义、指标引导搜索和人工制品重用的提示优化框架。

Abstract: This methods article presents a reproducible calibration workflow for prompt-based large language models (LLMs) in structured evidence-synthesis tasks. The method separates the rules that define the scientific task from the mutable prompt harness that frames and applies them. It optimises that harness against labelled or reference examples and an explicit task metric, then preserves the calibrated workflow as an inspectable artefact with its specification, metric, settings, and evaluation traces. The example code instantiates the protocol with DSPy and GEPA tools, but the underlying logic can transfer to other prompt-optimisation frameworks that support structured task definitions, metric-guided search, and artefact reuse. Title and abstract screening is the worked validation case because it provides labelled benchmark data and clear evaluation metrics. The demonstrated workflow uses a smaller student LLM for performing the scientific task execution and a larger reflection LLM to steer the prompt optimisation process during calibration. This work shows compilation, artefact round-tripping, and how optimisation budget affects a smaller student model.

</details>


### [153] [Bias and Uncertainty in LLM-as-a-Judge Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06939)
*James Fiedler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM-as-a-Judge评估中，基于原始判断的朴素估计存在系统性偏差，而共享校准的纠正估计在判断质量低或跨模型校准不稳定时可能产生严重偏差（包括符号反转），本文通过理论分析、模拟和案例研究提出了J和ΔJ诊断指标及报告指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge评估已成为基准模型性能的标准工具，但原始判断的朴素估计存在系统性偏差。虽然已有纠正偏见的估计器，但其可靠性严重依赖于判断质量和跨模型校准稳定性，且共享校准可能引入严重偏差，因此需要系统研究这些故障模式并提出诊断方法。

Method: 通过理论分析推导偏差产生机制，在判断质量J和跨模型校准不稳定性ΔJ的参数空间上进行仿真实验，并结合MMLU-Pro真实数据案例研究（出现符号反转现象），提出J和ΔJ作为诊断指标。

Result: 共享校准的比较估计在判断质量低或校准不稳定性高时可能产生严重偏差，包括以高置信度指向错误方向；真实数据案例中观察到符号反转现象。

Conclusion: 提出J和ΔJ作为诊断工具用于判断纠正估计（尤其是共享校准比较）是否可靠，并为LLM-as-a-Judge评估提供报告指南。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation has become a standard tool for assessing base model performance. However, characterizing performance via the naive estimator, i.e., raw judge outputs, is systematically biased. Recent work has proposed estimators to correct this bias, but their reliability depends critically on judge quality and, for model comparisons, on calibration stability. Sharing calibration across compared models is practically attractive but can introduce severe bias, including cases where the comparison estimate points in the wrong direction with high apparent confidence. We study these failure modes through analytical results, simulations over judge quality ($J$) and cross-model calibration instability ($ΔJ$), and a real-data MMLU-Pro case study with sign reversal. We propose $J$ and $ΔJ$ as diagnostics for when corrected estimates, especially shared-calibration comparisons, are likely unreliable, and provide reporting guidance for LaaJ evaluation.

</details>


### [154] [Adaptive Memory Decay for Log-Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06946)
*Yaxita Amin,Helen Zichen Li,Mengfan Zhang,Samet Ayhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过学习输入相关的衰减参数λ来改进对数线性注意力模型，使其能够自适应地为Fenwick树各层级分配权重，在保持对数线性复杂度不变的前提下，在长程记忆任务上显著优于固定衰减的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对数线性注意力模型使用固定的、与输入无关的记忆衰减参数λ，在所有层级和位置分配均匀权重，这种刚性设计无法根据内容动态调整记忆权重，限制了模型在长程记忆场景下的性能。

Method: 通过一个轻量级两层MLP从输入中学习衰减参数λ，为每个token和每个Fenwick树层级生成输入依赖的衰减值；使用Softplus激活函数使各层级独立缩放，避免了Softmax引入的层级间竞争。

Result: 在关联召回、选择性复制和语言建模任务上，输入依赖的衰减方法一致优于固定衰减基线，在长程记忆设置中改进最为显著，解决了基线模型λ退化或完全崩溃的问题。

Conclusion: 输入依赖的衰减参数使对数线性注意力在保持log-linear计算复杂度不变和极小参数量增加的前提下，显著提升了长程上下文建模能力，有效解决了固定衰减带来的刚性限制。

Abstract: Sequence models face a fundamental tradeoff between memory capacity and computational efficiency. Transformers achieve expressive context modeling at quadratic cost, while linear attention and state-space models run in linear time by compressing context into a fixed-size hidden state, inherently limiting recall. Log-linear attention navigates this tradeoff by organizing memory across a Fenwick tree hierarchy, growing its hidden state logarithmically with sequence length at log-linear compute cost. However, its memory decay parameter λ is fixed and independent of the input, assigning uniform weights across all hierarchy levels regardless of the content, which introduces unnecessary rigidity. We propose learning λ directly from the input via a lightweight two-layer MLP, producing per-token, per-level decay that adapts to content rather than position. A softplus activation lets each Fenwick tree level scale independently, avoiding the inter-level competition that softmax introduces. This modification preserves log-linear complexity exactly and adds negligible parameter overhead. We evaluate on associative recall, selective copying, and language modeling, finding that input-dependent decay consistently outperforms the baseline, with the largest gains in long-range memory settings where baseline λ degrades or collapses entirely.

</details>


### [155] [Rollback-Free Stable Brick Structures Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06947)
*Chenhui Xu,Ziyue Bai,Fuxun Yu,Heng Huang,Jinjun Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于强化学习的砖块结构生成方法，通过在训练阶段优化物理有效性，实现了无需回滚的稳定砖块结构生成，推理速度提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归3D生成模型依赖外部物理模拟器在推理时进行拒绝采样和逐砖回滚，效率极低，无法高效生成满足重力和互连性约束的稳定砖块结构。

Method: 采用强化学习范式，将物理有效性约束从测试时纠正转移到训练时策略优化，通过装配级奖励函数（碰撞避免、全局连通性、结构互锁、形状符合度）让模型内化物理先验。

Result: 实现了首个无需回滚的稳定砖块结构生成，生成质量达到SOTA，推理速度提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 强化学习范式能有效替代传统基于物理模拟器的测试时纠正策略，在保证生成质量的同时大幅提升推理效率。

Abstract: While autoregressive models have advanced 3D generation, creating physically stable brick structures remains a challenge due to the strict requirements of gravity and interconnectivity. Existing approaches rely on external physical simulators during inference to perform rejection sampling and brick-by-brick rollbacks, which severely bottlenecks efficiency. To address this, we propose a reinforcement learning paradigm that shifts physical validity enforcement from test-time correction to training-time policy optimization. By utilizing assembly-level rewards, the model optimizes for collision avoidance, global connectivity, structural interlocking, and shape conformity. This paradigm allows the model to internalize physical priors, enabling the first rollback-free generation of stable brick structures. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art generation quality while accelerating inference speed by orders of magnitude. Our code and dataset are available at https://github.com/miniHuiHui/STABLE. Our models are available at https://huggingface.co/miniHui/STABLE.

</details>


### [156] [$f$-Divergence Regularized RLHF: Two Tales of Sampling and Unified Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06977)
*Di Wu,Chengshuai Shi,Jing Yang,Cong Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为在线RLHF建立了通用f-散度正则化的理论框架，提出了两种算法并证明了O(log T)遗憾界和O(1/T)次优性差距，首次为一般f-散度正则化下的在线RLHF提供了可证明的性能保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLHF大多使用反向KL散度正则化，但近期实验开始探索其他散度（如前向KL、卡方散度），然而缺乏对这些通用f-散度正则化的统一理论理解。

Method: 提出两种算法：1）扩展经典乐观主义原则，设计探索奖励；2）利用f-散度正则化下最优策略对奖励扰动的敏感性。

Result: 两种算法均实现了O(log T)的遗憾界和O(1/T)的次优性差距，首次为通用f-散度正则化下的在线RLHF提供了性能界。

Conclusion: 本文为在线RLHF中一般f-散度正则化建立了统一的理论框架，证明了所提算法的统计效率，填补了该领域的理论空白。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) has become a cornerstone technique for post-training large language models. While most existing approaches rely on the reverse KL-regularization, recent empirical studies have begun exploring alternative divergences (e.g., forward KL, chi-squared) as regularizers in RLHF. However, a unified theoretical understanding of general $f$-divergence regularization remains under-explored. To fill this gap, this work develops a comprehensive theoretical framework for online RLHF with a general $f$-divergence regularized objective. Rather than treating each possible divergence function individually, we adopt a holistic perspective across the entire function class and propose two algorithms based on distinct sampling principles. The first extends the classical optimism principle with a carefully designed exploration bonus, while the second introduces a new method that exploits the sensitivity of the optimal policy to reward perturbations under $f$-divergence regularization. Theoretical analysis shows that $O(\log T)$ regret and $O(1/T)$ sub-optimality gap are achievable, establishing provable efficiency of both algorithms and, to the best of our knowledge, the first performance bounds for online RLHF under general $f$-divergence regularization.

</details>


### [157] [PLOT: Progressive Localization via Optimal Transport in Neural Causal Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06979)
*Jonathn Chang,Arya Datla,Ziv Goldfeld*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PLOT（渐进定位通过最优传输）是一个基于最优传输的框架，通过分析抽象变量与神经干预的输出效应几何来定位因果变量，实现高效的因果抽象对齐，在无需先验知识的情况下快速找到干预句柄，并可引导DAS达到同等精度但大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果抽象方法（如DAS）需要学习表达性子空间干预，但相关神经位点先验未知，搜索候选位点计算负担沉重，亟需高效的定位引擎。

Method: PLOT通过最优传输耦合抽象变量与候选神经位点，从输出效应几何中学习全局软对应关系，并将其校准为干预句柄。在简单场景中使用单次单神经元耦合；在大型模型中逐步从粗粒度位点（token、时间步、层）定位到细粒度支持（坐标组、PCA跨度），并可选择性引导DAS。

Result: 仅使用传输的PLOT句柄在速度上极为高效且准确率有竞争力；PLOT引导的DAS达到DAS级准确率，但仅消耗完整DAS运行时的一小部分。

Conclusion: PLOT为大规模因果抽象研究提供了一个高效的定位引擎，显著降低了计算成本，同时保持了高精度。

Abstract: Causal abstraction offers a principled framework for mechanistic interpretability, aligning a high-level causal model with the low-level computation realized by a neural network through counterfactual intervention analysis. Existing methods such as distributed alignment search (DAS) learn expressive subspace interventions, but the relevant neural site is unknown a priori, so finding a handle requires a computationally burdensome search over candidate sites. We introduce PLOT (Progressive Localization via Optimal Transport), a transport-based framework that localizes causal variables from the output effect geometry of abstract and neural interventions. PLOT fits an optimal transport coupling between abstract variables and candidate neural sites, yielding a global soft correspondence that can be calibrated into intervention handles. In simple settings, a single coupling over individual neurons suffices. In larger models, PLOT is applied progressively, moving from coarse sites such as tokens, timesteps, or layers to finer supports such as coordinate groups or PCA spans, and optionally guiding DAS based on the localized signal. Across experiments of increasing complexity, transport-only PLOT handles are exceedingly fast and competitive on accuracy, while PLOT-guided DAS reaches DAS-level accuracy at a fraction of full DAS runtime, providing an efficient localization engine for causal abstraction research at scale.

</details>


### [158] [FastOmniTMAE: Parallel Clause Learning for Scalable and Hardware-Efficient Tsetlin Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06982)
*Ahmed K. Kadhim,Lei Jiao,Rishad Shafik,Ole-Christoffer Granmo,Mayur Kishor Shende*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastOmniTMAE 通过两阶段并行训练（评估与更新）替代 Omni TM-AE 的顺序依赖，实现最高 5 倍训练加速，并在 SoC-FPGA 平台实现资源高效部署，在保持嵌入质量的同时显著提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Omni TM-AE 虽将 Tsetlin Machine 应用于静态嵌入学习，但其训练过程存在顺序依赖，导致训练速度缓慢，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 提出 FastOmniTMAE，将 Omni TM-AE 的顺序训练重构为两阶段并行过程：评估阶段与更新阶段。此外，在 SoC-FPGA 平台上实现可复用加速器，解决 TM 在常规 GPU 上效率有限的问题。

Result: 在分类任务上实现最高 5 倍训练加速；在 Spearman 和 Kendall 相似度指标下保持可比嵌入质量；在资源受限 FPGA 上达到 0.669 相似度分数，在 UltraScale+ SoC 上达到 0.696。

Conclusion: FastOmniTMAE 在保持嵌入质量的前提下显著加速了逻辑基嵌入训练，并在小型硬件平台上实现了高效部署，验证了 Tsetlin Machine 在嵌入学习中的实用潜力。

Abstract: Embedding models in natural language processing (NLP) increasingly rely on deep architectures such as BERT, while simpler models such as Word2Vec provide efficient representations but limited interpretability. The Tsetlin Machine (TM) offers an alternative logic-based learning paradigm. Omni TM Autoencoder (Omni TM-AE) applies this paradigm to static embedding by exploiting automaton state distributions within a single clause layer, but its training process remains slow. In this work, we propose FastOmniTMAE, a reformulation of Omni TM-AE that replaces sequential training dependencies with a two-stage parallel process: evaluation and update. Using a Single-Run Multi-Environment Benchmark covering classification, similarity, and clustering, FastOmniTMAE achieves up to 5$\times$ faster training in classification while maintaining comparable embedding quality under both Spearman and Kendall similarity measures. To address the limited efficiency of TM training on conventional GPUs, we further implement FastOmniTMAE as a reusable accelerator on SoC-FPGA platforms. The Multi-Hardware Benchmark shows that FastOmniTMAE achieves similarity scores of 0.669 on a resource-constrained FPGA and 0.696 on an UltraScale+ SoC, demonstrating efficient logic-based embedding training with a small hardware footprint.

</details>


### [159] [Response Time Enhances Alignment with Heterogeneous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06987)
*Federico Echenique,Alireza Fallah,Baihe Huang,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过收集用户响应时间这一简单辅助信号，结合漂移扩散模型（DDM），在匿名标签者仅提供单次选择的情况下，也能一致估计人群平均偏好，有效纠正传统二元选择数据导致的偏好扭曲和政策偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法依赖聚合的二元选择数据，但忽略了标注者偏好的异质性和匿名性，导致真实人群平均偏好不可识别，学习到的策略存在系统性偏差。

Method: 将每次决策建模为漂移扩散模型（DDM），利用响应时间作为辅助信号，提出一个异质性偏好的一致估计量，在理论上证明其渐近收敛到真实平均偏好。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，该方法一致优于仅依赖二元选择的基线方法，后者会停滞在偏差下限；即使在每个匿名标签者仅贡献一个选择的最极端情况下，该方法依然有效。

Conclusion: 响应时间几乎零成本记录且无需用户追踪或身份识别，为未来数据收集管线提升社会效益开辟了新途径，无需用户级标识或重复征询。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) to human preferences typically relies on aggregating pooled feedback into a single reward model. However, this standard approach assumes that all labelers share the same underlying preferences, ignoring the fact that real-world labelers are highly heterogeneous and usually anonymous. Consequently, relying solely on binary choice data fundamentally distorts the learned policy, making the true population-average preference unidentifiable. To overcome this critical limitation, we demonstrate that augmenting preference datasets with a simple, secondary signal -- the user's response time -- can restore the identifiability of the population's average preference. By modeling each decision as a Drift-Diffusion Model (DDM), we introduce a novel, consistent estimator of heterogeneous preferences that successfully corrects the distortions of standard choice-only labels. We prove that our estimator asymptotically converges to the true average preference even in extreme cases where each anonymous labeler contributes only a single choice. Empirically, across both synthetic and real-world datasets, our method consistently outperforms standard baselines that otherwise fail and plateau at a bias floor. Because response times are essentially free to record and require zero user tracking or identification, our results bring promises and open up new opportunities for future data-collection pipelines to improve the social benefit without requiring user-level identifiers or repeated elicitations.

</details>


### [160] [Dual-Agent Co-Training for Health Coaching via Implicit Adversarial Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07011)
*Da Long,Lingyi Fu,Diya Michelle Rao,Jasmine Ruales Carrera,Yang Bai,Shandian Zhe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双智能体框架，通过交互式协同训练健康教练智能体和客户模拟器，利用DPO优化教练、对抗性训练客户，以提升AI健康教练的辅导质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于训练有素的人类教练稀缺且服务成本高昂，AI健康教练成为可扩展且经济实惠的替代方案。现有方法仅优化单侧交互（固定环境训练对话智能体或固定助手训练客户模拟器），限制了交互空间的探索，难以有效提升目标智能体的能力边界。

Method: 提出双智能体协同训练框架：教练智能体使用DPO基于多维LLM评判器识别的帕累托优势响应对进行优化；客户模拟器通过反向偏好进行对抗性训练，形成隐式对抗训练动态。该协同训练过程具有自然随机博弈解释。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在多个重要维度上有效提升了教练质量。

Conclusion: 双智能体协同训练框架比单侧优化方法更有效地探索交互空间，提升AI健康教练在多个维度的表现，且具有随机博弈的理论基础。

Abstract: Motivational-interviewing-based health coaching is an effective approach for improving mental health and promoting healthy behavior change. However, the scarcity of trained human coaches and the high cost of coaching services make such support inaccessible to many people who could benefit from it. This motivates the development of AI health coaches that can provide scalable and affordable support. Existing methods typically optimize only one side of the interaction: they either train a dialogue agent against a fixed client environment or train a client simulator against a fixed assistant. This one-sided setup can limit exploration of the interaction space and may be inefficient at developing the capabilities required by the target agent and pushing its performance boundaries. In this paper, we propose a dual-agent framework that interactively co-trains both the health coach agent and the client simulator. The coach is optimized with DPO using Pareto-dominant response pairs identified by a multi-dimensional LLM judge. In turn, the client is trained adversarially by reversing these preferences, inducing an implicit adversarial training dynamic. We further show that this co-training process admits a natural stochastic-game interpretation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method effectively improves coaching quality across several important dimensions.

</details>


### [161] [Self Driving Datasets: From 20 Million Papers to Nuanced Biomedical Knowledge at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07022)
*Haydn Jones,Yimeng Zeng,Alden Rose,Li S. Yifei,Yining Huang,Kaiwen Wu,Jiaming Liang,Maggie Ziyu Huan,Yoseph Barash,Cesar de la Fuente-Nunez,Osbert Bastani,Zachary Ives,Mark Yatskar,Jacob R. Gardner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Starling系统，基于LLM从PubMed中自动提取结构化数据。系统包含三个贡献：(1) 基于9个生物医学本体的实体标注管线，在2250万篇论文、2.5万亿token的PubMed语料库中标注45亿实体；(2) 混合稀疏-稠密检索支持实体过滤语义查询；(3) 多智能体深度研究系统Starling，根据自然语言任务描述自动设计检索过滤器、生成提取模式并输出结构化记录。在血脑屏障渗透性、口服生物利用度、急性毒性（LD50）、基因-疾病关联、蛋白亚细胞定位和化学反应6个任务上产生约630万条记录，前沿模型拒绝率0.6-7.7%，远低于人工精选数据库的错误率（如BBB_Martins为16.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工维护的生物医学数据库成本高昂、更新滞后于前沿文献，且丢弃了实验上下文，掩盖了评估数据正确性和覆盖范围所需的细微信息。因此需要一种自动化、成本效益高的方法将PubMed转化为结构化数据集。

Method: (1) 基于9个生物医学本体的LLM实体标注管线，对2250万篇PubMed论文标注45亿实体；(2) 混合稀疏-稠密检索支持实体过滤语义查询；(3) Starling多智能体深度研究系统，根据自然语言任务自动设计检索过滤器、生成提取模式并输出带支持文本的结构化记录。

Result: 在6个任务上产生约630万条记录（每任务9.1万-300万条），部分为最大公开数据集。前沿模型拒绝率0.6-7.7%，远低于人工精选数据库错误率（如16.5%）。支持文本保留了表格数据库丢弃的细微信息（如口服生物利用度可能依赖进食/空腹状态）。

Conclusion: 该语料库、检索系统和智能体为AI驱动的治疗设计奠定了基础。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Manually curated biomedical repositories -- spanning bioactivity, genomics, and chemistry -- are expensive to maintain, lag behind primary literature, and discard experimental context, obscuring nuances needed to assess data correctness and coverage. We show that PubMed itself can be autonomously and cost-effectively turned into structured datasets that are larger, more nuanced, and more accurate than the curated databases they replace. We present three coupled contributions: (1) an LLM-based entity-tagging pipeline, grounded in nine biomedical ontologies, that tags 4.5B entities across 19 categories in a 22.5M-paper, 2.5T-token PubMed corpus; (2) hybrid sparse-dense retrieval supporting entity-filtered semantic queries over the tagged corpus; and (3) Starling, a multi-agent deep research system that, given only a natural-language task description, designs precision- and recall-targeted retrieval filters, induces an extraction schema, and emits structured records with nuance-rich fields and supporting passages. Across six tasks -- blood-brain barrier permeability, oral bioavailability, acute toxicity (LD50), gene-disease associations, protein subcellular localization, and chemical reactions -- Starling produces ~6.3M records (91K-3M per task); several are, to our knowledge, the largest public datasets for their property. Frontier-model rejection of our extractions is 0.6-7.7% across tasks, far below error rates we measure on widely used curated counterparts (e.g., 16.5% on BBB_Martins, 7.3% on Bioavailability_Ma). Beyond scale and accuracy, the supporting passages carry nuance tabular databases discard -- e.g., oral bioavailability may depend on fed vs. fasted state. Together, the corpus, retrieval, and agent establish a foundation for AI-driven therapeutic design. Code and datasets: https://github.com/starling-labs/starling.

</details>


### [162] [Delulu: A Verified Multi-Lingual Benchmark for Code Hallucination Detection in Fill-in-the-Middle Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07024)
*Mahdi Erfanian,Nelson Daniel Troncoso,Aashna Garg,Amabel Gale,Xiaoyu Liu,Pareesa Ameneh Golnari,Shengyu Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Delulu是一个包含1,951个多语言Fill-in-the-Middle代码补全样本的基准测试，专门用于检测LLM在代码生成中的幻觉问题。最优模型仅达84.5% pass@1，表明该任务存在固有难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在Fill-in-the-Middle代码生成任务中频繁产生看似合理但实际错误的幻觉（如虚构API、无效参数、未定义变量、不存在导入），这些错误能通过表面审查但引入运行时错误，缺乏专门针对此类问题的多语言基准测试。

Method: 通过对抗性流程构建：前缘LLM生成看似合理的幻觉→四种不同判别模型评估→基于嵌入的聚类挖掘更难的样本→Docker容器验证正确补全能编译而幻觉变体产生预期运行时错误→人类专家审查移除偏差或可轻易判定的样本。最终得到覆盖7种语言、4种幻觉类型的1,951个样本。

Result: 评估11个开源FIM模型（0.5B-32B参数）：最强模型（Qwen2.5-Coder）仅达84.5% pass@1，所有模型族的Edit Similarity不超过0.77，且每个模型族都在非平凡比例的样本上产生与幻觉对齐的补全。

Conclusion: Delulu揭示的难度是任务固有的而非特定模型族特有的，现有最强模型在FIM幻觉检测任务上仍有显著提升空间。已公开基准测试、Docker容器和评估框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models for code generation frequently produce hallucinations in Fill-in-the-Middle (FIM) tasks -- plausible but incorrect completions such as invented API methods, invalid parameters, undefined variables, or non-existent imports. These failures pass superficial review yet introduce runtime errors. We introduce Delulu, a verified multi-lingual benchmark of 1,951 FIM samples across 7 languages and 4 hallucination types. Samples are curated through an adversarial pipeline: a frontier LLM generates plausible hallucinations, four diverse judge models evaluate them, embedding-based clustering mines progressively harder examples, self-contained Docker containers verify that golden completions compile while hallucinated variants produce the expected runtime error, and a final human-expert review removes any remaining biased or trivially decidable samples. We evaluate 11 open-weight FIM models from five families spanning 0.5B-32B parameters: a six-point Qwen2.5-Coder scaling slate, plus a cross-family slate (CodeLlama, DeepSeek-Coder-V2, StarCoder2). The strongest model reaches only 84.5% pass@1, no family exceeds 0.77 Edit Similarity, and every family produces hallucination-aligned completions on a non-trivial share of samples, confirming that the difficulty exposed by Delulu is task-intrinsic rather than family-specific. We release the benchmark, containers, and evaluation framework at https://github.com/microsoft/delulu.

</details>


### [163] [A Systematic Investigation of The RL-Jailbreaker in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07032)
*Montaser Mohammedalamen,Kevin Roice,Reginald McLean,Alyssa Lefaivre Škopac*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统分解了强化学习（RL）越狱攻击框架，将其拆解为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法措施（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑造）两个维度，揭示了环境形式化（尤其是密集奖励和延长回合长度）是越狱成功的主要驱动力。实验表明RL越狱器成功攻破了所有目标模型和安全防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RL已成功用于框架越狱攻击，但对其为何成功的机制性理解仍不完整。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统识别RL越狱成功的结构性决定因素。

Method: 将RL越狱框架分解为问题形式化（奖励函数、动作空间、回合长度）和算法措施（RL算法、训练数据、奖励塑造）两个维度，通过实验分析各因素对越狱成功率的贡献。

Result: RL越狱器成功攻破了所有目标模型和安全防护；环境形式化（密集奖励和延长回合长度）是越狱成功的主要驱动力。

Conclusion: 该分析为提高RL越狱器效率提供了工具，并最终有助于加固生成模型以抵御基于RL的攻击。

Abstract: The evolution of generative models from next-token predictors to autonomous engines of complex systems necessitates rigorous safety hardening. Adversarial jailbreaking, the strategic manipulation of models to elicit harmful output, remains a primary threat to safe deployment. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) frames jailbreaking as a multi-step attack through sequential optimization, a mechanistic understanding of why the framework succeeds remains incomplete. To fill this gap, we present the first systematic decomposition of RL jailbreaking. We deconstruct the framework into problem formalization (reward function, action space, episode length), and algorithmic measures (RL algorithm, training data, reward-shaping) to identify the structural determinants of adversarial success. Our results reveal that the RL-jailbreaker successfully compromised all targeted models and safeguards. Through this first-of-its-kind analysis, we demonstrate that environment formalization, specifically dense rewards and extended episode lengths, is the primary driver of jailbreaking success. This work provides a tool for improving RL-jailbreaker efficiency and, ultimately, harden generative models resistant to RL-based attacks.

</details>


### [164] [Unlocking High-Fidelity Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra via Dual-Stream Line Graph Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07048)
*Xujun Che,Xiuxia Du,Depeng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DualLGD双流线图扩散模型，将分子图去噪分解为原子级和键级两个耦合子问题，通过双流架构和交叉注意力机制实现原子-键信息的显式同步，在质谱分子生成任务上取得约3倍于此前最优方法的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单流图扩散方法中原子与键信息只能跨层隐式同步，存在架构瓶颈；质谱到分子的逆问题存在原子级与键级推理的循环依赖关系，需要显式的双流分离与同步机制。

Method: 提出DualLGD：1) 将分子图去噪重构为原子级和键级两个耦合子问题，各自在独立表示空间中运算；2) 利用线图作为键空间的数学构造，使键角、二面角、共轭链、环等结构对应键之间的局部拓扑模式；3) 设计关联约束的双向交叉注意力机制，每层同步两个流，确保原子仅关注其入射键、键仅关注其端点原子。

Result: 在NPLIB1和MassSpecGym基准上分别达到34.37%和23.89%的top-1准确率，约为此前最优方法的3倍；消融实验证明架构是主要改进来源，无需预训练的DualLGD已超越此前最佳的全预训练模型。

Conclusion: 双流分离+线图+交叉注意力的架构设计比聚合核选择更重要，有效解决了原子-键推理的循环依赖问题，为分子逆问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: De novo molecular generation from tandem mass spectra is a challenging inverse problem whose core difficulty lies in the circular dependency between atom-level and bond-level reasoning: determining a bond's type requires knowing its endpoint atoms' chemical environment, yet an atom's environment is in turn defined by its incident bonds. Existing graph diffusion methods process atoms and bonds within a single computation stream, where atom-bond information synchronization can only occur implicitly across layers. We argue that this single-stream paradigm, rather than the choice of any particular aggregation kernel, is a key architectural bottleneck. We propose DualLGD (Dual-stream Line Graph Diffusion), which reformulates molecular graph denoising as the alternating solution of two coupled subproblems: atom-level reasoning and bond-level reasoning, each operating in its own dedicated representation space. The line graph provides a natural mathematical construction for the bond space, in which bond angles, dihedrals, conjugation chains, and rings correspond to local topological motifs between bonds. Incidence-constrained bidirectional cross-attention synchronizes the two streams at every layer, ensuring that each atom attends only to its incident bonds and vice versa, respecting the fundamental chemical principle that an atom's environment is determined by its bonding context. On the NPLIB1 and MassSpecGym benchmarks, DualLGD achieves top-1 accuracy of 34.37\% and 23.89\%, approximately $3\times$ the previous state of the art. Ablation studies confirm the architecture as the primary source of improvement: DualLGD without any pre-training already surpasses the previous best fully pretrained model.

</details>


### [165] [Towards Differentially Private Reinforcement Learning with General Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07049)
*Yi He,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为具有通用函数逼近的差分隐私在线强化学习提供了理论保证，在模型无关设置下实现了与线性情况相同的遗憾界 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$，同时发现了线性函数近似下私有RL中的基本差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私在线强化学习的理论工作仅限于表格和线性设置，缺乏对通用函数逼近场景的理论保证。本文旨在弥合这一差距，为更一般的函数逼近场景提供隐私保护下的理论分析。

Method: 结合批量策略更新方案与指数机制，并辅以新颖的遗憾分析。具体而言，利用批量更新下的覆盖性（coverability）复杂度度量进行遗憾界推导。

Result: 在通用函数逼近的模型无关设置下，实现了与线性情况相匹配的遗憾界 $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$；首次建立了基于覆盖性复杂度度量的批量更新在线RL的遗憾界；揭示了线性函数近似下私有RL中的基本差距。

Conclusion: 本文证明了即使在通用函数逼近下，差分隐私在线强化学习的遗憾界也能达到与线性情况相当的水平，同时澄清了该领域的理论图景，指出了现有线性函数近似结果中的不足。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical guarantees for differentially private online reinforcement learning (RL) with general function approximation, extending beyond prior work restricted to tabular and linear settings. Our approach combines a batched policy update scheme with the exponential mechanism, together with a novel regret analysis. We show that, even under general function approximation, the regret in the model-free setting under differential privacy matches the state of the art for the linear case, scaling as $\widetilde{O}(K^{3/5})$, where $K$ denotes the number of episodes. As an important by-product, we also establish the first regret bound for online RL with batch update that depends on the standard complexity measure of coverability, complementing existing results based on a newly introduced Eluder-Condition class. In addition, we uncover fundamental gaps in recent results for private RL with linear function approximation, thereby clarifying its landscape.

</details>


### [166] [Dr. Post-Training: A Data Regularization Perspective on LLM Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07063)
*Pingbang Hu,Xueshen Liu,Z. Morley Mao,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dr. Post-Training 框架，将通用训练数据重新概念化为数据诱导的正则化器，通过构建模型更新方向的可行集并投影目标数据更新方向，有效防止过拟合。该方法在 SFT、RLHF、RLVR 任务上均优于现有数据选择方法，并具备高效的系统实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 后训练中，高质量目标数据稀缺而通用训练数据丰富但存在对齐偏差，现有数据选择方法仅从"选择"角度处理通用数据，未能充分利用其正则化价值来防止过拟合。

Method: 提出 Dr. Post-Training 框架：每一步训练中，用通用训练数据构建模型更新方向的可行集，然后将稀缺目标数据指定的更新方向投影到该可行集上。标准训练和现有数据选择方法均为此框架的特例，对应不同的正则化强度与偏差-方差权衡点。同时引入系统级优化以降低大规模 LLM 训练的开销。

Result: 在 SFT、RLHF 和 RLVR 三个后训练场景中，所提方法一致优于最先进的数据选择基线方法，系统基准测试也验证了其训练效率。

Conclusion: 将通用数据作为数据诱导的正则化器（而非数据选择池）的视角更为合理，提供了更丰富的设计空间和更灵活的偏差-方差权衡，且在实际 LLM 规模下高效可行。

Abstract: Data selection methods address a critical challenge in LLM post-training: effectively leveraging scarce, high-fidelity target data alongside abundant but imperfectly aligned general training data. In this work, we move beyond the data-selection framing and introduce Dr. Post-Training (Data-Regularized Post-Training), a novel framework that reconceptualizes general training data as a data-induced regularizer that prevents overfitting to the scarce target objective, rather than serving as a pool for selection. Specifically, our framework proposes that at each training step, construct a feasible set of model update directions using the general training data, and project the model update direction specified by the scarce target data onto that feasible set. Standard training and existing data selection methods arise as special cases with different choices of the data-induced regularizer, and these methods correspond to different points on a bias--variance spectrum with different regularization strength. Building on this view, we propose a family of methods offering a richer design space and more flexible bias--variance tradeoffs. For practical LLM-scale use, we introduce careful system optimizations that realize these methods with minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across SFT, RLHF, and RLVR show that our methods consistently outperform state-of-the-art data selection baselines, and system benchmarks confirm their efficiency.

</details>


### [167] [ModelLens: Finding the Best for Your Task from Myriads of Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07075)
*Rui Cai,Weijie Jacky Mo,Xiaofei Wen,Qiyao Ma,Wenhui Zhu,Xiwen Chen,Muhao Chen,Zhe Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ModelLens是一个统一框架，通过从公开排行榜交互中学习隐式能力图谱，无需在目标数据集上运行候选模型即可推荐未见过的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源模型生态已包含数十万预训练模型，但为新数据集挑选最佳模型越来越不可行：新模型和未基准测试的数据集持续涌现，实践者缺乏任何先验记录。现有方法只处理了这种野外场景的碎片化部分。

Method: 核心洞察是公开排行榜交互虽然分散且有噪声，但共同描绘了模型跨异构评估设置的隐式能力图谱。通过学习模型-数据集-指标三元组上的性能感知潜在空间，ModelLens无需在目标数据集上运行候选模型即可对未见模型进行排序。

Result: 在包含162万评估记录、4.7万模型和9.6K数据集的新基准上，ModelLens超越依赖元数据或需要在目标数据集上运行候选模型的基线方法。其推荐的Top-K池在多种路由方法上提升高达81%，案例研究证实了对文本和视觉-语言任务的泛化能力。

Conclusion: ModelLens为野外模型推荐提供了一个统一框架，有效解决了新模型和新数据集的冷启动问题，具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: The open-source model ecosystem now contains hundreds of thousands of pretrained models, yet picking the best model for a new dataset is increasingly infeasible: new models and unbenchmarked datasets emerge continuously, leaving practitioners with no prior records on either side. Existing approaches handle only fragments of this in-the-wild setting: AutoML and transferability estimation select models from small predefined pools or require expensive per-model forward passes on the target dataset, while model routing presupposes a given candidate pool. We introduce ModelLens, a unified framework for model recommendation in the wild. Our key insight is that public leaderboard interactions, though scattered and noisy, collectively trace out an implicit atlas of model capabilities across heterogeneous evaluation settings, a signal rich enough to learn from directly. By learning a performance-aware latent space over model--dataset--metric tuples, ModelLens ranks unseen models on unseen datasets without running candidates on the target dataset. On a new benchmark of 1.62M evaluation records spanning 47K models and 9.6K datasets, ModelLens surpasses baselines that either rely on metadata alone or require running each candidate on the target dataset. Its recommended Top-K pools further improve multiple representative routing methods by up to 81% across diverse QA benchmarks. Case studies on recently released benchmarks further confirm generalization to both text and vision-language tasks.

</details>


### [168] [Test-Time Compositional Generalization in Diffusion Models via Concept Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07078)
*Zekun Wang,Anant Gupta,Tianyi Zhu,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需预定义概念库的测试时组合生成方法，通过利用扩散模型时间索引分数几何中的密度模式概念，从单次分布外查询中恢复局部密度模式，并通过产品-of-专家模型组合生成新配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的组合生成方法通常假设相关概念或条件信号已经可用，无法处理查询特定的概念发现与组合。本文旨在探索预训练扩散模型能否从时间索引分数中自主发现查询特定概念并在测试时进行组合。

Method: (1) 从分布外查询出发，对扩散模型分数 s_θ(x_t,t) 进行梯度上升以恢复多个加噪时间步的局部密度模式；(2) 将这些模式映射到干净空间的高斯分布；(3) 通过子模似然目标贪婪选择相关原型；(4) 组合成产品-of-专家（PoE）教师模型，得到解析分数；(5) 通过无分类器引导直接采样，或生成样本池用于训练新类别嵌入和低秩适配器。

Result: 在ColorMNIST和CelebA的组合基准上，解析PoE采样器和低秩适配模型均优于仅查询基线和最近训练类别基线。

Conclusion: 扩散模型的时间索引分数几何中包含可重用的密度模式概念，支持无需预定义概念库的测试时组合生成。

Abstract: Compositional generalization requires models to produce novel configurations from familiar parts. In diffusion models, prior compositional generation methods typically assume that the relevant concepts or conditioning signals are already available. We instead ask whether a pretrained diffusion model can discover query-specific concepts from the time-indexed scores it learns for the noisy marginals $p_t(x_t)$ and compose them at test time. Given a single out-of-distribution query, our method performs gradient ascent on $s_θ(x_t,t) \approx \nabla_{x_t}\log p_t(x_t)$ at multiple noising timesteps to recover local density modes, maps these modes into clean-space Gaussians, greedily selects relevant prototypes with a submodular likelihood objective, and combines them into a product-of-experts (PoE) teacher model with an analytic score. This teacher model can be sampled directly through classifier-free guidance or used to generate a sample pool for training a new class embedding and low-rank adapter. On held-out composition benchmarks built from ColorMNIST and CelebA, both the analytic PoE sampler and the low-rank adapted model outperform query-only and nearest trained-class baselines. These results suggest that the time-indexed score geometry of the diffusion model contains reusable density-mode concepts that support test-time compositional generation without a predefined concept library.

</details>


### [169] [Actor-Critic with Active Importance Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07094)
*Majid Molaei,Gabor Paczolay,Matteo Papini,Alberto Maria Metelli,Marcello Restelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AISAC算法，通过重要性采样优化行为策略以降低策略梯度估计方差，在连续动作空间中采用高斯行为策略和交叉熵最小化，实验表明能提升学习速度、样本效率和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Actor-Critic方法在策略梯度估计中存在高方差问题，导致学习不稳定和样本效率低下，需要设计一种能主动降低梯度估计方差的行为策略优化方法。

Method: 基于重要性采样原理，优化行为策略以最小化梯度方差同时保持无偏估计；在连续动作空间中使用高斯行为策略，通过交叉熵最小化进行优化。

Result: 在Inverted Pendulum和Half Cheetah任务上，AISAC相比标准Actor-Critic方法在收敛速度、样本效率和训练稳定性方面均有提升，且在不同超参数设置下表现更优。

Conclusion: 优化行为策略能同时改进目标策略更新和评论家估计精度，AISAC具有实际应用潜力；未来计划与SAC、TD3等先进算法集成以应对更复杂环境。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Active-Importance-Sampling Actor-Critic (AISAC) algorithm, an extension of the Actor-Critic framework for reducing variance in policy gradient estimation. AISAC optimizes the behavior policy to minimize gradient variance while preserving unbiased gradient estimates. Using importance sampling principles, the algorithm adapts the behavior policy toward efficient data collection distributions aligned with target policy gradients.
  For continuous action spaces, AISAC employs Gaussian behavior policies optimized through cross-entropy minimization. We provide theoretical analysis demonstrating variance reduction and unbiasedness. Experiments on Inverted Pendulum and Half Cheetah tasks show improved learning speed, sample efficiency, and training stability compared to standard Actor-Critic methods.
  Results indicate that optimizing the behavior policy improves both target policy updates and critic estimation accuracy across different hyperparameter settings. AISAC accelerates convergence and stabilizes reinforcement learning training, making it promising for real-world applications. Future work includes integration with advanced algorithms such as Soft Actor-Critic and TD3 for more complex environments.

</details>


### [170] [Query-efficient model evaluation using cached responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07096)
*Hayden Helm,Ben Johnson,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）的方法，利用缓存模型响应来减少评估新模型时所需的查询数量，在保持预测精度的同时显著降低查询预算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代评估框架中，对每个查询都生成和评估响应成本过高；而已评估模型的响应通常被缓存，可以利用这些额外信息来减少准确评估新模型所需的查询数量。

Method: 基于数据核视角空间（DKPS）量化黑盒设置下模型间的关系，利用缓存模型响应预测新模型的基准性能；同时提出一种离线查询选择方法，通过最大化参考模型上的拟合优度来提升预测精度。

Result: 理论上证明DKPS方法在特定条件下具有查询高效性；实验表明DKPS方法在显著减少查询预算的条件下，能达到与基线方法相同的平均绝对误差。

Conclusion: DKPS方法能够有效利用缓存响应降低评估新模型的计算成本，且离线查询选择策略相比随机选择能进一步提升预测准确性。

Abstract: Evaluating a new model on an existing benchmark is often necessary to understand its behavior before deployment. For modern evaluation frameworks, generating and evaluating a response for all queries can be prohibitively expensive. In practice, responses from previously-evaluated models are often cached -- creating a potential opportunity to use this additional information to decrease the number of queries required to accurately evaluate a new model. In this paper, we introduce an approach for predicting benchmark performance that leverages cached model responses based on the Data Kernel Perspective Space (DKPS), a method for quantifying the relationship between models in the black-box setting. Theoretically, we show that DKPS-based methods are query-efficient under certain conditions. Empirically, we demonstrate that DKPS-based methods achieve the same mean absolute error as baselines with a substantially decreased query budget. We conclude by proposing an offline method for selecting a set of queries that maximizes the goodness-of-fit on reference models, improving prediction accuracy over random query selection.

</details>


### [171] [CarCrashNet: A Large-Scale Dataset and Hierarchical Neural Solver for Data-Driven Structural Crash Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07098)
*Mohamed Elrefaie,Dule Shu,Matthew Klenk,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CarCrashNet，一个用于数据驱动结构碰撞仿真的高保真开源基准数据集，包含超过14,000个保险杠支柱碰撞模拟和825个整车碰撞模拟，并提出了CrashSolver机器学习模型用于整车碰撞预测。通过实验数据和商业求解器验证了工作流程的可靠性，为可复现的碰撞仿真研究奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞仿真是现代车辆开发的核心，但结构碰撞力学建模极具挑战性（涉及非线性接触、大变形、材料塑性、失效及复杂多体相互作用）。现有研究缺乏公开、高保真的基准数据集来推动数据驱动的碰撞仿真研究发展。

Method: 1) 构建CarCrashNet基准数据集，包含组件级（14,000+保险杠碰撞模拟，变几何/材料/边界条件）和整车级（825个模拟，基于Dodge Neon、Toyota Yaris、Chevrolet Silverado三种车型）仿真数据，采用多模态格式；2) 基于OpenRadioss开源有限元工作流，通过实验数据和LS-DYNA商业求解器进行验证；3) 提出CrashSolver机器学习模型，并对比几何深度学习与基于Transformer的神经求解器。

Result: CarCrashNet被定位为结构仿真、耐撞性建模和AI驱动虚拟碰撞测试领域可复现研究的基础基准。CrashSolver在整车碰撞预测任务上得到了全面的基准测试评估。

Conclusion: CarCrashNet为数据驱动的结构碰撞仿真提供了首个公开高保真基准，有望推动该领域的可重复研究和AI虚拟碰撞测试发展。

Abstract: Crash simulation is a cornerstone of modern vehicle development because it reduces the need for costly physical prototypes, accelerates safety-driven design iteration, and increasingly supports virtual testing workflows. At the same time, modeling structural crash mechanics remains exceptionally challenging: the response is governed by nonlinear contact, large deformation, material plasticity, failure, and complex multi-body interactions evolving over space and time on high-resolution finite-element meshes. In this work, we introduce \textsc{CarCrashNet}, a public high-fidelity open-source benchmark for data-driven structural crash simulation. \textsc{CarCrashNet} combines component-scale and full-vehicle simulations in a multi-modal format, including more than 14{,}000 bumper-beam pole-impact simulations with varying geometry, materials, and boundary conditions, together with 825 full-vehicle crash simulations built from three industry-standard vehicle models of increasing structural complexity: Dodge Neon, Toyota Yaris, and Chevrolet Silverado. To establish the reliability of the benchmark, we validate our open-source finite-element workflow based on OpenRadioss against both experimental crash data and the commercial solver Ansys LS-DYNA. We also introduce \textsc{CrashSolver}, a machine-learning model designed for full-vehicle crash prediction from high-resolution finite-element crash data. We further perform extensive benchmarking across the released datasets and evaluate \textsc{CrashSolver} against state-of-the-art geometric deep learning and transformer-based neural solvers. Our results position \textsc{CarCrashNet} as a foundation for reproducible research in structural simulation, crashworthiness modeling, and AI-driven virtual crash testing. The dataset is available at https://github.com/Mohamedelrefaie/CarCrashNet.

</details>


### [172] [Almost Sure Convergence Rates of Stochastic Approximation and Reinforcement Learning via a Poisson-Moreau Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07104)
*Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对带有马尔可夫噪声的随机逼近和强化学习算法，建立了几乎必然收敛速率。对于幂律学习率 $O(n^{-η})$ ($η∈(1/2,1)$)，得到接近 $o(n^{1-2η})$ 的几乎必然收敛速率；对于调和学习率 $O(n^{-1})$，得到接近 $o(n^{-1})$ 的速率。关键创新在于结合泊松方程修正和Moreau-envelope平滑的新型Lyapunov漂移构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在马尔可夫噪声下建立随机逼近和强化学习算法的几乎必然收敛速率是一个基础理论挑战，此前尚未得到充分解决。

Method: 提出一种新颖的Lyapunov漂移构造方法，将基于泊松方程的马尔可夫噪声修正与针对压缩映射的Moreau-envelope平滑技术相结合。

Result: 对于幂律学习率 ($η∈(1/2,1)$)，得到任意接近 $o(n^{1-2η})$ 的几乎必然收敛速率；对于调和学习率 ($O(n^{-1})$)，得到任意接近 $o(n^{-1})$ 的速率，接近由迭代对数律给出的最优速率 $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$。

Conclusion: 该方法为马尔可夫噪声下压缩型随机逼近算法的收敛速率提供了理论保证，适用于Q学习和线性时序差分学习等强化学习算法。

Abstract: Establishing almost sure convergence rates for stochastic approximation and reinforcement learning under Markovian noise is a fundamental theoretical challenge. We make progress towards this challenge for a class of stochastic approximation algorithms whose expected updates are contractive, a setting that arises in many reinforcement learning algorithms such as $Q$-learning and linear temporal difference learning. Specifically, for a power-law learning rate $O(n^{-η})$ with $η\in (1/2, 1)$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{1 - 2η})$. For a harmonic learning rate $O(n^{-1})$, we obtain an almost sure convergence rate arbitrarily close to $o(n^{-1})$, which we argue is a strong result because it is close to the optimal rate $O(n^{-1}\log\log n)$ given by the law of the iterated logarithm (for a special case of i.i.d. noise). Key to our analysis is a novel Lyapunov drift construction that applies a Poisson-equation based correction for Markovian noise to the well-established Moreau-envelope smoothing for the contractive mapping.

</details>


### [173] [Where to Spend Rollouts: Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation for Group-Based RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07114)
*Tao Wang,Shuo Li,Yan Sun,Dongsheng Ding,Edgar Dobriban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HORA，一种无需学习的自适应 rollout 分配策略，通过最大化后验命中效用（hit utility）来替代GRPO中固定的均匀分配，在保持Pass@1的同时提升Pass@K，计算效率更高且与多种组策略优化方法兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等基于组的策略优化方法为每个提示分配固定数量的rollout，这种均匀分配效率低下：对于已经饱和的提示过度分配计算资源，而对可能通过更多采样发现正确轨迹的提示探索不足。

Method: 定义hit utility为新增rollout中至少一个正确的后验概率；提出HORA策略，在每个分配批次内最大化总后验命中效用，自适应地重新分配rollout预算，不改变下游奖励评估和基于组的优势估计器。

Result: 在四个数学推理基准和三种模型规模上，HORA在10/12个配置中保持相当Pass@1的同时提升了Pass@K（1个平局，1个饱和例外）；消融实验表明HORA的均匀先验与五种基于提示的学习先验竞争力相当。

Conclusion: HORA是一种高效、即插即用的rollout分配策略，可作为GRPO、RLOO等组策略优化方法的drop-in替代，提升计算效率与推理性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. Group-based policy optimization methods, such as GRPO, typically allocate a fixed number of rollouts to every prompt. This uniform allocation can be inefficient: it over-allocates compute to prompts whose sampled groups are already saturated while under-exploring prompts for which additional samples may reveal useful correct trajectories. To address this limitation, we introduce hit utility, the posterior probability that at least one rollout in a proposed additional allocation for a prompt will be correct. Building on this notion, we propose Hit-Utility Optimal Rollout Allocation (HORA), a learning-free rollout allocation policy that maximizes total posterior hit utility within each allocation batch. HORA adaptively reallocates rollout budgets while leaving the downstream reward evaluation and group-based advantage estimator unchanged. Across four mathematical reasoning benchmarks and three model scales, HORA preserves comparable Pass@1 and improves Pass@K over compute-matched GRPO in ten of twelve model--benchmark configurations, with one tie and one saturated exception. It is also drop-in compatible with other group-based estimators such as RLOO. Ablation studies indicate that the uniform prior used by HORA is competitive with five prompt-conditioned learned-prior alternatives.

</details>


### [174] [Conformal-Style Quantile Analyses for Stochastic Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07115)
*Chengyu Du,Mengfan Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACP-UCB1算法，将自适应共形预测与UCB类乐观奖励结合，用于随机老虎机问题中上尾分位数（而非均值）的优化，并实现了对数级上分位数遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机老虎机算法以均值奖励为标准，但许多实际问题更青睐具有强上尾性能的臂（即高分位数表现好的臂）。均值排序与上尾排序可能存在差异，现有算法无法直接优化上尾目标。

Method: 提出ACP-UCB1策略，包含两部分：自适应共形估计（Adaptive Conformal Prediction）用于估计上端点 \(F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)\)；UCB型乐观奖励（optimism bonus）用于探索。技术核心包括：奖励-分位数集中性、重计算得分分位数的扰动论证、自适应水平的确定性定位。

Result: ACP-UCB1实现了对数级上分位数遗憾，每臂贡献为 \(O(\log n / \Delta_j^{\mathrm{ACP}})\)。同时提供了与UCB1的度量特定遗憾分解，并通过数值实验验证了性能改进。

Conclusion: ACP-UCB1成功将共形预测框架引入老虎机上尾优化问题，在理论上保证了上分位数遗憾的对数界，并实验证明了其有效性。

Abstract: Stochastic bandit algorithms are usually analyzed under a mean-reward criterion, yet many problems favor arms with strong upper-tail performance, which we study herein. For a fixed miscoverage level \(α\), the natural upper-tail target of arm \(j\) is the upper endpoint \(F_j^{-1}(1-α/2)\) of a central prediction interval. This target can rank arms differently from their means, creating a central mismatch with the classical bandit objective. To this end, we propose ACP-UCB1, a conformal-style policy that combines an adaptive conformal estimate of the upper endpoint with a UCB-type optimism bonus. The technical challenge is that the conformity scores used by ACP-UCB1 are recomputed from evolving empirical quantile estimates and evaluated at an adaptive level. We control this endpoint through reward-quantile concentration, a perturbation argument for recomputed score quantiles, and deterministic localization of the adaptive level. ACP-UCB1 achieves logarithmic upper-quantile regret with per-arm contribution \(O(\nicefrac{\log n}{Δ_j^{\mathrm{ACP}}})\). We also provide metric-specific regret decompositions comparing ACP-UCB1 with UCB1 and use numerical experiments to validate performance and improvement.

</details>


### [175] [When Symbol Names Should Not Matter: A Logistic Theory of Fresh-Symbol Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07120)
*Wenjie Guan,Jelena Bradic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Transformer核回归框架下，研究了固定标签模板任务中模型的符号抽象推理能力。通过将学习到的预测器分解为理想模板级分类器和由训练数据中偶然token重叠引起的有限样本扰动，证明了新鲜符号分类的边际传递保证，并提出了一个通用的边际-碰撞准则来识别何时提示策略能改善泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板任务已成为测试Transformer是否用抽象符号而非具体token名称进行推理的干净实验平台。作者希望理解在固定标签分类设定下，模型如何学习对符号重命名保持不变的决策规则，尤其是在训练和测试使用不相交词汇表的情况下。

Method: 采用正则化核逻辑分类在Transformer核机制下进行分析。将预测器分解为理想模板级分类器和有限样本扰动项；通过有色碰撞图编码训练数据中的偶然token重叠；推导了新鲜符号分类的高概率边际传递保证；并将该扰动框架扩展到抽象增强输入，提出通用边际-碰撞准则。

Result: 词汇量控制平均碰撞率，但碰撞几何结构决定理想分类边际能否保持。该框架可推广到抽象增强输入，提供识别何时提示策略能改善新鲜符号泛化的边际-碰撞准则。合成模板实验验证了正则化、样本量和Transformer核结构的作用。

Conclusion: 该视角将模板分析扩展到逻辑分类，细化了标量多样性条件，并提供了理解提示策略如何影响抽象泛化的统一框架。

Abstract: Template tasks have emerged as a clean testbed for asking whether transformers reason with abstract symbols rather than concrete token names. We study the fixed-label classification version of this problem, where train and test examples share latent templates but may use disjoint vocabularies. Unlike next-token prediction, the model need not emit unseen symbols; it must learn a decision rule invariant to symbol renaming. We analyze regularized kernel logistic classification in the transformer-kernel regime. Our main result decomposes the learned predictor into an ideal template-level classifier and a finite-sample perturbation caused by accidental token overlaps in the training data. We encode these overlaps by a colored collision graph and prove high-probability margin-transfer guarantees for fresh-symbol classification. This perspective extends template-based analyses to logistic classification and refines scalar diversity conditions: vocabulary size controls the average rate of collisions, but collision geometry controls whether the ideal classification margin is preserved. More broadly, the same perturbation framework applies to abstraction-augmented inputs, yielding a general margin-versus-collision criterion for identifying when prompting strategies improve fresh-symbol generalization. Synthetic template experiments illustrate the predicted roles of regularization, sample size, and transformer-kernel structure.

</details>


### [176] [Convergence and Emergence of In-Context Reinforcement Learning with Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07123)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了链式思维（CoT）生成如何与上下文强化学习（ICRL）相互作用，证明在线性Transformer的策略评估设置中，CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时序差分学习更新，且策略评估误差随CoT长度几何级下降并最终饱和于由上下文长度决定的统计下限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有实证研究表明CoT可以增强ICRL能力，但缺乏理论层面的理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，从理论上揭示CoT与ICRL之间的相互作用机制。

Method: 在策略评估框架下使用线性Transformer进行分析。通过证明特定Transformer参数下CoT生成过程等价于重复执行时序差分学习更新，并提供有限样本收敛性分析来建立理论保证。

Result: 1) CoT生成过程等价于重复执行TD学习更新；2) 策略评估误差随CoT长度几何级下降；3) 误差最终饱和于由上下文长度决定的统计下限；4) 理想的Transformer参数是预训练损失的全局最小值。

Conclusion: 本文为CoT增强ICRL能力提供了严格的理论基础，证明了CoT相当于隐式执行多步TD更新，且最优参数可通过预训练损失最小化获得，从而解释了相关实证现象。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) refers to the ability of RL agents to adapt to new tasks at inference time without parameter updates by conditioning on additional context. Recent empirical studies further demonstrate that Chain-of-Thought (CoT) generation can amplify this ICRL capability. This paper is the first to provide a theoretical understanding on how CoT interacts with ICRL. We conduct our analysis in a policy evaluation setup with linear Transformer. We prove that with specific Transformer parameters, the CoT generation process is equivalent to repeatedly executing temporal difference learning updates. Additionally, we provide finite sample convergence analysis showing that the policy evaluation error decreases geometrically with CoT length and eventually saturates at a statistical floor determined by the context length. We also prove that the desired Transformer parameters are a global minimizer of the pretraining loss, providing a theoretical understanding on the empirical emergence of those parameters.

</details>


### [177] [The Position Curse: LLMs Struggle to Locate the Last Few Items in a List](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07127)
*Zhanqi Zhang,Hua-Dong Xiong,Robert C. Wilson,Mikio Aoi,Marcelo G. Mattar,Li Ji-An*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现并系统研究了大型语言模型中的“位置诅咒”现象：模型在长文本中定位单个事实（大海捞针）表现近乎完美，但在短列表中检索最后几个项目时却频繁失败。作者设计了正向/反向位置检索任务，发现反向检索显著落后于正向检索。通过构建PosBench训练集进行LoRA微调，虽然能提升性能并泛化到代码理解基准测试，但绝对性能仍未饱和。该问题对LLM编程助手处理大型代码库至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示LLM在位置检索任务中存在的系统性缺陷（位置诅咒），特别是反向检索能力的严重不足，并探索通过后训练方法缓解该问题的可能性。

Method: 设计了两种互补的位置检索查询：（1）给定序列中的位置（正/反向偏移），检索对应项目；（2）给定项目，返回其位置。系统评估了开源和闭源模型的正反向检索能力。构建了专注于位置检索的训练数据集PosBench，采用LoRA微调方法进行后训练。

Result: 反向检索性能显著低于正向检索；LoRA微调能同时提升正反向检索能力，并能泛化到代码理解基准测试PyIndex，但绝对性能仍未达到饱和。

Conclusion: 位置检索是LLM（尤其是编程助手）的关键能力缺陷，现有模型在该任务上远未饱和，需在预训练目标和模型设计中予以重视。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) can find a needle in a haystack (locating a single relevant fact buried among hundreds of thousands of irrelevant tokens) with near-saturated accuracy, yet fail to retrieve the last few items in a short list. We call this failure the Position Curse. For instance, even in a two-line code snippet, Claude Opus 4.6 misidentifies the second-to-last line most of the time. To characterize this failure, we evaluated two complementary queries: given a position in a sequence (of letters or words), retrieve the corresponding item; and given an item, return its position. Each position is specified as a forward or backward offset from an anchor, either an endpoint of the list (its start or end) or another item in the list. Across both open-source and frontier closed-source models, backward retrieval substantially lags forward retrieval. To test whether this capability can be rescued by post-training, we constructed PosBench, a position-focused training dataset. LoRA fine-tuning improves both forward and backward retrieval and generalizes to a held-out code-understanding benchmark (PyIndex), yet absolute performance remains far from saturated. As LLM coding agents increasingly operate over large codebases where precise indexing becomes essential for code understanding and editing, position-based retrieval emerges as a key capability for future pretraining objectives and model design.

</details>


### [178] [Simple KNN-Based Outlier Detection Achieves Robust Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07130)
*Tianle Jiang,Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在最优簇大小的实际假设下，简单地移除具有较大K近邻距离的点，即可实现从鲁棒k-Means到标准k-Means的常数因子约简，无需额外引入中心或丢弃多余离群点，并且在真实数据集上表现优于或匹敌更复杂的算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒k-Means（即带离群点的k-Means）在实际应用中至关重要，但经典的离群点检测启发式方法在鲁棒k-Means中的理论保证和实证效果尚不明确，现有方法通常需要额外引入中心或丢弃多余离群点。

Method: 在最优簇大小的实际假设下，通过移除具有较大K近邻距离的点，将鲁棒k-Means问题约简为标准k-Means问题，并给出常数因子近似保证。在真实数据集上进行实验对比。

Result: 该方法实现了从鲁棒k-Means到标准k-Means的常数因子约简，无需额外引入中心或丢弃多余离群点；在真实数据集上，其聚类代价和运行时间优于或匹敌多种更复杂的算法。

Conclusion: 简单的KNN启发式方法在鲁棒聚类中可能出奇有效，为连接离群点检测与聚类技术提供了新的机会。

Abstract: Being robust to the presence of outliers is crucial for applying clustering algorithms in practice. In the $\textit{robust $k$-Means}$ problem (i.e., $k$-Means with outliers), the goal is to remove $z$ outliers and minimize the $k$-Means cost on the remaining points. Despite the close connection between robust $k$-Means and outlier detection, both theoretical and empirical understanding of the effectiveness of $\textit{classic outlier detection heuristics}$ for robust $k$-Means remains limited. In this paper, we prove that under a practical assumption on the optimal cluster sizes, simply removing points with large $K$-Nearest-Neighbor distances achieves performance comparable to prior work in terms of approximation guarantees: it yields a constant-factor reduction from robust $k$-Means to standard $k$-Means, without introducing additional centers or discarding extra outliers, as is commonly required by existing approaches. Empirically, experiments on real-world datasets show that our method outperforms or matches several more sophisticated algorithms in terms of clustering cost and runtime. These results demonstrate that simple KNN-based heuristics can be surprisingly effective for robust clustering, highlighting new opportunities to bridge techniques from outlier detection and clustering.

</details>


### [179] [GAD in the Wild: Benchmarking Graph Anomaly Detection under Realistic Deployment Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07133)
*Jingjing Zhou,Shiyu Huang,Qing Qing,Zuquan Yuan,Huafei Huang,Ziqi Xu,Mingliang Hou,Xikun Zhang,Renqiang Luo,Ivan Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多维度图异常检测（GAD）基准，系统评估GAD模型在百万级图、极端异常稀疏性和缺失节点属性三种部署相关挑战下的表现。实验发现大多数GNN方法无法扩展至百万节点图，检测性能在真实异常比例下急剧下降，重建模型对属性填充策略高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GAD基准局限于小规模、精心整理的图且异常比例相对均衡，导致学术评估与实际部署之间存在巨大差距。为弥合这一差距，需要构建一个能反映真实部署场景的多维度基准。

Method: 从五个不同图（包括两个超过370万节点的工业级数据集）中衍生出一组受控基准变体，系统评估九种代表性GAD模型在百万级图、极端异常稀缺和缺失节点属性三种挑战下的表现。

Result: (1) 大多数GNN方法因内存需求过大无法扩展到百万节点图；(2) 在真实异常比例（如0.1%）下检测性能急剧下降，常出现零召回率；(3) 基于重建的模型对属性填充策略高度敏感。

Conclusion: 实验室环境中的强性能并不能保证生产环境中的鲁棒性，需要开发面向大规模、不完美图的鲁棒且可扩展的GAD系统。

Abstract: Graph Anomaly Detection (GAD) is a critical task in graph machine learning with vital applications in financial fraud detection and social platform governance. However, existing GAD benchmarks are often restricted to small-scale, curated graphs with relatively balanced anomaly ratios, leaving a substantial gap between academic evaluation and real-world deployment. To bridge this gap, we present a multi-dimensional benchmark that systematically evaluates GAD models under three deployment-relevant challenges: million-scale graphs, extreme anomaly scarcity, and missing node attributes. We derive a family of controlled benchmark variants from five diverse graphs, including two native industrial-scale datasets with over 3.7 million nodes. Our extensive evaluation of nine representative GAD models reveals three major limitations: (1) most GNN-based methods fail to scale to million-node graphs due to prohibitive memory requirements; (2) detection performance drops sharply under realistic anomaly ratios (e.g., 0.1\%), often resulting in zero recall; and (3) reconstruction-based models are highly sensitive to attribute imputation strategies. Our findings suggest that strong performance in laboratory settings does not guarantee robustness in production environments. We release this benchmark and empirical evaluation as a diagnostic testbed to promote the development of robust and scalable GAD systems for large-scale, imperfect graphs encountered in practice. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Benchmark_GAD-E7A3.

</details>


### [180] [Adaptive Negative Reinforcement for LLM Reasoning:Dynamically Balancing Correction and Diversity in RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07137)
*Yash Ingle,Jaival Chauhan,Ankit Yadav,Sudhakar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种增强型负样本强化学习方法A-NSR（自适应负样本强化）和CW-NSR（置信度加权负样本强化），分别通过时间依赖调度函数和置信度感知的惩罚权重来改进固定惩罚的NSR框架，在数学推理任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NSR方法在训练过程中使用固定惩罚，且对所有错误响应施加相同的权重，未能区分错误的重要程度和训练阶段的不同需求。

Method: 1) A-NSR：采用时间依赖调度函数，训练初期侧重纠错稳定模型，后期转向更精细的更新；2) CW-NSR：基于模型对错误路径的归一化序列似然分配惩罚权重，高置信度错误受更大惩罚，不确定错误则轻罚。

Result: 在MATH、AIME 2025、AMC23等困难推理数据集上，采用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B架构进行评估。

Conclusion: 理论分析表明这些机制能控制令牌级更新，实现基于先验的概率重分布，并提供对过拟合的自然防御。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a highly effective method for improving the reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). Recent research shows that Negative Sample Reinforcement (NSR) -- which focuses on penalizing incorrect steps rather than simply rewarding correct ones -- can match or even exceed the performance of more complex frameworks like PPO and GRPO across the entire Pass@k spectrum. However, current NSR techniques usually apply a fixed penalty throughout the training process and treat every incorrect response with the same weight.
  To address these limitations, we propose two extensions to the NSR framework: Adaptive Negative Sample Reinforcement. Rather than using a fixed update rule, A-NSR uses time-dependent scheduling functions. In the initial training phases, the system focuses heavily on correcting errors to stabilize the model. As training continues, it shifts toward more subtle and controlled updates. We also introduce Confidence-Weighted Negative Reinforcement, which operates on the principle that different mistakes carry different levels of importance. CW-NSR assigns specific penalty weights based on the model's normalized sequence likelihood. If the model is highly confident in a wrong path, it receives a larger penalty and for uncertain errors -- where the model is effectively exploring -- are penalized less strictly. Our formal analysis shows how these mechanisms govern token-level updates, allowing the model to leverage prior-guided probability redistribution while providing a natural defense against overfitting. We evaluated these methods on difficult reasoning datasets, including MATH, AIME 2025, and AMC23, using the Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B architecture.

</details>


### [181] [Regret-Oracle Complexity Tradeoffs in Agnostic Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07155)
*Idan Attias,Steve Hanneke,Arvind Ramaswami*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于弱一致性预言机的自适应agnostic-to-realizable归约方法，将在线学习的预言机复杂度从双指数级$O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$降低到多项式级$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$，同时保持近最优的期望遗憾，并形式化了遗憾-预言机复杂度之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的agnostic在线学习通过归约到realizable设置来解决，但依赖于计算上不可行的SOA。现有的oracle-efficient方法虽然实现了近最优期望遗憾，却面临双指数级的预言机复杂度。本文旨在显著降低这种复杂度，同时使用更弱的原始操作（弱一致性预言机）。

Method: 提出一种自适应的动态agnostic-to-realizable归约方法，主动在线剪枝不可实现的标签序列。利用VC维($d_\mathrm{VC}$)来界定向动态维护的活跃路径数量，将总查询复杂度降至$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$。

Result: 总查询复杂度从$O(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}})$降低到$O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$；完美保留了近最优的期望遗憾；同时实现了内存开销的降低；给出了遗憾-预言机复杂度权衡的上界和下界，证明在$Q = o(\sqrt{T})$的查询预算下，期望遗憾至少为$\Omega(T/Q)$。

Conclusion: 通过动态剪枝实现了从双指数级到多项式级的预言机复杂度改进，并系统刻画了遗憾与预言机复杂度之间的基本权衡，为实际受限查询场景提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Agnostic online learning is classically solved via a reduction to the realizable setting, utilizing Littlestone's Standard Optimal Algorithm (SOA) as a base learner. However, the SOA is computationally intractable to execute even for a single round. To overcome this barrier, recent work in oracle-efficient online learning replaces the SOA with a realizable base learner that accesses the concept class exclusively through an offline empirical risk minimization (ERM) oracle. While such agnostic learners achieve near-optimal expected regret, they suffer from a doubly-exponential oracle complexity of $O\big(T^{2^{O(d_\mathrm{LD})}}\big)$, where $d_\mathrm{LD}$ is the Littlestone dimension and $T$ is the number of rounds. In this work, we significantly improve this oracle complexity while relying on an even weaker primitive: a weak-consistency oracle, which merely decides whether a given labeled dataset is realizable. At the core of our approach is an adaptive and dynamic agnostic-to-realizable reduction that actively prunes non-realizable label sequences on the fly. By using the VC dimension ($d_\mathrm{VC}$) to bound the number of dynamically maintained active paths, our algorithm reduces the total query complexity down to $O(T^{d_\mathrm{VC}+1})$ while perfectly preserving near-optimal expected regret. Crucially, this dynamic pruning also yields a memory reduction over the standard reduction. Furthermore, we formally quantify the regret--oracle complexity tradeoff, providing upper bounds that smoothly interpolate between restricted query budgets and attainable expected regret. We complement these with lower bounds proving that any learner restricted to $Q = o(\sqrt{T})$ queries must suffer an expected regret of $Ω(T/Q)$.

</details>


### [182] [HyperEyes: Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Parallel Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07177)
*Guankai Li,Jiabin Chen,Yi Xu,Xichen Zhang,Yuan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HyperEyes，一种并行多模态搜索智能体，通过将视觉定位与检索融合为单一原子动作，实现多实体并发搜索，并采用双粒度效率感知强化学习框架（宏观TRACE奖励+微观策略蒸馏）来优化推理效率。在6个基准上，HyperEyes-30B以平均5.3倍更少的工具调用轮次，超越最强开源智能体9.9%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态搜索智能体顺序处理目标实体，每次查询分解为独立子检索时会产生冗余交互轮次。作者认为有效多模态智能体应"搜索更宽而非更长"——在一轮内同时发起多个接地查询。

Method: 两阶段训练：(1) 冷启动阶段：开发并行适配数据合成管道，通过渐进式拒绝采样构建效率导向轨迹；(2) 双粒度效率感知强化学习：宏观层面提出TRACE奖励（工具使用参考自适应成本效率），单调收紧参考线以抑制多余工具调用；微观层面采用策略内蒸馏，从外部教师模型注入密集令牌级纠正信号。

Result: 在6个基准上，HyperEyes-30B超越最强开源智能体9.9%准确率，平均减少5.3倍工具调用轮次。

Conclusion: 并行多模态搜索结合效率感知训练能显著提升准确率并大幅降低推理成本，现有基准仅评估准确率而忽略推理成本的评估方式存在不足。

Abstract: Existing multimodal search agents process target entities sequentially, issuing one tool call per entity and accumulating redundant interaction rounds whenever a query decomposes into independent sub-retrievals. We argue that effective multimodal agents should search wider rather than longer: dispatching multiple grounded queries concurrently within a round. To this end, we present HyperEyes, a parallel multimodal search agent that fuses visual grounding and retrieval into a single atomic action, enabling concurrent search across multiple entities while treating inference efficiency as a first-class training objective. HyperEyes is trained in two stages. For cold-start supervision, we develop a Parallel-Amenable Data Synthesis Pipeline covering visual multi-entity and textual multi-constraint queries, curating efficiency-oriented trajectories via Progressive Rejection Sampling. Building on this, our central contribution, a Dual-Grained Efficiency-Aware Reinforcement Learning framework, operates at two levels. At the macro level, we propose TRACE (Tool-use Reference-Adaptive Cost Efficiency), a trajectory-level reward whose reference is monotonically tightened during training to suppress superfluous tool calls without restricting genuine multi-hop search. At the micro level, we adapt On-Policy Distillation to inject dense token-level corrective signals from an external teacher on failed rollouts, mitigating the credit-assignment deficiency of sparse outcome rewards. Since existing benchmarks evaluate accuracy as the sole metric, omitting inference cost, we introduce IMEB, a human-curated benchmark of 300 instances that jointly evaluates search capability and efficiency. Across six benchmarks, HyperEyes-30B surpasses the strongest comparable open-source agent by 9.9% in accuracy with 5.3x fewer tool-call rounds on average.

</details>


### [183] [Coupling Models for One-Step Discrete Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07193)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Avishek Joey Bose,Anru R. Zhang,Alexander Tong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Coupling Models，一种一步离散生成模型，通过在高斯潜变量与离散序列之间建立直接耦合，实现单步生成。在LM1B、Fly Brain和MNIST-Binary上分别将困惑度降低33%、FBD提升18%、FID降低46%，优于现有一步基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散结构上的生成模型（如自回归解码、迭代精炼）通常需要多步采样，效率低下。而现有的蒸馏方法需先预训练多步采样器再压缩，过程复杂。本文旨在设计一种无需预训练多步模型、无需复杂连续流或手工指定数据-噪声耦合的**单步离散生成模型**。

Method: 提出Coupling Models，学习离散序列与高斯潜变量之间的直接耦合（direct coupling）。训练一个专门设计的解码器来逆转该耦合，从而在单步中生成样本。该方法避免了在单纯形上构建复杂连续流，也不需手工指定数据到噪声的耦合方式。

Result: 在三个领域均显著优于最强的一步基线：LM1B文本生成困惑度降低33%；Fly Brain增强子设计FBD提升18%；MNIST-Binary FID降低46%。

Conclusion: 有效的单步离散生成在很大程度上依赖于数据与噪声在解码之前的耦合方式，而非多步迭代或复杂连续流。这表明Coupling Models的设计原则可为未来高效离散生成提供新方向。

Abstract: Generative modeling over discrete structures underpins applications across deep learning, from biological sequence design and code generation to large language models, yet generation often remains sequential, relying on autoregressive decoding or iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce Coupling Models(Coupling Models), a one-step discrete generative model that learns a direct coupling between discrete sequences and Gaussian latents. Unlike recent distillation methods that compress a pretrained multi-step sampler into a few steps, Coupling Model trains a purpose-built decoder to invert this coupling and generate samples in a single step. The model also avoids complex continuous flows over the simplex and hand-specified data-to-noise couplings. Empirically,Coupling Model improves the strongest one-step baselines in each domain: it reduces LM1B text-generation perplexity by 33% at its lowest-perplexity operating point, Fly Brain enhancer-design FBD by 18%, and MNIST-Binary FID by 46%. These results suggest that effective one-step discrete generation depends strongly on how data and noise are coupled before decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/Coupling-Models.

</details>


### [184] [Arrow: A Foundation Model for Causal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07204)
*Ryan Thompson,He Zhao,Daniel M. Steinberg,Edwin V. Bonilla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Arrow，一个基于Transformer的零样本因果发现基础模型，通过将DAG分解为无向骨架和拓扑顺序保证无环性，在大规模合成数据上预训练后，能以较低推理成本在多种数据集上匹配或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的因果发现方法要么计算代价高，要么依赖特定假设，缺乏一个通用、高效、可复用的零样本解决方案。作者希望利用大规模预训练范式，构建一个可以直接应用于新数据集而无需重新训练的因果发现模型。

Method: Arrow将DAG分解为无向骨架和拓扑顺序，使用Transformer架构对变量在观测内和跨观测间进行上下文编码，预测骨架边的概率和节点顺序分数。模型采用端到端可微的有向边复合似然损失（由骨架-顺序分解诱导），在覆盖多种图族、函数形式、噪声模型和数据形状的合成数据集上进行监督训练。
