Result: 在分布内和分布外的合成、半合成及真实数据集上，Arrow匹配或优于现有因果发现方法，且推理成本显著低于竞品方法。

Conclusion: 在大规模多样化合成数据上的预训练可以产生快速、准确且可复用的零样本因果发现模型，为因果发现领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Arrow, a foundation model for zero-shot causal discovery on observational tabular data. Arrow factorizes a directed acyclic graph into an undirected skeleton and a topological order, guaranteeing acyclicity by construction. Given a new dataset, it uses a transformer-based architecture to contextualize variables within and across observations, then predicts skeleton edge probabilities and node order scores that together define a graph. Arrow is trained in a supervised fashion on synthetic datasets with ground-truth graphs, using an end-to-end differentiable directed edge composite likelihood induced by the skeleton-order factorization. The training distribution spans diverse graph families, functional forms, noise models, and dataset shapes. Across in- and out-of-distribution synthetic, semi-synthetic, and real datasets, Arrow matches or outperforms existing causal discovery methods at substantially lower inference cost than competitive alternatives. Our results demonstrate that large-scale pretraining on diverse synthetic data can yield zero-shot causal discovery models that are fast, accurate, and reusable on new datasets.

</details>


### [185] [HARMONY: Bridging the Personalization-Generalization Gap by Mitigating Representation Skew in Heterogeneous Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07211)
*Jiseok Youn,You Rim Choi,Goodsol Lee,Sangtae Ha,Hyung-Sin Kim,Saewoong Bahk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HARMONY提出首个支持异构客户端架构的混合拆分联邦学习框架，通过元学习模拟多样特征提取器、服务器端对比学习对齐特征，在非IID数据和资源受限的移动设备场景下，将测试准确率提升最高43.0%（无OOD）/28.3%（有OOD），同时保持可接受的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合拆分联邦学习（Hybrid SFL）在客户端架构异构性下面临表征偏斜问题——不同客户端的定制化特征提取器产生的特征无法在共享空间中对齐，导致负责OOD预测的服务器模型性能严重下降。

Method: HARMONY包含两大核心设计：(1) 修改元学习机制，模拟不同参数和架构的多样化特征提取器，并学习个性化； (2) 在服务器端进行对比学习，对齐客户端提取的特征，既不牺牲个性，也不共享原始标签。

Result: 在多个数据集和模型族上，相比现有最优方法，HARMONY在无OOD场景下测试准确率提升最高43.0%，在有OOD场景下提升最高28.3%，且延迟可接受。

Conclusion: HARMONY是首个支持异构客户端架构的混合SFL框架，有效解决了表征偏斜问题，实现了精度与开销的更好权衡。

Abstract: Mobile devices face diverse resource constraints and non-IID data class distributions, requiring fast on-device inference for local in-distribution (ID) classes and on-demand remote support for client-specific out-of-distribution (OOD) classes. Hybrid split federated learning (Hybrid SFL) couples personalized client-side front ends (supporting early exit) with a generalized server-side backend for fallback inference, balancing accuracy and cost. However, under client architectural heterogeneity, the existing hybrid SFL suffers from representation skew, where features from customized extractors fail to align in the shared space, leading to a sharp degradation in the server model responsible for OOD prediction. We propose HARMONY, the first hybrid SFL framework to support heterogeneous client architectures. HARMONY modifies meta-learning to simulate diverse extractors across parameters and architectures, and to learn to personalize. To mitigate representation skew, HARMONY conducts server-side contrastive learning to align extracted features, neither sacrificing clients' personalization nor sharing raw labels. Compared to the state of the art across multiple datasets and model families, HARMONY improves test accuracy by up to 43.0%/28.3% without/with OOD, respectively, while maintaining acceptable latency.

</details>


### [186] [Improved Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Smooth Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07218)
*Kun Long,Yuqiang Li,Xianyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核平滑的模型驱动在线强化学习方法，通过引入Bernstein风格的探索奖励，在Lipschitz连续假设下实现了比现有方法更优的遗憾界，其水平因子依赖得到改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典强化学习理论主要依赖于低秩MDP的结构性假设，而核平滑方法提供了一种利用MDP平滑性的替代范式。本文旨在改进现有核平滑方法在水平因子上的遗憾界依赖。

Method: 提出基于核平滑的模型驱动方法，在有限时域设置下利用Lipschitz连续性假设，结合Bernstein风格探索奖励，并推导了新的鞅Bernstein型集中不等式。

Result: 实现了比现有最优遗憾界更好的水平因子依赖关系，理论分析揭示了Bernstein奖励与核平滑之间的协同作用。

Conclusion: 所提方法在连续状态-动作空间的在线强化学习中显著提升了遗憾界，新推导的鞅Bernstein型集中不等式具有独立理论价值。

Abstract: For continuous state-action space scenarios, classical reinforcement learning (RL) theory predominantly focuses on low-rank Markov decision processes (MDPs), which provide sample-efficient guarantees at the expense of restrictive structural assumptions. Kernel smoothing model-based approaches offer a promising alternative paradigm that instead leverages the smoothness of the MDP and employs non-parametric kernel smoothing estimates of transition dynamics. This paper proposes a new kernel-smoothing model-based approach for online reinforcement learning in finite-horizon settings under Lipschitz continuity assumptions on the MDP. By incorporating a Bernstein-style exploration bonus into the kernel smoothing framework, our method achieves a regret bound which improves upon the state-of-the-art regret bound in its dependence on the horizon. The theoretical advancement relies on a delicate analysis of the synergy between Bernstein-style bonuses and kernel smoothing, where a new tight Bernstein-type concentration inequality for martingales may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [187] [On the Robustness of Distribution Support under Diffusion Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07220)
*Ruijia Cao,Yuchen Wu,Nisha Chadramoorthy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过建立"支持鲁棒性"性质，从理论上解释了扩散引导（diffusion guidance）为何能持续生成高质量、结构合理的样本。研究表明，在精确访问分数函数的前提下，引导扩散过程几乎总能生成靠近目标支持的样本，覆盖DDIM和DDPM以及多种指数积分器离散化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散引导技术虽在可控生成和高保真采样中取得巨大经验成功，但其理论性质尚不明确，人们对其为何能持续生成高质量样本缺乏深入理解。

Method: 建立"支持鲁棒性"（robustness of support）理论性质，在精确分数函数条件下，证明引导扩散过程生成的样本几乎总是靠近目标支持集。分析覆盖DDIM和DDPM两种主流扩散模型，并推广到由指数积分器导出的多种离散化方案。

Result: 理论证明了扩散引导生成的样本具有支持鲁棒性，即偏离目标支持集的样本（通常结构不合理且影响下游任务）几乎不会出现，从而解释了扩散引导为何能产生物理上有意义且结构合理的样本。

Conclusion: 该工作为扩散引导的有效性提供了严格的理论基础，揭示了支持鲁棒性是扩散引导产生高质量样本的关键机制，并统一了DDIM和DDPM等不同框架下的理论解释。

Abstract: Diffusion guidance is a powerful technique that enables controllable and high-fidelity sample generation with diffusion models. At a high level, it modifies the score function by incorporating a guidance term that steers the generative process toward a desired condition. Despite its empirical success, the theoretical properties of diffusion guidance remain largely unexplored, and it is not well understood why it consistently produces high-quality samples.
  In this work, we explain the effectiveness of diffusion guidance by establishing a \emph{robustness of support} property. Specifically, we show that, given exact access to the score functions, guided diffusion processes almost always generate samples that remain close to the target support. This property is particularly desirable, as samples that lie off the support are often structurally implausible and may adversely affect downstream tasks. Our analysis covers both Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models (DDIM) and Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Models (DDPM), and applies to a wide range of discretization schemes induced by exponential integrators. Our results provide a rigorous foundation for understanding why diffusion guidance produces physically meaningful and structurally plausible samples.

</details>


### [188] [Don't Learn the Shape: Forecasting Periodic Time Series by Rank-1 Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07222)
*Takato Honda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAIR方法，揭示周期性时间序列预测中，当数据具有高秩-1特性且周期数充足时，额外灵活性只会引入估计噪声。仅用28个标量（小时级）或57个标量（周级）即可达到与PatchTST相当的性能，无需GPU、预训练或每任务调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究周期性时间序列预测到底需要多少参数，质疑现有复杂模型在周期性强、数据量充足场景下的必要性。

Method: 将小时级电力序列重塑为24行×天数矩阵，利用其近似秩-1结构，采用SVD分解+GCV平均Ridge回归，综合平滑、收缩和低秩拟合策略，形成FLAIR方法。

Result: 在97个GIFT-Eval配置上，8种候选方法（Fourier、EWMA、James-Stein、秩-r SVD等）均未显著优于简单基线（最近K=2周期平均）；FLAIR以relMASE 0.838 vs 0.849匹配PatchTST，计算仅需22分钟单核CPU。

Conclusion: 在高秩-1、多周期场景下，模型复杂度带来的灵活性本质上成为估计噪声，简单低参数方法已足够优秀。

Abstract: How few parameters do we really need to forecast a periodic time series? An hourly electricity series, reshaped as a 24-row matrix with one column per day, is approximately rank-1: a daily shape modulated by a daily level (median centered rank-1 energy 0.82 on GIFT-Eval). Should we learn the shape? Smoothing, shrinkage, and low-rank fits all seem like obvious upgrades over the simple average of the last K=2 cycles. On all 97 GIFT-Eval configurations, we tested 8 such alternatives (e.g., Fourier, EWMA, James-Stein, rank-r SVD): none significantly beats the frozen baseline under Holm correction; two are significantly worse. The resulting method, FLAIR, is (a) Effective: matches PatchTST on aggregate GIFT-Eval (relMASE 0.838 vs 0.849); (b) Compact: 28 scalars for hourly, 57 for weekly; (c) Fast: 22 minutes on one CPU core of a MacBook Pro; (d) Closed-form & Hands-Off: one SVD per period candidate, GCV-averaged Ridge, no GPU, no pre-training, no per-task tuning. In the high-rank-1, many-cycle regime, extra flexibility is estimation noise.

</details>


### [189] [Sample Complexity of Stochastic Optimization with Integer Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07239)
*Hongyu Cheng,Yinghao Zheng,Marco Molinaro,Amitabh Basu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了整数随机优化的样本复杂度，发现与连续优化相比，整数优化有时需要更多样本，有时需要更少样本，具体取决于目标和约束的结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解整数随机优化相对于连续随机优化的样本复杂度差异，揭示离散性对统计复杂性的本质影响。

Method: 通过理论分析，分别在$\ell_\infty$球和$\ell_2$球上的Lipschitz目标以及强凸光滑目标三种场景下，建立并比较整数优化与连续优化的样本复杂度上下界。

Result: 1. 在$\ell_\infty$球上，混合整数非线性非凸优化的统计复杂度与带界约束的线性优化相同。2. 在$\ell_2$球上，整数优化在特定区间内所需样本量严格小于连续优化。3. 对强凸光滑目标，整数优化需要$\Omega(1/\varepsilon^2)$样本，而连续优化仅需$O(1/\varepsilon)$样本。

Conclusion: 整数优化的样本复杂度并非总是高于连续优化，其相对复杂性依赖于问题的几何结构和目标函数性质，为随机整数优化的理论理解提供了新见解。

Abstract: We establish sample complexity results for stochastic optimization over the integers, especially with a view to understand the complexity with respect to the corresponding continuous optimization problem. We show that integer optimization can sometimes require strictly more samples and sometimes strictly smaller number of samples, depending on the structure of the objective and constraints. 1. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_\infty$ ball, the statistical complexity of general stochastic mixed-integer, nonlinear, nonconvex optimization is exactly the same as stochastic linear optimization with just bound constraints. 2. For Lipschitz objectives over subsets of the $\ell_2$ ball, we show that integer optimization can require strictly *smaller* sample size compared to the continuous setting in a certain regime. To get to this result, we also establish tight sample complexity results for nonconvex continuous stochastic optimization which, to the best of our knowledge, do not appear in prior work. 3. For strongly convex, smooth objectives, integer optimization has high statistical complexity compared to the continuous setting. In particular, we show that integer optimization requires $Ω(1/ε^2)$ samples to report an $ε$-approximate solution, compared to the well-known $O(1/ε)$ sample complexity from the continuous optimization literature.

</details>


### [190] [Experience Sharing in Mutual Reinforcement Learning for Heterogeneous Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07244)
*Xiaoze Liu,Dhananjay Ram,Yuting Zhang,Zhaoyang Zhang,Wei Xia,Stefano Soatto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了互强化学习框架，通过共享经验、多工作器资源分配和分词器异构层，实现异构LLM策略的并发RL后训练。实验评估了三种探针方法，发现结果级共享在稳定性-支持权衡中占据有利位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后训练方法通常针对单一模型，未能有效利用异构LLM策略之间的经验共享，缺乏一个统一的框架来协调不同模型家族（不同参数、目标函数、分词器）之间的并发训练。

Method: 提出Mutual Reinforcement Learning框架，包含三个核心组件：Shared Experience Exchange (SEE)、Multi-Worker Resource Allocation (MWRA)、Tokenizer Heterogeneity Layer (THL)。在GRPO之上实例化三种探针：Peer Rollout Pooling (PRP)、Cross-Policy GRPO Advantage Sharing (XGRPO)、Success-Gated Transfer (SGT)，并通过上下文bandit分析其稳定-支持权衡。

Result: 结果级共享（SGT）在稳定性-支持权衡中占据最有利位置；PRP面临密度比方差和THL残差代价，XGRPO保持策略支持但改变标量基线。

Conclusion: 互强化学习框架有效支持异构LLM策略的并发训练，结果级成功传递是最优的经验共享策略。

Abstract: We introduce Mutual Reinforcement Learning, a framework for concurrent RL post-training in which heterogeneous LLM policies exchange typed experience while keeping separate parameters, objectives, and tokenizers. The framework combines a Shared Experience Exchange (SEE), Multi-Worker Resource Allocation (MWRA), and a Tokenizer Heterogeneity Layer (THL) that retokenizes text and aligns token-level traces across incompatible vocabularies. This substrate makes the experience-sharing design question operational across model families. We instantiate three controlled probes on top of GRPO: data-level rollout sharing via Peer Rollout Pooling (PRP), value-level advantage sharing via Cross-Policy GRPO Advantage Sharing (XGRPO), and outcome-level success transfer via Success-Gated Transfer (SGT). A contextual-bandit analysis characterizes their structural positions on a stability-support trade-off: PRP pays density-ratio variance and THL residual costs, XGRPO preserves on-policy actor support while changing scalar baselines, and SGT supplies a rescue-set score direction toward verified peer successes. In the evaluated regime, outcome-level sharing occupies the favorable point of this trade-off.

</details>


### [191] [When Are Experts Misrouted? Counterfactual Routing Analysis in Mixture-of-Experts Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07260)
*Youngsik Yoon,Siwei Wang,Wei Chen,Jungseul Ok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoE模型的标准top-k路由器在推理困难的脆弱token上会错误分配专家，而存在同等计算量的更优路由路径未被选择；仅微调最后一层路由器即可提升推理任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准top-k路由器在训练后是否选择了最优路由路径很少被直接评估，作者希望系统性地检验路由器分配的质量。

Method: 固定模型参数，对每个token采样同等计算量的替代路由候选，用下一个token预测概率作为效用评分；对比标准路由与替代路由的效果。并仅对最终层的路由器进行最小化更新（冻结专家和其他层路由器）。

Result: 标准路由器在置信度高的token上表现良好，但在驱动推理的脆弱token上不可靠；所有测试模型（Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, OLMoE-1B-7B）均呈现相同模式；仅更新最终层路由器即可提升AIME 2024+2025和HMMT 2025的pass@K。

Conclusion: MoE路由失败部分源于路由器可达的错误分配（而非仅专家容量不足），根源在于标准top-k训练中语言模型损失仅评估已执行路径，负载均衡仅依赖聚合统计，导致对脆弱token的分配不优。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models route each token to a small subset of experts, but whether the routes selected by a trained top-$k$ router are good ones is rarely evaluated directly. Holding the model fixed, we compare each standard route against sampled equal-compute alternatives for the same token and score each by the next-token probability it assigns to the realized token in a verified reasoning trajectory. The result is sharply token-conditional: the standard router is well-aligned with route utility on confident tokens but uninformative on the fragile tokens that drive hard reasoning, where lower-loss equal-compute routes consistently exist inside the frozen model but are not selected. The same pattern holds across Qwen3-30B-A3B, GPT-OSS-20B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and OLMoE-1B-7B, and follows structurally from how standard top-$k$ training evaluates routing decisions: the language modeling loss scores only the executed route, and load balancing depends only on aggregate routing statistics. A minimal router-only update to the final-layer router, leaving every expert and every other router frozen, is sufficient to shift pass@K on AIME 2024+2025 and HMMT 2025 for both Qwen3-30B-A3B and GPT-OSS-20B, suggesting that at least part of the failure reflects router-reachable misallocation rather than expert capacity alone.

</details>


### [192] [PerCaM-Health: Personalized Dynamic Causal Graphs for Healthcare Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07267)
*Elahe Khatibi,Ziyu Wang,Saba A. Farahani,Di Huang,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PerCaM-Health是一个从纵向健康数据中学习个性化动态因果图的框架，通过知识引导的群体图与患者特定时间证据结合，支持反事实干预推理，在动态健康基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间因果发现方法在个性化医疗决策中存在不足：群体级模型稳定但非个性化，单个患者发现不可靠（轨迹短、噪声大、不规则、非平稳），缺乏能支持干预推理的患者特异性时变机制建模。

Method: 先学习知识引导的群体时间因果图，再利用患者特定时间证据和滚动窗口更新进行保守自适应演化，生成可解释的因果图序列，并耦合时间结构方程实现反事实查询。

Result: 在半合成动态健康基准上，PerCaM-Health在图恢复、动态边追踪和干预方向准确性方面优于群体级、单患者级和非个性化时间基线方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模个性化和时间演化能够产生更可靠的因果结构和干预推理能力。

Abstract: Personalized healthcare decisions require reasoning about how physiological and behavioral variables influence an individual patient over time. Existing temporal causal discovery methods are poorly matched to this setting: cohort-level models provide stable but non-personalized structures, while per-patient discovery is unreliable because individual trajectories are short, noisy, irregular, and non-stationary. This creates a fundamental gap between population-level causal modeling and the patient-specific, time-varying mechanisms needed for intervention reasoning. We introduce PerCaM-Health, a framework for learning personalized dynamic causal graphs from longitudinal health data. The framework learns a knowledge-guided population temporal graph, then conservatively adapts and evolves it using patient-specific temporal evidence and rolling-window updates, producing interpretable and auditable graph sequences. By coupling these graphs with temporal structural equations, the framework enables patient-level counterfactual queries, such as estimating short-horizon outcome changes under hypothetical behavioral interventions. Experiments on a semi-synthetic dynamic health benchmark show that PerCaM-Health improves graph recovery, dynamic edge tracking, and intervention direction accuracy compared to cohort-level, per-patient, and non-personalized temporal baselines. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling personalization and temporal evolution yields more reliable causal structure and intervention reasoning.

</details>


### [193] [bispectrum: Selective $G$-Bispectra Made Practical](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07270)
*Johan Mathe,Adele Myers,Simon Mataigne,Nina Miolane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 bispectrum，一个开源 PyTorch 库，实现了七种群作用下的选择性 G-双谱计算，作为可微模块可直接集成到机器学习流水线中，将计算复杂度从 O(|G|²) 降至 O(|G|)，并在低数据、中等容量场景下优于现有池化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: G-双谱是信号在群作用下完备不变量的理论工具，在机器学习中有显著优势，但因其高计算成本和缺乏统一实现而难以推广使用。

Method: 开发了 bispectrum 开源库，实现了七种群作用的可选 G-双谱，作为可微 PyTorch 模块；对有限群利用选择性将复杂度从 O(|G|²) 降至 O(|G|)；对平面旋转使用圆盘双谱；对球面三维旋转引入增强选择性双谱，将系数从 O(L³) 降至 Θ(L²)。

Result: 在 GPU 上亚毫秒级完成选择性 G-双谱计算；在三个经典基准数据集上，G-双谱在低数据、中等容量场景下一致优于 norm pooling、gated pooling、Fourier-ELU pooling、max pooling 和数据增强卷积基线。

Conclusion: 选择性 G-双谱能高效提供近乎精确的 G-不变性，作为池化层在数据有限时具有明显优势，且该库统一了多种群作用的实现，便于实际部署。

Abstract: Many machine learning tasks are invariant under the action of a group $G$ of transformations: signal classification can be invariant under translations, image classification under 2D rotations, and spherical-image classification under 3D rotations. The $G$-bispectrum is a principled complete invariant of a signal (retaining all all signal's information up to the group action) with proven benefits in machine learning and as a pooling layer in deep networks. However, its deployment has been hampered by high computational cost and a patchwork of group-specific implementations. We present bispectrum, an open-source, fully unit-tested PyTorch library that implements selective $G$-bispectra for seven different group actions, as differentiable modules that can be directly incorporated into machine learning pipelines and deep learning architectures. For finite groups $G$, selectivity reduces the computational cost from $O(|G|^2)$ to $O(|G|)$. For planar rotations, we leverage the disk bispectrum. For spherical 3D rotations, we introduce an augmented selective bispectrum at band-limit $L$ which reduces the cost from $O(L^3)$ to $Θ(L^2)$ coefficients. We profile the entire library (for which we implemented various compute optimizations), showing that it delivers near-exact $G$-invariance with its selective $G$-bispectra computed in sub-millisecond time on GPU (up to commonly used bandlimits). We evaluate the benefits of incorporating $G$-bispectra as pooling layers into deep learning architectures on three classical benchmark datasets --comparing against norm pooling, gated pooling, Fourier-ELU pooling, max pooling, and (non-equivariant) data-augmented convolutional baselines. Results show that $G$-bispectra consistently outperform alternatives in the low-data, moderate-capacity regime.

</details>


### [194] [Bifurcation Models: Learning Set-Valued Solution Maps with Weight-Tied Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07277)
*Caleb Jore,Jialin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分岔模型（bifurcation models），通过权重共享的动力学视角，让不同初始化收敛到不同稳定均衡，从而学习解集的吸引子景观，而非单一分支。理论证明宽泛集值映射可由正则均衡动力学表示，实验表明该方法能无标签发现多个有效均衡并优于单分支监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学与组合问题存在多个正确解，但标准监督学习通过选择单一解作为目标来回避歧义，这种隐藏的选择器可能是任意的、不连续的，且比底层解集更难学习。因此需要一种能够自然表示多解集的学习范式。

Method: 提出分岔模型（bifurcation models），采用权重共享的动力学系统，不同初始化可收敛到不同稳定均衡，模型表示的是吸引子景观而非单一人为选择的分支。理论证明具有局部Lipschitz分支的宽泛集值映射可由正则均衡动力学表示，且诱导的选择器几乎处处正则。

Result: 在受挫Ising模型实验中，该动力学方法无需分支标签即可发现多个有效均衡，且性能优于单分支监督。Allen-Cahn实验表明多样性并非自动产生，需要显式鼓励，但存在准确率-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 分岔模型为多解问题提供了一种无需显式多标签标注的学习框架，通过动力学吸引子自然表示解集，理论完备且实验有效，同时指出了多样性激励的必要性与权衡。

Abstract: Many scientific and combinatorial problems admit multiple correct solutions, not a single label. Standard supervised learning resolves this ambiguity by choosing one solution as the target, but this hidden selector can be arbitrary, discontinuous, and harder to learn than the underlying solution set. We study bifurcation models, a weight-tied dynamical view in which different initializations can converge to different stable equilibria, so the model represents an attractor landscape rather than one chosen branch. We prove that broad set-valued maps with locally Lipschitz branches can be represented by regular equilibrium dynamics and that the induced selectors are almost everywhere regular, while manual selectors can be arbitrarily irregular. Experiments on frustrated Ising models show that such dynamics can discover multiple valid equilibria without branch labels and outperform single-branch supervision. Allen--Cahn experiments further show that diversity is not automatic: it can be encouraged explicitly, but with an accuracy--diversity tradeoff.

</details>


### [195] [Predictive but Not Plannable: RC-aux for Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07278)
*Wenyuan Li,Guang Li,Keisuke Maeda,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RC-aux辅助目标，通过时间轴上的多步开环预测和空间轴上的预算约束可达性监督，修正潜世界模型中潜空间与规划之间的时空错配问题，显著提升长期目标导向规划性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型在短时预测上表现良好，但其潜空间与长期规划目标之间存在时空错配：局部预测监督训练出的潜空间，其欧氏距离无法反映有限动作预算下的实际可达性。

Method: 提出RC-aux辅助目标，包含两个轴：1) 时间轴：多时域开环预测超越单步一致性；2) 空间轴：预算条件化可达性监督+时间硬负样本，区分最终可达与当前规划时域内可达的状态。测试时可被可达性感知规划器利用。

Result: 在LeWorldModel上实例化RC-aux，在目标条件像素控制任务和LIBERO-Goal扩展上，以较小额外成本显著提升LeWM风格的规划性能。

Conclusion: 潜世界模型规划不仅依赖于预测精度，还依赖于学习到的表示是否编码了下游搜索所需的时间和几何结构信息。

Abstract: A latent world model may achieve accurate short-horizon prediction while still inducing a latent space that is poorly aligned with planning. A key issue is spatiotemporal mismatch: these models are often trained with local predictive supervision, but deployed for long-horizon goal-directed search in latent spaces where Euclidean distance may not reflect what is reachable within a finite action budget. We present the Reachability-Correction auxiliary objective (RC-aux), a lightweight correction for this mismatch in reconstruction-free latent world models. RC-aux keeps the world-model backbone unchanged and adds planning-aligned supervision along two axes. Along the time axis, multi-horizon open-loop prediction trains the model beyond one-step consistency. Along the space axis, budget-conditioned reachability supervision, together with temporal hard negatives, encourages the latent space to distinguish states that are eventually reachable from those reachable within the current planning horizon. At test time, the learned reachability signal can also be used by a reachability-aware planner to favor trajectories that are both goal-directed and attainable under the available budget. We instantiate RC-aux on LeWorldModel and evaluate it under both continuation-training and matched-from-scratch settings. Across goal-conditioned pixel-control tasks and a LIBERO-Goal extension, RC-aux improves LeWM-style planning with modest additional cost. These results suggest that planning with latent world models depends not only on predictive accuracy, but also on whether the learned representation encodes the temporal and geometric structure required by downstream search. The code is available at https://github.com/Guang000/RC-aux.

</details>


### [196] [Mask2Cause: Causal Discovery via Adjacency Constrained Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07280)
*Omar Muhammad,Pasupuleti Dhruv Shivkant,Deepak N. Subramani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mask2Cause是一个端到端框架，通过前向传播中的掩码注意力机制直接恢复因果图，在多种基准上实现了SOTA的因果发现性能，并将预测模型参数平均减少70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的时序因果发现方法要么依赖组件式架构无法捕获共享系统动态，要么采用解耦的后处理图提取方式容易过拟合虚假相关，缺乏端到端的学习框架。

Method: 提出Mask2Cause框架，包含倒置变量嵌入（Inverted Variable Embedding）和邻接约束掩码注意力机制（Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention），使用同方差或异方差目标进行训练，在前向传播过程中直接恢复底层因果图。

Result: 在从合成混沌动力学到真实生物模拟等多种基准上实现了SOTA因果发现，参数复杂度显著低于基线方法；推断的因果结构可使预测模型参数平均减少70%以上，同时保持预测精度。

Conclusion: Mask2Cause通过端到端学习方式有效解决了现有方法的局限性，在因果发现效率和参数效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Leveraging deep learning for causal discovery in time series remains challenging because existing neural methods predominantly rely on component-wise architectures that fail to capture shared system dynamics or employ decoupled post-hoc graph extraction that risks overfitting to spurious correlations. We propose $\textbf{Mask2Cause}$, an end-to-end framework that recovers the underlying causal graph directly during the forecasting forward pass. Our approach introduces an Inverted Variable Embedding and an Adjacency-Constrained Masked Attention mechanism, trained with homoscedastic or heteroscedastic objectives to capture causal influences in both mean and variance. Empirical results on diverse benchmarks, from synthetic chaotic dynamics to realistic biological simulations, demonstrate state-of-the-art causal discovery with significantly reduced parameter complexity compared to standard baselines. We further show that inferred causal structures can be used to reduce parameter count of forecasting models by more than 70% on average while maintaining predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [197] [The Convergence Gap: Instruction-Tuned Language Models Stabilize Later in the Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07282)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“收敛差距”（convergence gap）诊断方法，通过逐层解码next-token分布并测量与最终分布的距离，发现经过指令微调的模型比预训练模型更晚收敛到最终预测。实验表明，晚期MLP层是影响这一延迟的最强双向操作点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注模型最终输出质量，但忽视了模型内部各层在推理过程中何时“承诺”最终预测。理解这一收敛动态对于解释指令微调如何改变模型行为至关重要。

Method: 提出convergence gap诊断方法：逐层解码next-token分布，计算与模型最终分布的KL散度。在6组预训练与指令微调配对模型上实验，使用多种控制条件（端点匹配读头、无端点同历史检查、固定历史模板重放）。进一步通过匹配前缀干预（matched-prefix interventions）定位关键层。

Result: 指令微调模型在更深的层才收敛到最终预测。晚期MLP移植实验显示：将IT晚期MLP移植到PT主机使晚期KL增加+0.34 nats，反向减少-0.51 nats；随机扰动仅产生+0.003 vs +0.327的效果。

Conclusion: 指令微调模型表现出“更晚收敛”的鲁棒预测动力学特征，晚期MLP计算是控制该延迟的最强双向操作点。

Abstract: Final outputs hide when a checkpoint commits to its next-token prediction. We introduce the convergence gap, a model-diffing diagnostic that decodes each layer's next-token distribution and measures its distance to the model's own final distribution. Across six paired pretrained and instruction-tuned checkpoints in native prompting regimes, instruction-tuned checkpoints remain farther from their final predictions later into the stack. The effect persists under endpoint-matched raw and tuned readouts, endpoint-free same-history checks, and fixed-history template replay. Matched-prefix interventions identify late MLP windows as the largest tested leverage point: late IT grafts into PT hosts increase late KL by +0.34 nats, while PT-late swaps into IT hosts reduce it by -0.51 nats; matched random late perturbations give only +0.003 versus +0.327 for the true late graft. A preselected Gemma case study provides behavior-facing plausibility for the same late swap, without serving as a benchmark claim. These results identify a robust predictiondynamics signature of post-training: released instruction-following checkpoints tend to settle later, and late MLP computation is the strongest tested bidirectional handle on that delay under matched histories.

</details>


### [198] [Instruction Tuning Changes How Upstream State Conditions Late Readout: A Cross-Patching Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07284)
*Yifan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文通过"首次分歧交叉修补"方法，研究了预训练基础模型与指令微调模型在早期层状态与晚期层堆栈的交互机制，发现指令微调的晚期层堆栈需要读取自身微调后的上游状态才能产生更大效果，最终层MLP中的稀疏特征部分中介了这一效应。研究对五种密集模型家族进行了验证，并提出了操作性建议：在将行为定位到晚期层时，需检验该效应在另一检查点的上游状态下是否依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性工作已识别出模型内部的行为控制点（如拒绝方向、助手/人格轴、稀疏聊天微调特征），但这些结果通常将行为定位在中晚期层。作者想知道早期计算与晚期堆栈如何协作，将那些差异转化为下一个token的边际（margin），即从内部表征差异到最终输出概率差异的因果路径尚不明确。

Method: 提出"首次分歧交叉修补"（first-divergence cross-patching）方法：在预训练基础模型（PT）与指令微调模型（IT）首次产生分歧的token处，交叉替换每个模型的早期层状态与晚期层堆栈。通过这种因果干预，分离出不同训练方式（SFT、CPT）对早期/晚期层依赖的差异。实验在五种密集模型家族（4B-32B）上进行，并结合稀疏特征分析和强制token评分进行机制验证。

Result: 1) 相同基座的指令微调模型显示出依赖自身早期层状态的晚期效应；2) OpenMath2数学域SFT和代码/生物医学CPT对照中，晚期效应可大部分从基础模型的早期层状态移植；3) 在所有模型家族中，IT晚期堆栈在IT上游状态下+2.44 logits，在PT上游状态下+0.76 logits，交互效应+1.68为正；4) 最终MLP层的稀疏特征部分中介了该效应，且由上游修补驱动；5) 强制token评分显示局部token选择可改变后续精确答案的成功率。

Conclusion: 指令微调的晚期层堆栈并非自包含的功能模块，其效果高度依赖于读取自身微调后的上游状态。研究者提出操作性建议：配对检查点研究中，若将行为差异定位到晚期层，应先检验该效应在另一检查点的上游状态下是否仍存在，才能将晚期堆栈视为独立的功能单元。

Abstract: Recent interpretability work has identified model-internal handles on post-trained behavior, including refusal directions, assistant/persona axes, and sparse chat-tuning features. These results localize where behaviors can be read out or controlled, often in middle-to-late layers. We ask how earlier computation and the late stack cooperate to turn those differences into next-token margins. To test this, we introduce first-divergence cross-patching: at the first token where pretrained base (PT) and instruction-tuned (IT) checkpoints disagree, we cross each model's earlier-layer state with each model's late stack. The diagnostic separates training recipes: same-base instruction-following descendants show late effects that depend on their own earlier-layer state, while OpenMath2 math-domain SFT and controlled code/biomed CPT controls with verified domain learning do not; for OpenMath2, the late effect is already largely portable from base earlier-layer state. Across five dense families (4B-32B), the IT late stack adds +0.76 logits from PT upstream and +2.44 from IT upstream, giving a +1.68 interaction that is positive in every family. Thus the late stack has a real PT-upstream effect, but its larger effect in the IT checkpoint appears only when it reads its own post-trained upstream state. Sparse features in final MLP layers partially mediate the effect and are driven by upstream patches, supporting a handoff from earlier state to final-layer feature activation to IT-token margin. Forced-token scoring shows that the local token choice can change later exact-answer success. Operationally, paired-checkpoint studies that localize a difference to late layers should test whether it survives under the other checkpoint's upstream state before treating the late stack as self-contained.

</details>


### [199] [Pretraining Induces a Reusable Spectral Basis for Downstream Task Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07302)
*Junjie Yu,Yue Wang,Zihan Deng,Yan Zhu,Wenxiao Ma,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统谱分析发现，预训练模型微调时，权重矩阵的主要奇异向量保持高度稳定，并在不同下游任务间共享，这揭示了预训练建立了一个可重用的谱坐标系。基于此，提出仅优化前导谱系数的参数高效方法，在GLUE上仅用0.2%可训练参数达到竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解微调过程中某些方向为何未被探索：这些稳定方向是与下游任务无关的噪声，还是已经编码了任务相关结构且无需调整？回答这一问题对于理解预训练知识如何迁移至关重要。

Method: 对视觉和语言模型进行系统谱分析；提出冻结预训练奇异向量、仅优化前导谱系数的参数高效微调方法。

Result: 预训练权重矩阵的前导奇异向量在微调中高度稳定且跨无关下游任务共享；在更大数据集上预训练的模型在分布偏移或任务变化下表现出更大的谱稳定性；所提方法在GLUE上以0.2%可训练参数达到竞争性能。

Conclusion: 稳定方向编码了可迁移的结构而非无关噪声：成功的预训练发现了下游任务继承并运行的谱基。

Abstract: Finetuning pretrained models occurs in a low-dimensional subspace of the full parameter space. Prior work has focused on characterizing this optimization subspace, but largely ignored the complementary question: why do certain directions remain unexplored during finetuning? Are these stable directions irrelevant to downstream tasks, or do they already encode task-relevant structure that requires no further adjustment? Answering this question is central to understanding how pretrained knowledge transfers. Through systematic spectral analysis across vision and language models, we show that the leading singular vectors of pretrained weight matrices remain highly stable under finetuning and are shared across unrelated downstream tasks, revealing that pretraining establishes a reusable spectral coordinate system. Models pretrained on larger datasets exhibit greater spectral stability under distribution shift or task change, directly linking pretraining scale to geometric transferability. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient method that freezes pretrained singular vectors and optimizes only leading spectral coefficients, achieving competitive performance on GLUE with 0.2% trainable parameters. Our results reveal that the stable directions encode transferable structure rather than irrelevant noise: successful pretraining discovers spectral bases that downstream tasks inherit and operate within.

</details>


### [200] [Latent Order Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07304)
*Emil Carlsson,Newton Mwai,Fredrik D. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在序老虎机（LOB）模型，放松了传统潜在老虎机对离散潜在状态下奖励分布精确共享的严格假设，仅要求状态内动作偏好呈偏序关系。通过上置信界和后验采样两种算法，LOB在同状态实例共享奖励参数时与完整先验方法竞争力相当，在奖励尺度不同时表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜在老虎机算法虽能利用跨实例结构减少探索，但其要求潜在状态的奖励后验分布精确已知且共享参数，这在现实中难以满足（如不同用户对同一类电影评分尺度不同）。我们需要一种更鲁棒的方法，仅依赖动作偏好的偏序关系而非精确奖励值来建模跨实例结构。

Method: 提出潜在序老虎机（LOB）框架，将传统潜在老虎机的严格假设放松为每个状态下仅需已知动作偏好的偏序关系。设计上置信界（UCB）算法求解LOB问题，可同时处理完全序和偏序两种情况，并给出遗憾上界。另提出后验采样算法以提升实际性能。

Result: 理论层面给出了UCB算法的遗憾上界。实验表明：当同状态实例共享奖励参数时，两种LOB算法与完整先验潜在老虎机性能相当；当奖励尺度存在差异时，LOB方法显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: LOB通过仅依赖偏序先验知识，有效解决了传统潜在老虎机对精确奖励参数共享的过度依赖问题，在保持竞争力的同时增强了实用性，尤其适用于不同实例间奖励分布尺度不一致的实际场景。

Abstract: Bandit algorithms solve diverse sequential decision-making problems, but are often too sample-inefficient for from-scratch personalization. To substantially reduce exploration times, latent bandit algorithms exploit cross-instance structure implied by discrete latent states, provided that the posterior distribution of rewards and latent states is known and accurate. However, obtaining an accurate model of this structure is difficult, and a small number of latent states may be insufficient to characterize the reward distributions in all problem instances. We propose latent order bandits (LOB), relaxing the assumptions of latent bandits to require only prior knowledge of a partial order of action preferences in each state. This allows instances of the same state to vary in reward distributions, as long as the partial order of actions is shared. For example, groups of users on a streaming service may agree on which movie genres are the best but rate experiences on different scales. We give an upper-confidence bound procedure for the LOB problem, applicable to both total and partial latent orders, and give an upper bound on its regret. To improve empirical performance, we propose a posterior-sampling algorithm and show, in a suite of experiments, that both are competitive with full-prior latent bandits when same-state instances share reward parameters, and preferable to them when reward scales differ between instances with the same latent state.

</details>


### [201] [Generative Modeling with Flux Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07319)
*Peter Pao-Huang,Xiaojie Qiu,Stefano Ermon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux Matching 是一种新的生成模型范式，它将基于分数的模型推广到更广泛的非保守向量场族，通过放宽条件使得向量场的设计成为可调节的选择，从而支持更快的采样、可解释的机制模型以及变量间的有向依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于分数的生成模型要求模型精确匹配数据得分（即保守向量场），这限制了模型设计的灵活性。作者希望提出一种更宽松的框架，允许存在无穷多个向量场其稳态分布为数据分布，从而将向量场本身从固定目标转变为可设计的选择。

Method: Flux Matching 目标函数不要求模型等于数据得分，而是施加了一个更弱的条件——只要向量场的稳态分布是数据分布即可。这使得模型可以学习到非保守的向量场，并允许在训练过程中直接施加归纳偏置、结构先验和动力学性质。

Result: 在高维图像数据集上表现强劲，并实现了更快的采样速度、可解释的机制模型，以及能够编码变量间有向依赖关系的动力学。

Conclusion: Flux Matching 通过将向量场从固定目标转变为设计选择，开辟了生成建模的新维度，在保持性能的同时提供了更大的灵活性和更广泛的应用场景。

Abstract: We introduce Flux Matching, a new paradigm for generative modeling that generalizes existing score-based models to a broader family of vector fields that need not be conservative. Rather than requiring the model to equal the data score, the Flux Matching objective imposes a weaker condition that admits infinitely many vector fields whose stationary distribution is the data. This flexibility enables a class of generative models that cannot be learned under score matching, in which inductive biases, structural priors, and properties of the dynamics can be directly imposed or optimized. We show that Flux Matching performs strongly on high-dimensional image datasets and, more importantly, that our added freedom unlocks a range of applications including faster sampling, interpretable and mechanistic models, and dynamics that encode directed dependencies between variables. More broadly, Flux Matching opens a new dimension in generative modeling by turning the vector field itself into a design choice rather than a fixed target. Code is available at https://github.com/peterpaohuang/flux_matching.

</details>


### [202] [Rethinking Importance Sampling in LLM Policy Optimization: A Cumulative Token Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07331)
*Yuheng Zhang,Chenlu Ye,Shuowei Jin,Changlong Yu,Wei Xiong,Saurabh Sahu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CTPO（累积令牌策略优化），通过累积令牌IS比率结合位置自适应裁剪，解决了现有强化学习后训练方法中IS比率的偏差-方差困境，在数学推理基准上取得最佳平均性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法面临偏置-方差困境：令牌级IS比率（PPO、GRPO）忽略前缀状态分布差异引入偏差；完整序列比率提供精确修正但方差过大；GSPO通过长度归一化改善数值稳定性但偏离精确修正。需要一个兼具无偏性和低方差的解决方案。

Method: 1) 识别累积令牌IS比率（逐令牌比率的累积乘积）作为理论上有原则的解决方案，证明其在令牌级策略梯度下为每个梯度项提供无偏前缀修正且方差严格低于完整序列比率；2) 提出CTPO，结合累积令牌IS比率与位置自适应裁剪，根据累积对数比率的√t自然增长缩放对数空间裁剪边界。

Result: 在工具集成推理设置下的多个数学推理基准上，CTPO在两种模型规模下相比GRPO和GSPO基线均达到最佳平均性能。

Conclusion: 累积令牌IS比率结合位置自适应裁剪是解决off-policy策略梯度估计中偏置-方差困境的有效方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning, including reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), has emerged as a powerful approach for LLM post-training. Central to these approaches is the design of the importance sampling (IS) ratio used in off-policy policy-gradient estimation. Existing methods face a fundamental bias-variance dilemma: token-level IS ratios, as adopted by PPO (Schulman et al., 2017) and GRPO (Shao et al., 2024), introduce bias by ignoring prefix state distribution mismatch; full sequence ratios provide exact trajectory-level correction but suffer from high variance due to the multiplicative accumulation of per-token ratios, while GSPO (Zheng et al., 2025) improves numerical stability via length normalization at the cost of deviating from the exact full-sequence IS correction. In this work, we identify the cumulative token IS ratio, the product of per-token ratios up to position $t$, as a theoretically principled solution to this dilemma. We prove that, under the token-level policy-gradient formulation, this ratio provides an unbiased prefix correction for each token-level gradient term and has strictly lower variance than the full sequence ratio. Building on this insight, we propose CTPO (Cumulative Token Policy Optimization), which combines the cumulative token IS ratio with position-adaptive clipping that scales log-space clip bounds according to the natural $\sqrt{t}$ growth of the cumulative log-ratio. This yields more consistent regularization across token positions. We implement and evaluate CTPO in the tool-integrated reasoning setting on several challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks, achieving the best average performance across both model scales compared with strong GRPO and GSPO baselines. Code will be available at https://github.com/horizon-llm/CTPO.

</details>


### [203] [Beyond Linear Attention: Softmax Transformers Implement In-Context Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07333)
*Zixuan Xie,Xinyu Liu,Claire Chen,Shuze Daniel Liu,Rohan Chandra,Shangtong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在理论上分析了使用标准softmax注意力的Transformer在上下文强化学习（ICRL）中的工作机制，证明了其前向传播等价于一种新的加权softmax时序差分（TD）学习算法的迭代更新，并给出了收敛性分析和预训练损失下的全局最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICRL理论分析大多依赖线性注意力（将softmax替换为单位映射）这一不切实际的简化假设，缺乏对实际中广泛使用的softmax注意力的理论理解。

Method: 1) 证明具有特定参数的softmax注意力Transformer的前向传播等价于加权softmax TD学习的迭代更新；2) 给出了加权softmax TD算法（一种在核空间进行策略评估的新RL算法，涵盖线性TD和表格TD作为特例）；3) 在一定收缩条件下证明了策略评估误差随层数增加而衰减；4) 证明了这些参数是预训练损失的全局最小值点。

Result: 首次在没有线性注意力简化假设的情况下，为ICRL提供了严格的理论分析，建立了softmax注意力与加权softmax TD算法之间的等价关系，并给出了收敛性和最优性保证。

Conclusion: softmax注意力Transformer可以通过其前向传播隐式地执行一种核空间的策略评估算法，且该机制可以从预训练中自然涌现。

Abstract: In-context reinforcement learning (ICRL) studies agents that, after pretraining, adapt to new tasks by conditioning on additional context without parameter updates. Existing theoretical analyses of ICRL largely rely on linear attention, which replaces the softmax function in the standard attention with an identity mapping. This paper provides the first theoretical understanding of ICRL without making the unrealistic linear attention simplification. In particular, we consider the standard softmax attention used in practice. We show that, with certain parameters, the layerwise forward pass of a Transformer with such softmax attention is equivalent to iterative updates of a weighted softmax temporal difference (TD) learning algorithm. Here, weighted softmax TD is a new RL algorithm that performs policy evaluation in kernel space and adopts both linear TD and tabular TD as special cases. We also prove that under a certain contraction condition, the policy evaluation error decays as the number of layers grows, with the identified parameters above. Finally, we prove that those parameters are a global minimizer of a pretraining loss, explaining their emergence in our numerical experiments.

</details>


### [204] [MISA: Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention for Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07363)
*Ruijie Zhou,Fanxu Meng,Yufei Xu,Tongxuan Liu,Guangming Lu,Muhan Zhang,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MISA提出了一种混合专家式索引器稀疏注意力机制，通过轻量级路由从多个索引器头中动态选择少量活跃头进行细粒度令牌评分，在保持表达力的同时大幅降低计算成本，兼容DeepSeek-V3.2和GLM-5，无需额外训练即可匹配DSA性能，并实现约3.82倍速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA的索引器因使用大量查询头（如64头）进行细粒度前缀令牌评分，在长上下文场景下成为主导计算开销。现有方法在保持表达力与降低计算成本之间存在矛盾。

Method: 将DSA索引器头视为混合专家池，通过轻量级路由器基于块级统计信息选择少量活跃头，仅这些头执行细粒度令牌评分；并提出层级变体，先用路由结果扩大候选集，再用DSA索引器重排序以精确恢复最终选中的令牌。

Result: 仅用8个活跃头，在LongBench上匹配DSA性能，索引器头减少8倍（DeepSeek-V3.2）和4倍（GLM-5）；在128K上下文中保持完整Needle-in-a-Haystack热力图，每层恢复92%以上DSA选中令牌；TileLang内核在单张H200上实现约3.82倍加速。

Conclusion: MISA有效解决了DSA索引器在多查询头下的计算瓶颈，通过混合专家路由机制在保持稀疏注意力表达力的同时显著降低推理开销，是一种高效且无需额外训练的即插即用方案。

Abstract: DeepSeek Sparse Attention (DSA) sets the state of the art for fine-grained inference-time sparse attention by introducing a learned token-wise indexer that scores every prefix token and selects the most relevant ones for the main attention. To remain expressive, the indexer uses many query heads (for example, 64 on DeepSeek-V3.2) that share the same selected token set; this multi-head design is precisely what makes the indexer the dominant cost on long contexts. We propose MISA (Mixture of Indexer Sparse Attention), a drop-in replacement for the DSA indexer that treats its indexer heads as a pool of mixture-of-experts. A lightweight router uses cheap block-level statistics to pick a query-dependent subset of only a few active heads, and only those heads run the heavy token-level scoring. This preserves the diversity of the original indexer pool while reducing the per-query cost from scoring every prefix token with every head to scoring it with only a handful of routed heads, plus a negligible router term computed on a small set of pooled keys. We further introduce a hierarchical variant of MISA that uses the routed pass to keep an enlarged candidate set and then re-ranks it with the original DSA indexer to recover the final selected tokens almost exactly. With only eight active heads and no additional training, MISA matches the dense DSA indexer on LongBench across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5 while running with eight and four times fewer indexer heads respectively, and outperforms HISA on average. It also preserves fully green Needle-in-a-Haystack heatmaps up to a 128K-token context and recovers more than 92% of the tokens selected by the DSA indexer per layer. Our TileLang kernel delivers roughly a 3.82 times speedup over DSA's original indexer kernel on a single NVIDIA H200 GPU.

</details>


### [205] [StreamPhy: Streaming Inference of High-Dimensional Physical Dynamics via State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07384)
*Panqi Chen,Yifan Sun,Shikai Fang,Xiao Fu,Lei Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StreamPhy是一个端到端框架，用于从不规则稀疏测量数据中实时流式推断高维多模态物理场，相比现有方法在精度上提升至少48%，推理速度提升20-100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（扩散生成模型、函数张量方法）通常在离线场景下工作，依赖完整时间观测数据，或推理代价高昂，无法高效实时地从稀疏不规则测量中推断高维多模态物理场演化。

Method: StreamPhy集成了三个关键组件：1) 数据自适应观测编码器，对任意观测模式具有鲁棒性；2) 结构化状态空间模型，支持跨不规则时间间隔的内存高效在线更新；3) 函数张量特征线性调制（FT-FiLM）解码器，用于连续场生成。理论上证明了FT-FiLM比函数Tucker模型更具表达能力。

Result: 在三个代表性物理系统上的实验表明，StreamPhy一致优于现有基线方法，精度提升至少48%，推理速度比扩散方法快20-100倍。

Conclusion: StreamPhy为实时流式推断稀疏测量中的物理场演化提供了一种高效准确的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring the evolution of high-dimensional and multi-modal (e.g., spatio-temporal) physical fields from irregular sparse measurements in real time is a fundamental challenge in science and engineering. Existing approaches, including diffusion-based generative models and functional tensor methods, typically operate in offline settings, depend on full temporal observations, or incur substantial inference cost. We propose StreamPhy, an end-to-end framework that enables efficient and accurate streaming inference of full-field physical dynamics from incoming irregular sparse measurements. The framework integrates a data-adaptive observation encoder that is robust to arbitrary observation patterns, a structured state-space model that supports memory-efficient online updates across irregular time intervals, and an expressive Functional Tensor Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FT-FiLM) decoder for continuous-field generation. We prove that FT-FiLM is more expressive than the functional Tucker model, admitting a richer function class for handling complex dynamics. Experiments on three representative physical systems under challenging sampling patterns show that StreamPhy consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, with at least 48\% improvement in accuracy and up to 20--100X faster inference than diffusion-based methods.

</details>


### [206] [Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07386)
*Karthick Krishna M.,Haricharan Balasundaram,Rahul Vaze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了嵌套演化可行集下的凸优化问题（CONES），其中目标函数固定但可行域随时间嵌套收缩。针对凸损失函数，提出了惰性算法，实现了$O(T^{1-\beta})$后悔度和$O(T^\beta)$移动成本的折中；针对强凸或$\alpha$-sharp损失函数，提出了Frugal算法，实现了零后悔度和$O(\log T)$移动成本，并证明了该结果的最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的嵌套凸体追逐问题推广到更具一般性的优化视角（CONES），旨在同时最小化在线算法相对于 hindsight 静态最优基准的后悔度与总移动成本，同时保证所有时刻的可行性。

Method: 针对凸损失函数，提出惰性算法（lazy-algorithm），通过调节参数$\beta$实现后悔度与移动成本的折中。针对强凸或$\alpha$-sharp损失函数，提出Frugal算法。

Result: 对于凸损失，惰性算法达到$O(T^{1-\beta})$后悔度和$O(T^\beta)$移动成本；对于强凸或$\alpha$-sharp损失，Frugal算法达到零后悔度和$O(\log T)$移动成本，且下界证明移动成本$\Omega(\log T)$是最优的。

Conclusion: CONES框架统一并推广了嵌套凸体追逐问题，Frugal算法在强凸/$\alpha$-sharp损失下达到最优的零后悔与对数移动成本权衡。

Abstract: Convex Optimization with Nested Evolving Feasible Sets (CONES)} is considered where the objective function $f$ remains fixed but the feasible region evolves over time as a nested sequence $S_1 \supseteq S_2 \supseteq \cdots \supseteq S_T$. The goal of an online algorithm is to simultaneously minimize the regret with respect to hindsight static optimal benchmark and the total movement cost while ensuring feasibility at all times. CONES is an optimization-oriented generalization of the well-known nested convex body chasing problem. When the loss function is convex, we propose a lazy-algorithm and show that it achieves $O(T^{1-β}), O(T^β)$ simultaneous regret and movement cost for any $β\in (0,1]$, over a time horizon of $T$. When the loss function is strongly convex or $α$-sharp, we propose an algorithm Frugal that simultaneously achieves zero regret and a movement cost of $O(\log T)$. To complement this, we show that any online algorithm with $o(T)$ regret has a movement cost of $Ω(\log{T})$ for both cases, proving optimality of Frugal.

</details>


### [207] [Unsolvability Ceiling in Multi-LLM Routing: An Empirical Study of Evaluation Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07395)
*Saloni Garg,Amit Sagtani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过206,000个查询-模型对的大规模研究，揭示了多层级LLM路由中报告的"不可解性天花板"在很大程度上源于评估伪影（如评判偏差、截断和格式不匹配），而非模型真实能力不足，导致路由性能估计被显著高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将跨LLM路由的优化空间归因于查询的"不可解性"，即池中无模型能解决的查询。然而，这种不可解性的测量可能受到评估方法缺陷的污染，导致路由性能估计失真。

Method: 使用206,000个查询-模型对，在六个基准上评估Gemma 4和Llama 3.1系列模型，采用LLM-as-a-judge和精确匹配两种指标，通过双评判验证和精确匹配锚定分解不可解性来源，并通过随机特征和打乱标签对照验证路由器的退化行为。

Result: 发现不可解性主要来自：评判偏好冗长胜于正确、固定生成长度截断、输出格式不匹配。标准路由器退化为多数类预测（~79%选择最小模型），造成13-17个百分点的机会成本损失。

Conclusion: 现有路由性能估计被显著高估，需要更可靠的评估协议，包括双评判验证、精确匹配锚定和成本敏感目标函数。

Abstract: Efficient routing across multiple LLMs enables cost-quality tradeoffs by directing queries to the cheapest capable model. Prior work attributes routing headroom to an "unsolvability ceiling", queries no model in the pool can solve. We present a large-scale study of multi-tier LLM routing with 206,000 query-model pairs across six benchmarks (MMLU, MedQA, HumanEval, MBPP, Alpaca, ShareGPT) using the Gemma 4 and Llama 3.1 families. Evaluating with both LLM-as-a-judge and exact-match metrics, we show that a substantial portion of reported unsolvability stems from evaluation artifacts: (i) systematic judge biases favoring verbosity over correctness, (ii) truncation under fixed generation budgets, and (iii) output format mismatches. Through dual-judge validation and exact-match grounding, we reduce measured unsolvability across tasks. We introduce a decomposition framework attributing failures to these artifacts, revealing consistent patterns across domains and model families. These artifacts also distort router training signals: standard routers collapse to majority-class prediction (~79% smallest-tier optimal), confirmed via random-feature and shuffled-label controls, incurring a 13-17 percentage point opportunity cost. We provide actionable recommendations including dual-judge validation, exact-match anchoring, and cost-sensitive objectives. Our findings suggest existing routing headroom estimates are substantially inflated, underscoring the need for reliable evaluation protocols in multi-LLM systems.

</details>


### [208] [Rubric-based On-policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07396)
*Junfeng Fang,Zhepei Hong,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Gengsheng Li,Houcheng Jiang,Dan Zhang,Haiyun Guo,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROPD框架，利用结构化语义评分规则（rubrics）替代教师logits，实现仅需教师生成回答即可进行同策略蒸馏，性能超越传统基于logits的OPD方法，样本效率提升高达10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同策略蒸馏（OPD）依赖教师logits，限制了其在黑盒场景（如专有LLM）中的应用。作者认为结构化语义评分规则可作为教师logits的可扩展替代方案，使OPD仅需教师生成的回答即可运行。

Method: ROPD框架：通过教师-学生对比生成提示特定的评分规则（rubrics），然后利用这些规则对学生同策略采样结果进行评分，用于同策略优化。

Result: ROPD在大多数场景下超越先进的基于logits的OPD方法，样本效率提升高达10倍。

Conclusion: 基于评分规则的OPD可作为基于logits的OPD的灵活、兼容黑盒的替代方案，为跨专有和开源LLM的可扩展蒸馏提供了简单而强大的基线。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is a powerful paradigm for model alignment, yet its reliance on teacher logits restricts its application to white-box scenarios. We contend that structured semantic rubrics can serve as a scalable alternative to teacher logits, enabling OPD using only teacher-generated responses. To prove it, we introduce ROPD, a simple yet foundational framework for rubric-based OPD. Specifically, ROPD induces prompt-specific rubrics from teacher-student contrasts, and then utilizes these rubrics to score the student rollouts for on-policy optimization. Empirically, ROPD outperforms the advanced logit-based OPD methods across most scenarios, and achieving up to a 10x gain in sample efficiency. These results position rubric-based OPD as a flexible, black-box-compatible alternative to the prevailing logit-based OPD, offering a simple yet strong baseline for scalable distillation across proprietary and open-source LLMs. Code is available at https://github.com/Peregrine123/ROPD_official.

</details>


### [209] [Have Graph -- Will Lift? The Case for Higher-Order Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07397)
*Bastian Rieck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出拓扑深度学习领域模型多样性与基准数据集稀缺性之间的严重不匹配，呼吁社区在将现有图数据集提升为高阶数据集的同时，积极收集新的数据集以支撑该领域的研究基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑深度学习领域模型快速发展，但缺乏合适的基准数据集，研究者往往只能将现有图数据集提升为高阶形式，亟需新的数据集来支撑研究。

Method: 作为观点论文，作者通过分析当前领域现状，指出模型多样性（消息传递、层论等）与数据集稀缺之间的矛盾，并倡导社区收集新数据集。

Result: 指出现有研究常通过提升图数据集来获得高阶信息，但这种方式不足以支撑领域发展。

Conclusion: 鼓励社区积极寻找和收集新的数据集，以巩固拓扑深度学习的研究基础。

Abstract: After a somewhat rocky start, geometry and topology have established a foothold in machine learning. Message passing, either on graphs or higher-order complexes, is one of the main drivers of geometric deep learning, and paradigms that were once considered to be firmly in the realm of the abstract-like sheaves-have been "tamed" to serve as novel inductive biases for model architectures in topological deep learning. The veritable diversity of models, however, is in stark contrast to the scarcity of suitable benchmark datasets. As a result, researchers often resort to lifting existing graph datasets to include higher-order information. In this opinion paper, I want to encourage the community to also source new datasets, which may be used to prop up the foundations of our research field.

</details>


### [210] [Emergent Symbolic Structure in Health Foundation Models: Extraction, Alignment, and Cross-Modal Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07407)
*Gajendra Katuwal,Advait Koparkar,Salar Abbaspourazad,Anshuman Mishra,Sarvesh Kirthivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种后训练框架，将健康基础模型的冻结嵌入分解为可解释的方向（符号），并利用这些符号实现跨模态对齐，无需重新训练。实验表明，符号与健康状况和生理属性选择性关联，跨模态迁移可保留95%以上的性能，且对齐恢复了一个共享的低维生理信息子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 健康基础模型从可穿戴传感器中学习有用表征，但解释其编码内容以及在训练后将知识跨模态迁移仍然困难。

Method: 提出后训练框架，将冻结嵌入分解为可解释的符号方向，利用这些符号对齐不同模态的嵌入空间，无需重新训练。在PPG和加速度计数据的三个基础模型上评估，模型在约172K参与者、~2000万分钟无标签数据上预训练，在30K受试者上分析。

Result: 提取的符号与健康状况和生理属性选择性关联，且这些关联在模态和架构间部分共享。跨模态迁移保留95%以上领域内性能，在领域方向上几乎对称，有限配对数据下饱和。

Conclusion: 健康基础模型的嵌入包含跨模态共享的可解释符号组织，支持无需联合训练的跨域迁移。

Abstract: Health foundation models (FMs) learn useful representations from wearable sensors, but interpreting what they encode and transferring that knowledge across modalities after training remains difficult. We present a post-training framework that decomposes frozen embeddings into interpretable directions, referred to as symbols, and use these symbols to align the embedding spaces without retraining. We evaluate the framework on three FMs for photoplethysmography (PPG) and accelerometer data, independently pretrained on ~20M minutes of unlabeled data from ~172K participants, and analyzed on a held-out cohort of 30K subjects. We find that extracted symbols associate selectively with health conditions and physiological attributes, and these associations are partially shared across modalities and architectures. Cross-modal transfer via symbols retains more than 95% of in-domain performance, is nearly symmetric across domain directions, and saturates with limited paired data, together indicating that alignment recovers a shared low-dimensional subspace rich in physiological information. Overall, these results suggest that health FM embeddings contain an interpretable symbolic organization that is shared across modalities and supports cross-domain transfer without joint training.

</details>


### [211] [Tracking Large-scale Shared Bikes with Inertial Motion Learning in GNSS Blocked Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07412)
*Feng Liu,Kejia Li,Zhiwei Yang,Chunwei Yang,Qun Li,Guobin Wu,Qiang Ni,Ruipeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型的惯性跟踪框架，利用踏板-后轮传动关系将骑行者的周期性蹬踏行为转化为轮速进行动态校准，实验表明该方法在共享单车数据集上相比基线提升至少12%的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNSS在城市峡谷等复杂骑行环境下失效，而纯惯导系统存在累积漂移和滤波鲁棒性差的问题；视觉和激光雷达虽精度高但不适合大规模部署。因此需要在低成本惯性传感器基础上提升定位精度和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出惯性跟踪框架：1）利用混合专家模型，通过多个专家模块捕获共享表征并由门控机制加权，实现多任务学习和不确定性感知的轨迹估计；2）基于自行车踏板与后轮的机械传动关系，探索骑行者周期性蹬踏行为与加速度变化的内在联系，将其转化为车轮转速进行动态校准。

Result: 基于滴滴出行共享单车真实骑行数据的实验表明，所提系统相比基线方法精度提升至少12%，轮速误差在95百分位下低于0.5 m/s。

Conclusion: 融合自行车机械约束与混合专家模型能有效提升低成本惯性传感器在骑行场景下的定位性能，为大规模部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Although Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) provide a general solution for bike tracking outdoors, there still exist complex riding environments where only inertial navigation systems work, such as urban canyons. Despite decades of research, localization using only low-cost inertial sensors still faces challenges such as cumulative drifts and poor robustness caused by filtering methods. Furthermore, sensors such as visual and LiDAR could provide reliable measurements, but they are not suitable for large-scale deployment. In this paper, we propose an inertial tracking framework that integrates bicycle mechanical constraints with a mixture-of-experts model. Specifically, we leverage multiple expert modules to capture shared representations and weight them through the gating mechanism, thus improving multi-task learning performance and enabling uncertainty-aware trajectory estimation. Furthermore, based on the mechanical transmission between the pedal and the rear wheel of a bike, we explore the intrinsic relationship between the rider's periodic pedalling behaviors and acceleration variations, and convert such patterns into bike's wheel speed for dynamic calibration. Experiments with real-world riding data from shared bikes of the DiDi ride-hailing platform demonstrate that our system improves the accuracy of baselines by at least 12%, with wheel speed errors below 0.5 m/s at 95-percentile.

</details>


### [212] [Risk-Consistent Multiclass Learning from Random Label-Subset Membership Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07413)
*Jiaxu Su,Junpeng Li,Changchun Hua,Yana Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机标签子集查询的弱监督多分类学习框架，通过查询-响应观测直接生成弱监督信号，设计了无偏风险估计器和修正风险估计器，并建立了理论保证和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督学习依赖精确类标签，但获取精确标签成本高、不可靠或受隐私限制；相比询问精确类别，询问标签是否属于某个子集更容易实现。然而现有弱监督学习缺乏对这种直接查询-响应观测机制的系统性刻画。

Method: 1) 建模查询-响应观测的数据生成分布；2) 在ERM框架下推导目标风险的无偏估计量；3) 针对负经验风险和过拟合问题，引入基于非负校正和绝对值校正的修正风险估计器；4) 建立无偏估计量的条件泛化误差和超额风险界，以及修正估计量的偏差与一致性理论结果。

Result: 理论分析证明了无偏估计量的条件泛化界和超额风险界，修正估计量具有偏差和一致性保证；实验表明直接查询-响应学习的可行性及风险校正的稳定化效果。

Conclusion: 该框架为标签子集查询这种弱监督形式提供了系统的理论基础，通过风险校正有效解决了负经验风险和过拟合问题，扩展了弱监督学习的理论边界。

Abstract: Obtaining accurate class labels is often costly or unreliable, and may also be limited by privacy or other practical conditions. Compared with asking an annotator to provide the exact class, it is often easier to ask whether the true label belongs to a certain label subset. This query-response form defines a distinct weak-supervision mechanism: weak supervision information is generated through feedback on a label subset. Although weakly supervised learning has studied many learning frameworks, most existing work starts from established weak label objects. A systematic characterization is still lacking for weakly supervised learning generated directly by such query response observations. This paper proposes a multiclass learn ing framework under random label-subset queries. We model the data-generating distribution of query-response observations and derive an unbiased estimator of the target risk under the empirical risk minimization (ERM) framework. To address negative empirical risk and the associated overfitting problem, we introduce corrected risk estimators based on non-negative and absolute-value corrections. Theoretical analysis establishes a conditional generalization and excess-risk bound for the unbiased estimator, and a bias-and-consistency result for the corrected risk estimator. Experiments under the matched random-query mechanism demonstrate the feasibility of direct query-response learning and the stabilization effect of risk correction.

</details>


### [213] [SR$^2$-LoRA: Self-Rectifying Inter-layer Relations in Low-Rank Adaptation for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07420)
*Fengqiang Wan,Yipeng Lin,Kan Lv,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SR$^2$-LoRA方法，通过约束层间关系漂移来缓解参数高效微调模型在类增量学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。该方法对齐当前任务样本上前模型和当前模型诱导的关系矩阵的奇异值，理论上证明了该对齐方式比直接逐元素对齐对估计扰动更具鲁棒性。实验表明该方法有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，且优势随任务数量增加而更加明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型结合参数高效微调在类增量学习中展现出潜力，但灾难性遗忘问题依然存在。本文从层间关系漂移（即学习新任务时逐层表示之间关系的逐步破坏）这一新颖角度分析灾难性遗忘，并理论证明了这种漂移的增加会减少先前学习任务的分类边界，从而降低模型性能。

Method: 提出SR$^2$-LoRA（Self-Rectifying inter-layer Relation Low-Rank Adaptation），通过约束层间关系漂移来缓解灾难性遗忘。具体地，构建由前模型和当前模型在当前任务样本上诱导的关系矩阵，并对齐它们对应的奇异值。

Result: 在标准CIL基准上的大量实验表明，SR$^2$-LoRA有效缓解了灾难性遗忘，而且其优势随着任务数量的增加而更加明显。

Conclusion: 本文从层间关系漂移的新视角分析灾难性遗忘，提出的SR$^2$-LoRA方法通过奇异值对齐方式约束关系漂移，理论上相比逐元素对齐更具鲁棒性，实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Pre-trained models with parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) have demonstrated promising potential for class-incremental learning (CIL), yet catastrophic forgetting still persists when adapting models to new tasks. In this paper, we present a novel perspective on catastrophic forgetting through the analysis of inter-layer relation drift, i.e., the progressive disruption of relationships among layer-wise representations during the learning of new tasks. We theoretically show that the increase of such drift reduces the classification margins of previously learned tasks, thereby degrading overall model performance. To address this issue, we propose \underline{S}elf-\underline{R}ectifying inter-layer \underline{R}elation Low-Rank Adaptation~(SR$^2$-LoRA), a simple yet effective method that mitigates catastrophic forgetting by constraining inter-layer relation drift. Specifically, SR$^2$-LoRA constructs the relation matrices induced by the previous and current models on current-task samples, and aligns the corresponding singular values. We further theoretically show that this alignment exhibits greater robustness to estimation perturbations than direct entry-wise alignment. Extensive experiments on standard CIL benchmarks demonstrate that SR$^2$-LoRA effectively mitigates catastrophic forgetting, with its advantages becoming more pronounced as the number of tasks increases. Code is available in the \href{https://github.com/FqWan24/SR-2-LoRA}{repository}.

</details>


### [214] [A Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering Algorithm for Archive-Scale Online Mass Spectrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07424)
*Shao Shi,Xin Yang,Huiran Feng,Jianhuai Ye,Tianlong Hu,Yaling Zeng,Tzung-May Fu,Lei Zhu,Huizhong Shen,Chen Wang,Shu Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FASC的动态系统聚类框架，通过解耦相似性核与优化逻辑，在保证确定性收敛的同时实现线性时间复杂度，成功应用于2500万大气气溶胶质谱数据的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有聚类方法在可扩展性、度量灵活性和算法稳定性之间存在权衡，难以高效处理现代在线质谱产生的多TB级数据流，阻碍了从环境数据中提取化学洞察。

Method: FASC框架采用密度增强相似性选择（Density-Augmented Similarity Selection）规则和几何约束，将相似性核与严格优化逻辑在架构上解耦，保证确定性的、顺序无关的收敛，并实现严格线性的经验运行时扩展（O(N)）。

Result: 在标准机器学习基准上实现>99.5%聚类纯度和0.99调整兰德指数；在2500万大气气溶胶质谱数据上展示线性运行时间，自主映射二次无机气溶胶的大气老化路径，并分离出丰度<0.2%的超稀有工业示踪物。

Conclusion: FASC框架有效解决了大规模质谱数据聚类中的计算瓶颈，为环境大数据挖掘提供了可扩展的基础设施。

Abstract: Modern online mass spectrometry generates multi-terabyte data streams critical for understanding Earth's environmental systems. However, extracting actionable chemical insights from these repositories is impeded by a computational bottleneck: existing clustering methods force a compromise among scalability, metric flexibility, and algorithmic stability. Here, we introduce Flexible Adaptive Stable Clustering (FASC), a dynamical systems framework that resolves these constraints by architecturally decoupling the similarity kernel from rigorous optimization logic. Unlike legacy heuristics that suffer from stochastic drift and algorithmic blending, FASC employs a Density-Augmented Similarity Selection rule and geometric constraints to guarantee deterministic, order-independent convergence. After validating FASC on canonical machine-learning ground truths (achieving >99.5% cluster purity and 0.99 Adjusted Rand Index), we deployed the framework on 25 million mass spectra of atmospheric aerosols. Demonstrating strictly linear empirical runtime scaling (O(N)), FASC autonomously mapped atmospheric aging pathways of secondary inorganic aerosols while isolating ultra-rare industrial tracers (<0.2% abundance), providing a scalable infrastructure for mining environmental big data.

</details>


### [215] [GameGen-Verifier: Parallel Keypoint-Based Verification for LLM-Generated Games via Runtime State Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07442)
*Chaobo Jia,Ruipeng Wan,Ting Sun,Weihao Tan,Borui Wan,Yuxuan Tong,Guangming Sheng,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GameGen-Verifier提出了一种针对LLM生成游戏的自动化验证范式，通过将规范分解为可验证关键点并转化为独立验证单元，在100个游戏数据集上达到92.2%的准确率，相比基线方法提升16.6倍速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent-as-a-Verifier方法将验证简化为开放式的游戏过程，导致判决结果受可达性限制、耗时、覆盖有限且对Agent游戏能力敏感，缺乏可靠的自动化验证手段。

Method: 将规范分解为可验证关键点并转化为独立验证单元；每个单元将游戏运行时修补到具体目标状态，执行有限交互，并根据关键点断言判断结果；实现GGV-Harness提供并发管理、运行时隔离和故障恢复。

Result: 在VeriGame数据集（100个游戏，7个类别）上，GameGen-Verifier对人工判断的准确率达92.2%，而覆盖增强的Agent-as-a-Verifier基线仅为58.8%，同时墙上时钟时间减少16.6倍。

Conclusion: GameGen-Verifier通过分解验证任务和状态修补机制，有效解决了LLM生成游戏的自动化验证难题，在准确性和效率上显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: LLM-based game generation promises to turn natural-language specifications into executable games, but progress is limited by the lack of reliable automated verification. Unlike conventional code generation, game correctness is defined over long-horizon interaction: a game may appear correct while violating core mechanics such as state updates, interaction rules, and phase transitions. Existing Agent-as-a-Verifier approaches collapse verification into open-ended gameplay, making verdicts reachability-bound, time-consuming, coverage-limited, and sensitive to the agent's gameplay ability.
  We present GameGen-Verifier, an automated verification paradigm for LLM-generated games that decomposes a specification into verifiable keypoints and grounds them into independent verification units. Each unit patches the game runtime into a concrete target state, executes a bounded interaction, and judges the outcome against the keypoint assertion. We implement GGV-Harness, a scalable agentic harness providing concurrency management, runtime isolation, and fault recovery.
  On VeriGame, our dataset of 100 games across seven genres, GameGen-Verifier achieves up to 92.2% accuracy against human judgments versus 58.8% for the coverage-enforced Agent-as-a-Verifier baseline, while reducing wall-clock time by up to 16.6x.

</details>


### [216] [Inference-Time Attribute Distribution Alignment for Unconditional Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07456)
*Hao Luan,See-Kiong Ng,Chun Kai Ling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于最优控制的方法，在推理阶段为预训练的无条件扩散模型调整属性分布，无需重新训练或微调，通过添加时变扰动作为控制信号，优化可微的分布匹配目标并惩罚控制代价以保持数据保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可控生成方法主要关注单个样本的属性控制，难以满足整个样本群体遵循特定属性分布（如人口统计平衡或语义比例）的需求。作者将这一场景形式化为推理阶段属性分布对齐问题，并寻求无需重新训练预训练模型的解决方案。

Method: 将推理阶段的属性分布对齐问题构建为逆扩散过程上的最优控制问题，将逆扩散过程视为动力系统的 rollout，并添加与时间相关的加性扰动作为控制信号。通过基于最优控制的算法求解扰动，以优化可微的分布匹配目标，同时通过惩罚控制代价来保持数据保真度。

Result: 在图像生成实验结果表明，所提出的即插即用方法相比基线方法，能够更好地将属性分布对齐到多样化且灵活的测试时目标分布，且无需重新训练或微调预训练扩散模型。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的推理阶段属性分布对齐框架，通过最优控制理论解决了无条件扩散模型在群体层面上的可控生成问题，具有良好的灵活性和即插即用特性。

Abstract: Inference-time controllable generation is essential for real-world applications of unconditional diffusion models. However, most existing techniques focus on individual samples, struggling in applications that require the sample population to follow specific attribute distributions (e.g., demographic balance or semantic proportions). We formalize this setting as the inference-time attribute distributional alignment problem for pretrained unconditional diffusion models. To address this, we cast inference-time attribute distributional alignment as an optimal control problem over the reverse diffusion process, viewing the process as the rollout of a dynamical system and augmenting it with additive, time-dependent perturbations as control. We solve for the perturbations using an optimal-control-based algorithm to optimize a differentiable distribution-matching objective while penalizing control effort to preserve data fidelity. Experiment results in image generation demonstrate that our proposed plug-and-play approach can better align attribute distributions to diverse and flexible test-time targets compared to baselines, without retraining or finetuning the pretrained diffusion model.

</details>


### [217] [Estimation of Motor Unit Parameters from Surface Electromyograms using an Informed Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07458)
*Kaja Balzereit,Malte Mechtenberg,Axel Schneider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自编码器的逆问题求解方法，可从表面肌电记录中同时估计多个运动单位参数（如神经支配区中心和传导速度），通过将物理定律融入潜空间学习，在合成数据上实现了较高的估计精度（神经支配区中心平均绝对误差2.6mm，传导速度0.17m/s），减少了传统白盒建模所需的手动工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动单位参数（如神经支配区中心、传导速度）对提高神经力学模型的保真度至关重要，但现有非侵入式估计方法依赖白盒建模，需要大量手动建模工作，且参数具有个体特异性并随肌肉收缩变化，难以同时估计多个参数。

Method: 提出一种**有信息自编码器**（informed autoencoder），该模型在潜空间中学习运动单位参数，同时重建表面肌电信号，并在训练过程中强制遵循连接参数与EMG信号之间的物理定律，从而将数据驱动机器学习与物理先验结合，解决非线性损失函数的逆问题。

Result: 在合成数据实验中，神经支配区中心估计的平均绝对误差为2.5989 mm，电位的传导速度估计的平均绝对误差为0.1697 m/s。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了同时估计多个运动单位参数的可行性，通过整合数据驱动学习显著减少了手动建模工作量，为后续在真实肌电数据上的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Motor unit parameters such as the innervation zone centre or the conduction velocity of the electrical potential harbour the potential to improve the fidelity of neuromechanical models used for movement and force prediction. Determining these parameters in a non-invasive way is challenging, as they are subject-specific and may vary with muscle contraction. Existing work on the estimation of motor unit parameters mainly relies on white-box modelling and therefore requires substantial manual modelling effort. This work targets the simultaneous estimation of multiple subject-specific motor unit parameters from electromyography (EMG) recordings measured non-invasively at the skin surface. This results in an inverse problem with a nonlinear loss function. To address this problem, an informed autoencoder is developed. This autoencoder reconstructs the surface EMG recordings while learning the parameters in its latent space and adhering to physical laws that relate the parameters to the EMG signals. In experiments on synthetic data, innervation zone centres are estimated with a mean absolute error of 2.5989 $\mathrm{mm}$, and conduction velocities of the electric potential are estimated with a mean absolute error of 0.1697 $\mathrm{m}\mathrm{s}^{-1}$. These results demonstrate the plausibility of this novel approach, which enables the simultaneous estimation of several motor unit parameters while reducing manual modelling effort through the integration of data-driven machine learning.

</details>


### [218] [NPMixer: Hierarchical Neighboring Patch Mixing for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07476)
*Jung Min Choi,Vijaya Krishna Yalavarthi,Lars Schmidt-Thieme*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NPMixer，一种利用可学习平稳小波变换和邻域混合器块的分层架构，在7个基准数据集的28个实验设置中71.4%的MSE指标上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列预测面临局部时间动态与全局依赖关系的建模挑战，现有方法难以同时捕捉多尺度时间模式与跨变量相关性。

Method: 1) 可学习平稳小波变换(Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform)：自适应学习滤波器系数，以数据驱动方式将信号分解为趋势分量和细节分量；2) 邻域混合器块(Neighboring Mixer Block)：在非重叠patches上通过分层MLP层捕获局部时间动态，并扩展感受野以捕捉多尺度依赖；3) 通道混合编码器(Channel-Mixing Encoder)：对高频分量应用以学习通道相关性，同时保持全局趋势的稳定性。

Result: 在7个基准数据集的28个实验设置中，NPMixer在20个设置(71.4%)上取得了最优MSE，一致超越现有SOTA模型。

Conclusion: NPMixer通过分层架构结合可学习小波分解和邻域混合机制，有效解决了多尺度时间动态与跨变量依赖的联合建模问题，为多元时间序列预测提供了新的有效方案。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting remains a challenge due to the complexity of local temporal dynamics and global dependencies across multiple variables.
  In this paper, we propose \textbf{N}eighboring \textbf{P}atching \textbf{Mixer} (\textbf{NPMixer}), a hierarchical architecture featuring a Learnable Stationary Wavelet Transform that adaptively learns filter coefficients to decompose signals into trend and detail components in a data-dependent manner.
  Our framework introduces a Neighboring Mixer Block that captures local temporal dynamics through a series of hierarchical MLP layers operating on non-overlapping patches.
  Specifically, the mixer block utilizes MLPs to learn temporal patterns within and across these patches, expanding the receptive field to capture multi-scale dependencies.
  A Channel-Mixing Encoder is applied to high-frequency components to learn channel correlations while preserving the stability of the underlying global trend.
  Extensive experiments on seven benchmark datasets demonstrate that NPMixer consistently outperforms state-of-the-art models, achieving better performance in 20 out of 28 ($71.4\%$) evaluated experimental setups for MSE.

</details>


### [219] [SHRED: Retain-Set-Free Unlearning via Self-Distillation with Logit Demotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07482)
*Zizhao Hu,Ameya Godbole,Johnny Tian-Zheng Wei,Mohammad Rostami,Jesse Thomason,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SHRED方法，一种无需保留集的大语言模型机器遗忘方法，通过识别遗忘样本中高信息量token进行选择性遗忘，在遗忘效果与模型效用间实现帕累托最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器遗忘方法大多依赖保留集来防止模型效用退化，这增加了数据依赖性和部署复杂性。本文旨在消除对保留集的依赖，同时实现有效的遗忘和模型效用保持。

Method: 两阶段方法：(1) 选择阶段：对遗忘样本进行前向传播，收集每个token的自回归概率，选择最低概率（最高香农信息量）的token作为遗忘位置，其余作为良性锚点。(2) 训练阶段：构建修改后的KL目标，在遗忘位置降级记忆token的logit，在良性位置保持原始分布，通过单一top-KL自蒸馏目标同时驱动遗忘和效用保持。

Result: 在四个标准遗忘基准上，SHRED建立了新的遗忘效果与模型效用之间的帕累托最优平衡，超越了依赖保留集的方法。对重学习攻击和成员推断攻击具有鲁棒性，且在多次连续遗忘后仍能保持稳定的模型效用。

Conclusion: SHRED成功实现了无需保留集的LLM机器遗忘，核心洞察在于遗忘样本中的token承载的记忆信息量不均等，通过选择性地遗忘高信息token可同时达到有效遗忘和效用保持。

Abstract: Machine unlearning for large language models (LLMs) aims to selectively remove memorized content such as private data, copyrighted text, or hazardous knowledge, without costly full retraining. Most existing methods require a retain set of curated examples to prevent catastrophic degradation of general model utility, creating an extra data dependency that complicates deployment. We propose SHRED (Self-distillation via High-surprisal-only Retain-set-free Entropy Demotion), a retain-set-free unlearning method built on a key insight: not all tokens within a forget set instance carry memorized information equally. High-information tokens concentrate the model's memorized knowledge, while low-information tokens reflect general language competence. SHRED operates in two stages. (1) Selection: We perform a forward pass on a forget set instance, collect per-token autoregressive probabilities, and select the bottom (lowest probability, highest Shannon information) as forget positions; the remaining positions are retained as benign anchors. (2) Training: We construct modified KL targets that demote the memorized token's logit at forget positions while preserving the original distribution at benign positions. The model is then trained via a single top KL self-distillation objective that simultaneously drives forgetting and utility preservation. We evaluate SHRED across four standard unlearning benchmarks and demonstrate that it establishes a new Pareto-optimal trade-off between forget efficacy and model utility, outperforming retain-set-dependent methods. Our analysis shows that SHRED is robust against relearning attacks and membership-inference attacks, and it maintains stable utility even after many sequential unlearning runs.

</details>


### [220] [Excluding the Target Domain Improves Extrapolation: Deconfounded Hierarchical Physics Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07485)
*Tsuyoshi Okita*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出去混淆层次门控（DHG）机制，通过反事实估计和后门调整消除温度混淆变量，结合由粗到细的物理约束，在锂离子电池温度外推任务上实现46%的RMSE提升（0.215 vs 0.397），并发现预训练时排除目标域数据可提升39%外推性能（RMSE 0.224 vs 0.324）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理约束深度生成模型将物理约束作为单一静态正则项，既未考虑物理定律的层次结构，也未解决混杂变量问题，导致分布外泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出去混淆层次门控（DHG），包含：(1) 利用do-算子的反事实估计和后门调整去除温度混杂；(2) 由粗到细的层次化物理约束；(3) 基于门控机制诊断各约束层被混杂的程度。

Result: (1) 预训练排除目标域数据比包含目标域数据外推RMSE低39%（0.224 vs 0.324）；(2) 在24°C训练、4.0-43.0°C测试的电池温度外推任务上，RMSE=0.215，比无约束基线（0.397）提升46%。

Conclusion: 去混淆层次门控能有效消除温度混杂变量对物理约束学习的影响，且预训练时排除目标域数据反而有助于FNO学习领域无关的物理模式，提升外推性能。

Abstract: Extrapolation to out-of-distribution conditions is a fundamental challenge for physics-constrained deep generative models. Existing methods apply physical constraints as a single static regularization term uniformly across the generation process, and address neither the hierarchical structure of physical laws and the confounding variable problem. We propose the Deconfounded Hierarchical Gate (DHG), which serves as a diagnostic and control mechanism: it identifies when and how strongly temperature confounding contaminates each constraint level, so that hierarchical gates reflect intrinsic physical inconsistency rather than spurious temperature effects. DHG combines counterfactual estimation via the do-operator with backdoor adjustment to remove confounding, then applies Coarse-to-Fine physical constraints progressively. We report a counter-intuitive finding in pretraining: excluding the target-domain data from pretraining outperforms including it by 39% in extrapolation performance (RMSE 0.224 vs. 0.324). This occurs because FNO learns domain-agnostic physical patterns that transfer more effectively when the target domain is withheld. On a lithium-ion battery temperature extrapolation benchmark (trained at 24 degrees Celsius, evaluated at 4.0--43.0 degrees Celsius), our method achieves RMSE = 0.215, a 46% improvement over the unconstrained baseline (Pure CFM: 0.397).

</details>


### [221] [ExpThink: Experience-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Chain-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07501)
*Tingcheng Bian,Yuzhe Zhang,Jing Jin,Jinchang Luo,MingQuan Cheng,Haiwei Wang,Wenyuan Jiang,Miaohui Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ExpThink是一个面向大型推理模型（LRM）的强化学习框架，通过经验引导的奖励塑形（基于最短正确解动态调整阈值）和难度自适应优势函数（基于正确计数归一化）两个机制，在压缩思维链（CoT）长度的同时保持甚至提升推理准确性。实验表明，该方法在多个数学推理基准上平均响应长度减少77%，准确率-效率比提升高达3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）通过长思维链（CoT）推理取得了强性能，但存在过度的token消耗和高推理延迟问题。现有面向CoT压缩的强化学习方法依赖统一、静态的长度惩罚，忽略了模型能力的动态变化和问题级别的难度差异。

Method: 提出ExpThink框架，包含两个互补机制：(1) 经验引导的奖励塑形：跟踪每个问题当前发现的最短正确解，采用三层奖励结构（简洁正确得满分、冗长正确得折扣分、错误得零分），阈值随模型改进自动收紧形成自演进课程；(2) 难度自适应优势函数：用正确计数归一化替代标准差归一化，生成单调难度标度的梯度——放大难问题上的学习信号以保持准确率，抑制简单问题上的梯度以鼓励简洁性。

Result: 在多个数学推理基准上，平均响应长度减少高达77%，同时准确率提升；准确率-效率比（准确率除以平均token数）相比原始基线提升高达3倍，在两个指标上均优于现有基于RL的压缩方法。

Conclusion: 经验引导的奖励塑形与难度自适应优势相结合，能够有效实现"准确率优先、压缩次之"的训练目标，在显著压缩推理长度的情况下不损失甚至提升模型性能。

Abstract: Large reasoning models (LRMs) achieve strong performance via extended chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, yet suffer from excessive token consumption and high inference latency. Existing reinforcement learning (RL) approaches for CoT compression rely on uniform, static length penalties that neglect model capability dynamics and problem-level difficulty variation.
