We propose \textbf{ExpThink}\xspace, an RL framework that addresses both dimensions through two complementary mechanisms. First, \emph{experience-guided reward shaping} tracks the shortest correct solution found so far for each problem and applies a three-tier reward: full credit for concise correct responses, discounted credit for verbose correct ones, and zero for incorrect ones. The threshold tightens automatically with model improvement, forming a self-evolving curriculum that requires no manual scheduling. Second, \emph{difficulty-adaptive advantage} replaces standard deviation normalization with correct-count normalization, yielding monotonically difficulty-scaled gradients that amplify learning on hard problems to preserve accuracy while suppressing gradients on easy ones to encourage brevity. Together, these mechanisms enforce an accuracy-first, compression-second training objective. Experiments on multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that \textbf{ExpThink}\xspace reduces average response length by up to 77\% while simultaneously improving accuracy, achieving up to $3\times$ higher accuracy-efficiency ratio (accuracy divided by average token count) than the vanilla baseline and outperforming existing RL-based compression methods on both metrics.

</details>


### [222] [SGD for Variational Inference: Tackling Unbounded Variance via Preconditioning and Dynamic Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07531)
*Hippolyte Labarrière,Cesare Molinari,Silvia Villa,Lorenzo Rosasco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对黑盒变分推理中随机梯度方差无界的问题，证明了椭圆位置-尺度分布族下ELBO解的存在性，并为配备动态批处理和预条件的投影SGD建立了从有限时间到渐近的完整收敛保证，弥合了随机优化理论与BBVI实践之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBVI中随机梯度的方差无界，违反了标准SGD的假设，仅满足较弱的Blum-Gladyshev条件（方差随距离最优点的距离二次增长）。现有理论分析与实际算法之间存在差距，需要为BBVI提供更严格的优化理论基础。

Method: 聚焦椭圆位置-尺度分布族，使用Minibatch Projected SGD（PSGD），结合动态批处理（dynamic batching）和预条件（preconditioning）策略，在BG条件下进行收敛分析。

Result: 1) 证明了ELBO解的存在性；2) 在BG条件下，为配备动态批处理和预条件的PSGD建立了有限时间和渐近收敛保证；3) 数值实验验证了理论结果的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态批处理与预条件的组合能够在复杂设置下系统性地提供严格的收敛保证，为现代推理任务中的BBVI提供了理论支撑和实践指导。

Abstract: Black-Box Variational Inference (BBVI) typically relies on Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD) to optimize the Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO). However, the stochastic gradients in BBVI inherently exhibit unbounded variance, violating standard assumptions and instead satisfying the weaker Blum-Gladyshev (BG) condition, where variance grows quadratically with distance from the optimum. In this paper, we bridge the gap between stochastic optimization theory and the practical instances of BBVI. Focusing on the broad elliptic location-scale family of parameterized distributions, we offer two main contributions. First, we prove the existence of an ELBO solution, a foundational property usually assumed a priori in the literature. Second, we establish comprehensive convergence guarantees spanning finite-time and asymptotic regimes for Minibatch Projected SGD (PSGD) equipped with dynamic batching and preconditioning under the BG condition. Our theoretical framework demonstrates that dynamic batching combined with preconditioning systematically enables rigorous guarantees even in complex settings. We illustrate our theoretical findings with numerical results, highlighting the efficacy of our approach for modern inference tasks.

</details>


### [223] [On the Invariance and Generality of Neural Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07546)
*Xing Han,Ziyin Liu,Suchi Saria,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可泛化的缩放定律（scaling laws），通过在资源丰富的源领域拟合定律，并将其可靠地迁移到无法进行完整实验的新领域。核心发现是：缩放定律在双射（信息保持）数据变换下保持不变，在非双射变换下会以信息论可预测的方式发生变化，且信息分辨率ρ是跨域迁移的关键轴。在语言、视觉和语音任务上验证了该方法，并展示了两个跨域应用案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的缩放定律拟合需要昂贵的完整扫描实验，消耗大量计算资源，且无法在资源受限的新领域直接应用。因此需要一种可泛化的缩放定律，能够从资源充足的源域迁移到新域，避免重复计算开销。

Method: 识别缩放定律不变性的关键条件：在双射（信息保持）变换下缩放定律保持不变；在非双射变换下，缩放定律以信息论可预测的方式变化，并引入信息分辨率ρ作为跨域迁移的统一轴。在语言、视觉和语音三个领域进行验证。

Result: 成功验证了跨域迁移的可泛化缩放定律：在从通用文本迁移到电子健康记录的语言模型缩放预测中有效，以及在噪声注入场景下预测时间序列分类缩放，数据缩放指数误差在3%以内。

Conclusion: 缩放定律可以基于信息论原理实现跨域迁移，信息分辨率ρ是决定缩放行为变化的关键因素，为资源受限领域提供了实用的缩放预测工具。

Abstract: Neural scaling laws establish a predictable relationship between model performance and data or compute, offering crucial guidance for resource allocation in new domains and tasks. Yet such laws are most needed precisely where they are hardest to obtain: fitting one for a new model task pair demands expensive sweeps that typically exhaust the very compute budget the law is meant to economize. This paper poses the research question of how to develop generalizable scaling laws: laws fit once on a well-resourced source domain and reliably transported to new domains where running a full sweep is infeasible, which requires a fundamental understanding of when and why scaling properties change. We address this by identifying the right invariants: scaling laws are preserved under bijective (information-preserving) transformations of the data and modified in predictable, information-theoretically grounded ways under non-bijective transformations that lower its information resolution $ρ$: a single axis along which a law fit in one domain can be transported to another. We validate this across language, vision, and speech, and demonstrate two cross-domain applications: predicting scaling for language models trained on electronic health records from laws fit on general text, and predicting time-series classification scaling under varying levels of noise injection, recovering the data-scaling exponents to within $3\%$ error.

</details>


### [224] [Beyond Distribution Estimation: Simplex Anchored Structural Inference Towards Universal Semi-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07557)
*Yaxin Hou,Jun Ma,Hanyang Li,Bo Han,Jie Yu,Yuheng Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对标签数据稀缺、无标签数据分布未知的现实场景，提出通用半监督学习范式UniSSL，并设计SAGE方法，通过表示级结构推断（而非伪标签）来建立结构共识，利用单纯形等角紧框架引导类间分离，并引入分布无关的加权策略和辅助分支处理错误伪标签，在五个基准上平均提升8.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有半监督学习方法大多假设无标签数据服从均匀分布或需要充足标签数据来估计其分布，而在现实场景中标签数据稀缺且无标签数据分布未知任意，导致伪标签大量错误，引发表示混淆。本文旨在解决这一被忽视但关键的通用半监督学习问题。

Method: 提出SAGE方法：1) 利用样本间表示关系比伪标签更可靠的观察，转向表示级结构推断，通过Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition捕获高阶样本间依赖建立结构共识；2) 采用满足单纯形等角紧框架的向量作为坐标框架引导类间表示分离；3) 引入基于分布无关度量的加权策略优先利用可靠伪标签，并设计辅助分支隔离潜在错误伪标签。

Result: 在五个标准基准数据集上，SAGE方法持续优于现有最先进方法，平均准确率提升8.52%。

Conclusion: 表示级结构推断相比伪标签方法在通用半监督学习场景下更鲁棒有效，单纯形等角紧框架能有效缓解表示混淆，所提出的加权和辅助分支策略能进一步提升模型性能。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning faces significant challenges in realistic scenarios where labeled data is scarce and unlabeled data follows unknown, arbitrary distributions. We formalize this critical yet under-explored paradigm as Universal Semi-supervised Learning (UniSSL). Existing methods typically leverage unlabeled data via pseudo-labeling. However, they often rely on the idealized assumption of a uniform unlabeled data distribution or require sufficient labeled data to estimate it. In the UniSSL setting, such dependencies lead to numerous erroneous pseudo-labels, thereby triggering representation confusion. Fortunately, we observe that inter-sample relations captured by representations are more reliable than pseudo-labels. Leveraging this insight, we shift our focus to representation-level structural inference to bypass distribution estimation. Accordingly, we propose Simplex Anchored Graph-state Equipartition (SAGE), which captures high-order inter-sample dependencies to establish structural consensus for guiding representation learning. Meanwhile, to mitigate representation confusion, we employ vectors that satisfy a simplex equiangular tight frame to serve as a coordinate frame for guiding inter-class representation separation. Finally, we introduce a weighting strategy based on distribution-agnostic metrics to prioritize reliable pseudo-labels and an auxiliary branch to isolate potentially erroneous pseudo-labels. Evaluations on five standard benchmarks show that SAGE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, with an average accuracy gain of $\textbf{8.52\%}$.

</details>


### [225] [Bilevel Graph Structure Learning, Revisited: Inner-Channel Origins of the Reported Gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07577)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现双层图结构学习的性能提升很大程度上归因于内循环训练动态（隐式梯度正则化）而非图结构重连本身，并提出了frozen-φ控制变量法来分解这两种效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层图结构学习通常被认为通过联合优化模型参数和图结构来提升GNN性能，但现有研究未明确区分性能提升究竟来自图结构重连还是训练过程中的动力学效应。

Method: 提出frozen-φ控制变量法：冻结学习到的图结构，但保留内循环训练调度，从而将双层增益分解为内通道（T步训练动态+隐式梯度正则化）和图通道（图重连本身）。在时空流预测和节点分类任务上进行实验验证。

Result: 时空流预测中，内通道贡献了78-101%的双层增益；节点分类中，在伯努利边级参数化下贡献37-44%。经典谱诊断指标与任务增益可解耦。提出三前提框架可预测所有六个基准上双层增益的符号。

Conclusion: 双层图结构学习的增益被高估，训练动力学效应是关键因素；frozen-φ可作为标准化诊断工具，图蒸馏作为方法无关的补充手段。

Abstract: Bilevel graph structure learning is widely understood to improve graph neural networks by jointly optimizing model parameters and a learned graph structure, with the resulting performance gain attributed to the rewired adjacency. We find that this attribution may be overstated: training-dynamics effects in the inner loop, rather than the rewiring itself, capture a substantial share of the gain. To establish this, we introduce frozen-$φ$, a control that freezes the graph while retaining the inner-loop training schedule. This decomposes the bilevel gain into an inner channel of $T$-step training dynamics with implicit gradient regularization and a graph channel of the graph rewiring itself. On spatio-temporal flow forecasting the inner channel matches or exceeds the full bilevel pipeline, accounting for 78-101% of the gain; on node classification it accounts for 37-44% under a Bernoulli edge-level parameterization. We also verify that classical spectral diagnostics can dissociate from task gain. We propose frozen-$φ$ as a standardized diagnostic for bilevel graph structure learning, with graph distillation as a method-agnostic complement. A three-precondition framework further predicts the sign of the bilevel gain on all six benchmarks.

</details>


### [226] [Your Language Model is Its Own Critic: Reinforcement Learning with Value Estimation from Actor's Internal States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07579)
*Yunho Choi,Jongwon Lim,Woojin Ahn,Minjae Oh,Jeonghoon Shim,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POISE利用策略模型前向传播中已计算的内部状态来估计基线，通过轻量级探针预测可验证奖励，并采用跨轨迹构造保持梯度无偏，从而以更低计算成本实现与DAPO相当的数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法（PPO需要策略规模的价值模型、GRPO需要多次采样保持组均值稳定）成本过高，亟需一种轻量级基线估计方法。

Method: 从策略模型前向传播的隐状态和token熵统计中，用轻量级探针在线预测期望可验证奖励；采用跨轨迹构造（用独立rollout的隐状态预测当前rollout的价值）保持梯度无偏；每次训练只需单次rollout。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B的数学推理基准上，POISE匹配DAPO性能且计算需求更低；价值估计性能与独立LLM规模价值模型相当。

Conclusion: 利用模型自身内部表示可实现更稳定高效的策略优化，无需额外大规模价值模型或多重采样。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for Large Reasoning Models hinges on baseline estimation for variance reduction, but existing approaches pay a heavy price: PPO requires a policy-model scale critic, while GRPO needs multiple rollouts per prompt to keep its empirical group mean stable. We introduce Policy Optimization with Internal State Value Estimation), which obtains a baseline at negligible cost by using the policy model's internal signals already computed during the policy forward pass. A lightweight probe predicts the expected verifiable reward from the hidden states of the prompt and generated trajectory, as well as token-entropy statistics, and is trained online alongside the policy. To preserve gradient unbiasedness despite using trajectory-conditioned features, we introduce a cross-rollout construction that predicts each rollout's value from an independent rollout's internal states. Because POISE estimates prompt value using only a single rollout, it enables higher prompt diversity for a fixed compute budget during training. This reduces gradient variance for more stable learning and also eliminates the compute overhead of sampling costs for detecting zero-advantage prompts. On Qwen3-4B and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B across math reasoning benchmarks, POISE matches DAPO while requiring less compute. Moreover, its value estimator shows similar performance to a separate LLM-scale value model and generalizes to various verifiable tasks. By leveraging the model's own internal representations, POISE enables more stable and efficient policy optimization.

</details>


### [227] [Revisiting Transformer Layer Parameterization Through Causal Energy Minimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07588)
*Jin Xu,Camille Couturier,Victor Rühle,Saravan Rajmohan,James Hensman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出因果能量最小化（CEM）框架，将Transformer层重新解释为对条件能量函数的优化步骤，揭示了多头注意力和门控MLP的能量基础，并识别出包含权重共享、对角加低秩交互等参数化设计空间。在亿级参数语言模型实验中，CEM派生层能以受限参数化稳定训练并匹配基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer块通常结合多头注意力（MHA）和门控MLP，但其参数化选择多基于经验，缺乏理论理解。作者希望建立一个统一的能量最小化框架来揭示这些设计的理论基础。

Method: 提出因果能量最小化（CEM）框架，将Transformer层重铸为对条件能量函数的优化步骤。证明权重共享的MHA可推导为交互能量的梯度更新，门控MLP可视为元素级能量视角。识别出层内权重共享、对角加低秩交互、轻量预条件子和递归更新等设计空间。

Result: 在亿级参数规模的语言模型实验中，CEM派生层训练稳定，尽管参数化受限，仍能匹配对应Transformer基线模型的性能。

Conclusion: CEM为理解Transformer层参数化提供了有用视角，将Transformer架构与基于能量的模型联系起来，激励进一步探索能量引导的层设计。

Abstract: Transformer blocks typically combine multi-head attention (MHA) for token mixing with gated MLPs for token-wise feature transformation, yet many choices in their parameterization remain largely empirical. We introduce Causal Energy Minimization (CEM), a framework that recasts Transformer layers as optimization steps on conditional energy functions while explicitly accounting for layer parameterization. Extending prior energy-based interpretations of attention, CEM shows that weight-tied MHA can be derived as a gradient update on an interaction energy, and that a gated MLP with shared up/down projections can be viewed through an element-wise energy. This perspective identifies a design space for Transformer layers that includes within-layer weight sharing, diagonal-plus-low-rank interactions, lightweight preconditioners, and recursive updates. We evaluate CEM-derived layers in language-modeling experiments at the moderate hundred-million-parameter scale. Despite their constrained parameterizations, these layers train stably and can match corresponding Transformer baselines. Overall, our results suggest that CEM provides a useful lens for understanding Transformer layer parameterization, connecting Transformer architectures to energy-based models and motivating further exploration of energy-guided layer designs.

</details>


### [228] [Optimal Recourse Summaries via Bi-Objective Decision Tree Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07598)
*Ioannis Chatzis,Jason Liartis,Athanasios Voulodimos,Giorgos Stamou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SOGAR框架，将追索摘要学习转化为最优决策树学习问题，通过Pareto前沿同时优化追索有效性和成本，生成稳定、低成本且有效的全局追索摘要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有追索方法虽在实例层面有效，但难以进行全局审计和偏差检测，因为聚合局部行动成本高昂且不一致。追索摘要虽能通过分区共享行动解决此问题，但现有方法未能充分处理追索有效性与成本之间的根本权衡。

Method: 将追索摘要学习形式化为最优决策树学习问题，利用浅层轴对齐决策树和稀疏叶节点行动，通过寻找Pareto前沿获得完整的最优解集，支持事后选择期望的权衡而无需重新训练。

Result: SOGAR在有效性和成本指标上均优于现有方法，能够生成稳定、低成本且有效的追索摘要。

Conclusion: SOGAR通过Pareto最优框架有效解决了追索摘要中有效性与成本的权衡问题，为全局审计和偏差检测提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Actionable Recourse provides individuals with actions they can take to change an unfavorable classifier outcome. While useful at the instance level, it is ill-suited for global auditing and bias detection, since aggregating local actions is costly and often inconsistent. Recourse Summaries address this limitation by partitioning the population and assigning one shared action per subgroup, enabling comparison across subgroups. Designing summaries involves a fundamental trade-off between recourse effectiveness and recourse cost, which existing methods do not adequately address. We introduce Summaries of Optimal and Global Actionable Recourse (SOGAR), which formulates recourse summary learning as an optimal decision tree learning problem and finds the Pareto front -- the complete set of solutions where improving one objective necessarily worsens the other. SOGAR enables post-hoc selection of the desired trade-off without retraining. Using shallow axis-parallel decision trees and sparse leaf actions, SOGAR produces stable, low-cost, and effective recourse summaries that outperform existing approaches across effectiveness and cost metrics.

</details>


### [229] [Learning Large-Scale Modular Addition with an Auxiliary Modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07648)
*Hanato Kikuchi,Ryosuke Masuya,Kazuhiko Kawamoto,Hiroshi Kera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无协变量偏移的模加法学习方法，通过引入辅助模数$Kq$在训练时减少环绕频率和问题难度，同时保持训练和测试输入分布一致，显著提升了大规模模加法学习的可扩展性和样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏方法通过增加训练序列中的零来减少有效加数数量以控制训练难度，但这引入了训练与测试输入分布之间的协变量偏移，导致性能下降。

Method: 引入辅助模数$Kq$进行训练，降低环绕频率和问题难度，同时保持训练与测试时相同的输入分布，避免协变量偏移。

Result: 在$N=64, q=974269$时，使用100K样本训练的模型达到97.0%的$τ$-accuracy（$τ=0.05$），而稀疏方法在相同数据量下仅9.5%，即使扩展到1M样本也只达到93.9%。

Conclusion: 所提方法在大输入长度、大模数和小数据集条件下均优于稀疏方法，具备更强的可扩展性和样本效率。

Abstract: Learning parity functions, more general modular addition, is a challenging machine learning task due to its input sensitivity. A recent study substantially scaled modular addition learning in both the number of summands and the modulus. Its key idea is to increase zeros in training sequences, reducing the effective number of summands and thus controlling training difficulty; however, this induces covariate shift between training and test input distributions. This study theoretically and empirically analyzes this side effect and proposes a covariate-shift-free method for modular addition. Specifically, we introduce an auxiliary modulus $Kq$ during training, which reduces wrap-around frequency and problem difficulty while preserving the same input distribution across training and testing. Experiments show strong scalability and sample efficiency: even for large input length $N$, large modulus $q$, and small datasets -- where the sparse method fails to learn -- our method achieves equal or better match accuracy and relaxed $τ$-accuracy. For example, at $N=64$ and $q=974269$, our method trained on 100K samples achieves $97.0\%$ $τ$-accuracy at $τ=0.05$, while the sparse method achieves only $9.5\%$ with the same data size and $93.9\%$ even when extended to 1M samples.

</details>


### [230] [Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation for Fast Probabilistic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07661)
*George Rapakoulias,Peter Garud,Lingjiong Zhu,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STMD（随机转移图蒸馏），一种无教师框架，通过蒸馏完整转移图来加速扩散模型推理，同时保持随机样本生成能力，无需预训练教师模型、双层优化或轨迹模拟缓存，在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CelebA数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然生成质量高、多样性和分布覆盖好，但推理代价昂贵。现有蒸馏方法依赖预训练教师模型、双层优化或轨迹模拟缓存，训练效率低。作者希望设计一种无需教师、训练高效且保持随机生成能力的加速框架。

Method: 提出STMD框架，蒸馏与采样SDE相关的完整转移图，而非仅建模后验分布的均值（如基于分数的扩散模型）。用条件均值流模型参数化SDE转移，得到一步或几步的随机采样器，保留原始扩散过程的转移结构。方法无需预训练教师、双层优化或轨迹模拟缓存。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10和CelebA数据集上验证了图像生成效果。推导了Wasserstein距离下的收敛界，提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: STMD是一种高效、可扩展的扩散模型加速蒸馏方法，特别适用于需要随机推理的下游任务（如扩散后验采样、逆问题、能量基微调），理论上有收敛保证，实验上生成了高质量样本。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve strong generation quality, diversity, and distribution coverage, but their performance often comes with expensive inference. In this work, we propose Stochastic Transition-Map Distillation (STMD), a teacher-free framework for accelerating diffusion model inference while preserving probabilistic sample generation. In contrast to score-based diffusion models, whose denoising parametrization models the mean of the posterior distribution, STMD distills the full transition map associated with the sampling stochastic differential equation (SDE). We parameterize these SDE transitions with a conditional Mean Flow model, yielding a one- or few-step stochastic sampler that retains the transition structure of the underlying diffusion process. This perspective is especially useful for downstream tasks that require stochastic inference, such as diffusion posterior sampling, inverse problems, and energy-based fine-tuning. Compared to recent distillation methods, STMD requires no pretrained teacher, bi-level optimization, or trajectory simulation and caching, enabling efficient and scalable training. We derive convergence bounds for our method in the Wasserstein distance, providing a strong theoretical foundation for our approach, and validate STMD on various image generation examples on the MNIST, CIFAR-10, and CelebA datasets.

</details>


### [231] [Direction-Preserving Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07662)
*Bardia Zadeh,George A. Constantinides*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了几何框架，用于分析低精度数值格式（如定点、浮点）在表示向量方向时的效率。理论量化了乘积结构码与球面码之间的次优差距，证明标准数值格式并非最优，并通过数值优化找到了更优的标量字母表。实验表明NVIDIA的E2M1格式接近最优，并解释了其在低精度机器学习中的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低精度数值格式广泛应用于机器学习系统，向量的方向表示精度对向量运算至关重要。然而，现有标准格式（如补码、定点、浮点）在方向表示上的理论最优性尚未被严格分析，存在量化其与理论最优之间差距的需求。

Method: 引入几何框架分析乘积结构码（即从有限字母表中选取标量元素构成向量）的方向覆盖能力；理论量化乘积结构码与球面码之间的次优差距；证明标准格式（补码、定点、浮点）在乘积码类中非最优并给出量化差距；通过数值优化寻找最优标量字母表，并在多个块维度上进行实验验证。

Result: 理论给出了乘积结构码与球面码之间的方向覆盖差距量化结果；证明了标准格式的次优性并给出差距量化；数值优化得到了更优的标量字母表；发现NVIDIA的E2M1格式（4比特）接近最优字母表，为其在低精度机器学习中的优秀性能提供了几何解释。

Conclusion: 标准低精度数值格式在向量方向表示上存在理论可量化的次优性，通过优化标量字母表可以提升方向覆盖能力。NVIDIA的E2M1格式在4比特下已接近最优，这为未来设计更优的低精度数值格式提供了理论指导。论文提供了Lean形式化证明和开源代码。

Abstract: Low-precision number formats are widely used in modern machine learning systems due to their efficiency. Accurate direction representation is key to the accuracy of vector operations. This work precisely explores the extent to which the direction of a vector can be represented by selecting its scalar elements from a common finite alphabet of a given size. This is standard practice in machine learning, where low-precision significands may be narrow-width floating-point or integer values. A geometric framework is introduced for analyzing the directional coverage of such product-structured codes.
  This work analytically quantifies the suboptimality gap between such product-structured codes and spherical codes for the vector as a whole, in both low and asymptotically high dimensions. Furthermore, within the product code class, it is proven that the standard formats of two's complement, fixed-point, and floating-point are suboptimal, again with quantified gap, pointing to the potential to develop new scalar number formats.
  Such scalar alphabets are numerically optimized across multiple block dimensions for directional coverage, including the dimension used in NVIDIA's NVFP4 format. Experimental results are presented comparing the performance of standard formats and the optimized alphabet. We find that for four bits, NVIDIA's choice of E2M1 closely approximates the optimized alphabet, providing a geometric explanation for its strong performance in low-precision machine learning workloads and an analytical understanding of the link between that superiority and block size.
  We provide open-source formal proofs in Lean for the theorems in this work, along with the experimental code and the optimized alphabets obtained.

</details>


### [232] [Fortifying Time Series: DTW-Certified Robust Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07690)
*Shijie Liu,Tansu Alpcan,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了时间序列异常检测中基于动态时间规整（DTW）的认证鲁棒防御方法，通过将随机平滑范式扩展到DTW距离，并在ℓ_p范数和DTW距离之间建立下界变换，实现了对时间序列对抗攻击的可认证鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在DTW攻击下F1分数最高提升18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列异常检测在高风险应用中至关重要，而现有防御方法要么是启发式的，要么仅在ℓ_p范数约束下提供认证鲁棒性。然而ℓ_p范数无法捕捉时间序列的时序结构，小的时间扭曲会显著改变ℓ_p度量。DTW是时间序列领域更合适的相似性度量，但目前尚无基于该度量的可认证鲁棒性结果。

Method: 通过桥接ℓ_p范数与DTW距离之间的下界变换，将随机平滑范式扩展到DTW距离，从而构建首个DTW认证鲁棒防御方法。

Result: 在多种数据集和模型上的大量实验验证了理论方法的有效性和实用性。在基于DTW的对抗攻击下，与传统认证模型相比，F1分数最高提升18.7%。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了时间序列异常检测中基于DTW度量的可认证鲁棒防御，填补了该领域的空白，并验证了方法的实用性和显著性能提升。

Abstract: Time-series anomaly detection is critical for ensuring safety in high-stakes applications, where robustness is a fundamental requirement rather than a mere performance metric. Addressing the vulnerability of these systems to adversarial manipulation is therefore essential. Existing defenses are largely heuristic or provide certified robustness only under $\ell_p$-norm constraints, which are incompatible with time-series data. In particular, $\ell_p$-norm fails to capture the intrinsic temporal structure in time series, causing small temporal distortions to significantly alter the $\ell_p$-norm measures. Instead, the similarity metric \emph{Dynamic Time Warping} (DTW) is more suitable and widely adopted in the time-series domain, as DTW accounts for temporal alignment and remains robust to temporal variations. To date, however, there has been no certifiable robustness result in this metric that provides guarantees. In this work, we introduce the first \emph{DTW-certified robust defense} in time-series anomaly detection by adapting the randomized smoothing paradigm. We develop this certificate by bridging the $\ell_p$-norm to DTW distance through a lower-bound transformation. Extensive experiments across various datasets and models validate the effectiveness and practicality of our theoretical approach. Results demonstrate significantly improved performance, e.g., up to 18.7\% in F1-score under DTW-based adversarial attacks compared to traditional certified models.

</details>


### [233] [Toward Better Geometric Representations for Molecule Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07693)
*Shaoheng Yan,Zian Li,Cai Zhou,Qiaojing Huang,Kai Liu,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LENSEs框架，通过表示头、分子感知损失和节点级表示对齐三种机制，改善预训练分子编码器表示不平滑、利用不充分的问题，在分子生成任务中实现97.28%有效性和98.51%稳定性，超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何表示条件分子生成方法将分子表示建模与结构生成解耦，但其效果受限于表示空间质量：预训练分子编码器（如UniMol）产生的表示不平滑，且在生成训练过程中未被充分利用。

Method: LENSEs框架引入三种互补机制：(1) 表示头，与生成任务同时训练，从预训练编码器中提取多层级表示；(2) 分子感知损失，在语义信息丰富的表示空间中优化生成器；(3) 节点级表示对齐损失，显式对齐生成器隐状态与编码器表示，缩小预训练与生成之间的语义鸿沟。

Result: 在GEOM-DRUG数据集上达到97.28%有效性和98.51%分子稳定性，超越现有先进方法。Lipschitz常数降低4.6倍，QM9探针任务验证了更平滑、信息更丰富的精炼表示。

Conclusion: LENSEs有效提升了表示条件生成方法中分子表示的利用效率，同时表明带对齐目标的生成训练可作为分子编码器的一种有潜力的预训练范式。

Abstract: Geometric representation-conditioned molecule generation provides an effective paradigm that decouples molecule representation modeling from structure generation. By decoupling molecule generation into two stages-first generating a meaningful molecule representation, and then generating a 3D molecule conditioned on this representation-the efficiency and quality of the generation process can be significantly enhanced. However, its effectiveness is fundamentally limited by the quality of the representation space: pretrained molecular encoders, such as UniMol, produce representations that are non-smooth and not fully exploited during the generative training process. In this work, we propose LENSEs, a framework that better exploits the potential of molecule representations in representation-conditioned generation methods. In particular, LENSEs introduces three complementary mechanisms: (1) a representation head, simultaneously trained during generative tasks, that extracts multi-level representations from the pretrained encoder; (2) a molecule perceptual loss that optimizes the generator in a semantic-informative representation space; and (3) a node-level representation alignment (REPA) loss that explicitly aligns the generator's hidden states with encoder representations, reducing the semantic gap between pretraining and generation. We demonstrate the effectiveness of these improvements through extensive molecule generation tasks. Specifically, on the challenging molecule generation dataset GEOM-DRUG, LENSEs achieves 97.28% validity and 98.51% molecule stability, surpassing existing advanced methods. Further analyses through Lipschitz constant reduction (4.6x) and QM9 probing tasks also demonstrate the smoother, more informative refined representations, establishing generative training with alignment objectives as a potential pretraining paradigm for molecular encoders.

</details>


### [234] [Bayesian Fine-tuning in Projected Subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07706)
*Viktar Dubovik,Patryk Marszałek,Jacek Tabor,Tomasz Kuśmierczyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种参数高效的贝叶斯微调框架，通过在极低维参数空间中建模不确定性，实现良好校准与泛化，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LoRA缺乏不确定性量化机制，导致模型过度自信且校准不良；现有贝叶斯LoRA变体虽能解决此问题，但大幅增加可训练参数数量，部分抵消了LoRA的效率优势，且训练困难、收敛不稳定。

Method: 提出一种新的参数高效贝叶斯微调框架，通过将权重空间不确定性投影到低维空间进行有效建模，利用权重协方差的低秩特性实现极低维参数空间中的不确定性量化。

Result: 该方法在保持计算效率的同时，实现了更好的校准性能和泛化能力，经验表明权重协方差具有低秩特性，不确定性可在低维空间有效建模。

Conclusion: 通过适当的权重空间投影，可以在极低维参数空间中有效建模不确定性，实现参数高效的贝叶斯微调，兼顾校准、泛化与计算效率。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables parameter-efficient fine-tuning of large models by decomposing weight updates into low-rank matrices, significantly reducing storage and computational overhead. While effective, standard LoRA lacks mechanisms for uncertainty quantification, leading to overconfident and poorly calibrated models. Bayesian variants of LoRA address this limitation, but at the cost of a significantly increased number of trainable parameters, partially offsetting the original efficiency gains. Additionally, these models are harder to train and may suffer from unstable convergence. In this work, we propose a novel framework for parameter-efficient Bayesian fine-tuning, demonstrating that effective uncertainty quantification can be achieved in very low-dimensional parameter spaces. The proposed method achieves strong performance with improved calibration and generalization while maintaining computational efficiency. Our empirical findings show that, with the appropriate projection of the weight space uncertainty can be effectively modeled in a low-dimensional space, and weight covariances exhibit low ranks.

</details>


### [235] [An Efficient Hybrid Sparse Attention with CPU-GPU Parallelism for Long-Context Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07719)
*Feiyu Yao,Zhixiong Niu,Xiaqing Li,Yongqiang Xiong,Juan Fang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fluxion是一个针对长上下文推理中CPU驻留KV缓存的混合稀疏注意力系统，通过输出感知的KV预算分配、头部特定粒度感知的稀疏配置和跨设备协调执行，在保持模型质量（最差平均下降-0.26）的同时实现1.5倍-3.7倍的速度提升，优于固定稀疏混合基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理中KV缓存通常驻留在CPU内存中（因GPU显存不足或预填充-解码分离架构），现有GPU-only设计受限于PCIe带宽和元数据内存开销，而CPU-GPU混合设计存在GPU空闲时间和CPU端top-k选择及稀疏注意力计算的瓶颈。

Method: Fluxion结合了轻量级头部属性预测器、粒度预算选择器和基于优先级的调度器，通过三个关键洞察（输出感知的KV预算、头部特定和粒度感知的稀疏配置、跨设备协调执行）联合优化预算分配、稀疏配置和CPU-GPU执行重叠。

Result: 在2个模型、3个基准测试和40个任务上，Fluxion相对于完全注意力（FULL）的最差平均退化仅为-0.26，同时相比最强固定稀疏混合基线（KV预算仅为0.05）实现1.5倍-3.7倍的加速。

Conclusion: 通过协同设计混合稀疏注意力，实现了长上下文推理中的准确性和系统效率的双重目标，证明了输出感知的预算分配和跨设备协调执行的有效性。

Abstract: Long-context inference increasingly operates over CPU-resident KV caches, either because decoding-time KV states exceed GPU memory capacity or because disaggregated prefill-decode systems place KV data in host memory. Although block-sparse attention reduces attention cost in this setting, sparsity alone is insufficient for end-to-end efficiency. GPU-only designs remain constrained by PCIe bandwidth and metadata memory overhead, while CPU-GPU hybrid designs still suffer from substantial GPU idle time and bottlenecks in CPU-side top-k selection and sparse attention computation.
  Fluxion is built on three key insights: output-aware KV budgeting, head-specific and granularity-aware sparse configuration, and cross-device coordinated execution for sparse attention over CPU-resident KV caches. Guided by these insights, Fluxion combines a lightweight head-property predictor, a granularity-budget selector, and a priority-based scheduler to jointly optimize budget allocation, sparse configuration, and CPU-GPU execution overlap. This co-design enables hybrid sparse attention to achieve both accuracy and system efficiency in long-context inference. Across 2 models, 3 benchmarks, and 40 tasks, Fluxion preserves quality well -- the worst average degradation is only -0.26 relative to FULL, while delivering 1.5$\times$-3.7$\times$ speedup over the strongest fixed sparse hybrid baseline, whose KV budget is only 0.05.

</details>


### [236] [Curated Synthetic Data Doesn't Have to Collapse: A Theoretical Study of Generative Retraining with Pluralistic Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07724)
*Ali Falahati,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Kate Larson,Lukasz Golab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成模型递归训练中的模式崩溃问题，并提出通过基于多个奖励函数的筛选策略来缓解崩溃，证明了模型在异构偏好下收敛到保持多样性的稳定分布，该分布满足加权纳什议价解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型递归训练中，基于单一奖励信号筛选合成输出会导致模型坍缩到过度优化该目标的狭窄输出集上。先前工作认为这种崩溃不可避免，除非混入真实数据。本文从对齐视角重新审视这一结论。

Method: 形式化定义了异构偏好下递归训练的动态过程，通过理论分析证明在基于多个奖励函数进行筛选的条件下，模型能够收敛到在竞争性高奖励区域间分配概率质量的稳定分布。

Result: 证明了在特定条件下，递归训练过程收敛到保持多样性的稳定分布，该分布满足加权纳什议价解，为合成数据递归训练中的价值聚合提供了形式化解释。

Conclusion: 通过多奖励函数的筛选策略可以有效缓解生成模型递归训练中的模式崩溃问题，无需引入真实数据，为合成数据循环训练提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Recursive retraining of generative models poses a critical representation challenge: when synthetic outputs are curated based on a fixed reward signal, the model tends to collapse onto a narrow set of outputs that over-optimize that objective. Prior work suggests that such collapse is unavoidable without adding real data into the mix. We revisit this conclusion from an alignment perspective and show that collapse can be mitigated through curation based on multiple reward functions. We formalize the dynamics of recursive training under heterogeneous preferences and prove that, under certain conditions, the model converges to a stable distribution that allocates probability mass across competing high-reward regions. The limiting distribution preserves diversity and provably satisfies a weighted Nash bargaining solution, offering a formal interpretation of value aggregation in synthetic retraining loops.

</details>


### [237] [Robust and Reliable AI for Predictive Quality in Semiconductor Materials Manufacturing with MLOps and Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07752)
*Min Gao,Julia Maria Perathoner,Anton Ludwig Bonin,Steven Eulig,Gianni Klesse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于五年真实制造数据，发现每五个生产批次固定重训（不调整超参数）是最优的MLOps策略，能有效应对设备退化与工艺变化，同时结合保形预测实现可靠的不确定性量化，将传统反应式质量管理转变为预测性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体制造中工艺条件演变、设备退化及原材料变异会导致机器学习模型性能随时间下降，亟需系统研究最优的MLOps重训策略以维持模型在动态环境中的预测准确性。

Method: 使用五年真实制造数据，基准测试多种重训频率与超参数优化策略，以控制限归一化残差作为关键性能指标，并引入保形预测生成具有统计保证的预测置信区间。

Result: 每五个生产批次固定重训且不调整超参数的策略在所有漂移条件下表现最优，显著降低计算开销，同时能有效应对突变与渐变退化；保形预测成功实现了具有强统计保证的预测区间。

Conclusion: 研究为制造环境提供了实用的MLOps实施指南，强调在计算效率与不确定性量化可靠性的权衡中，简单固定频率重训策略优于复杂的超参数优化方案。

Abstract: Semiconductor materials manufacturing presents unique challenges for machine learning deployment due to evolving process conditions, equipment degradation, and raw material variability that can cause model performance deterioration over time. This study benchmarks machine learning operations (MLOps) retraining strategies using five years of real manufacturing data to identify optimal retraining approaches for quality prediction. We evaluate various retraining frequencies and hyperparameter optimization strategies using control limit normalized residuals as key performance metric. Results demonstrate that a fixed retraining cadence every five production batches without hyperparameter retuning achieves superior performance across all drift conditions while significantly reducing computational overhead compared to strategies incorporating hyperparameter optimization. This approach effectively maintains model accuracy during both abrupt process changes and gradual equipment degradation patterns. To address the critical need for uncertainty quantification in manufacturing decision-making, we implement conformal prediction to generate prediction confidence intervals with strong statistical guarantees. This enables proactive quality control by identifying when prediction intervals fall within acceptable control limits, transforming traditional reactive quality management into a predictive framework. The findings provide practical guidelines for implementing robust MLOps strategies in manufacturing environments where computational efficiency and reliable uncertainty quantification are paramount for operational success.

</details>


### [238] [When Losses Align: Gradient-Based Composite Loss Weighting for Efficient Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07756)
*Ivan Karpukhin,Andrey Savchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于梯度的双层优化方法，在线学习预训练损失权重，将超参数调优开销降低至单次训练的30%左右，在事件序列建模和自监督视觉任务上匹配或超越精心调优的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度模型常使用复合目标函数在缺失标签的大规模数据上进行预训练，其中多个损失项的相对权重作为超参数。传统调优方法（随机搜索或贝叶斯优化）需要多次独立训练，计算成本过高。

Method: 提出一种基于梯度的双层优化方法，通过将复合预训练梯度与下游目标对齐，在线学习预训练损失权重。利用损失结构避免截断反向传播所需的多次反向传播，显著减少超参数调优开销。

Result: 该方法将超参数调优的计算开销降低到仅比单次训练高出约30%，在事件序列建模和自监督计算机视觉任务上匹配或超越精心调优的基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的梯度-based双层优化方法能高效在线学习预训练损失权重，大幅降低超参数调优成本，同时保持或提升模型性能。

Abstract: Modern deep models are often pretrained on large-scale data with missing labels using composite objectives, where the relative weights of multiple loss terms act as hyperparameters. Tuning these weights with random search or Bayesian optimization is computationally expensive, as it requires many independent training runs. To address this, we propose a gradient-based bilevel method that learns pretraining loss weights online by aligning the composite pretraining gradient with a downstream objective. By exploiting the structure of the loss, the method avoids the multiple backward passes typically required by truncated backpropagation through the full model, reducing the overhead of hyperparameter tuning to approximately 30% above a single training run. We evaluate the approach on event-sequence modeling and self-supervised computer vision, where it matches or improves upon carefully tuned baselines while substantially reducing the cost of hyperparameter tuning compared to random or Bayesian search.

</details>


### [239] [POETS: Uncertainty-Aware LLM Optimization via Compute-Efficient Policy Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07775)
*Nicolas Menet,Andreas Krause,Abbas Rahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: POETS框架通过策略集成直接捕捉认知不确定性，绕过了传统的不确定性建模与策略拟合的嵌套过程，理论上证明了其隐式执行KL正则化汤普森采样并具有强累积遗憾界，实验上在蛋白质搜索、量子电路设计等科学发现领域以及强化学习离线策略设置中取得了最先进的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索与利用的平衡是序列决策和黑箱优化的核心挑战。现有方法通常需要繁琐的训练不确定性感知奖励模型再单独拟合策略的嵌套过程，计算效率低。

Method: POETS通过将策略与隐式编码的奖励函数匹配到在线自助采样数据上，直接训练策略集成以捕捉认知不确定性。为克服大语言模型集成的计算和内存限制，采用共享预训练主干加独立LoRA分支的高效架构。

Result: 理论证明了O(√T γ_T)的累积遗憾界；在蛋白质搜索、量子电路设计等科学发现领域以及强化学习的离线策略和小样本设置中取得了最先进的样本效率。

Conclusion: POETS桥接了不确定性量化与策略优化，为高效探索提供了一种理论保证且计算实用的解决方案，尤其适用于大语言模型和科学发现场景。

Abstract: Balancing exploration and exploitation is a core challenge in sequential decision-making and black-box optimization. We introduce POETS ($\textbf{Po}$licy $\textbf{E}$nsembles for $\textbf{T}$hompson $\textbf{S}$ampling), a novel framework that bridges uncertainty quantification and policy optimization. Our approach is grounded in the insight that policies trained with Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization implicitly encode an underlying reward function. Building on this, POETS bypasses the complex, nested process of training an uncertainty-aware reward model and separately fitting a policy to this model. Instead, we directly train a policy ensemble to capture epistemic uncertainty by matching implicitly encoded reward functions to online, bootstrapped data. To overcome the prohibitive compute and memory constraints of ensembling Large Language Models (LLMs), POETS utilizes an efficient architecture: the ensemble shares a pre-trained backbone while maintaining diversity through independent Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) branches. Theoretically, we prove that POETS implicitly conducts KL-regularized Thompson sampling and thus inherits strong cumulative regret bounds of ${\mathcal O}(\sqrt{T γ_T})$. Empirically, we demonstrate that POETS achieves state-of-the-art sample efficiency across diverse scientific discovery domains, including protein search and quantum circuit design. Furthermore, it improves the optimization trajectories of reinforcement learning, proving particularly robust in off-policy settings with experience replay or in small dataset regimes.

</details>


### [240] [Toward Privileged Foundation Models:LUPI for Accelerated and Improved Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07799)
*Xueying Ding,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PIQL是首个系统性地利用特权信息（PI）来加速表格基础模型（TFM）训练并提升泛化能力的框架，通过构建两种互补的PI形式（聚合数据集统计信息和底层数据生成程序编码）以及专门设计的架构，在训练时使用PI并在推理时从观测上下文重建，实现了更快的收敛、更低的最终损失和更好的泛化，减少了数据和计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练基础模型计算密集且收敛缓慢，现有方法缺乏系统性利用特权信息来同时加速学习和提升泛化的方案。

Method: 构建两种互补形式特权信息：(i) 聚合数据集级统计信息，减轻上下文学习负担；(ii) 底层数据生成程序编码，提供超越观测数据的知识。设计专门架构在训练时使用PI，推理时从观测上下文重建PI。

Result: PIQL实现了更快的收敛速度、更低的最终损失和更好的泛化能力，有效减少了数据和计算需求。理论分析也证明了PI在特定条件下能缩小种群级近似差距并加速有限数据下的收敛。

Conclusion: PI-guided预训练是一种有原则且实用的范式，能够提升基础模型的效率和性能。

Abstract: Training foundation models is computationally intensive and often slow to converge.We introduce PIQL,Privileged Information for Quick and Quality Learning, the first framework to systematically integrate privileged information (PI) to simultaneously accelerate learning and improve generalization in tabular foundation models (TFMs). We construct two complementary forms of PI: (i) aggregate dataset-level statistics that reduce the burden on in-context learning, and (ii) encodings of the underlying data-generating program, providing knowledge beyond observable data. We further design an architecture that effectively transfers the train-time-only PI by learning to reconstruct it from observed context at inference. We provide a theoretical analysis characterizing conditions under which PI reduces the population-level approximation gap and accelerates convergence in finite-data regimes. Empirical evidence shows that PIQL enables TFMs to achieve faster convergence, lower final loss, and better generalization, in effect, reducing data and compute requirements. Our work establishes PI-guided pretraining as a principled and practical paradigm for improving the efficiency and performance of foundation models.

</details>


### [241] [Prune-OPD: Efficient and Reliable On-Policy Distillation for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07804)
*Zhicheng Yang,Zhijiang Guo,Yifan Song,Minrui Xu,Yongxin Wang,Yiwei Wang,Xiaodan Liang,Jing Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Prune-OPD通过实时检测学生与教师模型之间的前缀漂移，动态截断无效生成并重分配计算资源，在保持或提升推理性能的同时减少37.6%-68.0%的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: On-policy distillation (OPD)在长程任务中面临前缀漂移问题：学生生成的轨迹逐渐偏离教师思维过程，导致教师密集奖励失去局部可剥削性，且继续在漂移轨迹上生成和评估token既降低奖励质量又造成计算浪费。

Method: 提出Prune-OPD框架，通过实时监控学生与教师预测间的局部兼容性（如top-k重叠）检测前缀漂移事件，在检测到严重漂移时单调降低后续不可靠奖励的权重并触发动态 rollout 截断，从而终止无效生成并将计算重新分配给可靠的教师监督。

Result: 在多种教师-学生组合下，Prune-OPD在AMC、AIME、HMMT等基准上保持甚至提升性能，同时减少37.6%-68.0%的训练时间；当兼容性高时自动保留长上下文监督。

Conclusion: Prune-OPD改进OPD的方式并非盲目缩短rollout，而是将计算重分配给局部可剥削的教师奖励。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) leverages dense teacher rewards to enhance reasoning models. However, scaling OPD to long-horizon tasks exposes a critical flaw: as the student's generated prefix inevitably diverges from the teacher's thought process, the teacher's dense reward loses local exploitability. Continuing to generate and evaluate tokens on these ``drifted'' trajectories not only degrades reward quality but also incurs massive computational waste. To address this, we introduce \textbf{Prune-OPD}, a framework that dynamically aligns training budgets with supervision quality. By continuously monitoring the local compatibility between student and teacher predictions (e.g., via top-$k$ overlap), Prune-OPD detects prefix-drift events in real time. Upon detecting severe drift, it monotonically down-weights subsequent unreliable rewards and triggers dynamic rollout truncation. This allows the training process to halt futile generation and reallocate compute strictly to reliable teacher supervision. Across diverse teacher-student combinations, Prune-OPD consistently aligns computation with supervision reliability. When prefix drift makes dense teacher rewards unreliable, it reduces training time by 37.6\%--68.0\% while preserving, and often improving, performance on challenging benchmarks (AMC, AIME, HMMT). When student-teacher compatibility remains high, it automatically preserves long-context supervision by expanding the training window. These results suggest that Prune-OPD improves OPD not by blindly shortening rollouts, but by reallocating computation toward locally exploitable teacher rewards.

</details>


### [242] [Flexible Routing via Uncertainty Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07805)
*Charlotte Peale,Siddartha Devic,Parikshit Gopalan,Udi Wieder,Aravind Gollakota*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的不确定性感知路由方法，通过将总不确定性分解为可约和不可约两部分，实现了在弱模型、强模型（oracle）和弃权之间的动态路由，无需重训即可适应不同损失函数和成本参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器学习系统中，平衡性能与成本的关键策略是动态路由，即在低成本模型和高成本oracle（如大模型或人类专家）之间进行选择。现有方法存在两个问题：(1) 无法避免对本质上模糊（高不可约不确定性）的查询调用oracle造成浪费；(2) 适应不同损失函数和成本参数时需要重训模型。

Method: 基于高阶预测器将总不确定性分解为不可约不确定性和可约不确定性。路由策略为：低不确定性时用弱模型预测；高可约不确定性时路由到oracle；高不可约不确定性时弃权。该方法只需简单调整超参数即可适应不同损失函数和成本参数，无需重新训练。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在适当场景（即可约与不可约不确定性相关性不高时）优于现有方法，并具有相对于最优任务特定路由器的强理论保证（后悔界）。

Conclusion: 不确定性分解为路由和弃权提供了统一框架，使得路由器能够同时学习何时求助强模型以及何时应该完全放弃预测，在性能、成本和可靠性之间实现更优平衡。

Abstract: A key strategy for balancing performance and cost in modern machine learning systems is to dynamically route queries to either a low-cost model or a more expensive oracle (such as a large pretrained model or human expert), an approach known as model routing. In this work we present a new uncertainty-aware router that (1) avoids unnecessary oracle calls on inherently ambiguous queries, and (2) adapts dynamically to different loss functions and cost parameters through simple hyperparameter changes, without retraining. Our method, applicable to any classification setting where multiple independent annotations per input are available, is based on decomposing total uncertainty into irreducible and reducible components using higher-order predictors [Ahdritz et al., 2025]. This enables a unified approach to both routing and abstention: predict with the weak model when uncertainty is low, route to the oracle when reducible uncertainty is high, and abstain when irreducible uncertainty is high. Our router comes with strong theoretical guarantees bounding regret relative to optimal task-specific routers. We conduct experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets that demonstrate the benefits of our approach in suitable regimes -- in particular, whenever reducible and irreducible uncertainty are not too correlated.

</details>


### [243] [The Minimax Rate of Second-Order Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07808)
*Kamil Ciosek,Banafsheh Rafiee,Sina Ghiassian,Nicolò Felicioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了二元分类中二阶校准误差的极小极大估计率，利用sech摄动核使校准函数解析化，通过多项式回归实现了$\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$的估计速率（匹配下界$Ω(1/\sqrt{n})$），相比传统分桶法$O(n^{-1/4})$有本质提升；并首次给出二阶Platt缩放的有限样本保证，为高阶预测器提供了后处理重校准方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶校准误差估计主要依赖分桶或核平滑方法，速率仅为$O(n^{-1/4})$；亟需更高效的估计方法，并建立匹配的下界以确认最优性。同时，缺乏二阶Platt缩放的有限样本理论保证。

Method: (1) 观察到sech摄动核不仅使校准函数光滑，还使其在宽度为$hπ/2$的带状区域内解析；(2) 利用多项式回归估计校准误差，推导出显式常数的$\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$速率；(3) 构造匹配的$Ω(1/\sqrt{n})$下界证明极小极大最优性；(4) 提出无分桶的二阶校准定义，并与Ahdritz等人的分桶形式建立定量联系；(5) 将理论应用于二阶Platt缩放，给出首个有限样本保证。

Result: 估计速率从$O(n^{-1/4})$提升至$\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$，且该速率在多项式因子上极小极大最优；实验验证了理论速率和重校准后的不确定性质量。

Conclusion: sech摄动核的解析性性质为高阶校准估计提供了理论突破，多项式回归结合该核能显著提升估计效率；二阶Platt缩放的有限样本保证使其成为可靠的后处理工具。

Abstract: We characterize the minimax rate of estimating the second-order calibration error for binary classification, which quantifies whether a higher-order predictor's epistemic-uncertainty estimate matches the conditional variance of the label probability on its level sets. Our key observation is that the sech perturbation kernel, previously used only to enforce smoothness of calibration functions, in fact makes them analytic in a strip of half-width $hπ/2$. Polynomial regression then estimates the calibration error at rate $\tilde{O}(1/\sqrt{n})$, with explicit constants, a qualitative improvement over the $O(n^{-1/4})$ rate achievable by bucketing or kernel smoothing. A matching $Ω(1/\sqrt{n})$ lower bound establishes minimax optimality up to logarithmic factors. As a corollary, we give the first finite-sample guarantee for second-order Platt scaling, yielding a post-hoc procedure that recalibrates both the mean prediction and the epistemic-variance estimate of any higher-order predictor. Along the way, we provide a bucket-free definition of second-order calibration and relate it quantitatively to the bucketed formulation of Ahdritz et al. [2025]. Our experiments confirm the predicted rate and the quality of the recalibrated uncertainties.

</details>


### [244] [Scaling Categorical Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07820)
*Oscar Davis,Anastasiia Filippova,Pierre Ablin,Victor Turrisi,Amitis Shidani,Marco Cuturi,Louis Béthune*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在1.7B参数规模上训练了基于连续扩散和流匹配的语言模型，通过自蒸馏得到类别流映射（CFM）模型，可在4步推理中生成高质量文本，并首次为CFM建立了半离散环境下的似然界，揭示了大规模训练中的挑战及解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索连续扩散和流匹配模型在语言建模中的可扩展性，验证其在大规模参数（>1B）下的性能，并解决此前仅在较小规模（<1B）评估的空白。

Method: 训练1.7B参数的基座流模型（在2.1T tokens上），通过自蒸馏得到类别流映射（CFM）模型；引入半离散设置下的似然界；研究大规模训练中的损失加权和时间调度策略。

Result: CFM模型在仅4步推理下生成多样且高质量的文本，保持接近数据水平的token熵；似然界得分与离散扩散方法相当；揭示了大规模训练的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 连续扩散和流匹配模型在语言建模中具有可扩展性，能实现快速采样且保持质量，为解决相关问题提供了可行的指导方案。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion and flow matching models could represent a powerful alternative to autoregressive approaches for language modelling (LM), as they unlock a host of advantages currently reserved for continuous modalities, including accelerated sampling and tilting. Recently, several works have demonstrated the possibility of generating discrete data continuously by a simple flow matching process between a Gaussian and the one-hot encoded data distribution. They have further shown the feasibility of accelerated sampling via Categorical Flow Maps (CFMs), resulting in competitive sample quality in the few-step regime. However, this method had only been evaluated at relatively modest scales ($<1$B), leaving the question of its scalability completely open. In this article, we train a $1.7$B-parameter base flow model on $2.1$T tokens and self-distill it into a CFM that generates diverse, high-quality text in as few as $4$ inference steps while maintaining near-data-level token entropy. Furthermore, we introduce a likelihood bound for CFMs in the semi-discrete setting, and show that they can be used to score the model on standard LM benchmarks, achieving results in the same range as discrete diffusion methods. Finally, we uncover some of the challenges that arise from training these models at scale, and we provide prescriptive insights on loss weighting and time scheduling.

</details>


### [245] [Distributional simplicity bias and effective convexity in Energy Based Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07844)
*Aurélien Decelle,Alfonso de Jesús Navas Gómez,Beatriz Seoane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过有效模型（广义伊辛模型/能量傅里叶展开）的动力学分析，揭示了能量基学习在二值变量正分布上的梯度流存在数据一致点（精确复制目标分布）和伪点（仅满足平稳性）两类固定点，并证明数据一致点附近扰动稳定或中性，且动力学诱导低阶交互先于高阶交互学习，解释了分布简单性偏好机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量基学习训练固有非凸性导致对初始化敏感、易陷入局部最优和梯度动力学不稳定，缺乏对其动力学行为的深入理论理解。

Method: 通过有效模型（可解释为高阶交互的广义伊辛模型或能量的傅里叶展开）对二值变量上严格正分布的梯度流进行动力学分析。

Result: 1) 存在两类固定点：数据一致点（精确复制目标分布）和伪点（仅满足平稳性而不匹配分布）；2) 数据一致点附近扰动稳定或中性，中性方向保持有效模型不变；3) 梯度动力学诱导学习层级：低阶交互先于高阶交互学习。

Conclusion: 为分布简单性偏好提供了机制性解释，阐明了低阶非数据一致固定点在实践中无法观测的原因。

Abstract: Energy-based learning is a powerful framework for generative modelling, but its training is inherently non-convex, leading potentially to sensitivity to initialisation, poor local optima, and unstable gradient dynamics. We present a dynamical analysis of energy-based learning through the lens of the effective model, which can be interpreted as either a generalised Ising model with higher-order interactions or the Fourier expansion of the energy. Under sufficient expressivity, we show that the gradient flow induced by learning strictly positive distributions over binary variables admits two types of fixed points: data-consistent points, which exactly reproduce the target distribution, and spurious points, which satisfy stationarity without matching the target distribution. Around data-consistent points, we show that perturbations are either stable or neutral, with neutral directions leaving the effective model invariant. Finally, we show that gradient dynamics induce a hierarchy in which lower-order interactions are learned before higher-order ones. This provides a mechanistic explanation for the distributional simplicity bias and clarifies why fixed points that are not data-consistent at low orders are not observed in practice.

</details>


### [246] [On the Tradeoffs of On-Device Generative Models in Federated Predictive Maintenance Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07860)
*Usevalad Milasheuski,Piero Baraldi,Enrico Zio,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了VAE、GAN和扩散模型在联邦预测性维护中的性能与通信开销，提出了部分组件共享的联邦生成模型新分类法，并在真实时序数据集上揭示了不同联邦设置下的效用、稳定性和可扩展性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在分布式物联网环境中保护客户端数据所有权，但现有研究多聚焦于判别模型。生成模型（VAE、GAN、扩散模型）在时序异常检测和预测性维护中具有潜力，然而它们在联邦场景下的表现与通信开销尚缺乏系统性分析。

Method: 对VAE、GAN和扩散模型在完整联邦和部分联邦（仅共享子组件）设置下进行性能与通信开销分析，提出基于部分组件共享的联邦生成模型分类法，并在真实工业时序数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: GAN在全联邦下训练稳定性优于独立本地训练，但整体鲁棒性不如VAE和扩散模型；扩散模型在带宽受限、非独立同分布场景下，部分联邦（尤其是仅共享解码器）可优于全联邦。

Conclusion: 不同生成模型在联邦预测性维护中存在差异化的效用-稳定性-可扩展性权衡，部分组件共享为模型个性化提供了原则性机制，为带宽受限的工业物联网联邦学习部署提供了指导。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for preserving client data ownership and control over distributed Internet of Things (IoT) environments. While discriminative models dominate most FL use cases, recent advances in generative models -- such as Variational Autoencoders (VAE), Generative Adversarial Networks (GAN), and Diffusion Models (DM) -- offer new opportunities for unsupervised anomaly detection in time series analysis, with relevant applications in predictive maintenance (PdM) in critical industrial infrastructures. In this work, we present a comprehensive analysis of VAEs, GANs, and DMs in the context of federated PdM. We analyze their performance and communication overhead under both full and partial federation setups, where only subsets of model components are shared. Building on this analysis, the paper proposes a novel taxonomy for federated generative models that formalizes partial component sharing as a principled mechanism for model personalization. Our experiments over a real-world time series dataset reveal distinct trade-offs in model utility, stability, and scalability, especially in heterogeneous and bandwidth-constrained FL settings. For the evaluated GAN-based configurations, full federation improves training stability relative to independent local training, although the model remains less robust than the VAE- and DDPM-based alternatives. For DMs, however, partial federation -- especially decoder sharing -- can outperform full federation in bandwidth-constrained, non-IID settings.

</details>


### [247] [KL for a KL: On-Policy Distillation with Control Variate Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07865)
*Minjae Oh,Sangjun Song,Gyubin Choi,Yunho Choi,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: vOPD通过引入控制变量基线（价值函数）来稳定在线策略蒸馏（OPD），该价值函数可从前向传播中直接以闭式解（每个token的负逆向KL散度）获得，无需额外评论家或推理。vOPD保留单样本估计器，通过减去分离基线降低方差，同时保持梯度无偏，在数学和科学推理基准上优于标准OPD，达到与全词汇基线相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在推理领域表现优异，但其单样本蒙特卡洛估计器的高梯度方差导致训练不稳定，且缺乏成熟的稳定训练方案。

Method: 将OPD重新表述为策略梯度强化学习，并从强化学习文献中引入控制变量基线（价值函数）。证明OPD的价值函数存在闭式解，即学生与教师之间每个token的负逆向KL散度，可直接从已计算的前向传播中获得。通过减去分离基线（离焦）来降低方差，同时保持无偏梯度估计；进一步提出top-k近似基线以降低计算成本。

Result: 在数学和科学推理基准上，vOPD持续优于标准OPD，并且与计算开销更大的全词汇基线方法性能相当。

Conclusion: vOPD通过原则性的强化学习方差缩减技术，为在线策略蒸馏提供了一种高效的稳定方案，在保持轻量计算的同时显著提升训练稳定性与下游性能。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a dominant post-training paradigm for large language models, especially for reasoning domains. However, OPD remains unstable in practice due to the high gradient variance of its single-sample Monte Carlo estimator, and recipes for stable training are still immature. We propose vOPD (On-Policy Distillation with a control variate baseline), which casts OPD as policy-gradient RL and stabilizes it by introducing a control variate baseline-canonically a value function -- from the RL literature. We show that the OPD value function admits a closed form as the per-token negative reverse KL divergence between the student and the teacher, available directly from the already-computed forward pass with no additional critic or inference. Existing stabilization methods either compute the full token-level reverse KL over the entire vocabulary, adding significant overhead, or restrict it to a top-k support, biasing the objective. vOPD instead preserves the lightweight single-sample estimator, subtracting the value function as a detached baseline to keep the gradient unbiased while reducing variance. Furthermore, we show that a top-k approximation of the baseline further lowers cost without compromising performance. Across mathematical and scientific reasoning benchmarks, vOPD consistently outperforms vanilla OPD and matches the most expensive full-vocabulary baseline, offering an efficient stabilization of On-Policy Distillation through principled RL variance reduction.

</details>


### [248] [Black-box model classification under the discriminative factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07878)
*Hayden Helm,Merrick Ohata,Carey Priebe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出判别性分解（discriminative factorization）框架，用于在黑盒模型级别分类任务中区分高质量和低质量查询集，证明在查询预算下随机分类概率呈指数衰减，并通过三个审计任务验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成式系统通常仅提供API查询接口（黑盒设置），用户在推理时无法获知系统内部属性。虽然已有研究表明，通过模型对一组查询的嵌入响应之间的关系可以构建低维表示来推断模型级别属性，但这些表示的质量对查询集高度敏感，因此需要一种有效区分高质量与低质量查询集的方法。

Method: 提出判别性分解（discriminative factorization）框架，用于在黑盒模型级别分类场景中区分高质量与低质量查询集。在该框架下，随机水平分类的概率随查询预算的增加呈指数衰减。通过估计分解参数来预测经验性能衰减率。

Result: 在三个审计任务上，估计的分解参数能够预测经验性能的衰减率。此外，使用估计的判别性场选择的查询集能够复现经验上的真实查询集排序。

Conclusion: 判别性分解框架能够有效区分高质量与低质量查询集，并且可以通过估计的判别性场来选择更优的查询集，从而提升黑盒模型级别分类的性能。

Abstract: Access to modern generative systems is often restricted to querying an API (the ``black-box" setting) and many properties of the system are unknown to the user at inference time. While recent work has shown that low-dimensional representations of models based on the relationship between their embedded responses to a set of queries are useful for inferring model-level properties, the quality of these representations is highly sensitive to the query set. We introduce the \emph{discriminative factorization} to distinguish between high- and low-quality query sets in the context of black-box model-level classification. Under this framework, the probability of chance-level classification decays exponentially in the query budget. On three auditing tasks, estimated factorization parameters predict the empirical performance decay rate. We conclude by showing that query sets selected using the estimated discriminative field reproduce the empirical ordering of oracle query sets.

</details>


### [249] [Enhancing Federated Quadruplet Learning: Stochastic Client Selection and Embedding Stability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07888)
*Ozgu Goksu,Nicolas Pugeault*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedQuad通过在客户端联合最小化正样本对距离和最大化负样本对距离，显式地压缩类内表示并分离类间表示，从而缓解联邦学习中的数据异构性导致的表示对齐问题，在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，数据异构性（non-IID）导致全局模型的泛化性能严重下降，尤其在有限数据和类别不平衡场景下，现有方法未能有效解决特征表示的对齐问题。

Method: 提出FedQuad方法，通过度量学习同时最小化同类样本（正对）的表示距离并最大化异类样本（负对）的表示距离，在模型聚合过程中显式约束类内紧凑性和类间可分离性，从而缓解表示坍塌。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet数据集上，针对多种non-IID设置和不同客户端数量，FedQuad一致性地优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 度量学习策略在联邦异构环境下能有效缓解表示坍塌问题，FedQuad通过正负样本对的双重约束提供了鲁棒的特征对齐方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables decentralised model training across distributed clients without requiring data centralisation. However, the generalisation performance of the global model is usually degraded by data heterogeneity across clients, particularly under limited data availability and class imbalance. To address this challenge, we propose FedQuad, a novel method that explicitly enforces minimising intra-class representations while enabling inter-class splits across clients. By jointly minimising distances between positive pairs and maximising distances between negative pairs, the proposed approach mitigates representation misalignment introduced during model aggregation. We evaluate our method on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet under diverse non-IID settings and varying numbers of clients, demonstrating consistent improvements over existing baselines. Additionally, we provide a comprehensive analysis of metric learning-based approaches in both centralised and federated environments, highlighting their effectiveness in alleviating representation collapse under heterogeneous data distributions.

</details>


### [250] [Adaptive Regularization for Sparsity Control in Bregman-Based Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07892)
*Ahmad Aloradi,Tim Roith,Emanuël A. P. Habets,Daniel Tenbrinck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应正则化方案，通过动态调整λ来精确控制模型稀疏度，避免Bregman优化器中λ参数敏感性问题，在说话人验证任务上达到75%-99%稀疏度目标，收敛更快且性能不降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏训练中，Bregman优化器（LinBreg和AdaBreg）对正则化参数λ极其敏感，相同稀疏度下λ值可相差两个数量级，需要大量试错才能达到目标稀疏度，缺乏直接的稀疏度控制手段。

Method: 提出自适应正则化方案，根据模型当前稀疏度与目标稀疏度的差值动态更新正则化参数λ，实现对稀疏度的直接控制。

Result: 在ECAPA-TDNN和ResNet34上，使用VoxCeleb和CNCeleb数据集，可靠达到75%-99%稀疏度目标；早期训练收敛速度快于oracle调参的非自适应基线；最终EER指标匹配或超越非自适应基线；继承了非自适应方法的OOD鲁棒性优势。

Conclusion: 自适应λ调度方案有效解决了Bregman优化器中λ参数敏感性问题，无需昂贵的超参数搜索即可精确控制稀疏度，同时保持甚至提升模型性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Sparse training reduces the memory and computational costs of deep neural networks. However, sparse optimization methods, e.g., those adding an $\ell_1$ penalty, often control sparsity only indirectly through a regularization parameter $λ$, whose mapping to the final sparsity rate is non-trivial. In our experiments, we found this parameter sensitivity to be particularly pronounced for Bregman-based optimizers. Specifically, the two variants LinBreg and AdaBreg reach the same sparsity at $λ$ values that differ by up to two orders of magnitude, requiring expensive trial-and-error sweeps to achieve a user-specified sparsity. To address this, we propose an adaptive regularization scheme that updates $λ$ based on the difference between the model's current sparsity and the target sparsity. We analyze the resulting algorithm and evaluate it on automatic speaker verification with ECAPA-TDNN and ResNet34 on VoxCeleb and CNCeleb. The proposed method reliably achieves sparsity targets ranging between 75% and 99%. It also converges faster than the oracle-tuned non-adaptive baseline during early training and matches or surpasses its final performance in equal error rate. We further show that the adaptive scheme inherits key properties from its non-adaptive counterpart, including improved out-of-distribution robustness over the dense baselines.

</details>


### [251] [Curvature Beyond Positivity: Greedy Guarantees for Arbitrary Submodular Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07902)
*Yixin Chen,Alan Kuhnle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将曲率（curvature）概念推广至所有子模函数（包括非单调、负值情形），通过带剪枝的贪心算法实现了曲率控制的多乘性近似比，首次在非单调且负值条件下给出有界保证，并拓展至多线性松弛框架下的组合约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有子模函数优化理论要求非负性保证乘性近似比，但实际目标函数常因引入代价而取负值，且非单调性与负值问题分别处理缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 1) 将曲率参数推广至任意子模函数（涵盖非单调、负值情形）；2) 设计带剪枝的贪心算法，证明曲率控制的多乘性近似比；3) 通过多线性扩展变体处理一般组合约束。

Result: 在非单调情形（1≤c_g<2.2）下，近似比严格优于已知最佳统一比0.401（对非负函数）；恢复单调函数的经典（1-e^{-c_g})/c_g保证；实验验证了代价惩罚实验设计、覆盖、特征选择及Multi-News段落选择上的有效性。

Conclusion: 曲率可统一处理子模函数的非单调性和负值问题，首次在负值场景下给出乘性近似保证，拓展了经典子模优化理论。

Abstract: Submodular functions -- functions exhibiting diminishing returns -- are central to machine learning. When the objective is monotone and non-negative, the greedy algorithm achieves a tight $63\%$ approximation. But many practical objectives incorporate costs that make them negative on some inputs, and all existing multiplicative guarantees require non-negativity. Prior work handles negativity through additive bounds for the special class of decomposable functions and non-monotonicity through partial-monotonicity parameters, but these address each difficulty in isolation and neither extends the classical structural theory. We extend \emph{curvature} -- a parameter measuring how far a function deviates from linearity -- to all submodular functions, handling both non-monotonicity and negativity through a single classical concept. A greedy algorithm with pruning achieves a curvature-controlled multiplicative ratio for \emph{any} submodular function, including those taking negative values -- the first such guarantee beyond monotonicity and non-negativity. In the non-monotone regime $1 \le c_g < 2.2$, the bound strictly beats the best known uniform ratio of $0.401$ (for non-negative $f$), and it recovers the classical $(1-e^{-c_g})/c_g$ guarantee for monotone functions. A multilinear-extension variant extends the framework to general combinatorial constraints via multilinear relaxation. Experiments on cost-penalized experimental design, coverage, feature selection, and a curvature sweep on Multi-News passage selection support the theory.

</details>


### [252] [Flatness and Gradient Alignment Are Both Necessary: Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration for Multi-Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07914)
*Aristotelis Ballas,Christos Diou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文理论证明在多重分布学习中，平坦性和梯度对齐必须同时考虑，并提出SAGE方法同时优化这两个几何性质，在域泛化和多任务学习任务上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有锐度感知方法和梯度对齐方法各自只关注损失景观的单一几何性质（平坦性或梯度对齐），忽略了另一个性质。本文证明这种忽略在结构上是不可避免的，且两种性质在多重分布学习中都至关重要。

Method: SAGE方法包含两个组件：(1) 曲率组件：用每层梯度矩阵的极因子（通过Newton-Schulz迭代计算）替换SAM中基于梯度缩放的扰动，使上升步以相似幅度探测所有方向；(2) 对齐组件：在下降步注入各向同性噪声，其幅度与跨分布梯度分歧成正比。

Result: 在DomainBed的5个域泛化基准和2个多任务学习基准上，SAGE达到了新的SOTA，且可作为多任务学习基础求解器的通用改进方法。

Conclusion: 平坦性和梯度对齐在多重分布学习中必须同时考虑，SAGE通过同时优化这两个互补的性质取得了优异性能。

Abstract: Sharpness-aware and gradient-alignment methods have been shown to improve generalization, however each family of methods targets a single geometric property of the loss landscape, while ignoring the other. In this paper, we show that this omission is structurally unavoidable and that both flatness and gradient alignment should be considered in multi-distribution learning settings. Specifically, we derive an excess-risk decomposition that yields two additive leading-order terms: (i) an alignment term, controlled by the trace of $\bar{H}^{-1}Σ_g$ and (ii) a curvature term, controlled by $\bar{H}$, where $\bar{H}$ is the average Hessian and $Σ_g$ is the covariance of the gradient across distributions. Notably, $\bar{H}$ appears inverted in one and non-inverted in the other. We further show, via a counterexample, that neither quantity bounds the other in general, so no algorithm targeting only one term can guarantee low excess risk. Motivated by this decomposition, we propose SAGE (Spectral-Aware Gradient-Aligned Exploration) that targets both terms. The curvature component replaces SAM's gradient-scaled perturbation with the polar factor of each layer's gradient matrix, computed via Newton-Schulz iteration, so that the ascent step probes all directions with similar magnitude. On the other hand, the alignment component injects isotropic noise at the descent step, the magnitude of which scales with cross-distribution gradient disagreement. Experiments on five domain-generalization and two multi-task learning benchmarks show that the proposed method establishes a new state-of-the-art on DomainBed and acts as a general-purpose improvement to base MTL solvers, remaining competitive with, or even surpassing, state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [253] [Tree SAE: Learning Hierarchical Feature Structures in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07922)
*Tue M. Cao,Hoang X. Nhat,Raed Alharbi,Phi Le Nguyen,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree SAE，通过结合激活覆盖和重构约束来学习稀疏自编码器中的层次化特征结构，解决了现有方法中父子特征语义不相关（假阳性）的问题，在多项基准测试中达到领先水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）在层次化特征学习中存在两个问题：一是仅依赖激活覆盖条件（子特征仅在父特征激活时激活）会导致大量假阳性，即父子特征在语义上不相关；二是层次结构的学习不够精确，无法捕捉真实世界数据的结构化特性。

Method: Tree SAE结合了两种约束来学习层次结构：1）激活约束（activation constraint）——子特征仅在父特征激活时激活；2）重构约束（reconstruction constraint）——在重建层面强制父子特征之间存在更深层的功能联系。通过联合优化这两个条件，直接从特征集中学习层次化结构。

Result: Tree SAE在层次化对（hierarchical pairs）学习任务上显著优于现有SAE方法，同时在多个关键基准测试中与最先进方法保持竞争力。此外，该方法在映射子特征子空间几何结构以及揭示大语言模型中编码的复杂层次概念结构方面展现了实际效用。

Conclusion: 仅靠激活覆盖条件不足以可靠地学习层次特征结构；引入重构约束可以有效消除假阳性，实现更精确的层次化特征学习。Tree SAE为理解和解释大语言模型内部的概念层次结构提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Learning hierarchical features in Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) is essential for capturing the structured nature of real-world data and mitigating issues like feature absorption or splitting. Existing works attempt to identify hierarchical relationships within independent feature sets by relying on activation coverage, the assumption that child feature should only activate when its parent feature activates. However, we demonstrate that this condition alone is insufficient; that is, it often produces false positives where parent and child concepts are semantically unrelated. To address this, we introduce a novel reconstruction condition that enforces a deeper functional link between hierarchical levels. By combining both activation and reconstruction constraints, we propose the Tree SAE, a model designed to learn hierarchical structures directly from within the feature set. Our results demonstrate that Tree SAEs significantly surpass the existing SAEs at learning hierarchical pairs while maintaining competitive performance to the state-of-the-art on several key benchmarks. Finally, we demonstrate the practical utility of our Tree SAE in mapping the geometry of child feature subspaces and uncovering the complex hierarchical concept structures encoded within large language models.

</details>


### [254] [Trajectory as the Teacher: Few-Step Discrete Flow Matching via Energy-Navigated Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07924)
*Amin Karimi Monsefi,Dominic Culver,Nikhil Bhendawade,Manuel R. Ciosici,Yizhe Zhang,Irina Belousova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TS-DFM方法，通过用轻量级能量指针对离散流匹配的训练轨迹进行引导式优化，替代原有的盲目随机跳跃。在仅需8步推理时，学生模型困惑度比1024步的教师模型低32%，速度快128倍，且超越多种基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散流匹配的蒸馏方法中，学生模型表现不佳通常被归因于容量不足，但本文认为瓶颈在于训练轨迹本身——原始轨迹由盲目的随机跳跃构成，早期一步错误决策会向后传播，而学生模型却必须模仿这一有缺陷的过程。

Method: 提出Trajectory-Shaped Discrete Flow Matching (TS-DFM)，在训练过程中引入轻量级能量指针对每个中间节点的候选续写进行评估，选择最连贯的路径，从而将盲目跳跃替换为引导式导航。所有指引操作仅在训练阶段进行，推理成本不变。

Result: 在170M参数的语言模型上，8步推理的引导式学生模型困惑度比1024步的教师模型低32%，速度快128倍。该增益在多种源分布和三种不同规模评估器下均保持一致。TS-DFM在离散生成任务中达到最佳困惑度，超越使用6倍数据或5倍模型规模的基线方法。

Conclusion: 证明了离散流匹配蒸馏的性能瓶颈在于训练轨迹质量而非学生容量，通过轨迹塑造可以有效提升学生模型的生成质量与推理效率。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching generates text by iteratively transforming noise tokens into coherent language, but may require hundreds of forward passes. Distillation uses the multi-step trajectory to train a student to reproduce the process in a few steps. When the student underperforms, the usual explanation is insufficient capacity. We argue the opposite: the trajectory is the bottleneck, not the student. Each training trajectory is built through a chain of blind stochastic jumps with no evaluation of sequence quality; a single bad decision at an early midpoint propagates through subsequent steps, yet the student must imitate the result. Trajectory-Shaped Discrete Flow Matching (TS-DFM) replaces these blind jumps with guided navigation: a lightweight energy compass evaluates candidate continuations at each midpoint, selecting the most coherent. All shaping is training-only; inference cost is unchanged. On 170M-parameter language modeling, the shaped student at 8 steps achieves 32% lower perplexity than the 1,024-step teacher while being 128x faster, with gains consistent across source distributions and three evaluators of increasing scale. TS-DFM achieves the best perplexity of any discrete-generation baseline we compare against, including methods trained on 6x more data or using 5x larger models.

</details>


### [255] [INO-SGD: Addressing Utility Imbalance under Individualized Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07930)
*Xiao Tian,Jue Fan,Rachael Hwee Ling Sim,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出INO-SGD算法，通过策略性地降低批次内数据权重，解决个性化差分隐私（IDP）中因隐私需求差异导致的效用不平衡问题，在满足IDP约束下提升高隐私需求数据的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化差分隐私（IDP）算法存在严重的效用不平衡问题：隐私需求更强的数据（如 stigmatized diseases 的阳性病例）在训练模型中代表性不足，导致后续部署中对相似数据性能较差。需要一种既能满足IDP约束又能缓解该问题的方法。

Method: 提出INO-SGD算法，在每次迭代中策略性地降低批次内数据的权重，以提升高隐私需求数据在模型中的表现。该算法专门设计以满足IDP约束，而现有解决效用不平衡的技术既不满足IDP也无法轻易适配。

Result: 通过实验验证了INO-SGD算法在满足IDP约束下的经验可行性，有效缓解了隐私需求差异导致的效用不平衡问题。

Conclusion: INO-SGD算法能够在个性化差分隐私框架下，通过批次内权重调整策略改善高隐私需求数据的模型性能，填补了现有方法在IDP场景下解决效用不平衡问题的空白。

Abstract: Differential privacy (DP) is widely employed in machine learning to protect confidential or sensitive training data from being revealed. As data owners gain greater control over their data due to personal data ownership, they are more likely to set their own privacy requirements, necessitating individualized DP (IDP) to fulfil such requests. In particular, owners of data from more sensitive subsets, such as positive cases of stigmatized diseases, likely set stronger privacy requirements, as leakage of such data could incur more serious societal impact. However, existing IDP algorithms induce a critical utility imbalance problem: Data from owners with stronger privacy requirements may be severely underrepresented in the trained model, resulting in poorer performance on similar data from subsequent users during deployment. In this paper, we analyze this problem and propose the INO-SGD algorithm, which strategically down-weights data within each batch to improve performance on the more private data across all iterations. Notably, our algorithm is specially designed to satisfy IDP, while existing techniques addressing utility imbalance neither satisfy IDP nor can be easily adapted to do so. Lastly, we demonstrate the empirical feasibility of our approach.

</details>


### [256] [Prototype Guided Post-pretraining for Single-Cell Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07938)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Natasha Darras,Sagar Kamarthi,Colles Price,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CellRefine是一种在单细胞基础模型预训练与微调之间进行的后训练方法，通过结合标记基因集作为结构先验的多目标优化来精炼细胞潜在嵌入流形，在多项计算生物学任务上实现了高达15%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞预训练模型受限于细胞类型分布的长尾特性和基因表达数据的协变量偏移，微调后的性能仍有瓶颈。

Method: 提出CellRefine，在预训练和微调阶段之间引入后训练步骤，采用结合标记基因集作为结构先验的多目标优化目标，精炼细胞的潜在嵌入流形。

Result: 在多项计算生物学任务上，CellRefine持续提升下游性能，增益高达15%。

Conclusion: CellRefine通过引入标记基因先验进行后训练，有效改善了单细胞基础模型的嵌入质量，克服了长尾分布和协变量偏移带来的泛化限制。

Abstract: Single-cell representation learning (SCRL) from gene expression data offers a way to uncover the complex regulatory logic underlying cellular function. Inspired by large language models in natural language modeling, several single-cell pretrained models have recently been proposed that treat genes as tokens and cells as sentences. However, these models are fundamentally limited by the long-tailed nature of cell-type distributions and struggle to generalize under covariate shifts in gene expression data. While fine-tuning is often used to mitigate these issues, we observe that performance remains bounded. To address this challenge, we introduce CellRefine, a post-pretraining method that operates between the pretraining and fine-tuning stages of a single-cell foundation model. CellRefine uses a multi-faceted objective that incorporates marker-gene sets as structural priors to guide post-pretraining and refine the latent embedding manifold of cells. Across multiple computational biology tasks, empirical results show that CellRefine consistently improves downstream performance, yielding gains up to 15%.

</details>


### [257] [Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics: Theory and Applications to Training-Free Guided Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07950)
*Atsushi Nitanda,Dake Bu,Yueming Lyu,Tanya Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究缓慢退火朗之万动力学（SALD）用于跟踪移动目标分布路径，并引入速度感知SALD（VA-SALD）实现无训练的引导生成，提供非渐近收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为训练-free的引导生成（如基于预训练得分模型的生成）提供理论框架和收敛保证，解决引导导致的额外偏差问题。

Method: 提出了SALD算法，通过时间减速跟踪移动目标分布；进一步引入VA-SALD，显式结合预训练模型的边际分布，利用减速纠正引导偏差。

Result: 建立了SALD的非渐近收敛保证（通过KL微分不等式），证明了减速通过中间目标的收缩改善跟踪效果；VA-SALD为扩散模型等生成模型族提供了无训练引导生成框架及收敛保证。

Conclusion: SALD和VA-SALD为基于得分的生成模型提供了理论完备的无训练引导生成方法，明确了中间目标泛函不等式和引导偏误的作用。

Abstract: We study Slowly Annealed Langevin Dynamics (SALD), a sampler for tracking a path of moving target distributions and approximating the terminal target through time slowdown. We establish non-asymptotic convergence guarantees via a KL differential inequality, showing that slowdown improves tracking through contraction of intermediate targets and the complexity of the path. Motivated by training-free guided generation with pretrained score-based generative models, we further introduce Velocity-Aware SALD (VA-SALD), which explicitly incorporates the underlying marginal distributions of the pretrained model and uses slowdown to correct the additional deviation induced by guidance. This yields a principled framework for training-free guided generation for diffusion-based and related generative model families, together with convergence guarantees that clarify the roles of intermediate functional inequalities and guidance bias. Code is available at https://github.com/anitan0925/sald.

</details>


### [258] [FLAM: Evaluating Model Performance with Aggregatable Measures in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07962)
*Fabian Stricker,Jose A. Peregrina,David Bermbach,Christian Zirpins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FLAM方法，通过可聚合度量解决联邦学习中分布式评估与集中式评估不一致的问题，无需全局测试集即可获得与集中评估相同的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中，由于数据分布在各参与者上，协调者依赖本地评估指标聚合来评估全局模型。但基于样本量的加权平均等常见聚合策略无法得到与集中评估一致的结果，且现有定义仅适用于准确率，无法泛化到其他评估指标，导致参与者评估与集中评估之间存在不一致，这与FL目标相悖。

Method: 通过分析分布式与集中评估不一致的根本原因，提出FLAM方法，基于可聚合度量实现性能评估，无需全局测试数据集即可获得与集中式评估一致的结果。

Result: FLAM方法能够在不依赖全局测试集的前提下，产生与集中式评估相同的评估结果，解决了现有聚合策略的评估偏差问题。

Conclusion: FLAM提供了一种通用的联邦学习性能评估方法，可泛化到多种评估指标，消除参与者评估与集中评估的不一致性，更准确地反映全局模型性能。

Abstract: Performance evaluation is essential for assessing the quality of machine learning (ML) models and guiding deployment decisions. In federated learning (FL), assessing the performance is challenging because data are distributed across participants. Consequently, the coordinator must rely on locally computed evaluation metrics and aggregate them to assess the global model. A key challenge is that common aggregation strategies, such as weighted averaging based on the local samples per participant, do not always produce the same results as centralized evaluation. Existing definitions of performance evaluation are largely tailored to accuracy and do not generalize to other metrics, leading to inconsistencies between participant-based and centralized evaluation. However, such discrepancies are inconsistent with the FL objective and lead to a wrong calculation of the metric.
  To address this issue, we examine the underlying reasons for these discrepancies and propose FLAM, a performance evaluation method based on aggregatable measures that yields the same results as centralized evaluation without the need for a global test dataset.

</details>


### [259] [Aggregation in conformal e-classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07963)
*Vladimir Vovk*

Main category: cs.LG
