TL;DR: 本文实验研究了交叉共形e预测及其简化改进版本，这些方法能更高效地聚合共形e预测器，在保持有效性的同时平衡预测与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测器的聚合是平衡预测效率与计算效率的标准方式，但传统方法在聚合时难以保持有效性。共形e预测器因其更易聚合且不牺牲有效性的特性而具有优势，因此需要研究其聚合方法（交叉共形e预测）及其改进。

Method: 通过实验方法研究交叉共形e预测（现有聚合方法）及其概念更简单、更灵活的改进版本。

Result: 交叉共形e预测及其改进版本能够有效聚合共形e预测器，在保持有效性的同时提供了更简单灵活的聚合方案。

Conclusion: 交叉共形e预测是聚合共形e预测器的有效方法，其改进版本在概念上更简单、更灵活，为实际应用提供了更好的选择。

Abstract: Aggregating conformal predictors is a standard way of balancing their predictive and computational efficiency while retaining their validity, at least approximately. An important advantage of conformal e-predictors is that they are easier to aggregate without sacrificing their validity. This paper studies experimentally cross-conformal e-prediction, which is an existing method of aggregating conformal e-predictors, and its modifications that are conceptually simpler and more flexible.

</details>


### [260] [When Diffusion Model Can Ignore Dimension: An Entropy-Based Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07969)
*Ahmad Aghapour,Erhan Bayraktar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明扩散模型的离散化误差受控于潜在混合成分的香农熵而非数据维度，解释了大维度下高效采样的原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型收敛理论大多以环境维度衡量离散化误差，无法解释实践中仅用少量反向步数即可高效处理高维数据（如图像）的现象，需要更合理的理论解释。

Method: 采用信息论视角，针对高斯混合目标分布，证明离散化误差由潜在混合成分的香农熵控制；同时将分析扩展到离散目标分布，证明复杂度由目标分布的熵决定。

Result: 扩散采样的主步骤复杂度与潜在熵呈线性关系，仅与数据二阶矩呈对数关系；在高斯混合和离散分布设定下，维度依赖性被替换为熵依赖性。

Conclusion: 当数据分布具有紧凑潜在表示时（如自然图像），扩散采样可保持高维空间中的高效性，理论解释了实践中观察到的现象。

Abstract: Diffusion models perform remarkably well on high-dimensional data such as images, often using only a modest number of reverse-time steps. Despite this practical success, existing convergence theory does not fully explain why such samplers remain efficient in high dimensions. Many prior KL guarantees bound the discretization error in terms of the ambient dimension, while other improved results replace this dependence using intrinsic-dimensional or geometric structure assumptions. In this work, we develop an alternative information-theoretic perspective on diffusion sampler convergence. We prove that, for Gaussian mixture targets, the discretization error is controlled by the Shannon entropy of the latent mixture component rather than by the ambient dimension. Consequently, the leading step complexity scales linearly with latent entropy and depends only logarithmically on the second moment of the data. Our analysis also extends to discrete target distributions, where the relevant complexity is the entropy of the target rather than the dimension of the embedding space. These results suggest that diffusion sampling can remain efficient in high-dimensional spaces when the data distribution admits a compact latent representation, as is widely believed to be the case for natural images.

</details>


### [261] [It Just Takes Two: Scaling Amortized Inference to Large Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07972)
*Antoine Wehenkel,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich,Chris Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦表示学习与后验建模的策略，通过最大规模为2的集合训练均值池化Deep Set编码器，使其泛化到任意集合大小，再对预聚合嵌入微调推理头，使训练成本与部署集合大小N基本无关。在标量、图像、多视角3D、分子和高维条件生成等基准测试中，该方法在数千N规模下以极低计算成本达到或超越标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经后验估计中，条件变量是一组观测值，元素之间因共享未知因素而相互依赖，需要联合处理整个集合。但按部署集合大小训练导致内存和计算开销随N急剧增长，难以扩展到大N场景。

Method: 训练阶段使用最大规模为2的集合训练均值池化Deep Set编码器，利用Deep Set的置换不变性和泛化能力，使编码器可处理任意大小集合；然后固定编码器，对预聚合的嵌入向量微调推理头（后验估计器），实现训练成本与N解耦。

Result: 在标量、图像、多视角3D、分子和高维条件生成等多个基准上，N可达数千规模，该方法以极低计算成本匹配或超越标准基线性能。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种简单、理论上有依据的策略，有效解决了神经后验估计中集合规模扩展的计算瓶颈，实现了训练成本与部署集合大小的近似无关性。

Abstract: Neural posterior estimation has emerged as a powerful tool for amortized inference, with growing adoption across scientific and applied domains. In many of these applications, the conditioning variable is a set of observations whose elements depend not only on the target but also on unknown factors shared across the set. Optimal inference therefore requires treating the set jointly, which in turn requires training the estimator at the deployment set size -- a regime where memory and compute quickly become prohibitive. We introduce a simple, theoretically grounded strategy that decouples representation learning from posterior modeling. Our method trains a mean-pool Deep Set on sets of size at most two, producing an encoder that generalizes to arbitrary set sizes. The inference head is then finetuned on pre-aggregated embeddings, making training cost essentially independent of the deployment set size N. Across scalar, image, multi-view 3D, molecular, and high-dimensional conditional generation benchmarks with N in the thousands, our approach matches or outperforms standard baselines at a fraction of the compute.

</details>


### [262] [Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement in Online Federated LLM Fine-Tuning with Real-Time Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07977)
*Seohyun Lee,Wenzhi Fang,Dong-Jun Han,Seyyedali Hosseinalipour,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPEAR是一种基于反馈引导自我博弈的在线联邦LLM微调算法，通过构建对比对进行最大似然学习和置信度加权的非似然学习，无需真实标注和分组生成即可高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反馈的学习方法受限于离线设置、需要特权真实上下文进行训练，且缺乏对联邦学习场景的考虑，无法满足资源受限边缘设备上高效训练的需求。

Method: SPEAR利用反馈引导的自我博弈循环，为每个提示构建自然对比对，在正确补全上执行标准最大似然学习，同时在错误补全的尾部token上执行置信度加权的非似然学习，以在线方式高效训练。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上，SPEAR相较于现有最先进基线方法表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: SPEAR无需昂贵的分组生成和真实上下文，仅需部分非答案反馈即可实现在线、资源高效的联邦LLM微调，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Recent works have advanced feedback-based learning systems, whereby a foundation model is able to intake incoming feedback (e.g., a user) to self-improve, creating a self-loop system of training. However, existing works are limited in needing to consider an offline setup to allow for such feedback-based methods, and are further limited in the need of requiring privileged ground-truth contexts for training. Moreover, there is limited consideration of federated learning (FL), which is particularly well-suited for incorporating external feedback across large networks of end users, for example, but requires methods to be efficient for training on resource-constrained edge devices. Therefore, we introduce SPEAR (Self-Play Enhancement via Advantage-Weighted Refinement), an efficient online learning algorithm for federated LLM fine-tuning. SPEAR utilizes a feedback-guided self-play loop to construct naturally contrastive pairs per prompt which are utilized to be trained on (i) standard maximum likelihood on correct completions and (ii) confidence-weighted unlikelihood on tail tokens of incorrect completions. Without the need of expensive group generations and ground-truth contexts for training (i.e., only partial, non-answer feedback), in contrast with existing works, SPEAR can be trained both online and in a resource-efficient manner. We validate SPEAR across various benchmark datasets, demonstrating its superior performance in comparison to state-of-the-art baselines. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/lee3296/SPEAR.

</details>


### [263] [Where's the Plan? Locating Latent Planning in Language Models with Lightweight Mechanistic Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07984)
*Nicole Ma,Nick Rui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在语言模型的前向传播过程中，未来约束标记的内部表征会在句边界处线性可解码（跨三个模型家族、十余种规模），但仅有 Gemma-3-27B 在因果层面依赖这种编码——在约第30层发生因果驱动从韵词到行边界的转移，并通过两阶段路径修补定位到5个注意力头，恢复了约90%的韵律路由能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在前向传播中是否形成面向未来约束标记（如押韵）的规划表征，以及这些表征是否因果性地驱动生成过程。

Method: 以押韵对联补全作为前向约束的干净测试任务，使用线性探针和激活修补两种轻量级方法，在 Qwen3、Gemma-3、Llama-3 三个模型家族的十余种规模上进行实验。

Result: 1) 未来韵律信息在行边界处可线性解码，且信号强度随模型规模增大而增强；2) 仅 Gemma-3-27B 在因果层面依赖这种编码，约第30层出现因果驱动从韵词到行边界的迁移；3) 其他模型全程依赖韵词表征，行边界处因果效应接近于零；4) 两阶段路径修补定位到5个注意力头，恢复约90%的韵律路由能力。

Conclusion: 语言模型普遍形成未来韵律的内部表征（可探针解码），但不同模型在因果依赖上存在显著差异——只有 Gemma-3-27B 真正利用行边界处的规划表征驱动生成，揭示了规划形成与因果利用之间的分离。

Abstract: We study planning site formation in language models -- where internal representations of structurally-constrained future tokens form during the forward pass, and whether they causally drive generation. Using rhyming-couplet completion as a clean test of forward-looking constraint, we apply two lightweight methods (linear probing and activation patching) across Qwen3, Gemma-3, and Llama-3 at more than ten scales. Probing shows that future-rhyme information is linearly decodable at the line boundary, with signal that strengthens with scale in all three families. Activation patching reveals that only Gemma-3-27B causally relies on this encoding, exhibiting a handoff in which the causal driver migrates from the rhyme word to the line boundary around layer 30. Every other model we test conditions on the rhyme word throughout generation, with near-zero causal effect at the line boundary despite strong probe signal. We localize the Gemma-3-27B handoff to five attention heads through two-stage path patching that recover ~90% of the rhyme-routing capacity at the newline.

</details>


### [264] [Bayesian Sensitivity of Causal Inference Estimators under Evidence-Based Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07993)
*Nikita Dhawan,Daniel Shen,Leonardo Cotta,Chris J. Maddison*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯敏感性值（BSV）框架，通过结合先验知识取代传统最坏情况敏感性分析，解决现有方法因依赖极端假设而导致的非信息性或反直觉问题，并在糖尿病治疗对体重影响的观察性研究中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有敏感性分析框架（如s-value）采用最坏情况准则，这通常会导致结论缺乏信息量，甚至与先验知识相矛盾。作者认为应利用真实世界的证据构建先验，从而获得更合理、更具信息量的敏感性评估。

Method: 将Gupta & Rothenhäusler (2023)的s-value框架推广至三种常见因果推断假设的敏感性分析；并进一步提出贝叶斯敏感性值（BSV），即基于真实世界证据构建先验分布，使用蒙特卡洛近似计算估计量对假设偏离的期望敏感性。

Result: 实证表明，最坏情况下的敏感性结论可能依赖于不切实际的数据生成过程变化；而BSV框架能提供更合理的敏感性评估。

Conclusion: BSV通过引入先验知识，克服了传统最坏情况敏感性分析过于悲观/不信息的问题，为观察性研究中的因果推断提供更实用的敏感性分析工具。

Abstract: Causal inference, especially in observational studies, relies on untestable assumptions about the true data-generating process. Sensitivity analysis helps us determine how robust our conclusions are when we alter these underlying assumptions. Existing frameworks for sensitivity analysis are concerned with worst-case changes in assumptions. In this work, we argue that using such pessimistic criteria can often become uninformative or lead to conclusions contradicting our prior knowledge about the world. To demonstrate this claim, we generalize the recent s-value framework (Gupta & Rothenhäusler, 2023) to estimate the sensitivity of three different common assumptions in causal inference. Empirically, we find that, indeed, worst-case conclusions about sensitivity can rely on unrealistic changes in the data-generating process. To overcome this, we extend the s-value framework with a new sensitivity analysis criterion: Bayesian Sensitivity Value (BSV), which computes the expected sensitivity of an estimate to assumption violations under priors constructed from real-world evidence. We use Monte Carlo approximations to estimate this quantity and illustrate its applicability in an observational study on the effect of diabetes treatments on weight loss.

</details>


### [265] [Graph-Structured Hyperdimensional Computing for Data-Efficient and Explainable Process-Structure-Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07999)
*Jingzhan Ge,Ajeeth Vellore,Ajinkya Palwe,Ahsan Khan,David Gorsich,Matthew P. Castanier,SeungYeon Kang,Farhad Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PSP-HDC是一种基于图结构超维度计算框架，通过编码有向PSP图作为内部先验，实现稀疏、异构、交互主导数据下的高保真3D微结构制造过程-结构-属性预测，在片电阻状态预测任务上达到0.910±0.077的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子光还原可实现复杂3D微结构的高保真制造，但可靠的PSP预测面临数据稀疏、异构且交互主导的挑战；传统特征向量模型统计欠定，易产生虚假相关和跨域泛化差，而机理模型依赖校准子模型，在早期工艺开发中难以获得。

Method: 提出PSP-HDC框架：1）可训练标量-超向量编码器在固定超维基上学习参数特定嵌入；2）通过图对齐的捆绑与绑定沿有向PSP依赖组合样本表示；3）通过联想记忆检索与类原型进行预测；4）利用相同原型记忆实现参数级、组级和组内级的固有解释。

Result: 在3D平台的片电阻状态预测任务上，PSP-HDC在1000次随机分割上达到0.910±0.077的准确率，在工艺折叠泛化下达到0.896，优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: PSP-HDC通过图结构的超维计算框架有效解决了稀疏异构数据下的PSP预测问题，提供了更高的准确率和固有的可解释性。

Abstract: Multiphoton photoreduction enables high-fidelity fabrication of complex 3D microstructures, yet reliable process-structure-property (PSP) prediction remains difficult because the available data are sparse, heterogeneous, and interaction-dominated. In this regime, conventional feature-vector models are statistically underdetermined, making them prone to spurious correlations, poor regime transfer, and unstable post hoc explanations, whereas mechanistic pipelines depend on calibrated submodels that are rarely available during early process development. We present PSP-HDC, a graph-structured hyperdimensional computing framework that encodes a directed PSP graph as an internal prior for representation, inference, and explanation. A trainable scalar-to-hypervector encoder learns parameter-specific embeddings on a fixed hyperdimensional basis to accommodate heterogeneous scales and noise. Sample representations are then composed through graph-aligned binding and bundling along directed PSP dependencies, and prediction is performed by associative-memory retrieval against class prototypes. Because the same prototype memories support both decision making and attribution, PSP-HDC provides intrinsic explanations at the parameter, group, and within-group levels, while memory alignment and separation quantify prototype formation during training. On sheet-resistance regime prediction for the 3D platform, PSP-HDC achieves an accuracy of 0.910 +/- 0.077 over 1000 random splits and 0.896 under process-fold generalization, outperforming strong baselines.

</details>


### [266] [Position: Mechanistic Interpretability Must Disclose Identification Assumptions for Causal Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08012)
*Zezheng Lin,Fengming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对机械可解释性领域中的因果性词汇使用进行了审计，发现10篇论文均缺乏明确的识别假设说明，存在用验证指标（如忠实度、完整性等）替代因果识别的普遍问题。作者提出了一种披露规范，要求明确区分验证与因果识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械可解释性论文越来越多地使用因果性语言（如电路、中介、因果抽象、单语义性），但这些因果主张需要明确的识别假设。现有文献普遍缺乏对识别假设的系统性说明，存在将验证指标误当作因果支持的倾向。

Method: 对四个方法论流派的10篇论文进行有目的的审计，检查是否存在专门的识别假设章节；同时进行两人编码者审计（n=30），验证主要发现的稳健性。

Result: 10篇审计论文均未设置专门的识别假设章节；验证指标（忠实度、完整性、单语义性、对齐度、消融效应）被报告为因果支持，但未说明使其具有识别性的假设；两人编码者审计复现了主要发现方向，但精确计数对编码规则敏感。

Conclusion: 验证不等于识别。论文提出披露规范：声明主张是否为因果性、命名识别策略、列举假设、强调至少一个假设、说明假设不成立时结论如何变化。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability papers increasingly use causal vocabulary: circuits, mediators, causal abstraction, monosemanticity. Such claims require explicit identification assumptions. A purposive audit of 10 papers across four methodological strands finds no dedicated identification-assumptions section and a recurring pattern: validation metrics such as faithfulness, completeness, monosemanticity, alignment, or ablation effects are reported as causal support without stating the assumptions that make them identifying. A two-human-coder audit on $n=30$ reproduces the direction of the main finding: dedicated identification sections are absent, and validation-metric substitution is common, though exact Dim B/D counts are coding-rule sensitive. The paper proposes a disclosure norm: state whether the claim is causal, name the identification strategy, enumerate assumptions, stress at least one, and explain how conclusions shift if assumptions fail. Validation is not identification.

</details>


### [267] [Don't Get Your Kroneckers in a Twist: Gaussian Processes on High-Dimensional Incomplete Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08036)
*Mads Greisen Højlund,August Smart Lykke-Møller,Henry Moss,Ove Christiansen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CUTS-GPR是一种用于高维设置下数值精确高斯过程回归的新方法，通过结合加性核与不完全网格实现极快的核矩阵-向量乘积，具有近线性甚至线性计算复杂度，成功应用于计算化学中的高维势能面贝叶斯建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决高维空间中高斯过程回归计算成本过高的问题，满足计算化学等领域对高维势能面贝叶斯建模的迫切需求。

Method: 将加性核与不完全网格相结合，利用核矩阵的特定结构实现高效的矩阵-向量乘积，使得计算复杂度随训练数据量N呈近线性甚至线性增长，随维度D呈低阶多项式增长。

Result: 在数十亿数据点和数千维度的基准测试中验证了矩阵-向量乘积的可扩展性；对于N=447,265和D=24的完整GPR计算（包括超参数优化）仅需数小时即可完成。

Conclusion: CUTS-GPR实现了高维数值精确的高斯过程回归，为计算化学中高维势能面的贝叶斯建模提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce CUTS-GPR, a new method for performing numerically exact Gaussian process regression (GPR) in high-dimensional settings. The key component of CUTS-GPR is an extremely fast kernel matrix-vector product, which exhibits near-linear or even linear scaling with the amount of training data, $N$, and low-order polynomial scaling with dimensionality, $D$. This is obtained by combining an additive kernel with an incomplete grid and exploiting the resulting structure of the kernel matrix. We demonstrate the scalability of the matrix-vector product by running benchmarks with billions of data points and thousands of dimensions. Full GPR calculations, including hyperparameter optimization, are completed in a matter of hours for $N = 447 265$ and $D = 24$. We demonstrate that our CUTS-GPR enables Bayesian modeling of high-dimensional potential energy surfaces - a longstanding challenge in computational chemistry.

</details>


### [268] [Beyond Pairs: Your Language Model is Secretly Optimizing a Preference Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08037)
*Ning Liu,Chuanneng Sun,Kristina Klinkner,Shervin Malmasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphDPO通过将有向无环偏好图引入DPO框架，利用多次rollout之间的排序关系进行偏好对齐，相比传统成对DPO更高效且能保持传递性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成对DPO无法利用多次rollout中的丰富偏好结构，将其分解为独立对会丢失传递性、引入冗余或冲突监督，导致优化不稳定。

Method: 提出GraphDPO，在有向无环偏好图上操作，以优势关系为边，优化基于图结构的Plackett-Luce目标函数，通过log-sum-exp聚合实现线性复杂度，并引入等价类构造处理离散/稀疏信号，以及可选的ground-truth锚定与退火调度。

Result: 在推理和程序合成任务上取得优于成对和列表式对齐目标的性能。

Conclusion: 图结构偏好建模是一种可扩展且鲁棒的偏好对齐方法，相比成对和列表式目标更具优势。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) aligns language models using pairwise preference comparisons, offering a simple and effective alternative to Reinforcement Learning (RL) from human feedback. However, in many practical settings, training data consists of multiple rollouts per prompt, inducing rich preference structure that pairwise DPO fails to exploit. Collapsing such data into independent pairs discards transitivity, introduces redundant or conflicting supervision, and can lead to unstable optimization. We propose Graph Direct Preference Optimization (GraphDPO), a principled generalization of DPO that operates over directed acyclic preference graphs induced by rollout rankings. GraphDPO encodes dominance relations as edges and optimizes a graph-structured Plackett--Luce-inspired objective that aggregates supervision over graph neighborhoods, enforcing transitivity while recovering standard DPO as a special case. To handle discrete or sparse signals, we introduce an equivalence-class construction where responses with identical preferences form graph layers, and intra-layer edges contribute zero loss, preventing spurious gradients. Despite leveraging full graph structure, GraphDPO maintains linear per-prompt complexity via efficient log-sum-exp aggregation. We further incorporate optional ground-truth anchoring by inserting verified solutions as dominant nodes and applying an annealed schedule that stabilizes early training while gradually relaxing oracle supervision. Experiments on reasoning and program synthesis tasks demonstrate superior performance, suggesting that graph-structured preference modeling is a scalable and robust alternative to pairwise and listwise alignment objectives.

</details>


### [269] [Reinforcement Learning for Exponential Utility: Algorithms and Convergence in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08053)
*Gugan Thoppe,L. A. Prashanth,Ankur Naskar,Sanjay Bhat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对指数效用函数下的折扣MDP，提出了两种基于Q学习的无模型强化学习算法，分别采用双时间尺度和单时间尺度更新，并提供了理论收敛性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数效用优化下的折扣MDP缺乏基于值函数的强化学习算法，作者旨在填补这一空白，为风险厌恶场景提供可理论保障的值函数方法。

Method: 基于Porteus提出的指数效用Bellman方程，推导出两种Q值形式的扩展，证明其算子在L∞和sup-log/Thompson度量下是压缩映射；进而提出双时间尺度Q学习算法（证明几乎必然收敛并给出有限时间收敛率）和单时间尺度算法（基于亚线性幂律算子，利用局部Lipschitz连续性、单调性、齐次性和Dini导数证明收敛）。

Result: 证明了两种Q值算子的不动点性质及贪心策略的最优性；双时间尺度算法具有几乎必然收敛和有限时间收敛率；单时间尺度算法在标量情形下提供了有限时间分析，揭示了向量情形下获取收敛率的挑战。

Conclusion: 本文为指数效用目标下的值函数强化学习奠定了理论基础，提供了可收敛的无模型算法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for exponential-utility optimization in discounted Markov decision processes (MDPs) lacks principled value-based algorithms. We address this gap in the fixed risk-aversion setting. Building on the Bellman-type equation for exponential utility studied in \cite{porteus1975optimality}, we derive two Q-value-style extensions and show that the associated operators are contractions in the $L_\infty$ and sup-log/Thompson metrics, respectively. We characterize their fixed points and prove that the induced greedy stationary policy is optimal for the exponential-utility objective among stationary policies. These structural results lead to two model-free algorithms: a two-timescale Q-learning--style algorithm, for which we establish almost-sure convergence and provide finite-time convergence rates via timescale separation, and a one-timescale algorithm governed by a sublinear power-law operator. Since the latter does not admit a global contraction in standard metrics, we prove its convergence using delicate arguments based on local Lipschitzness, monotonicity, homogeneity, and Dini derivatives, and provide a scalar finite-time analysis that highlights the challenges in obtaining convergence rates in the vector case. Our work provides a foundation for value-based RL under exponential-utility objectives.

</details>


### [270] [Zero-Shot Imagined Speech Decoding via Imagined-to-Listened MEG Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08075)
*Maryam Maghsoudi,Shihab Shamma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用更易获取的聆听MEG数据来辅助解码想象语音的三阶段方法，通过在训练有素的音乐家中采集成对的聆听和想象MEG记录，证明想象词汇的解码显著高于随机水平，且性能随训练数据规模提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象语音解码面临两大挑战：想象数据集稀缺且难以在跨被试和跨会话间实现时间对齐。本文旨在利用更丰富且标注更可靠的聆听语音记录来帮助解想象语音。

Method: 1) 采集训练有素的音乐家在节奏性旋律和口语刺激下的配对聆听与想象MEG记录；2) 三阶段解码流程：阶段一训练6个线性和神经模型将想象MEG映射到聆听MEG；阶段二基于聆听MEG训练对比性词汇解码器（使用语义、声学和音素四种嵌入策略）；阶段三将留出被试的想象MEG通过映射管道生成对应聆听响应，再由聆听解码器解码。

Result: 基于秩分析证明想象词汇的解码显著高于随机水平；性能随训练数据规模提升，表明该方法具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法可直接应用于现实的脑机接口场景，为想象语音解码提供了一种可扩展的有效途径。

Abstract: Decoding imagined speech from non-invasive brain recordings is challenging because imagined datasets are scarce and difficult to align temporally across subjects and sessions In this work, we propose a new approach to the decoding of imagined speech that leverages the richer and more reliably labeled recordings during listening to speech. We collected paired listened and imagined MEG recordings to rhythmic melodic and spoken stimuli from trained musicians. Using trained musicians helped improve temporal alignment across conditions. We then developed a three-stage decoding pipeline that revealed consistent and meaningful relationships between neural activity evoked by imagining and listening to the same stimuli. First, we trained six linear and neural models to map imagined MEG responses to listened responses. We evaluated these models against a null baseline from unseen subjects to validate that the predicted-listening responses preserve stimulus-specific information. In the second stage, we trained a contrastive word decoder exclusively on the listened MEG responses, and evaluated it using four embedding strategies including semantic, acoustic, and phonetic representations. In the third stage, we process the imagined MEG responses from held-out subjects through the mapping pipeline to compute the corresponding listening responses that are then decoded by the listened decoder. Using rank-based analysis, we show that the imagined words are decodable significantly above chance. We shall report here the results of a proof-of-concept implementation to decode imagined speech, where all evaluations are performed on held-out subjects. We also demonstrate that performance improves with training data size, suggesting that this approach is scalable and can directly be made applicable to realistic brain-computer interface scenarios.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Post-Moore Technologies for Plasma Simulation: A Community Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07722)
*Luca Pennati,Erik M. Åsgrim,Jeremy J. Williams,Stefan Costea,David Tskhakaya,Leon Kos,Ales Podolnik,Yi Ju,Tapish Narwal,Julian Lenz,Michael Bussmann,Urs Ganse,Minna Palmroth,Kallia Chronaki,Vassilis Papaefstathiou,Etienne Renault,Felix Jung,Martin Schulz,Valentin Seitz,Marta Garcia-Gasulla,Filippo Mantovani,Frank Jenko,Erwin Laure,Stefano Markidis*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文从社区视角评估了后摩尔时代三类加速技术（可重构/数据路径加速器、非冯·诺依曼架构、量子计算）在等离子体模拟中的应用潜力，发现没有单一技术能取代现有HPC平台，而是呈现出三个层级的机会：FPGA类加速器用于近期的核卸载和工作流数据服务，非冯·诺依曼架构作为中期的算子级加速方向，量子计算虽最不成熟但最具颠覆性潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着通用处理器扩展放缓，等离子体模拟面临数据移动、内存访问和功耗瓶颈，亟需探索后摩尔时代的加速技术来支持这一计算密集型科学负载。

Method: 采用协同设计方法，针对粒子网格法、连续Vlasov、回旋动理学、流体/MHD、混合以及温稠密物质方法等代表性等离子体工作负载，系统评估三类加速技术。

Result: 无单一技术能全面替代现有HPC平台；FPGA类加速器适合近期核卸载，非冯·诺依曼架构面向中期算子加速，量子计算虽最不成熟但在温稠密物质和惯性约束聚变微观物理领域最具颠覆潜力。

Conclusion: 提出了选择性采纳的最佳实践，并指出聚焦演示验证、基准测试和模块化软件生态是当前社区的首要任务。

Abstract: Plasma simulations are among the most computationally demanding scientific workloads, combining high-dimensional kinetic evolution, particle-mesh coupling, field solves, and data-intensive communication. As general-purpose processor scaling slows, post-Moore technologies are being explored to address bottlenecks in data movement, memory access, and power consumption. This paper provides a community perspective on the role of these technologies in plasma simulation, assessing three major classes: reconfigurable and data-path accelerators, non-von Neumann architectures, and quantum computing. Each is evaluated, in a co-design approach, against representative plasma workloads spanning particle-in-cell, continuum Vlasov, gyrokinetic, fluid/MHD, hybrid, and warm dense matter methods. We find that no single technology can replace existing HPC platforms. Instead, three tiers of opportunity emerge: FPGA-class and data-path accelerators offer near-term kernel offload and workflow-level data services, non-von Neumann architectures represent medium-term directions for operator-level acceleration, and quantum computing, although the least mature, is potentially the most disruptive for warm dense matter and inertial confinement fusion microphysics. We outline best practices for selective adoption and identify focused demonstrators, benchmarking, and modular software ecosystems as immediate community priorities.

</details>


### [272] [Per-Phase Fidelity Attribution for Quantum Compilers using HBR Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07876)
*Chandrachud Pati,Yogesh Simmhan*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出HBR分解模型，用于量化量子编译流水线各阶段（高层结构分解H、基翻译B、路由R）的保真度损失。在IBM Heron和IonQ Forte两种后端上评估了三种SDK（Qiskit、PennyLane、TKET）和八种算法。发现编译瓶颈强烈依赖于电路类型：搜索类电路中路由占60%保真度损失，哈密顿模拟中综合起主导作用。SDK在诊断优化级别（opt=0）和生产级别（opt=2）的排名会反转。HBR正确预测了SDK在噪声模拟和真实IBM硬件上的排名排序，揭示了聚合基准测试无法观察的阶段级瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子编译器基准测试仅报告聚合后编译指标，无法定位编译流水线中保真度损失的具体阶段，缺乏细粒度归因方法。

Method: 提出HBR分解模型，将编译流水线拆分为H（高层结构分解）、B（基翻译）、S（综合，原文隐含于B阶段）、R（路由）四个阶段，量化各阶段相对保真度损失。在IBM Heron（重六边形）和IonQ Forte（全连接）两种拓扑上评估Qiskit、PennyLane、TKET三种SDK及八种算法。

Result: 路由在搜索类电路中占60%保真度损失；综合在哈密顿模拟中占主导。早期综合选择会放大或压缩后续路由开销。SDK在opt=0和opt=2下的排名反转。HBR正确预测了噪声模拟（8电路×3 SDK×2层级）和真实IBM Fez硬件上的SDK排名排序。

Conclusion: HBR分解模型能够揭示聚合编译器基准测试无法观测的阶段级瓶颈，不同电路类型有截然不同的编译瓶颈，SDK诊断结果与生产结果回答不同问题，建议编译器开发者关注特定阶段的优化。

Abstract: Quantum compilers sit between an algorithm's theoretical promise and what executes on physical hardware. Existing benchmarks report aggregate post-transpilation metrics but cannot attribute where fidelity is lost within the compilation pipeline. We present HBR decomposition, a per-phase fidelity attribution model that quantifies relative fidelity loss across High-level structural decomposition (H), Basis translation (B), and Routing (R). We evaluate three production SDKs (Qiskit, PennyLane, TKET) across eight algorithms on two backend topologies: IBM Heron (heavy-hex) and IonQ Forte (all-to-all). The dominant compiler bottleneck is strongly circuit-class dependent: Routing accounts for up to 60% of relative fidelity loss in search-class circuits, while synthesis dominates Hamiltonian simulation workloads. Early synthesis choices amplify or compress downstream routing overhead depending on circuit connectivity. SDK rankings at diagnostic optimization level (opt=0) reverse at production levels (opt=2) for deep circuits, showing that stagewise diagnostics and production results answer different questions. HBR correctly predicts SDK rank ordering across noisy simulations (8 circuits x 3 SDKs x 2 tiers) and real IBM Fez hardware executions, revealing stage-specific bottlenecks that are not observable through aggregate compiler benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.SC'></div>

# cs.SC [[Back]](#toc)

### [273] [Relating the Computational and Logical Difficulty of Solving ODEs: From Polynomial to Discontinuous Right-Hand Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07128)
*Olivier Bournez,Alonso Núñez*

Main category: cs.SC

TL;DR: 本文通过逆数学的视角，证明常微分方程初值问题的计算复杂性可由右端函数$f$的正则性（光滑性）刻画，形成从多项式时间可解到超限计算的分层结构，从而统一分析符号计算、模拟计算和可计算分析三大传统中的计算障碍与可行性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机代数系统求解常微分方程失败时，无法区分是实现的局限性还是根本性的计算障碍。符号计算、模拟计算和可计算分析三大传统对同一问题的结论看似矛盾，但这种矛盾往往源于编码方式的不同而非算法内容的本质差异。需要一个与表示无关（representation-invariant）的框架来直接比较算法内容。

Method: 采用逆数学（reverse mathematics）作为与表示无关的分析工具，以Big Five层次结构为参照，证明经典ODE理论中的自然命题精确对应于该层次的每一层。通过分析右端函数$f$的正则性作为内在算法不变量，将初值问题划分为不同的计算层级。

Result: 证明了Big Five层次结构的每一层都存在来自经典ODE理论的自然命题作为精确等价关系。$f$的正则性构成了从多项式时间可解到超限计算的计算层级。通过抽象去表示依赖，分离了根本性障碍与符号求解器的技术缺陷、模拟编码的人为产物以及可计算分析的有效性约束。

Conclusion: 该分层可作为三大传统共享的实用诊断工具，识别出恢复可计算性的内在参数（长度界、收敛半径、连续性模量），为理解ODE计算复杂性的本质提供了统一框架。

Abstract: When a computer algebra system fails to solve an Ordinary Differential Equation, is this a limitation of its implementation, or a genuine computational barrier?
  Three traditions bear on the question. Modern computer algebra algorithms can be extremely efficient: Newton-type methods solve polynomial ODEs over $\mathbb{Q}[[X]]$ in quasi-linear time. Analog models of computation has shown that polynomial ODEs and Turing machines are two presentations of the same phenomenon, with solution length acting as time and precision as space. Computable analysis shows that ODEs can be intrinsically hard -- undecidable, even $\mathsf{PSPACE}$-complete, over compact domains. Comparing these traditions is natural and necessary, yet such comparisons routinely reduce to comparisons of encodings rather than of underlying algorithmic content.
  We argue that reverse mathematics provides a representation-invariant lens in which algorithmic content is compared directly. We prove that every level of the Big Five hierarchy is inhabited by a natural statement from classical ODE theory, as an exact equivalence: the regularity of $f$ is an intrinsic algorithmic invariant placing the initial value problem $y'(t)=f(t,y(t))$, $y(t_0)=y_0$, into one of several computational strata, ranging from polynomial-time solvability to transfinite computation.
  The resulting stratification acts as a practical diagnostic common to the three traditions. By abstracting from representation, it separates fundamental barriers from the technical shortcomings of symbolic solvers, the artefacts of analog encodings, and the effectivity constraints of computable analysis, identifying the intrinsic parameters (length bounds, radii of convergence, moduli of continuity) under which feasibility is restored.

</details>

### [274] [Broken-symmetry shape discrimination on a driven Duffing ring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07475)
*Kaspar Anton Schindler*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文在环图（cycle graph）这一最简单的连续对称封闭基底上，通过单一主运动方程的两个参数区间，研究了分布式计算基底的两种基本操作——绑定（binding）和捆绑（bundling）。线性区间将时间输入按U(1)对称性组织到特征模态上；Duffing区间激活三次模式混合操作，产生依赖输入形状的谐波内容。作者识别出一个单值可观测量φ₀，其对称性结构（π周期性精确，时间反演对称性被耗散破缺）使其能编码绑定与耗散对输入形状的联合响应。数值实验表明φ₀在加性带限噪声下（低至0 dB SNR）仍能保持信息内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究分布式计算基底上捆绑（存储可独立检索的组件）与绑定（组合成依赖关系的输出）这两种基本操作，探索在具有连续对称性的简单封闭基底上这些操作如何运作及其信息表征能力。

Method: 在N节点环图（U(1)对称基底）上构建单一主运动方程，分别在线性区间和Duffing（三次非线性）区间进行分析。通过对称性分析、特征模态分解、数值实验等方法，研究系统的绑定与捆绑操作，并识别单值可观测量φ₀的对称性结构。

Result: 线性区间实现了类似窗口FFT的特征表示，高SNR下与FFT基线持平，低SNR瞬态信号略优；Duffing区间通过对称性约束的稀疏选择规则产生线性区间无法实现的形状相关谐波；φ₀具有精确的π周期性，而时间反演对称性被基底耗散破缺；φ₀在0 dB SNR下仍能保持信息内容。

Conclusion: φ₀作为一个有意义的单值可观测量，其对称性（一个精确、一个被耗散破缺）的非对称状态是其信息编码能力的关键。该框架目前仅针对合成信号，向更丰富基底、更复杂驱动和真实生物信号的扩展是未来工作方向。

Abstract: Distributed computational substrates rely on two elementary operations: bundling, the act of populating a shared physical medium with independently retrievable components, and binding, the act of composing components into outputs whose identity depends on their relations. We study these two primitives on the simplest closed substrate carrying a continuous symmetry, a cycle graph of N nodes, in two parameter regimes of a single master equation of motion. The linear regime sorts a temporal input across the substrate's U(1)-organised eigenmodes, providing a feature representation that matches a windowed-FFT baseline at high signal-to-noise ratio and modestly outperforms it for transient signals at low SNR. The Duffing regime activates a cubic mode-mixing operation constrained by the substrate's symmetry into a sparse selection rule on integer wavenumbers, generating shape-dependent harmonic content that the linear regime cannot produce. We identify a single-number observable, $φ_0$, that summarises the bound representation's response to input shape, and we analyse its symmetry structure: a $π$-periodicity in the shape parameter is exact, while a time-reversal symmetry that would render $φ_0$ degenerate is broken by the substrate's dissipation. The asymmetric status of these two symmetries is what licenses $φ_0$ as a meaningful single-number observable; its trajectory across the quotient domain encodes the joint response of binding and dissipation to the input shape. Numerical experiments confirm that $φ_0$ retains its information content under additive band-limited noise, with seed-averaged means staying clearly above the symmetric-attractor value down to 0 dB input SNR. The framework is developed on synthetic signals only; extensions to richer substrates, more elaborate drives, and real biological signals are open questions for the work that follows.

</details>
